# Chapter 200: The raft overturns along the Yellow Dragon River

Spanning eight thousand kilometers, the Yellow Dragon River flows past Mount Qing Mao and Mount Bai Gu before emptying into the ocean.

The battered green raft was in desperate need of repair, its crude mast and white sail surrounded by stabilizing cargo. The bamboos were bound with hemp ropes, looped hastily in multiple places as the raft sailed downriver.

The fragile raft carried an ordinary youth in black robes and a fairy-like woman with silver hair and blue eyes.

They were Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

"It's been five days; that old guy probably isn't coming," Fang Yuan muttered, glancing back from the raft.

She laughed loudly. "Why worry? If that old geezer chases us, we'll fight to the death. Dying in the stomachs of fish sounds interesting too!"

After five days of sailing, they were nearing Bai Gu Mountain.

According to his memories, a Rank four righteous Gu Master had left a secret inheritance in Bai Gu Mountain for someone fated.

"I've only heard of this Bai Gu Mountain inheritance, never seen it myself. But rumors say its obstacles require two people to coordinate to pass."

Ta.

"What was that sound?" Fang Yuan immediately furrowed his brows.

Bai Ning Bing listened closely, doubtful: "What sound? I didn't hear anything."

Roots sprouted from Fang Yuan's ears, immediately picking up a rapid rattling as the bamboo raft began shaking.

A black streak shot from beside the raft, brushing past Bai Ning Bing.

"What the hell is this!" she cursed, looking up to see a spindle-shaped black fish fall back into the river.

"Shuttle Swordfish! Damn it, head for the shore!" Fang Yuan yelled, hauling the sail.

Shaped like shuttles with sharp ends, Shuttle Swordfish roamed in vast packs, hunting prey far larger than themselves.

The raft shook violently as numerous swordfish slammed into it, their relentless piercings pushing the bamboo to the brink of collapse.

The fish attacked relentlessly. Bai Ning Bing struggled helplessly; her self-detonation had frozen all her Gu worms to death, and they could not be revived.

Sky Canopy Gu!

Fang Yuan forcefully activated the Rank three Gu, rapidly draining his aperture's primeval essence.

This was despite his ninety percent A-grade aptitude and the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

Dull thuds echoed as the swordfish struck the white armor harmlessly, while the injured Bai Ning Bing frantically dodged. She sheltered behind Fang Yuan, letting him absorb the brunt of the assault.

As the Shuttle Swordfish relented, Bai Ning Bing watched tensely. "We've reached the shallows; they won't follow us here. Huff! We're safe for now." Fang Yuan exhaled turbid air, his aching body on the verge of collapse.

Sky Canopy Gu had drained his aperture dry within minutes; without it, his flesh could never withstand the swordfish.

Splashing through the shallows, the two stepped onto the soft beach, finally ashore.

Clutching her wounds, Bai Ning Bing slumped down, pale-faced. "I'll bleed to death at this rate! Bring out your healing Gu, quick!"

Fang Yuan smiled bitterly; how could he produce a healing Gu from thin air?

# Chapter 201: Resting at the shallow beach while lending Gu worms

Five days after his rebirth at Qing Mao Mountain, the injured Gu worms Fang Yuan had looted from the blood barrier had all perished one by one from lack of food while floating downriver.

"A pity my Gu perished in the detonation, or I wouldn't be hurt like this..." Bai Ning Bing sighed to the sky.

Fang Yuan laughed, "Don't be pessimistic—not everything requires Gu worms."

Fang Yuan channeled primeval essence to summon the lantern-like Tusita Flower.

"The powder reduces inflammation, and the paste stops bleeding," Fang Yuan explained, dividing the mortal medicines and handing half to Bai Ning Bing.

"Mortal remedies, though I learned them at the academy," Bai Ning Bing took them, pouting. "You certainly planned ahead."

Fang Yuan glanced at her, then used the Tusita Flower to retrieve spare clothes he had stocked up beforehand.

Bai Ning Bing changed, tossing her shredded white robe onto the sand. Her expression soured; though safe for now, bandaging and dressing made her keenly aware of her transformed body.

"What's the plan now? When will you return the Yang Gu?" she demanded, walking over with a frown.

Fang Yuan picked up his discarded clothes. "As I said, we head to Bai Gu Mountain. As for the Yang Gu, wait until I reach Rank three."

Bai Ning Bing's frown deepened, her voice rising: "Wait until you're Rank three?"

Fang Yuan looked at her in silence, then walked to the riverbank to wash his clothes.

Clever as she was, Fang Yuan's silence made her realize her helpless predicament.

She had Rank three cultivation but no Gu worms. Even if she had them, she couldn't harm Fang Yuan—he had refined the Yang Gu and could detonate it with a single thought.

The Yin Yang Rotation Gu were a pair; if destroyed, Bai Ning Bing could never regain his male body!

Fang Yuan added, "It's getting late. We'll camp here tonight and enter the forest tomorrow. Flanked by cliffs on three sides, this spot is relatively safe from beasts, but we must still take turns keeping watch."

Fang Yuan willed, and summoned the Chainsaw Golden Centipede and Sky Canopy Gu.

"I'll lend you these two Gu for now. Familiarize yourself with them," Fang Yuan said.

At Rank one initial stage, he could hardly wield Rank three Gu. Even with A-grade talent and the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, he could never unleash their true power.

Bai Ning Bing took the Gu, eyeing Fang Yuan meaningfully.

Though forced by circumstances, Fang Yuan's boldness only heightened Bai Ning Bing's vigilance.

# Chapter 202: Crocodile killing intent, a young girl’s sorrowful call

"Chainsaw Golden Centipede..." Bai Ning Bing murmured, stroking the Rank three Gu's dark golden carapace with mixed emotions.

"Sky Canopy Gu!" Placing it into her aperture, she infused white silver primeval essence with raised brows, instantly enveloping herself in white light armor.

Fang Yuan sat silently cross-legged beside the warm embers, eyes closed.

Inside his aperture, a full A-grade primeval sea spanned before his mind—ninety percent!

"Dropping from Rank three to Rank one wiped out years of effort, but it was worth it!" Fang Yuan felt satisfied.

"With the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus solving my resource needs, I have nothing to worry about before reaching Rank three." Within his ninety percent green copper sea, a blue-and-white lotus was rooted in the aperture's depths, its lush petals exuding a sacred, immortal aura.

While Rank three Gu Masters used white silver essence, Fang Yuan possessed only Rank one green copper essence. Yet with the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, his recovery speed reached astonishing heights.

"Using only Rank one Gu, my recovery makes my primeval essence nearly inexhaustible. One or two Rank two Gu will drain the sea, but the recovery will keep it relatively stable. However, a Rank three Gu consumes essence far faster than I can recover, draining my sea dry in moments," Fang Yuan calculated.

First of all was his vital gu: Spring Autumn Cicada.

After this second rebirth, its brilliant yellow-green light had vanished, leaving it dispirited and terribly frail.

Fang Yuan understood: "I cannot use the Spring Autumn Cicada anytime soon. In this weakened state, entering the River of Time would destroy it, turning my attempt into suicidal self-detonation."

Reviewing his aperture, crucially, he still lacked both healing and movement Gu.

"I need to capture wild Gu to resolve these gaps. If only I could find suitable ones... Escaping that school of Shuttle Swordfish was fortunate enough. But there is no way I could be so lucky every time." Sorting out his thoughts, he slowly opened his eyes.

He saw Bai Ning Bing walking over, holding a white eggshell.

"Look at this. I drilled into the sand testing the Chainsaw Golden Centipede and found an egg buried here—shame it got crushed," Bai Ning Bing said.

Fang Yuan glanced at the shell, his face tensing. "Bad news—this is a six-legged crocodile egg. Is this beach their breeding ground?"

Bai Ning Bing's eyes widened as she pointed behind him. "Too late, look!"

Fang Yuan turned to see hundreds of 'floating logs' drifting toward the beach.

The six-legged crocodiles hissed furiously, scuttling toward the two youths.

Meanwhile, amidst the ruins of Gu Yue village on Qing Mao Mountain...

"Father..." Tie Ruo Nan wept on her knees in the snow, calling out in grief.

Tie family reinforcements had arrived in her moment of peril, summoned beforehand by Tie Xue Leng's precautionary letter.

Divine investigator had fallen; her father had died!

# Chapter 203: Each with their own objectives

"The Divine Investigator fell in battle—a glorious end for a Tie family man. Lady Ruo Nan, my condolences," a young Gu Master consoled.

The young Gu Master frowned and continued, "We scoured the battlefield and found traces of several survivors. The Divine Investigator's letter also mentioned the Blood Sea inheritance. I worry the demonic inheritor of the Blood Sea might be among them."

Rising from the frozen ground amidst bitter winds, her haggard face hardened. "Avenging my father comes before all else. Whoever did this, I will uncover the truth!"

The young Gu Master sighed, "We will investigate—no Tie clansman dies unavenged. But Lady Ruo Nan, the clan leader ordered us to bring you safely back to the village."

Tie Ruo Nan glared, eyes wide. "What? Don't dream of chasing me away!"

As Tie Ruo Nan began to protest, her pupils suddenly shrank. "You..."

Her eyes slid shut and she collapsed softly into the snow, unconscious.

"I order your group to escort lady Ruo Nan back safely."

"Yes..." Gui Yi hesitated. "Then what about you, young master?"

"Me? I'll pursue them down the Yellow Dragon River," the young Gu Master laughed arrogantly.

As river waters surged and winds howled, hundreds of six-legged crocodiles swarmed the beach, attacking Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Her blue eyes scanning the terrain, Bai Ning Bing snapped, "Great campsite! With cliffs on three sides, where do we run?"

"Why panic? It's only a hundred-beast pack. We can slaughter them all," Fang Yuan said, swiftly retreating behind Bai Ning Bing.

"Kill them!" Fang Yuan urged. "Why else did I lend you those Gu? Don't forget I hold the Yang Gu."

Grasping the Chainsaw Golden Centipede, she charged forward.

The Chainsaw Golden Centipede tore through the beasts like a whirlwind, butchering twenty six-legged crocodiles in quick succession.

Though the tide turned, Fang Yuan's expression darkened as he shouted, "Avoid their armored backs—strike their bellies!"

"Haha, what a savage Gu, far better than Iceblade Gu! I love it!" Bai Ning Bing laughed, ignoring him.

But eventually, her attacks started to become slower, and weaker.

Inspecting the Chainsaw Golden Centipede, Bai Ning Bing saw its sawteeth were notched and severely dulled.

While their backs were armored, their soft white bellies were fatal weak points.

Drenched in sweat and breathing heavily, Bai Ning Bing's attacks slowed.

Physical strength was her weakness, repeatedly putting her at a disadvantage against Fang Yuan's two boars' strength.

"Fang Yuan, are you not helping?!" She breathed roughly, calling.

Fang Yuan said coldly, "If I help, I'll draw the six-legged crocodiles. You want me dead? If I die, you'll never get the Yang Gu."

She gritted her teeth, "Fang Yuan, if I die, can you live?"

"Rest assured, I am behind you." Leaning against the cliff, Fang Yuan willed a Blood Moon Gu from his palm toward Bai Ning Bing.

As Blood Moon Gu originated from Moonlight Gu, Bai Ning Bing was familiar with it. After a few uses, her bright red moonblades stabilized the situation.

Yet this relief was brief; though Bai Ning Bing regained her footing, her primeval essence was rapidly running low.

# Chapter 204: Carapace Gu and Crocodile Strength Gu

Seeing Fang Yuan casually leaning against the cliff, she flew into a rage, "Fang Yuan, I fight on the front line while you just watch the show?!"

Fang Yuan laughed coldly, "If I had Rank three cultivation to eliminate these six-legged crocodiles, why would I need you?"

Fang Yuan said seriously, "You no longer have the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique; fighting without restraint will quickly exhaust your primeval essence. An outstanding Gu Master uses primeval essence efficiently without wasting a drop. From now on, battle according to my instructions. Your fighting method is too crude; be more meticulous."

Fang Yuan's words hit the mark, their profound insight pinpointing every flaw in her technique.

Though arrogant, pressed by the crocodiles she instinctively executed his instructions, producing an immediate effect.

Her primeval essence and stamina were depleted, but by pacing herself, eliminating wasted attacks, and maximizing effectiveness, she allowed both to slowly recover amidst the battle.

Fifteen minutes later, with over two hundred corpses on the ground and half the pack injured, the six-legged crocodiles finally ceased attacking and retreated.

This was the six legged crocodile king.

Unlike other six-legged crocodiles, this crocodile king was as large as a yak and moved solely on its two hind legs.

She could have won against this hundred beast king alone, but drained of primeval essence and stamina after an intense battle, she could not handle it in its peak condition.

But at this time, Fang Yuan's voice came from behind her, "Catch."

A blue and white light entered her aperture, becoming a lotus rooted deep in her primeval sea.

Immediately, her primeval sea level started rising!

Bai Ning Bing was surprised and overjoyed: "What Gu is this?"

"Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus." Fang Yuan said.

"So this is the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus! No wonder First Gen Gu Yue wanted it," Bai Ning Bing sighed, then scolded, "With such a Gu, why didn't you lend it to me earlier?"

Fang Yuan chuckled dismissively, "Thankfully this is only a hundred beast king. Remember, its weakness is that white skin near its chest."

With that, his body rippled like water and slowly vanished.

"Scheming and cunning!" Bai Ning Bing scolded, turning her focus onto the crocodile king.

An hour later, the injured crocodile king futilely covered its white skin as blood continuously oozed from its wounds.

"This Blood Moon Gu's bleeding effect let me kill that crocodile king so easily," Bai Ning Bing thought.

With the crocodile king dead, the remaining hundred six-legged crocodiles lost morale and fled into the river.

"Finally over." Bai Ning Bing dismissed the Chainsaw Golden Centipede and sat on the beach in exhaustion.

Fang Yuan emerged from the shade, squatting by the crocodile king's corpse to search it.

"Found it!" When he withdrew his hands, a Gu rested in each palm.

Of the two Gu, one was dying yet still struggling—palm-sized like a turtle shell, its raised surface covered in crocodile scales.

The Carapace Gu.

The Crocodile Strength Gu.

Leaking a trace of Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, he instantly refined both Gu with his primeval essence.

The Carapace Gu flew onto Fang Yuan's back, forming a scale tattoo that stretched from his shoulders to his waist, covering his entire back.

Crocodile Strength Gu became a dim yellow light, entering Fang Yuan's aperture.

Like the Black and White Boar Gu, it permanently increased a Gu Master's strength by the power of a crocodile.

"Instantly refining Gu again!" Seeing this, Bai Ning Bing fumed, her pupils shrinking to pinpricks.

"This guy has so many trump cards! His tactics far surpass clan teachings. And what Gu did he use earlier?"

# Chapter 205: Sleeping in the Treetop

Fang Yuan had engaged the crocodiles to train Bai Ning Bing and acquire wild Gu for himself.

"Though I'm only at Rank one initial stage, my A-grade aptitude and Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus recover primeval essence fast enough for me to use Rank two Gu worms. What a pity that neither Crocodile Strength Gu nor Carapace Gu is ideal for me," Fang Yuan mused inwardly.

Having lent the Rank three Sky Canopy Gu to Bai Ning Bing, he needed a defensive Gu for himself. However, Carapace Gu's coverage was too small, defending only his back.

Though Crocodile Strength Gu was precious, Fang Yuan already had the strength of two boars; strengthening further would only harm him.

"The Jade Bone Gu in Bai Gu Mountain will strengthen my bones enough to use Crocodile Strength Gu, but the mountain is still ten to fifteen days away."

He handed the Crocodile Strength Gu to Bai Ning Bing while recalling the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

"Use this Crocodile Strength Gu to gain the strength of a crocodile. The process is tough, but repeated activation over a month should do it," Fang Yuan instructed.

"Let's go. The heavy scent of blood will attract wild beasts, and this collapsed cliff is too dangerous to camp on."

Before leaving, Fang Yuan gathered as much crocodile flesh and blood as possible into the tusita flower.

Collapsed by the crocodile king's attacks, the cliff had become easier to climb. At the top, a dense jungle greeted them.

Gritting her teeth, Bai Ning Bing had no choice but to drag her exhausted body forward, opening a path for Fang Yuan.

"Hey, how about making a fire to warm ourselves first?" Bai Ning Bing suggested while scouting ahead.

"Warm ourselves? Haha." Fang Yuan laughed. "Don't you feel this jungle is too quiet? Let's inspect the trees ahead."

The trees ahead were short and thick, with twisted roots sprawling across the ground. Branch ends grew into python-like vines, either coiling around each other or hanging to the soil.

The tips of the vines resembled open venus flytraps or gaping mussel shells, quietly awaiting prey.

"Beast-trap tree!" Bai Ning Bing recognized the species from her academy studies.

"A beast-trap tree grove—no wonder it's so quiet without living creatures. But I can easily force a path through with Rank three Gu," Bai Ning Bing said.

Fang Yuan shook his head. "We need a safe campsite right now, and this beast-trap tree grove looks good. Charging through won't guarantee a safer spot in this dense jungle."

Bai Ning Bing's eyes widened in disbelief. "In this place? These beast-trap trees are safer?"

Fang Yuan glanced at her without explaining; instead, he turned back along the way they came.

Fang Yuan selected two sturdy crocodile corpses, gutted and cleaned their bellies, and dragged them back to the jungle.

"Humans possess boundless wisdom to devise odd ways to survive. We sleep inside these tonight," Fang Yuan said, entering a crocodile belly after explaining the plan.

Inside the six-legged crocodile carcass, he rolled toward a beast-trap tree.

Swift as the wind, the nearest vine pounced, its huge leaves swallowing the crocodile carcass whole before snapping firmly shut.

The vines then twisted, gently hoisting the heavy leaves up to the treetop.

The treetop leaves rustled briefly before Fang Yuan's voice drifted out: "Go to sleep; we still need to continue our journey tomorrow."

Gritting her teeth, Bai Ning Bing crawled into the crocodile belly and rolled toward a beast-trap tree just as Fang Yuan had demonstrated.

However, the acid needed at least three months to dissolve a crocodile body, making hiding inside completely safe for now.

"I should use the Crocodile Strength Gu before sleeping..." Bai Ning Bing thought, but her eyelids felt heavy as mountains and gradually shut, plunging her into deep sleep.

A light sleeper, Fang Yuan faintly heard the wind and beasts drawn toward the bloody beach throughout the night.

# Chapter 206: Becoming the leader

Vines hung from the beast-trap tree's crown, eight or nine leaves forming cages like a gathering of standing seashells.

Suddenly among one of the cages, there was intense shaking.

A bright red moonblade shot out, tearing the leaves apart.

Bai Ning Bing sneezed and rubbed her nose, observing her surroundings to check her location.

"Bai Ning Bing, let me out," Fang Yuan called, having noticed the commotion even without the Earth Hearing Communication Grass.

Bai Ning Bing glanced at another beast-trap tree, where the leaf cage holding Fang Yuan remained in place.

Laughing inwardly, she did not reply, deliberately delaying as she closed her eyes to bask in the sunlight.

She eventually walked over and cut the leaves with a moonblade, letting Fang Yuan emerge.

"What's with the delay? I woke up ages ago and even cultivated for a while," Fang Yuan said, looking extremely refreshed.

Bai Ning Bing snorted. Fang Yuan's current situation was beyond her expectations.

"Let's eat first." Fang Yuan activated the Tusita Flower, taking out cookware and provisions.

He moved quickly, cooking a pot of meat stew in a short while.

Searching around, he found a large cluster of mushrooms beneath the beast-trap tree.

Watching him add them to the pot, Bai Ning Bing questioned, "Wild vegetation can be poisonous; you shouldn't eat them carelessly."

"Yup, you're right." Fang Yuan nodded. "Then don't eat."

Bai Ning Bing laughed coldly, "If you get poisoned, I don't have a healing Gu worm."

Under Bai Ning Bing's watchful gaze, Fang Yuan indifferently drank a large mouthful of soup from the ladle.

After drinking from the ladle, her eyes shone.

Though their sleeping conditions were identical, comparing his refreshed state to her own misery stirred a grudging trace of admiration in Bai Ning Bing.

Of course, had she known Fang Yuan secretly used his cloak and clothes to keep warm, she would have felt entirely different.

Without raising his head, he smiled faintly, pretending not to notice as he continued eating.

Ever since she resolutely saved him on Qing Mao Mountain, Fang Yuan had sensed her pure demonic nature.

Demons were irrational and unyielding, adamantly walking their own path. Bai Ning Bing's demonic nature gave Fang Yuan ideas on how to use her.

On one hand, she was still green; having gained a new life and resolved the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique, she now enjoyed life and refused to die.

On the other hand, her demonic nature drove an unrestrained pursuit of excitement; she did not fear death, and if it was thrilling enough, she would gladly chase it.

Such a person was like an untamable young dragon, full of curiosity and wild abandon, with her own distinct path, ambitions, and aspirations.

Though merely a demon child rather than a demon lord, as a true demon she would never change her path, alter her direction, or submit to anyone.

Fang Yuan understood her because he understood himself. She would never submit, but that did not mean she could not be subdued. Her path could not be altered, but she could still be used.

With Rank three cultivation, he would not need her; but at Rank one initial stage, Bai Ning Bing was of great value.

Smart and arrogant, Bai Ning Bing could not be forced; she could only be tamed gradually through subtle pressure and external influence.

Fang Yuan had kept warm with extra clothes not out of pettiness, and he had overlooked her deliberate delay not out of magnanimity.

"Subduing Bai Ning Bing will take time, but there's no rush; I need time to restore my cultivation anyway."

After the two ate, it was near noon already.

Amidst ground covered in beast tracks, the two pushed southeast, with Bai Ning Bing clearing the way.

Using Earth Hearing Communication Grass to avoid dangers, they relied on Bai Ning Bing's combat power to force through unavoidable areas.

Night fell again, and Fang Yuan found a safe campsite on a stone hill.

Exhausted, Bai Ning Bing fell asleep immediately even on the sharp, rough rocks.

When she woke the next day, her body ached all over, her neck was stiff, and frequent sneezing showed she had caught a cold.

Lacking movement-type Gu, they traveled extremely slowly. Previously, Fang Yuan had the Rank five Thousand Li Earthwolf Spider and Bai Ning Bing had the White Form Immortal Snake Gu, but one had died and the other escaped.

A few days later, Bai Ning Bing fell ill with a cold and fever, her headache sharply reducing her battle strength.

Fang Yuan had to stop, treating her with medicine from the tusita flower and applying medicinal paste that stung her skin to expel the chill.

This illness was a harsh lesson. Drowsy and drained of strength, she had been unable to apply the paste herself without Fang Yuan's help.

"If not for Fang Yuan, I'd be in a critical position this time..." Bai Ning Bing sighed inwardly after recovering, unable to deny the truth.

In the past, her tone was harsh and aggressive, but after this ordeal she grew much quieter, sometimes going half a day without speaking.

The less she spoke, the more Fang Yuan led and his authority grew, steadily becoming the leader between the two.

# Chapter 207: Xuan Yuan Divine Pheasant

Time passed quickly.

Under the blazing sun, the two hid in the shaded forest, carefully observing the valley.

In the valley, there was a huge crocodile, sleeping peacefully.

Most importantly, two volcano-like lumps sat on its back. As it breathed, twin columns of black smoke rose with varying intensity.

"This lava crocodile is a thousand beast king! Eliminating it is too risky," Bai Ning Bing said warily.

A thousand beast king harbored Rank three wild Gu. Combined with its powerful physique, even Rank three peak stage Gu Masters would struggle to win one-on-one.

"Great risk brings great reward! Crocodiles are rare in this forest, and the Crocodile Strength Gu needs meat—we have less than half left. Let's test its strength first," Fang Yuan said.

Bai Ning Bing gritted her teeth, standing up.

She had been using the Crocodile Strength Gu, greatly increasing her strength, though she had not yet gained the full strength of one crocodile.

The lava crocodile was asleep, but when Bai Ning Bing approached within fifty paces, it opened its reddish-golden eyes.

Solemn, Bai Ning Bing activated the Canopy Gu, but still felt the hot air blasting against her face.

Foregoing the Chainsaw Golden Centipede, she unleashed a blood moonblade.

The Rank three moonblade struck the lava crocodile's back, shaving off scales and enraging the beast king.

Aiming straight at Bai Ning Bing, it opened its maw and spat a dark red lava fireball.

"Rank three Lava Explosion Gu," Fang Yuan noted inwardly.

Minutes later, Fang Yuan lowered a rope from the cliff and pulled Bai Ning Bing up.

The lava crocodile king growled but did not pursue, lying back down to bask in the sun.

"This lava crocodile king houses three Rank three Gu: Lava Explosion Gu, Flame Stomach Gu, and Accumulating Ash Gu. Attack, defense, and healing—all three basic aspects are covered," Fang Yuan summarized as they left.

"Setting aside the Lava Explosion Gu, the Blood Moon Gu cannot breach Flame Stomach Gu's defense. Only the Chainsaw Golden Centipede can pierce it up close, but doing so would cripple the centipede. Its blades are already worn down. Furthermore, even if we break its defense, the Accumulating Ash Gu can heal it. Its stamina exceeds ours combined, so we would lose a battle of attrition. Most importantly, it can burrow back to its underground nest, and we cannot stop it," Bai Ning Bing said.

Fang Yuan nodded. "Your analysis is sound, making me want to kill it even more. The Accumulating Ash Gu feeds on ashes, making it easy to raise and ideal as our healing Gu."

"Hmph, a good Gu is useless if we die. Even with the Yang Gu, do not think of ordering me to risk my life in such combat," Bai Ning Bing snorted.

"We cannot fight it head-on, but we can outsmart it. We can lure other beast kings to fight the crocodile king, then reap the spoils," Fang Yuan replied, unwilling to give up.

Fang Yuan's words caused Bai Ning Bing's eyes to shine.

She nodded. "Lacking a movement Gu makes this risky, but our chances are far better than a direct battle. We can try it."

Over the next five days, the two scouted the surrounding area centered on the crocodile's valley.

After scouting the surrounding areas without finding a viable target, moving northeast, they discovered a beehive as large as a house.

Inside swarmed a terrifying swarm of frenzy needle bees.

As Gu, frenzy needle bees gained piercing power that penetrated all defenses; even Bai Ning Bing's Sky Canopy Gu would be pierced by Rank three frenzy needles.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing sat solemnly around the fire.

Fang Yuan remained expressionless as Bai Ning Bing sighed in disappointment, "We have fully scouted the area. Your idea is sound, but with no suitable targets, we must give up on the lava crocodile king."

"Man proposes, heaven disposes. We must rely on luck, but killing the lava crocodile king seems impossible. Tomorrow we resume our journey to Bai Gu mountain," Fang Yuan said, nodding helplessly.

Suddenly, the lava crocodile king's furious growls echoed outside.

Exchanging glances, the two stepped out of the cave to look into the distance.

Amid the brilliant rainbow light stood a mountain-sized pheasant with a towering golden crown and shifting plumage of otherworldly brilliance.

"A Xuan Yuan Divine Pheasant, a myriad beast king! The lava crocodile king is finished," Fang Yuan exclaimed.

"This solitary myriad beast king flies the skies, landing only to feed. Rare in number, divine pheasants harbor various rainbow Gu. In battle, they fill the sky with five to seven-colored light. The crocodile king is out of reach now. Get inside—this pheasant has eagle-sharp eyes. If it spots us, we are doomed." Saying so, Fang Yuan retreated into the cave.

Biting her lip, Bai Ning Bing followed closely behind.

# Chapter 208: Escape

An hour later, the lava crocodile king gave a final cry before falling silent.

After a sleepless night of listening to the beast rampage, only at dusk did the Xuan Yuan Divine Pheasant take flight, trailing a rainbow in its wake.

Once the rainbow trail faded from the sky, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing finally dared to venture out.

The valley lay devastated. The lava crocodile king lay belly-up on the ground, thoroughly dead.

Its belly was ripped open and its viscera devoured, leaving only a skeleton bound by dark red hide.

Missing the Accumulating Ash Gu was expected, but they did not return empty-handed.

Some flesh still remained on the crocodile king's corpse.

After an afternoon of work, they harvested all the meat into the Tusita Flower.

"This crocodile meat will feed the Crocodile Strength Gu for three months. Let's explore the other areas."

They proceeded to the white apes' territory.

The divine pheasant had devastated this thousand beast group; only two or three hundred survived, and the remaining hundred beast king apes were all injured.

Bai Ning Bing's eyes glinted. "The white apes are at their weakest—shall we attack?"

Fang Yuan stopped her.

"An enraged army fights to the death; do not provoke them. If provoked, they will attack relentlessly until we are dead. Even injured, several hundred beast kings are more than you can handle alone."

Hearing this, Bai Ning Bing glanced at Fang Yuan and dropped the idea.

The two headed southwest toward the rotten swamp.

Ravaged by the divine pheasant, the swamp was utterly transformed.

With their habitat destroyed and local hierarchy shattered, the swamp's venomous creatures slaughtered one another in chaotic frenzy.

Seeing the chaos, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing left immediately.

Finally, they reached the frenzy needle bee nest.

The house-sized hive had collapsed completely; the surroundings were silent, with not a bee in sight.

A sesame fragrance drifted over. Bai Ning Bing sniffed, "What is that smell?"

"The hive. Frenzy needle bees make no honey, but their hives serve as Gu refinement material and edible food," Fang Yuan said, reaching into the rubble.

"Mm, delicious!" Bai Ning Bing tried a piece; the mouth-watering sweetness eased her furrowed brow.

"Our rations are running low. We should store some hive in your Tusita Flower," Bai Ning Bing suggested.

Glancing warily at the sky, Fang Yuan said, "Agreed, but we must be quick."

Fang Yuan shook his head; before he could speak, his expression changed.

Buzz... The drone of flying bees filled their ears.

Bai Ning Bing looked up to see a dense cloud of frenzy needle bees swooping toward them.

Instantly, countless bees rained down upon the pair!

"Run!" Fang Yuan shouted, sprinting away in large strides.

Snapping to her senses, Bai Ning Bing turned and bolted after him.

Lacking movement Gu, Bai Ning Bing trailed behind Fang Yuan and was quickly overtaken by the swarm.

She activated Sky Canopy Gu; the white armor materialized and instantly flickered under thousands of strikes.

The Rank three frenzy needle bee Gu possessed piercing power that bypassed the Sky Canopy Gu. Needles stabbed into her back, drawing blood and spurring her to run beyond her normal limits.

With Bai Ning Bing absorbing the brunt behind him, Fang Yuan fared much better.

He channeled primeval essence into the Carapace Gu on his back.

Ordinary bees could not pierce the crocodile armor, while the bee Gu focused entirely on Bai Ning Bing.

Even after fifteen minutes, the bees pursued relentlessly.

"A lake ahead!" Fang Yuan shouted as the crisis deepened.

Without hesitation, Fang Yuan plunged into the water.

Moments later, the pair surfaced on the opposite shore.

With the scent washed away, the frustrated swarm buzzed fruitlessly over the water.

"I've had enough, when will we reach Bai Gu mountain?"

"Quiet!" Solemn, Fang Yuan crouched and touched the cold ashes of an extinguished campfire.

Undeniably, these were traces left behind by humans.

# Chapter 209: Encountering a demonic Gu Master

Before them lay distinct traces of someone building a fire and cooking meat.

Earth Communication Ear grass!

Roots sprouted from his ear and burrowed underground, greatly amplifying his hearing.

After listening closely, he relaxed; no ambushers lurked nearby.

"This fire was lit half a month ago. The traveler was alone and has long moved on," Fang Yuan deduced.

"Alone? Clan Gu Masters travel in groups of five. This must be a demonic cultivator," Bai Ning Bing sighed, frowning.

"Discovering this campfire warns us not to rush blindly. We must rest and heal our wounds first," Fang Yuan said.

Bai Ning Bing nodded, feeling the pain in her back flare up.

Fang Yuan retrieved bandages and ointment from the Tusita Flower, handing most to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing removed her shirt, revealing a back riddled with gruesome, fingertip-sized puncture holes.

The Rank three frenzy needle bees pierced Sky Canopy Gu with ease, but Bai Ning Bing's previous Ice Muscles Gu provided a second layer of defense that saved her life.

Bai Ning Bing gritted her teeth and inhaled deeply against the stinging pain as Fang Yuan cleaned and bandaged her wounds.

"Our medicine and bandages are nearly exhausted despite our thriftiness. We must acquire a healing Gu soon," Fang Yuan said, rising solemnly.

"We do need a healing Gu, but I have another problem first," Bai Ning Bing interjected.

"What problem?" Fang Yuan raised his eyebrows.

Bare-chested, Bai Ning Bing pointed at her breasts. "These two lumps of flesh get in the way. They bounce when I run and hinder me in battle. I want to cut them off, but without a healing Gu, the wound might be too dangerous."

Fang Yuan stared blankly. "Cutting them off causes too many complications. Just bind them."

"Wrap what?" Bai Ning Bing asked.

"Wrap bandages tightly around your chest as if treating an injury. That will keep them secure," Fang Yuan explained.

Bai Ning Bing sighed in exasperation, "That will have to do for now."

Fang Yuan located a cramped but safe cliffside cave, clearing out its nesting birds to serve as their temporary shelter.

Few days later, Bai Ning Bing's injuries recovered mostly.

The two continued on their journey, towards Bai Gu mountain.

Wary of the mysterious demonic cultivator, Fang Yuan traveled with extreme caution, frequently halting to scout with the Earth Communication Ear Grass.

On the third day, they discovered battle traces beside a stream.

The stripped skeleton of a giant green python lay on the ground, its flesh scavenged by wild beasts.

Fang Yuan examined the remains and relaxed slightly. "The demonic Gu Master battled this hundred beast king green python. To struggle like this, they are likely only Rank three."

Yet later that afternoon, they discovered fresh traces of the demonic cultivator once more.

"The demonic Gu Master is injured. Excised flesh on the ground shows they were poisoned," Fang Yuan noted.

Tracking the poisoned cultivator's trail over the next few days, finally, one afternoon, Fang Yuan retracted the roots from his ear and opened his eyes. "I found the demonic Gu Master! They're in that cave, drawing their last breath!"

"Strike while the enemy is weak!" Ruthlessness flared in Bai Ning Bing's eager eyes.

But she was stopped by Fang Yuan.

"Be patient. There's no need to strike—in a few days, she'll die of poison."

"The two outside, why bother hiding. I've already found you." A weak voice came from the cave.

Fang Yuan's expression changed, immediately retreating.

"Don't you want my primeval stones, my Gu worms?" The voice came from the cave again.

Though weak, her tone was firm; she had surely laid traps to make her fearless.

"Leaving already? Hehe, there's no easy way out—you'll all stay!" A figure suddenly emerged from the cave.

"All up to you." Fang Yuan's body flickered, hiding his presence.

Bai Ning Bing clicked her tongue, activating the Sky Canopy Gu and summoning the Chainsaw Golden Centipede, engaging the enemy.

After three rounds of combat, the demonic Gu Master was suppressed.

She was a barefoot middle-aged woman with thick sole fur, her face and limbs turned green from the python's poison.

Gradually overwhelmed, the demonic female Gu Master stomped the ground and leaped back into the cave.

Bai Ning Bing chased after relentlessly.

"Don't chase her!" Fang Yuan warned, but he was too late.

A loud explosion erupted beneath Bai Ning Bing's feet, blowing her away.

# Chapter 210: Plotting

Bai Ning Bing was hurled to the ground, but leaped back to her feet immediately.

Protected by the Sky Canopy Gu, she was unhurt, though the Gu took the impact and dimmed her white armor by thirty percent.

Inside the cave, the demonic female Gu Master laughed smugly, "Good! Come at me again if you have the guts, little girl!"

Bai Ning Bing sneered, "Think you're safe in that cave? I'll just camp here until you come out."

Fang Yuan cupped his fists toward the cave. "We met purely by coincidence while passing through. Making things hard for you only troubles us. May we never meet again. Farewell!"

Saying so, he turned around and left.

"Where to? She's only a Rank three Gu Master—once we figure out that explosion method, victory is assured!" Bai Ning Bing frowned.

Fang Yuan snorted, "You're Rank three, but I'm Rank one. Let's keep moving and avoid trouble. Better safe than sorry."

Realizing Fang Yuan was acting, Bai Ning Bing played along, feigning anger: "You're always so cowardly. Fine, I'll spare your life."

She gave the demonic female Gu Master a look brimming with killing intent before following Fang Yuan into the woods, out of sight.

Once they were far enough, Bai Ning Bing said, "Her explosion technique isn't an issue. She didn't use it in combat earlier, only triggering it when she entered the cave and I came into range. She must have buried the Gu beforehand and can't move it. We can lure wild beasts to trigger her traps."

But Fang Yuan laughed, asking instead, "Then what comes next?"

Fang Yuan's eyes narrowed. "Say we lure wild beasts to test her traps, then what? Cornered with no way out, she'll fight desperately to take one of us down. Even if we survive, we'll suffer losses."

"Furthermore, even if defeated, she'll destroy her Gu worms rather than leave them to us. A Gu Master needs only a thought to destroy their own Gu. We can't prevent it, so killing her yields no Gu worms. What's the benefit in that?"

"Don't you have a Plunder Gu?" Bai Ning Bing asked.

Bai Ning Bing grew concerned. "If we leave like this and let her escape, what then?"

Fang Yuan laughed confidently, "She won't escape anytime soon."

"This demonic female Gu Master has a crude accent, poor tactics, and lacks survival experience. She leaves traces everywhere and failed to conceal her blood after being injured. Her sturdy build and large hands and feet suggest she was a farmer who lucked into an inheritance."

Fang Yuan continued, "That explosion was a buried Rank two Charred Thunder Potato Gu that detonates when stepped on. How knowledgeable could a peasant farmer be? Unable to cure the python poison and frightened by worsening injuries, she reflexively planted many Charred Thunder Potatoes outside her cave."

"Leaving deliberately gives her room to relax, while outside dangers and fear of encountering us will keep her cautious. Those Charred Thunder Potatoes give her the greatest security, so for now, she won't leave."

"Good analysis, but the poison won't let her stall forever. She will eventually leave the cave," Bai Ning Bing countered.

Fang Yuan tapped his right ear. "Which is why we must surveil her."

Bai Ning Bing shook her head. "Hmph, that has flaws too. Earth Communication Ear grass requires constant primeval essence. Even with the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, one person's stamina is limited. You need sleep—you can't monitor her non-stop, can you?"

Fang Yuan rolled his eyes. "Are you getting stupid? There is one of her and two of us."

Bai Ning Bing's expression froze, shame flashing across her eyes.

To Fang Yuan, the demonic female Gu Master was merely one target—Bai Ning Bing was the other.

Chen Cui Hua was shellshocked.

Luckily, she had buried many Charred Thunder Potatoes beforehand, and the cowardly male chose to leave.

"I'll wait three days before going out," Chen Cui Hua thought, having learned vigilance and patience.

# Chapter 211: Road

"*As long as the mountain remains, water continues to flow*". Junior brother, remember today's grudge, I will repay it someday." First Generation Gu Yue laughed loudly, activating his bat wings, trying to fly away."

As First Generation Gu Yue attempted to flee, the wounded flying crane king descended. Intercepted by the crane king, First Gen Gu Yue was caught in a pincer attack by Lord Sky Crane. Eventually, the crane king crashed down dead, and the blood wight was completely destroyed.

Hovering in midair with First Generation Gu Yue's head in hand, Lord Sky Crane's dazed gaze slowly regained focus.

After venting his fury, he flew to Qing Mao Mountain, only to be suppressed by Bai Ning Bing's self-detonation and forced to use the Life-burial Jade Gu. Breaking free of the ice, he searched frantically, finding only the dying Fang Zheng.

The images on the wall, showed this scene truthfully.

Immortal Crane Sect disciples customarily carried a Gu worm to record emergency missions.

Supreme Elder He Feng Yang, Rank 6 Gu Master, titled Winged Crane Flying Immortal!

He had a youthful, jade-smooth face and long green eyebrows extending to his waist. His eerie dark eyes watched the wall projection before slowly turning away.

"Although you slew our traitor, you failed to retrieve the Blood Skull Gu. Instead, you were suppressed and toyed with by two juniors..." He Feng Yang's voice echoed through the room.

"However, one possessed the Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique, so your suppression is unsurprising. Fortunately, you made amends. Tell me, is the youth you brought back truly the twin of the one in the recording?" He Feng Yang asked.

"I have verified it. The elder brother who escaped is Fang Yuan; the younger one captured is Fang Zheng. Strangely, the two are on terrible terms. When shown the recording of his brother slaughtering their clan, he wished to kill him on the spot," Lord Sky Crane laughed eerily.

He Feng Yang nodded. "You have a plan, but is there enough time?"

Lord Sky Crane's expression changed, his face turning solemn: "Supreme elder is right. My lifespan is at its limit, I am about to die, with only a few days left to live. Even the life-burial jade gu cannot change my fate."

"Thus, I traded my life savings and Gu worms for a Spirit Incubating Flea. I brought Fang Zheng into the sect as my disciple to teach him and refine the Bloodkin worm. I will incite him to kill his brother and retrieve the Blood Skull Gu for our Immortal Crane Sect!" Lord Sky Crane said.

He Feng Yang raised an eyebrow. "You sound confident. But can Fang Zheng truly be manipulated so easily?"

"Though talented, he's just a child. After housing my soul in the Spirit Incubating Flea, I will guide his growth and cultivation. The path is paved—he only needs to walk it!"

Lord Sky Crane knelt and begged, "Please grant me another chance, Supreme Elder!"

He Feng Yang said solemnly, "Very well. This may be your last chance anyway."

"Thank you, Elder! Thank you!" Lord Sky Crane was overjoyed.

"Go, I await your good news."

"Within twenty years, I will succeed!" Lord Sky Crane declared with trembling excitement as he departed.

Southern borders, Wan Cheng mountain, Tie clan city.

"You've only been back a few days, yet this is the nineteenth time you've asked. I grieve your father's death, but remember you still have your uncle. I won't put my niece in danger," the Tie clan leader sighed.

Tie Ruo Nan stared directly at him with bright eyes. "Uncle, though grieving, I feel proud of Father. He spent his life punishing evil, never backing down even while injured. He walked his path resolutely to the end. Now, it's my turn to do the same."

Seeing their images overlap, the Tie clan leader shook off the memories and looked at the resolute girl with mixed warmth and worry: "The path you've chosen is not easy."

Tie Ruo Nan declared resolutely, speaking to her uncle and herself: "Wan Cheng Mountain has a Demon Suppression Tower, and so does my heart. I will finish the path Father could not!"

Bai Ning Bing raised her dagger toward her right ear.

"Hold it." Fang Yuan caught Bai Ning Bing's arm.

The blade was barely a millimeter from her right ear.

Using the Earth Communication Ear grass required severing one's right ear. For Bai Ning Bing, trading an ear for the rewards warranted no hesitation.

"No need, she's out," Fang Yuan said, starting to move.

Using the Earth Communication Ear grass, he tracked Chen Cui Hua from afar.

Not long later, he heard fighting noises.

Stalking through the dense shrubs, they found the demonic female Gu Master battling a twin-headed boar.

Soon, Fang Yuan saw the time was right and passed the Stealth Scales Gu to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing nodded, concealing her body and creeping closer.

Just as she moved, wind rushed past her ears.

Having crept close patiently, Bai Ning Bing struck like lightning, taking advantage of the Gu Master's lowered guard to kill her in a single strike!

The Chainsaw Golden Centipede smashed Chen Cui Hua's head, sending her corpse crashing to the ground.

"We did it." Fang Yuan laughed heartily, walking over quickly.

Squatting down, Bai Ning Bing probed the corpse's aperture and said with slight disappointment, "There are only three Gu inside."

"Plunder Gu!" He willed, easily retrieving these three Gu.

They were a Rice Bag grass Gu, Iron Thorn Thistle Gu, and Charred Thunder Potato Mother Gu.

Searching further, Fang Yuan found a Hairy Leg Gu, a Jumping Grass Gu, and a Bookworm in her shattered skull.

Unfortunately, the Bookworm had already been killed in Bai Ning Bing's strike.

"This Bookworm was surely the inheritance item, containing Gu Master knowledge and secret recipes," Fang Yuan said with regret.

"Never mind the Bookworm—did she really have no healing Gu?" Bai Ning Bing was deeply disappointed.

Fang Yuan was solemn, not speaking.

He had already expected as much; had she possessed a healing Gu, the poison would never have reduced her to such a state.

"So this is how breasts are tied," Bai Ning Bing remarked, unwrapping the corpse's chest.

Fang Yuan's gaze fixed on a silver triangular tattoo on the corpse's chest.

"The third watch Gu!" Fang Yuan was surprised.

"Third watch Gu, what's that?" Bai Ning Bing raised her eyebrows.

"It's an expendable Rank five Gu that accelerates time threefold, forming a silver triangular tattoo on the user's chest."

"With a Third Watch Gu, her inheritance was at least Rank five. What a waste on such a person," Bai Ning Bing scoffed at the corpse.

"Those who use the Third Watch Gu are bold and forward-looking. This woman was timid and indecisive, lacking the courage to advance. Such is life," Fang Yuan concluded.

Bai Ning Bing's expression was ugly.

The demonic female Gu Master was a stark reminder of what happened without a healing Gu.

Fang Yuan chuckled, patting her shoulder. "Paths are forged by people. Everyone has their own way; another's wide path may not suit you. As long as we walk our own road, what is there to worry about?"

Bai Ning Bing paused, then her expression softened as she nodded. "You're right."

Fang Yuan squinted, gazing at the dense forest ahead.

Killing the demonic Gu Master and obtaining her Gu had substantially boosted his strength.

Yet knowing he lacked a healing Gu, he refused to rely on luck.

He might find a healing Gu, or die empty-handed like this woman, or be trampled by beasts at any moment, ending his ambitions.

But so what?

Treading his path and forging ahead was all that mattered.

# Chapter 212: Monkey Brain Delicacy

"Finally, from Rank one initial to middle stage." A faint excitement stirred in Fang Yuan's heart.

"It has been over twenty days since we killed that demonic female Gu master. Cultivating non-stop without wasting a single minute, no wonder I reached middle stage." Fang Yuan slowly opened his eyes.

"What a pity I lack a liquor worm, otherwise my cultivation would be even faster."

"If I reverse refine the four flavor liquor worm, I can get the rank 1 liquor worm, but there are many requirements to reverse refine, I cannot fulfill the conditions."

Outside the cave, monkey screeches and footsteps could be heard.

As expected, not long after, Bai Ning Bing appeared.

A layer of thorn clothing woven from green vines and sharp black thorns covered her entire body, providing both defense and offense.

After killing the demonic female Gu master, Bai Ning Bing claimed this rank three grass Gu, refining it into her lifebound Gu.

"So troublesome. Every time I come and go, I have to watch out for those charred thunder potatoes. Can't you bury fewer?" she grumbled, hopping to avoid pitfalls.

"Better safe than sorry," Fang Yuan replied lightly, glancing at Bai Ning Bing's waist.

On her waist was a string of monkeys.

"Grill them, I'm hungry." Bai Ning Bing rubbed her stomach and dropped the bound monkeys to the ground.

"I'll prepare them first; you grill them. Yours tastes much better than mine." She squatted down to butcher them.

Fang Yuan stopped her: "Let me teach you a new eating method."

Afterwards, he moved his finger, and a Gu flew out of Bai Ning Bing's aperture with a bloody light.

Fang Yuan lifted a grass skirt monkey, gripping its neck so tightly its head could not move.

His red-glowing palm rested on the monkey's head, tracing a full circle.

As the red light faded, Fang Yuan smiled, grasped the monkey's head, and pulled open its skull.

"Taste this fresh monkey brain." Fang Yuan handed Bai Ning Bing a spoon.

"Raw, like this?" Bai Ning Bing hesitated, eyeing the white brain streaked with blood.

Fang Yuan scooped some monkey brain into his mouth, swallowing as it smoothly slid down his throat.

Bai Ning Bing tried as well, scooping a spoonful into her mouth.

Instantly, a grassy fragrance spread across her tongue without a trace of blood. With the texture of beancurd, it melted effortlessly—slightly sweet and refreshing throughout her mouth.

She swallowed the brain. Her crystal-blue eyes shone brightly as her fairy-like face showed an expression of pure enjoyment.

"So incredibly delicious!" The young girl exclaimed.

"Only grass skirt monkeys taste like this; their brain is a natural delicacy," Fang Yuan said, scooping another mouthful.

"How are those grass skirt monkeys." Fang Yuan asked.

Bai Ning Bing collected herself: "There's no issue on the outskirts, but three monkey kings appear deeper in. The wine is in the forest's center—it won't be easy to obtain."

# Chapter 213: Pitfall

The earliest records of wine came from the legend of Ren Zu.

Ren Zu's two eyes turned into his eldest son Verdant Great Sun and daughter Desolate Ancient Moon.

Constantly by Desolate Ancient Moon's side, Verdant Great Sun developed feelings for his sister, but she rejected his advances.

Frustrated and in need of help, Verdant Great Sun sought out Wisdom Gu.

At first, Wisdom Gu avoided and ignored him. But Verdant Great Sun persisted until Wisdom Gu, unable to endure the annoyance, offered a method: "A group of honey peach monkeys lives in the east. Drink their brewed wine, then return to me."

Thus, Verdant Great Sun went to the east and drank the wine.

The honey peach monkeys brewed fruit wine. After drinking it and returning, Verdant Great Sun's face had a rosy flush. Smacking his lips, he recalled: "So, wine is sweet."

Wisdom Gu smiled: "A group of psychic monkeys lives in the west. Go taste their wine."

The psychic monkeys brewed bitter wine. Verdant Great Sun went west and drank it; ever since, his tongue bore a yellowish-brown hue. With a bitter expression, he noted: "So, wine can also be bitter."

Wisdom Gu told him again: "Wine is both bitter and sweet; love and human lives are no different. In the north dwell vajra monkeys who also brew wine. Go taste it."

The vajra monkeys brewed strong wine.

Verdant Great Sun loved this wine, drinking until dead drunk.

Finding it thoroughly to his taste, he craved more even after becoming intoxicated, moving from bowls to drinking straight from the jar.

Finally, he began vomiting as the wine took effect, causing unbearable pain.

His insides felt ablaze, as though lava coursed through them.

"It's too hot!" he screamed. The flames rushed to his head, setting his hair ablaze to burn endlessly like fire.

When Verdant Great Sun awoke, Wisdom Gu was watching him.

"What did you think of the strong wine?" The Wisdom Gu asked.

Verdant Great Sun sighed: "I understand now. No matter how good the wine, drinking too much brings it back up; everything must be in moderation."

Wisdom Gu laughed heartily: "Skywater monkeys dwell in the north. Their wine is also quite good, go taste it."

Skywater monkeys brewed clear wine, the complete opposite of strong wine.

The moment Verdant Great Sun tasted it, all his worries vanished. He felt as though floating in air, his eyes hazy and intoxicated.

When Wisdom Gu asked about his experience, he waved his hand: "Having known the joys of wine, I will not pass it on to the sober."

The Wisdom Gu chuckled and left quietly....

Wild monkey groups brewed distinct wines, collectively called monkey wine.

Fang Yuan had stopped at this cave to focus on breaking through his cultivation bottleneck, and also to obtain the monkey wine.

Wine also served to disinfect wounds and warm the body. Furthermore, reverse refining the four flavors liquor worm required wine as an auxiliary ingredient, and even without full refinement conditions, it provided backup food.

This grass skirt monkey group was nearly a thousand-beast group in scale, possessing three hundred-beast monkey kings.

"So we need to use our brains. Follow me." Fang Yuan stood, patted Bai Ning Bing's shoulder, and carefully stepped around the buried charred thunder potatoes to exit the cave.

In moments, the two neared the territory of the monkey groups.

Surveying from high ground, he nodded with satisfaction, paced the area, and said: "Let's dig here."

In the time it took an incense stick to burn, the two excavated a sun-facing pit thirty feet deep and fifty feet across.

"My primeval essence won't be enough, I will lend it to you." Fang Yuan handed the Charred Thunder Potato Mother Gu to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing poured primeval essence into the Gu, and green sprouts quickly grew from tiny holes on its surface.

The sprouts grew rapidly, blossomed and bore fruit.

Fang Yuan sorted the dark green fruits, removing defective, dead, or empty shells until less than a third remained.

These fruits were rank two Charred Thunder Potato Gu. Once buried, they grew in fertile soil and would self-detonate from the vibrations of any living being stepping nearby.

Fang Yuan buried layers of potato Gu deep beneath the soil. "I see. A single rank two Charred Thunder Potato Gu has limited power, but layered like this, the combined blast could threaten even a thousand beast king. But how will you lure the three monkey kings here?"

"How difficult can that be? At the right time, we'll capture two or three young grass skirt monkeys and eat their brains here. Their shrieks will surely draw the furious monkey group. You can fend off the ordinary monkeys at first, followed by the three monkey kings," Fang Yuan replied while burying the Gu.

Bai Ning Bing continuously produced Charred Thunder Potato Gu for Fang Yuan to bury, alternating Gu and soil layer by layer until the pit was filled to the surface.

The next day, Fang Yuan showed no signs of stopping and proceeded to dig another pit.

Fang Yuan replied: "One pit isn't enough guarantee; having backup plans and extra preparation is always wise."

They dug five pits and buried numerous Charred Thunder Potato Gu, with the first pit being the largest.

Thanks to these thorough preparations, their plan proceeded extremely smoothly.

The monkey groups were all defeated with just two pitfalls.

Two monkey kings died and the wounded third fled with the remnants. The dead kings were blown to pieces, their Gu worms destroyed.

Fang Yuan gathered monkey wine until his tusita flower could hold no more.

"Before moving on, we must clean the battlefield and fill the two detonated pits."

"Is such caution really necessary?" Bai Ning Bing winced at the thought of digging more pits.

"Did you forget how we chased that demonic path female Gu master?"

# Chapter 214: BOOM!

"Bai Gu Mountain, I'm finally here," Fang Yuan sighed, gazing at the white mountain from a slope.

Bai Ning Bing was beside him, standing silently.

Five days after taking the monkey wine and enduring an arduous journey, Bai Gu Mountain was finally in reach.

Unlike the rolling hills of Qing Mao Mountain, Bai Gu Mountain stood like a solitary general over flat plains, surrounded by deathly white scenery.

As its name implied, Bai Gu Mountain's rocks were made of bone, known as bone rocks.

Fang Yuan was flipping through his memories at this moment.

In the future, the Bai clan twins would discover a hidden cave here. Inside, they would activate the complete inheritance of a rank four righteous Gu Master.

The master's name was lost, leaving only his title: "Flesh Bone Lord."

Benefiting immensely, Bai Sheng and Bai Hua became twin stars of the righteous path, eventually reaching rank five and leading Bai village a century later.

"A complete inheritance covers all six aspects: attack, healing, defense, movement, storage, and investigation. Obtaining it will give me the foundation to advance or retreat freely."

They lacked not only healing Gu, but also high cultivation.

"If I can obtain the Bai Gu Mountain inheritance, many problems will be resolved," Fang Yuan thought.

First was the Jade Bone Gu, which would temper brittle mortal bones into something far firmer and tougher. His body could currently sustain only two boars' strength, but Jade Bone Gu would allow him to add the strength of a crocodile.

Next was the inheritance's famous rank three healing Gu, Flesh White Bone, which had made Bai Hua a renowned healing master in his past life.

Finally came the one Fang Yuan deemed most important: the Bone Flesh Unity Gu.

A unique creation of the Flesh Bone Lord, its incredible utility had stirred major forces across the Southern Border in his previous life.

Like Yin Yang Rotation Gu, it was a paired Gu used by two Gu Masters for dual cultivation, accelerating their progress with half the effort.

"With the Bone Flesh Unity Gu and Bai Ning Bing's help, my cultivation will surge, especially in early stages where it surpasses even the liquor worm. I must get it no matter what!"

Fang Yuan glanced at Bai Ning Bing through the corner of his eyes.

Fang Yuan sneered inwardly. Just as they prepared to move out, several figures flew toward them.

"Righteous Gu Masters?" Both Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing were startled.

Four Gu Masters approached, landing a hundred paces away before advancing on Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Their leader was an old rank three Gu Master, while the other three were rank two.

Dressed in matching uniforms and moving in synergy, they were clearly an elite group.

"How can we encounter righteous Gu Masters in this desolate mountain range?"

Tie Ao Tian walked in the center of the group with a cold expression.

Eight clan experts had set out from Qing Mao Mountain, but only three now remained with him.

The losses were too severe!

Even with such a loss, Tie Ao Tian did not think of giving up.

Yet capturing the demonic scoundrel who obtained the Blood Sea inheritance and avenging Tie Xue Leng would be a glorious triumph.

"Though I lost companions, Tie Dao Ku and I are rank three. With two rank two assistants, our strength still dominates them. Once we capture them, our losses will only prove my iron will!" Tie Ao Tian's eyes shone with determination.

"Fourth young master, we found traces ahead—our heading is correct!" the investigative Gu Master reported.

"Oh? Quickly bring me there."

A short time later, they excavated two pits filled with numerous dead grass skirt monkeys.

"These monkeys died less than a week ago; we're catching up to them!" Tie Dao Ku said joyfully.

Tie Ao Tian breathed in deeply, his mood was suddenly lifted!

"Finally, we're nearing the end." He clenched his fists, walking forward excitedly.

"Though the sun sets, I see hope and the future..." Feeling triumphant, he climbed the slope to savor the moment.

But at the next moment, an intense explosion occurred.

BOOM!!!

# Chapter 215: Fang Yuan sheds tears

With the Bai clan in dire straits, dedicated clan elder Bai Mo Xing could not abandon his duty. Dispatched on a mission vital to the clan's fate, he unexpectedly sensed the aura of two Gu Masters along the journey.

"Two youngsters?" He was shocked when he saw Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Frosty and fearless, Bai Ning Bing met Bai Mo Xing's gaze with cold blue eyes, radiating a rank three aura.

"So young and already rank three—a genius!" Bai Mo Xing and his three companions all thought in amazement.

Bai Ning Bing stood protectively in a combative stance, while Fang Yuan remained utterly calm behind her.

"To be protected by such a genius girl, this young man seems to be their leader. Who is he?" Experienced as he was, Bai Mo Xing's discernment was sharp.

As Bai Mo Xing pondered, Fang Yuan stepped forward and cupped his fists: "I am Gu Yue Fang Zheng, young master of Qing Mao Mountain's Gu Yue clan. Greetings, senior."

Bai Ning Bing was shocked as well, but lowered her head to conceal her expression.

Understanding the situation, the rapport built over recent days made her step back to Fang Yuan's side. She glared at them angrily, like a fearless bodyguard.

"Young lad, you lie! Qing Mao Mountain was destroyed long ago, do you think I don't know?" Bai Mo Xing shouted coldly.

Fang Yuan smiled bitterly, waving his hands: "It's precisely because Qing Mao Mountain was destroyed that I ended up here. Might I ask senior's name?"

Bai Mo Xing hesitated, but a young man beside him spoke: "Listen closely, we are Bai clan elites and this is our number one elder, my uncle—Lord Bai Mo Xing!"

Ostensibly hunting to temper the young masters' bravery, their true motive was finding the spirit spring on Bai Gu Mountain and clearing out wild beasts.

"As expected, it was Bai clan." Fang Yuan sneered internally.

Bai clan's spirit spring was drying up; they needed a new one to relocate their clan.

Outwardly, Fang Yuan smiled and cupped his fists: "So you are from Bai clan, fellow members of the righteous path. It is an honor!"

He turned to Bai Ning Bing: "Ning Bing, stand down, we are safe. Bai clan has no demonic Gu Masters."

Bai Ning Bing got goosebumps hearing Fang Yuan speak so gently.

Suppressing her disgust, she withdrew her battle intent and stepped back silently.

Bai Mo Xing narrowed his eyes: "It sounds like Junior Gu Yue encountered demonic Gu Masters along the way?"

"Yes, it still terrifies me." Fang Yuan patted his chest fearfully. "Fortunately, our clan leader and elders arrived in time and killed that rank three Gu Master."

"Clan leader and elders..." Bai Mo Xing's heart jumped. He pressed, "Are they nearby?"

Fang Yuan shook his head, sighing: "We got lost from the main team."

Bai Mo Xing relaxed, but Fang Yuan's next words alerted him again: "Yet we will surely regroup soon, as our destination is Bai Gu Mountain."

Bai Mo Xing grew nervous: "Bai Gu Mountain? Why are you coming here?"

"This..." Fang Yuan hesitated and didn't speak up.

With Qing Mao Mountain destroyed, what else would the Gu Yue remnants do but find a new location to rebuild?

"Did they choose Bai Gu Mountain too? Damn it!" Killing intent surged in Bai Mo Xing's heart.

He decided to first stabilize Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Smiling enthusiastically, he said: "What a coincidence, junior! Our Bai clan's main group is nearby holding our annual hunt. As local hosts, we invite you to be our guests. Please allow us to entertain you."

Bai Mo Xing laughed heartily: "Don't hesitate, junior, or you won't be giving me face."

Fang Yuan bowed: "Then I shall impose upon your clan."

A banquet was held inside a spacious tent.

"Come, the lamb leg is the tenderest. Guests from afar, please taste it," the Bai clan leader said warmly.

As Fang Yuan ate, tears suddenly began streaming down his face.

Startled, the Bai clan leader asked: "Junior Fang Zheng, why are you crying?"

"The taste is divine, but thinking of how Ning Bing and I barely survived so many perils, while our clansmen suffer starving in the wild, I can't help my heartache. Please forgive me, Clan Leader." Fang Yuan stood and cupped his fists.

The Bai clan leader said: "We deeply sympathize with your clan's disaster. Junior, what on earth happened at Qing Mao Mountain?"

# Chapter 216: Green Copper Relic Gu

"I dare not recall; the memories are too painful." Fang Yuan sat down, full of grief.

Seeing Fang Yuan reluctant to speak, the Bai clan leader did not press, ordering servants to bring two vats of wine.

Fang Yuan unsealed a vat, gulping down a mouthful as tears fell again.

The Bai clan leader was stunned: "Junior, why cry again?"

"Your wine is intoxicating, but it reminds me of our green bamboo wine and drinking on Qing Mao Mountain," Fang Yuan said, wiping his tears.

Fang Yuan's plight resonated with them. With their own spirit spring drying up, Gu Yue clan's fate could soon be Bai clan's future without a new one.

"Who wouldn't grieve a destroyed home? But as long as people survive, hope remains. Don't sorrow, junior—I'm sure you will reunite with your clansmen in a few days," Bai Mo Xing probed.

Feigning oblivion, Fang Yuan wiped his tears and answered casually: "Yes, it should only be a few days."

After the banquet, Bai clan leader called Bai Mo Xing and entered a secret discussion.

"Clan Leader, this looks bad. Why did the Gu Yue remnants come to Bai Gu Mountain if not to seize this territory? Should we strike first?" Bai Mo Xing asked worriedly.

She squinted and explained: "Don't be rash, Elder Mo Xing. This situation brings both peril and opportunity. Managed well, it could save us immense effort."

Having just arrived, Bai clan had found nothing yet. But if Gu Yue remnants were heading here, they must possess intel.

Bai Mo Xing asked: "So you want to obtain that information from the Gu Yue clan?"

"Exactly." Her eyes shone. "Their leader and elders would be difficult to handle, but with those two youngsters in our grasp, this is a godsend!"

Bai Mo Xing frowned: "Those two are no fools. That girl is stubborn and unyielding, while the boy, though weak, stayed calm and unfazed in danger. Even when surrounded, he didn't show a hint of panic. It won't be easy getting them to talk."

When she finished, Bai Mo Xing praised: "Brilliant! Gu Yue Fang Zheng is deeply attached to his clan; he even wept twice at the banquet. He's still young. Dangle honey before a bear cub or carrots before a rabbit, and he will certainly take the bait."

Fang Yuan opened a corner of the tent's cover.

"Young Master Fang Zheng, is something wrong?" As Fang Yuan lifted the tent flap, two guards immediately stepped forward.

Fang Yuan burped wine fumes: "I drank too much. Where can I relieve myself?"

After using the wooden latrine, Fang Yuan feigned drunkenness and walked the wrong way. Within twenty steps, patrolling Gu Masters intercepted him: "Esteemed guest, that's the wrong way. Your tent is over there."

Beds lined the tent's east and west sides. Bai Ning Bing sat cultivating, nurturing her aperture with primeval essence.

Hearing him enter, she opened her eyes inquiringly.

Fang Yuan glanced at her and flopped onto his bed: "Ning Bing, sleep early. You've had an exhausting few days. We'll rejoin our clansmen soon."

Bai Ning Bing's pupils shrank. Fang Yuan was acting, speaking for hidden surveillance Gu. His swift return meant security was airtight, ruling out a quiet nighttime escape.

"How do we escape this?" Bai Ning Bing frowned, staring at Fang Yuan.

The next morning, the Bai clan launched their annual seven-day hunting competition. "Junior Fang Zheng, did you sleep well?" the Bai clan leader asked Fang Yuan with a smile.

Fang Yuan cupped his fists: "Thank you for your hospitality, Clan Leader. I slept soundly until morning."

She chuckled and patted his shoulder warmly: "Would junior like to join our hunting competition? Show us the valor of a Gu Yue clansman!"

Looking troubled, Fang Yuan demurred: "I am ashamed. A recent severe injury dropped my cultivation from rank three. Though saved by my clan, I am only rank one middle stage now."

"No matter, junior. As our esteemed guest, you'll be well looked after. If you hunt an adult black bear, this Green Copper Relic Gu is yours." She clapped, and an aide opened his palm, revealing a round, finger-sized Gu.

Sneering inwardly, Fang Yuan feigned eagerness: "Then this junior gladly accepts!"

# Chapter 217: Giving others a hold on oneself

In the forest, a plump two-meter-tall black bear reared on its hind legs.

It growled at Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing, but their indifference infuriated it. Dropping to all fours, it pounced.

Having anticipated its retreat, Fang Yuan had buried five Charred Thunder Potato Gu along its path.

*BOOM!*

With a loud explosion, the battle ended.

Meanwhile, wisps of smoke drifted inside the central tent.

Images flickered in the smoke, displaying Fang Yuan's battle in real time.

"Elder Mo Xing, what do you think?" Bai clan leader spoke after the battle ended.

She shook her head: "A good analysis, but you missed the point. Fang Zheng did everything alone—from planting the Charred Thunder Potato Gu to finishing the fight. He has a rank three bodyguard yet painstakingly planted rank two Charred Thunder Potato Gu himself, repeatedly using primeval stones to recover essence. What does that tell you?"

Bai Mo Xing's eyes lit up: "I see! Fang Zheng is upright and unyielding. Having entered the hunt, he refuses to rely on outside help to cheat, no matter the difficulty."

"Schemers have weak wills, but upright people are indomitable. To extract the spirit spring's location, we must outwit them indirectly. Hehe, I'm even more confident in last night's plan." The clan leader smiled.

Half an incense stick later, Fang Yuan presented the torn bearskin to the clan leader: "Fortunately, I succeeded."

"To kill an adult black bear so quickly—as expected of a Gu Yue young master." Feigning mild shock, the clan leader smiled.

"Junior, go rest; the Green Copper Relic Gu will be delivered shortly."

"Thank you clan leader, this junior will take his leave."

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing returned to their tent.

Soon, a Gu Master delivered the Green Copper Relic Gu.

Fang Yuan took and used it immediately, advancing his cultivation to rank one upper stage.

That night, the clan leader held another banquet for them.

During the feast, Bai Mo Xing spoke up: "Come, let me introduce our rising stars. You youngsters should mingle."

Among them, fair-skinned Bai Lian was renowned as the Bai clan's greatest beauty.

"Bai Zhan Lie greets the esteemed guests," a young male Gu Master cut in before Bai Mo Xing could speak.

Muscular and arrogant, he exuded intense battle intent. He cast a disdainful look at Fang Yuan before fixing his gaze entirely on Bai Ning Bing.

With silver hair and blue eyes, Bai Ning Bing outshone even Bai Lian in beauty. More importantly, her rank three peak stage cultivation drew Bai Zhan Lie's full attention.

He snorted: "It seems Gu Yue clan has strong women and weak men."

Bai Ning Bing sat like an ice block, completely unresponsive.

Fang Yuan's expression turned cold and slightly ugly.

The Bai clan leader interjected: "He is our top young expert; junior, please forgive his bluntness."

"Of course not." Fang Yuan replied to the leader, "Brother Zhan Lie is a dragon among men; I am deeply impressed."

His complex expression perfectly blended the restraint of a guest, helplessness over weak cultivation, and a youth's indignant pride.

Bai Zhan Lie snorted, while Fang Yuan sneered inwardly.

He understood Bai clan's situation while they knew none of his trump cards. Though seemingly in peril, he held the key advantage: information.

"Using this advantage is key to our escape. The Green Copper Relic Gu shows Bai clan fears the non-existent Gu Yue remnants. They prefer manipulation over force. Is Bai Zhan Lie their next chess piece? His hostility felt far too deliberate."

"If so, that could cause trouble. I should give them a handle instead by exposing a fake weakness..."

Throughout the banquet, Fang Yuan repeatedly peeked at Bai Lian while avoiding direct eye contact.

This did not escape the notice of the clan leader and elders.

Afterward, Bai Mo Xing eagerly approached the clan leader: "Clan Leader, did you notice during the banquet?"

Bai clan leader smiled: "Let me plan a bit more."

On the hunt's second day, the Bai clan leader summoned Fang Yuan to hunt an Earth Horn Rhino.

Using Charred Thunder Potato Gu again, Fang Yuan blew up the Earth Horn Rhino and retrieved its horn.

Praising him, the Bai clan leader awarded him a Clearing Heat Gu.

This was a rank two healing Gu used to purge poison.

With it, Fang Yuan finally resolved his greatest deficiency.

During the campfire banquet that night.

"These are my children. Bai Sheng, Bai Hua, toast your older brother," the clan leader instructed.

A pair of twins rose and politely raised their cups together: "Bai Sheng (Bai Hua) toasts Gu Yue Young Master."

Bowing solemnly, they displayed excellent upbringing without a trace of childishness.

Fang Yuan was slightly stunned, sizing up the siblings attentively.

In his previous life, these two would become the renowned Righteous Twin Stars. Both reached rank five and elevated Bai clan village to unprecedented heights.

They would inherit the Bai Gu mountain inheritance, and Bai Sheng would eventually become clan leader.

# Chapter 218: Performing a great show

That night, Bai Lian visited Fang Yuan unprompted. A poisoned friend needed a rare clearing heat Gu, so she came to borrow his.

"Can't endure it anymore, huh?" Fang Yuan sneered inwardly, readily lending the clearing heat Gu.

The fifth day, at the banquet.

Bai clan leader asked suddenly: "Junior, does your Gu Yue clan intend to settle on Bai Gu mountain?"

Fang Yuan rose immediately, trying to appear composed while failing to hide the panic in his eyes: "Bai Gu mountain is practically your esteemed clan's territory. How could we dare encroach upon it?"

Fang Yuan was asleep when quarreling outside the tent woke him.

Stepping outside, he saw Bai Zhan Lie aggressively pulling Bai Lian's hands.

"Bai Zhan Lie, stop bothering me. Love can't be forced!" Bai Lian flung his hand off, saying icily, "I have things to do, leave."

"The hunting competition is starting, what could you have to do? Are you going to see that Gu Yue gigolo?" Bai Zhan Lie roared furiously.

Bai Lian glared: "Bai Zhan Lie, enough! Ah, Young Master Fang Zheng..."

"Esteemed guest? He's nothing but a stray dog." Bai Zhan Lie spat contemptuously, pointing at Fang Yuan. "Kid, if you have guts, let's duel! The loser stays away from Lian Er."

"Hmph, I'm only rank one while you're rank three. Does the grand Bai clan have no sense of fairness?" Fang Yuan's expression darkened.

"What's going on?" Bai Mo Xing hurried over at this moment.

"If you want to compete, let's compete! Who's afraid of who?" Seemingly provoked, Fang Yuan stiffened his neck and shouted impulsively.

Bai Mo Xing frowned: "Junior Fang Zheng, your bravery is clear, but as an esteemed guest, how could we explain any mishap to Gu Yue clan? Besides, your differing cultivation makes a direct duel unfair."

Biting her lip, Bai Lian stepped before Fang Yuan and asked softly with wide, watery eyes: "Young Master Fang Zheng, may I ask a favor?"

"Oh, what request?"

"I hope Young Master will accept this challenge and rid me of Bai Zhan Lie's harassment. I can no longer endure it," Bai Lian pleaded tearfully.

Fang Yuan patted his chest in agreement: "Worry not, Lady Bai Lian. Your trouble is mine; I will give my all."

She turned to Bai Mo Xing: "Lord Elder, even if Young Master Fang Zheng agreed, a duel would damage harmony and lack fairness. I propose we use the ongoing hunt, forming five-person teams to compete by hunting achievements."

"A good proposal." Bai Mo Xing stroked his beard, nodding. "Pick your teams, but overall cultivation must remain balanced."

Fang Yuan's team included rank three Bai Ning Bing, Bai Lian, and two other rank two female Gu Masters of similar age.

"Young Master Fang Zheng, I heard rumors your Gu Yue clan is migrating to Bai Gu mountain. Is that true?" Bai Sheng Jing asked casually during their successful return.

Bai Lian appeared worried: "Establishing a village is difficult; the most crucial condition is locating a spirit spring. The vanguard must fight fierce battles to clear them out, enduring inevitable bloodshed and sacrifice."

Looking at Fang Yuan, she added, "Bai Gu mountain teems with tough, troublesome bone Gu. Furthermore, it has no soil, only bones and stone. Surviving and building a village here is possible, but the cost is immense."

"Is that so?" Fang Yuan smiled reluctantly, his gaze betraying deep worry.

Feigning nonchalance, he asked, "I'm quite curious about Bai Gu mountain. As half its owner, could you tell me what dangers lie there?"

She exaggeratedly spoke while secretly activating the Anxiety Accumulation Gu inside her aperture.
