Fang Yuan's smile gradually faded, the worry in his eyes becoming visible.

Following, he kept on asking many questions.

They were all about Bai Gu mountain, especially areas on its back mountain.

"The fish took the bait." Bai clan leader smiled smugly. "The back mountain..."

She started looking at the map on the table.

# Chapter 219: Entering Bai Gu mountain

Bai Gu mountain was vast; even narrowed to the back mountain, pinpointing the exact location remained difficult.

"That Gu Yue lad is guarded and won't ask directly. To uncover the spirit spring's location, we must step up our efforts!" Bai clan leader evaluated, gritting her teeth.

In fact, Fang Yuan obtained even more valuable information from Bai Lian.

The righteous inheritance was indeed on the back mountain; Fang Yuan had not faked that.

Filtering Bai Lian's exaggerations with his five hundred years of experience, Fang Yuan realized reaching the inheritance grounds with Bai Ning Bing would take at least half a year.

Back at the village, their hunting results were indeed superior to Bai Zhan Lie's.

"Junior, it has been days; where are your clansmen?" Bai clan leader asked at the banquet.

Fang Yuan appeared worried: "By my calculation, they should arrive any day now. Perhaps they hit trouble, but their strength should see them through."

"What exactly are you planning?" Bai Ning Bing stared at Fang Yuan.

As days passed without Gu Yue reinforcements appearing, the Bai clan leader grew impatient. Tapping the table, the clan leader said, "This cannot go on. Bai Lian emphasized Bai Gu mountain's dangers during the hunt and Fang Zheng seemed shaken, but time waits for no one. Once the Gu Yue group arrives, Fang Zheng will have his pillar of support, and our efforts will be wasted."

Bai Mo Xing pondered: "Clan Leader, expecting Fang Zheng to trust us and reveal the spirit spring quickly is unrealistic. I have a plan, but we must intensify the pressure on him."

On the duel's third day, Bai Zhan Lie caught a wild swimming dragon butterfly while hunting, sending the camp into commotion.

Bai Lian looked grim: "The situation is bad—a rank three swimming dragon butterfly Gu puts Bai Zhan Lie far ahead. To win, we must take a risk. I know the forest terrain well. We can only go into Bai Gu mountain to hunt, and kill bone beasts or capture wild bone Gu."

Bai Ning Bing heard this, and her heart jumped.

Fang Yuan shook his head: "This is too dangerous, lady Bai Lian, we have limited strength, forget it."

Bai Lian laughed: "No worries, Young Master. I can coax Bai Sheng and Bai Hua into joining tomorrow's hunt. Lacking cultivation, they won't count as reinforcements. But they have Gu Masters hiding around protecting them from danger. If we enter Bai Gu mountain, and encounter danger, how can they not help? We will not take the wild beasts they kill, but only hunt our own. Beast packs always have the weak and sickly, and Bai Gu mountain's beasts are far more valuable."

Fang Yuan was inwardly amazed: Bai clan went this far just to avoid his suspicion.

Outwardly, he praised: "Great idea! Lady Bai Lian is truly pure-hearted, I am amazed."

At dusk, in the central tent, Bai clan leader stared intently at the rainbow smoke.

Seven people appeared in the image: Fang Yuan's five-person group, plus the two Bai clan young masters.

To protect them, she had dispatched numerous Gu Masters and every clan elder to secretly clear the path.

In those six hours, Bai clan sacrificed dozens of outstanding Gu Masters, and three elders were injured.

Guided by Bai Lian, Fang Yuan continued ascending until they reached the back mountain.

"With limited information and dusk falling, perhaps we should head back," Bai Lian said deliberately.

"Since we're here, we might as well explore," Fang Yuan said.

He took the lead, remembering that the inheritance ground was nearby.

"Can't you at least try to pretend?" Bai Ning Bing screamed inwardly, seeing Fang Yuan act completely out of character.

"Good, this is it! The spirit spring must be nearby!" the clan leader declared excitedly in the tent.

Just as she grew puzzled, a scout reported from outside: "Clan leader, an investigative Gu Master has returned with a Tie clan Gu Master bearing urgent news!"

# Chapter 220: Turns out they were demonic scoundrels!

"Important information?" Bai clan leader frowned - what could be more important than the spirit spring?

"You captured a Tie clan Gu Master?" Bai clan leader frowned.

Tie clan was a powerhouse ten Bai clans could not match; mishandling this would invite immense trouble.

"While tracking the Gu Yue remnants, I found a heavily injured Gu Master. Suspecting him of being a Gu Yue clansman, I healed him, but he claimed to be from Tie clan. He shared shocking news: Qing Mao mountain's three clans were wiped out! Tie clan mobilized pursuers after a mysterious demonic Gu Master who escaped the disaster," the investigative Gu Master reported.

"Here is the Tie clan token from his body, and his Gu worms are characteristic of Tie clan," the scout said, presenting a metal token.

Disheveled and pale, Tie Dao Ku was brought before the Bai clan leader.

"Great acting." Raising an eyebrow, Bai clan leader sneered: "Speak, demonic scoundrel—how did you kill a Tie clan Gu Master and obtain this token?"

Tie Dao Ku spat bloody phlegm: "*Spit*! I am Tie Dao Ku of the grand Tie clan! Do as you wish to me, but framing me is despicable hypocrisy!"

Bai clan leader sneered: "Tie clan Gu Masters below rank five travel in spirited, iron-willed groups. Not only are you travelling alone, just look at your state, you should act better if you want to pass off as a Tie clan Gu Master."

Bai clan leader's thoughts turned: "Recently, our Bai clan hosted two Gu Yue youths, one claiming to be their young master..."

Before she finished, Tie Dao Ku's eyes snapped open, blazing with radiance.

He shouted: "It's them, definitely them! Two youngsters—they must be the demonic Gu Masters who escaped Qing Mao mountain!"

Bai clan leader's heart sank after hearing this.

She waved her sleeve, releasing colorful smoke that coiled in the air to form an image.

In the image, Fang Yuan had already turned on the Bai clan, clutching Bai Hua and Bai Sheng by their throats: "Your young masters are in our hands—everyone get out!"

"Turns out they were demonic scoundrels!"

# Chapter 221: Welcome to Bai Gu Mountain

She first ordered her subordinates to release Tie Dao Ku.

"We wronged you, Tie clan warrior. I apologize." She stood and bowed sincerely.

He spoke: "Bai clan leader, the priority is arresting these scoundrels and saving your two young masters."

In the cave, swords were brandished.

"All of you, get out! Must I repeat myself?" Fang Yuan threatened with a cold snicker.

"Fang Zheng, you dare hold our young masters hostage? Unforgivable!"

"You finally showed yourselves? Excellent..." Fang Yuan snickered as Bai Sheng and Bai Hua struggled violently in his grip.

These two defenseless children were future rank five Gu Masters and the Righteous Twin Stars of Bai clan.

"Weren't my words clear enough? All of you get out, or else..." Fang Yuan laughed deviously, signaling Bai Ning Bing with a look.

Bai Ning Bing understood, taking out the chainsaw golden centipede.

Injecting primeval essence into it, she moved the furiously rotating razors toward Bai Hua's tender arm.

Fang Yuan laughed loudly: "On the count of three, if you aren't out of this cave, your young master loses a limb!"

"Quickly, get out of the cave!" the clan elders shouted, unwilling to gamble with their young masters' lives.

Everyone gradually retreated, staring at Fang and Bai on the way out.

Once everyone exited, Fang Yuan handed Bai Hua and Bai Sheng to Bai Ning Bing and surveyed their surroundings.

Bai Gu mountain was pure bone without soil, and sharp spiral-tipped bone spikes lined the white cave walls.

This was the spiral bone cave, the entrance to the Grey Bone Scholar's inheritance.

Fang Yuan recalled accounts of the inheritance corroborated by the twins in his previous life.

"We entered the spiral bone cave first, then accidentally found a twistable bone spike that opened the entrance..."

After pondering briefly, Fang Yuan began searching for that special bone spike.

With a loud rumble, the bone spikes contracted and a secret door opened, revealing a square cave entrance.

"Ah, there's a cave!" Bai Sheng shouted.

"Keep quiet." Bai Ning Bing chopped his neck, knocking him unconscious.

Fang Yuan entered quickly while Bai Ning Bing knocked Bai Hua unconscious, carrying both children as she followed.

The secret path shone with pure white light, though bone spikes jutted from the walls everywhere like sharp spears.

These dense bone spears sealed the tunnel, leaving only small gaps through which Fang Yuan could see a hall beyond.

"I'll open a path!" Bai Ning Bing stepped forward with the chainsaw centipede, but Fang Yuan stopped her.

"These spears are tough; don't rush." Recalling Bai Hua's words, Fang Yuan pulled the shortest spiral bone spike.

Mechanisms activated with a sound; the cave sealed behind them as the bone spears in the tunnel contracted into the walls.

Fang Yuan laughed heartily, turning to Bai Ning Bing: "Welcome to Bai Gu mountain."

# Chapter 222: White Bone Inheritance (1)

The secret tunnel was short; before long, Fang and Bai arrived at a huge hall.

The pure white bone hall had a huge vat in its center.

The vat was filled with fragrant, milk-white liquid.

"There is Gu in this vat..." Fang Yuan said, indicating to Bai Ning Bing with a glance.

Bai Ning Bing snorted, activating her defense and reaching into the vat.

When she pulled her hand out, it was full of Gu worms.

This was the Bone Spear Gu.

Among them was a rank two Spiral Bone Spear Gu.

"This way, I finally have a standard attack method," Fang Yuan thought, holding a Spiral Bone Spear Gu.

Using Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, Fang Yuan rapidly refined Gu after Gu from the vat.

Fang Yuan refined over two hundred Bone Spear Gu in his aperture, and over twenty Spiral Bone Spear Gu.

On the surface, she merely curled her lips and asked calmly, "You took so many Gu at once—can you afford to raise them?"

Fang Yuan laughed: "Of course not."

"Even if I can't feed them, I'd rather take them along than let the Bai clan have them." Fang Yuan laughed, pointing at the vat. "Destroy the rest."

Leaving the hall, they followed another secret tunnel into the second white hall.

At the center of the hall, there were three white bone pillars.

In the white bone hand, they each held one Gu.

"Ribcage shield Gu, Flying bone shield Gu, Arm bone wings Gu..." Bai Ning Bing stared at the pillar, muttering.

Her gaze soon fixed on a line: "Three, choose one, satisfied at heart. Bai Gu inheritance, left for future descendants."

"Ribcage shield Gu can be used together with the carapace Gu, to form defenses front and back. But the carapace Gu is bound to be replaced, and only the Ribcage shield Gu alone is not wide enough. I have the jumping grass, and do not fight close combat, the Arm bone wings Gu is also useless."

Fang Yuan thought about it, and chose the Flying bone shield Gu.

He smashed the bone arm, refining the Flying bone shield Gu and keeping it in his aperture.

The trial tested self-control; taking all three Gu out of greed would alter future tunnels, yielding fewer rewards.

The two moved along the secret tunnel entering a third hall.

No further tunnels led from this hall, only a human skeleton sitting inside the cave.

Before the human skeleton, there was a huge book.

The "Grey Bone Huge Book" contained numerous refinement recipes along with Grey Bone Scholar's life experiences.

The book stated the corpse was Grey Bone Scholar; an inheritor kowtowing three times could split the skull for a Gu. This was Grey Bone Scholar's lifebound Gu, left for the inheritor to uphold justice and better the world.

Fang Yuan laughed, handed the huge book to Bai Ning Bing, and knelt to kowtow three times respectfully.

"In my past life, Bai Sheng kowtowed to open one path, then Bai Hua stopped him from disturbing the corpse, triggering a superior second path."

Bai Ning Bing sighed, looking at them. "It seems we must rely on these two to survive. Still, I'm curious what Grey Bone Scholar's lifebound Gu is. Let's split the skull."

Fang Yuan shook his head. "Curiosity makes people imagine the Gu is far greater than reality. Don't be rash."

Once he finished, a part of the bone wall shrunk, moving away and revealing a new tunnel.

"This passage is correct, but not the most valuable one. Wait a bit longer."

After five minutes, just as Bai Ning Bing grew impatient, another door slid open to reveal a second tunnel.

"Haha, this is it!" Fang Yuan laughed, stepping forward and kicking Grey Bone Scholar's corpse to pieces.

Fang Yuan extracted a rank three Bone Spike Gu from the skull.

# Chapter 223: White Bone Inheritance (2)

An explosion rang out, the smoke clearing to reveal a cave.

Bai Clan Leader arrived just as the group blasted through the wall, exposing the secret tunnel.

"This should be an inheritance ground," Tie Dao Ku deduced at a glance, accompanying the clan leader.

"So that's it—their true destination was here!" Bai Clan Leader gritted her teeth, cold light flashing in her eyes.

"You were in contact with them the entire time—did you really notice no flaws?" Tie Dao Ku asked.

Bai Clan Leader turned to the volunteer elder. "I order your group to pave the way through this secret tunnel!"

"Hold on. The uneven bone spikes seal this passage completely—how did the thieves enter? There must be a mechanism. Let me inspect it first," Tie Dao Ku objected.

"Bone spike Gu..." Fang Yuan held this Gu and observed its details.

This rank three Gu dealt severe damage to foes while inflicting backlash on oneself.

Upon activation, the Gu Master's skeleton altered, protruding sharp, hard bone spikes outward.

Hearing Fang Yuan's explanation, Bai Ning Bing lost all interest.

She had already refined protective ice muscles.

Bone spikes would only rupture them.

Since it was still a rank three Gu, Fang Yuan refined it and stored it in his aperture.

"Let's go." Fang Yuan chose the last hidden door to appear.

Behind the door lay a long descending staircase spanning at least a thousand meters.

At the dark bottom of the stairs stood a tightly shut door.

There were four words carved on the door — Flesh Sac Secret Chamber.

Tie Dao Ku touched his chin and analyzed to himself: "This mechanism is somewhat ingenius. To open the entrance, one would need to twist the spiral bone spike. Unfortunately, to shut down this mechanism would require one to pull up the spiral bone spike and not twist them."

Passing through the secret tunnel, the group arrived at the first hall.

Dismembered corpses lay all around—the result of Bai Ning Bing slaughtering the Gu worms in the vat.

"Move quickly. The sooner we catch them, the less we suffer," Tie Dao Ku urged, brightening everyone's spirits.

The group reached the second hall and saw the three stone pillars.

"Look, Clan Leader." Soon, the Arm Bone Wings Gu and Ribcage Shield Gu came into view.

"Isn't it odd? Why did they leave these Gu behind?" As an onlooker, Tie Dao Ku clearly sensed something fishy.

"Three, choose one, satisfied at heart. Bai Gu inheritance, left for future descendants," someone read from the stone pillar.

Urging one another on, the group reached the third hall.

Inside sat a cross-legged skeleton beside an enormous bone book.

"Grey Bone Scholar... so this inheritance belonged to him." An elder opened the book, confirming it was a righteous path inheritance.

Tie Dao Ku frowned, growing increasingly uneasy as the pillar's inscription stirred an indescribable worry.

A section of the stone wall slid open to reveal a new secret tunnel, and the group entered.

"Flesh Sac Secret Chamber..." Bai Ning Bing murmured, pushing against the door.

The door was not locked at all and easily opened.

Behind the door lay a small, grotesque hidden chamber.

The chamber's pulsing flesh walls resembled a monster's stomach.

Cracks split across the walls, revealing teeth everywhere.

A cacophony of laughter echoed from the mouths throughout the narrow chamber, making any listener's heart palpitate.

# Chapter 224: White Bone Inheritance (End)

Fang Yuan patted Bai Ning Bing's shoulder. "This is the effect of Flesh Laughter Gu. Like Earth Treasury Flower, it stores Gu worms. No need to worry."

An open mouth's scarlet tongue extended outward, presenting a book.

The book explained reaching this chamber qualified them for the true inheritance of Grey Bone Scholar and Flesh Bone Master. Successors could knock on the teeth to yield the hibernating Gu stored within.

Lacking detailed accounts of this trial, Fang Yuan tested the teeth at random.

Bai Ning Bing tapped teeth with her forefinger, producing musical tones.

A mouth opened, extending a long scarlet tongue that revealed a Gu.

"What Gu is this?" Bai Ning Bing's pleasant surprise quickly turned to curiosity upon inspecting it.

Resembling a set of dentures, the Gu bore exceptionally neat, spotless white upper and lower teeth with a pearly luster.

Fang Yuan looked over, pleasantly surprised. "Not bad, this is the famous rank three healing Gu—Flesh-Bone Gu!"

"Want it? You can trade the Yang Gu for it," Bai Ning Bing chuckled smugly, holding the Gu.

Fang Yuan sneered: "You think I am stupid?"

Bai Ning Bing stared coldly. "Hand over the Yang Gu and we can make a deal. My patience has limits, Fang Yuan—at worst, we die together."

"You don't fear death, but do you think I do?" Fang Yuan sneered, unsurprised by her threat.

Bai Ning Bing inspected the Flesh-Bone Gu, smirking: "Killing you might also be a good option. You have rank one cultivation, instant refinement, and Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus. Killing you gives me everything, including this inheritance."

"Did you forget the Bai clan chasing us down the stairs? Without me, do you know where to go next? This inheritance has many branching paths. You are too naive to think you can seize the treasures by killing me. Do you think I will give you the opportunity to take the Yang Gu? Just try it, if you don't believe me."

Turning his back, Fang Yuan resumed knocking on the teeth.

Bai Ning Bing stared at Fang Yuan's defenseless back; a single move seemed enough to kill him.

Yet despite herself, Bai Ning Bing did not dare act rashly.

"What is going on?" Standing outside the inheritance exit, the group looked around with grim expressions.

They had rushed through several bone halls and collected some gains, but never encountered Fang and Bai, ultimately exiting the secret tunnel onto the mountainside.

"Brother Tie, what do you mean?" Bai Clan Leader asked immediately. Normally sharp, she was now unnerved by her children's kidnapping and the passing time.

"The Bai Gu Mountain inheritance seems linear, but it actually branches—like this twig in my hand," Tie Dao Ku said, snapping a thin branch from a nearby bone tree.

"The path at the start should have been the same for both us and them, thus we could see their traces on our way. But afterwards, we should have walked through a branch path and they should have walked into another branch. Do you still remember the second hall? There were three pillars and I suspect that there was a contraption set up in the pillars; when we walked into the third hall, there was the Grey Bone Scholar's skeleton, but there were no signs of the skeleton being moved. The crucial point should be the secret tunnel after the second hall..."

Before Tie Dao Ku could finish, Bai Clan Leader barked, "We're going back!"

The inheritance structure could only deceive the Bai clan for so long; once they figured it out, he and Bai Ning Bing would be in grave danger.

"Could this secret chamber already be the final area? Did Bai Hua and Bai Sheng obtain all their Gu worms from these mouths? That can't be, the rumors in my previous life said Bai Hua and Bai Sheng arrived at a precipice after going through this inheritance site. This rumor has been publicly approved by Bai clan. Maybe the mechanism to open the passage is hidden in one of these mouths?"

"By then, we can only rely on Bai Hua and Bai Sheng as our protective amulets. But this is not a very reliable guarantee. This world has many magical Gu worms; there are many with control abilities like causing dizziness, hypnosis, paralysis and so on. Bai Clan is a powerful clan and definitely has such methods." When he thought of this, Fang Yuan couldn't help looking at Bai Hua and Bai Sheng.

Acting immediately, Fang Yuan walked over and kicked the twins.

# Chapter 225: Jade Bones, Steel Bones

With a loud crash, the bone wall collapsed to reveal a new path.

Bai Clan Leader ordered searches across every branch path.

A Gu Master reported ecstatically: "Eighth path's skeleton was smashed and skull Gu taken!"

"Lead the way!" Bai Clan Leader immediately moved.

The pursuers rushed down the staircase toward Flesh Sac Secret Chamber.

The door was smashed open, and the group surged inside.

However, there was not a soul in the secret chamber.

Locking onto the secret tunnel, Bai Clan Leader ordered: "Follow me!"

A while ago, in the same secret chamber.

Bai Sheng regained consciousness to a sharp pain in his stomach.

"Stand up if you don't want to die!" a ruthless voice ordered.

Grotesque flesh walls lined with laughing mouths surrounded him.

"Flesh Laughter Gu?" he blurted out, recalling what he had read in books.

"Young, but quite knowledgeable," Fang Yuan smiled.

"You two!" Bai Sheng gritted his teeth, glaring at Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing with deep hatred.

"Big brother!" Bai Hua, also kicked awake, ran sobbing to Bai Sheng's side.

Bai Sheng promptly protected his sister behind him.

Fang Yuan walked forward, grabbing Bai Hua's collar.

The little girl cried out in panic as Fang Yuan hoisted her up.

His defiance shattered, Bai Sheng shouted anxiously, "Scoundrel, let go of my sister!"

Pinching Bai Hua's cheek, Fang Yuan said in an unhurried tone, "Make another sound and I'll cut out both your tongues."

Terrified, Bai Hua choked back her tears, not daring to make a sound.

"Good. Cooperate obediently, and I might just let you live," Fang Yuan smiled.

"I captured you for safety, not malice. Obey and live; refuse, and I'll slice off fingers and tongues for fun."

"What do you want us to do?" Bai Sheng asked, terrified of Fang Yuan.

"Do you see these mouths in the walls?" Fang Yuan's smile widened.

He explained the details and set the siblings to knocking teeth.

Moments later, Bai Hua cried out in fear.

A mouth opened before her, its scarlet tongue extending to reveal a quail-egg-sized bone ball.

Fang Yuan stepped over and took the Gu in pleasant surprise.

Movement Gu—footless bird.

After a while, Bai Sheng also achieved success.

Knocking open a mouth, he revealed a bone on its tongue.

Jade bone Gu!

Fang Yuan caught it, immediately refining and storing it in his aperture.

Jade bone Gu permanently remodeled a skeleton into jade, making bones harder and more supple.

Combined with ice muscles, jade bones would complement Bai Ning Bing's physique greatly.

"Want it? Trade it for meat-bone Gu," Fang Yuan smiled.

Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly and turned away without answering, knocking teeth harder.

Bai Hua knocked down another set of teeth, giving Fang Yuan an iron bone Gu.

The mouth Bai Sheng struck opened wide, turning into a new secret tunnel.

With pursuers closing in and remaining mouths resilient, Fang Yuan abandoned the rest.

Fang Yuan knocked the siblings unconscious again, carrying one in each hand as he followed Bai Ning Bing into the giant mouth.

Walking through the secret tunnel, the two came upon a hall.

Spanning two square kilometers, the hall featured a central flat-topped bone pyramid.

Finding no danger after checking their surroundings, the two climbed to the pyramid's summit platform.

A ferocious beast skull sculpture rested on the platform—over half a man tall, resembling a lion or tiger with tightly clamped fangs.

# Chapter 226: Bone Flesh Unity

"Resembling a lion or tiger skull, isn't this the rumored two-person mechanism?" A thought flashed in Fang Yuan's mind.

"There are words carved on the fangs," Bai Ning Bing discovered, reading softly: "*Gemini of one mind, three souls unite. Fated or not, do not force it...* What does this mean?"

"The code to open this mechanism. Gemini means two people must cooperate, while three souls indicates mind, palm, and eye," Fang Yuan recalled.

"Come, put your palm on the pupils of the sculpture."

Translucent red gems in the skull's eye sockets reflected Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Yet even after placing their palms on the red gems for a long time, nothing happened.

Fang Yuan's expression darkened. In his previous life, Bai Hua had described this exact procedure—why was there no change?

"Gemini of one mind, three souls united..." Fang Yuan muttered. "Three souls are united, but one mind, one mind..." Light dawned in his eyes.

Though he and Bai Ning Bing cooperated out of necessity, their separate schemes prevented achieving 'one mind'.

At this thought, Fang Yuan looked back at Bai Sheng and Bai Hua.

Fang Yuan promptly kicked the siblings awake again.

"Evil thieves, what do you want?!" Bai Sheng shouted angrily upon waking.

Without bothering to explain, Fang Yuan seized their hands and pressed them onto the red gems.

Truly the fated inheritors, the moment their small hands touched the red gems, bright light shone forth.

*Crack, crack...*

The skull slowly opened its mouth, revealing a pile of coal stones and dry firewood.

In the center of the black coal lay a plain pottery pot with a scroll resting atop it.

"What is this?" Fang Yuan dropped the twins and read the scroll, soon coming to understand.

The inheritance's owner, Gray Bone Scholar, had possessed low aptitude and struggled with cultivation throughout his life.

In his final years, while setting up the White Bone Mountain inheritance, heaven seemed to pity him as he gained an ingenious inspiration.

The name of this experiment was —"Bone Flesh Unity Gu".

The scroll stated that refining this Gu required two blood-related Gu Masters—parent and child, or twins. Only through bloodline connection could primeval essence be transformed.

Gray Bone Scholar lacked time to test the concept, helplessly stopping at the final step despite completing most preparations.

Fang Yuan already knew the recipe worked: in his previous life, Bai Sheng and Bai Hua had used this dual cultivation to reach rank five as righteous twin stars, pushing Bai clan to its peak.

However, it was quite troublesome for Fang Yuan now.

He had assumed bone flesh unity Gu was a finished product, but it was not yet refined—not even half-finished.

Moreover, he and Bai Ning Bing did not meet the refinement conditions.

In his past life, Bai Sheng and Bai Hua had refined it. But years earlier now, though deeply bonded, they were not yet Gu Masters and failed the criteria.

He and Bai Ning Bing met half the criteria, while the twins met the other half. Combining all four might bring success.

"Come, help." Fang Yuan began igniting the dry firewood inside the skull.

The fire instantly soared and burned furiously.

"You plan to force the refinement?" Bai Ning Bing said in shock. "That's not sensible."

Even so, she placed her palm on the red gem pupil, pouring in primeval essence.

The blazing fire suddenly shifted from orange to eerie blue.

As the pot roasted, several hibernating Gu awoke and struggled frantically, rattling the container.

The refinement steps were simple: Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing alternated pouring primeval essence.

Soon, they reached the final critical juncture.

This step required fresh flesh thrown into the fire; the scroll noted more flesh produced better results.

"Fortunately, we have flesh-bone Gu; cutting off flesh won't be a problem." Bai Ning Bing moved to act.

"Slow down, I have a better idea."

Bai Ning Bing followed Fang Yuan's gaze to the twins, Bai Sheng and Bai Hua.

"You want to modify the refinement now?" A disturbed look flashed in Bai Ning Bing's eyes as she realized his intent.

"What do you want?!" Bai Sheng shielded his sister, gripped by a sudden panic as calamity loomed over them.

"You should feel honored that your sacrifice will birth a new Gu. Even Senior Gray Bone would rejoice," Fang Yuan grinned evilly.

"Sister, run!" Bai Sheng shouted, charging forward to grab Fang Yuan's leg.

"Big brother!" Tears spilled from Bai Hua's eyes, but while she hesitated, Fang Yuan had already knocked Bai Sheng unconscious.

Fang Yuan indifferently knocked her unconscious as well.

Moving efficiently, he stripped the siblings of their clothes and threw them one in each hand into the fire.

Searing heat woke them instantly as agonizing pain struck, making them scramble madly to escape.

Fang Yuan snorted coldly, kicking them back into the fire.

This repeated until Bai Sheng and Bai Hua finally burned to death.

Their bodies melted like candles, turning the fire from eerie blue to scarlet blood.

Yet the transformation stalled, failing to turn reddish-purple.

"What now?" Bai Ning Bing frowned; a failed refinement would inflict backlash on the Gu Masters.

Fang Yuan's mind raced: "The twins were fated inheritors—did it fail because they aren't Gu Masters? If so, we must try again!"

Without hesitation, Fang Yuan activated the tusita flower to spit out a sharp blade.

He slashed his forearm, letting blood flow as he threw his own flesh into the man-high flames.

"Your turn." He threw the sharp blade to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing slashed her forearm, but her ice muscles resisted the blade like solid ice.

With no other choice, Bai Ning Bing called out chainsaw golden centipede and sawed a piece of flesh.

When her flesh hit the fire, it immediately turned reddish-purple.

"Good, success hinges on this! Pour our primeval essence together," Fang Yuan rejoiced.

Pouring primeval essence simultaneously, the mechanism closed, swallowing the flames like a bone beast.

Two rows of fangs locked shut as the internal flames roasted the skull scarlet.

Bang! The pottery container seemed to have exploded.

The whole skull shook.

Hearing this sound, Fang and Bai finally released their hands.

Fang Yuan watched closely while extending a hand toward Bai Ning Bing.

She snorted, but had no choice except handing him the flesh-bone Gu.

She could not instantly refine Gu worms, but Fang Yuan could.

Under Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, the flesh-bone Gu offered no resistance and was instantly refined.

Unable to use it, Fang Yuan handed the refined Gu back to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing activated it; an orange glow covered her forearm, regrowing flesh until completely healed.

Following, Bai Ning Bing healed Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan was pale; lacking ice muscles to curb bleeding, he had lost a large amount of blood.

The skull slowly reopened, the fire having completely vanished.

Two Gu worms appeared in Fang Yuan's sight.

One green and one red, linked like jade bracelets, they hovered quietly in the air with a warm luster.

Without investigating further, Fang Yuan took and instantly refined the Gu into his aperture.

"Let's go!" He leapt off the platform and rushed into the new secret tunnel at the hall's end.

Shortly after, the Bai clan Gu Masters arrived at the hall.

"Someone refined a Gu here!" The lingering aura made the elders' expressions change.

"Look, the young masters' clothes!" They soon found the torn children's clothing on the platform.

An intensely bad feeling struck the Bai clan leader, nearly causing her to black out.

"Chase them! They can't have gone far—my children are definitely with them!" the Bai clan leader shouted, eyes bloodshot.

# Chapter 227: Footless bird, spread your wings and fly!

"The exit!" Bai Ning Bing shouted.

Suddenly, footsteps could be heard from behind.

Bai clan Gu Masters charged forward, seething with killing intent.

The narrow tunnel stretched the Bai clan into a long column.

Blitz Gu.

Suddenly, an elder surged forward like a cannonball, catching up to Fang Yuan.

"Bai Ning Bing!" Fang Yuan shouted.

Gritting her teeth, Bai Ning Bing swung her hand backward.

Blood moon Gu.

A blood blade flew out, striking the elder and slowing him.

Instantly, a barrage of glowing attacks erupted down the tunnel toward Fang and Bai.

"Bai Ning Bing, take this!" Fang Yuan shouted, lending her the canopy Gu again.

Bai Ning Bing activated Canopy Gu and her ice muscle defense to withstand the barrage.

"Bai clan, don't you want to know where your young masters are?" Fang Yuan shouted.

Remembering the young masters were in their hands, the Bai clan Gu Masters halted their attacks.

"Return my children!" Enraged, the Bai clan leader pointed forward, shooting out a cold light.

Fang Yuan snorted, letting the frost fish Gu hit him without dodging.

Cold air enveloped his body, drastically slowing his speed.

Stealth scales Gu.

Ripples undulated around his body with a thought, turning him invisible.

"Don't even think about it!" An elder lunged, clawing toward Fang Yuan.

With a soft snap, the stealth scales Gu inside Fang Yuan's aperture shattered and died.

Fang Yuan's heart sank, activating the jumping grass immediately.

Using the grass's elasticity, Fang Yuan leaped ahead with drastically increased speed.

"I'll take my leave first—remember to jump!" he called back oddly.

Bai clan leader punched directly, the huge force shattered the canopy Gu's white armor immediately.

Coughing up a mouthful of blood, Bai Ning Bing summoned the chainsaw golden centipede.

Turning into golden light, the chainsaw golden centipede coiled around the Bai clan leader.

Seizing the opportunity, Bai Ning Bing sprinted to the exit.

It was a cliff!

Bai Ning Bing suddenly understood Fang Yuan's meaning—he wanted her to jump off the cliff!

"You cannot escape!" the Bai clan leader roared, having torn the centipede apart.

Jumping from this height meant certain death, but under the circumstances, she had no other choice.

She gritted her teeth and jumped.

Looking down at Bai Ning Bing falling, veins bulged on the Bai clan leader's forehead as she growled: "Dead or alive, I will definitely catch them!"

Wind roared in Bai Ning Bing's ears as she plunged downward.

"Dying like this? Exhilarating as it is, I'm not satisfied..." Facing death, Bai Ning Bing's heart churned.

Turbulence intensified around her as she heard a voice: "Bai Ning Bing!"

Bai Ning Bing turned—who else could it be but Fang Yuan?

Fang Yuan was descending as well, riding atop a white bone bird.

Footless bird!

Composed entirely of bone, it had four wings and no legs.

Covered in blood and filth, Fang Yuan had clearly sustained numerous injuries in the secret tunnel.

"Grab my hand." Squatting on the bird's back, he reached out.

With a clap, two hands grasped each other tightly.

Fang Yuan exerted his strength, hauling Bai Ning Bing onto the bird's back.

Yet a crag loomed less than three hundred meters below as the footless bird plunged toward it like a shooting star.

"Careful, we're going to crash!" Bai Ning Bing shouted, heart leaping into her throat.

Fang Yuan's eyes blazed as he shouted ferociously: "How can my demonic path falter on tiny Bai Gu Mountain? RISE FOR ME!"

The footless bird flapped furiously to brake, cracks spreading across its four bone wings.

Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus continuously replenished his rapidly draining primeval essence.

Bai Ning Bing forgot to breathe while Fang Yuan glared fiercely ahead.

The footless bird narrowly cleared the crag, soaring steeply into the sky.

"Hahaha!" Bai Ning Bing laughed wildly on the bird's back.

Truly thrilling—the dance between life and death was always the most breathtaking. Wasn't such a life what she pursued?

"Footless bird, soar into the blue sky!" She was nearly singing with excitement.

"Clan leader! They're alive!" the Bai clan Gu Masters said darkly from the cliff.

"We can't catch them—that is the footless bird, able to fly ten thousand miles a day," Tie Dao Ku sighed helplessly.

Bai clan leader's eyes turned bloodshot; she gritted her teeth, clenching her fists until her knuckles nearly burst.

"No! We cannot let them escape! We cannot!" a Gu Master roared in fury as intense flames suddenly erupted across his body.

"Bai Zhan Wen." Bai clan leader's expression changed.

"Clan leader, avenging our young masters is paramount! After I am gone, please care for my grandson..." His voice faded at the end.

Human Torch Gu.

This rank four Gu immolated the user's body into flame until lifeforce was exhausted.

His purplish-black eyes swept over the surrounding crowd, lingering on his grandson one last time before soaring into the sky.

The human torch rapidly approached the footless bird.

"A strong foe!" Bai Ning Bing's expression was grim.

"Hold on tight!" Fang Yuan warned, fully focused on maneuvering the footless bird.

The footless bird accelerated and pulled away, leaving the human torch striking empty air.

Fang Yuan sneered as the footless bird folded its wings, plummeting toward the ground.

The human torch pursued closely, outspeeding the bird and closing the distance.

The footless bird suddenly spread its four wings, halting its plunge to reverse direction.

*BOOM!*

Lacking wings to change course quickly, the human torch crashed into the rocks.

Over six square kilometers erupted into flames, incinerating beasts and trees.

From the inferno, the flames coalesced back into human shape, taking flight to pursue Fang and Bai relentlessly.

The footless bird maneuvered smoothly through the air, repeatedly dodging the human torch.

"Young man, your bird-maneuvering skills are superb, hahaha!" Bai Ning Bing laughed heartily, lowering her guard.

Fang Yuan's expression froze: "Careful!"

*BOOM!*

Knowing he could not catch them, Bai Zhan Wen chose to self-detonate.

The explosion and flames engulfed the footless bird.

While the flames caused little damage, the shockwave from the explosion posed the true threat.

Cracks spiderwebbed across the footless bird's body as it lost balance, plummeting earthward.

After plunging a distance, Fang Yuan stabilized its flight and steered it away.

"Fang Yuan!" Bai Ning Bing screamed.

Fang Yuan had lent her the Canopy Gu earlier, leaving himself unprotected.

As the footless bird broke out of the flames, Fang Yuan was burning.

Flames incinerated the Earth Hearing Ear Grass in his right ear, yet his expression remained steel-cold.

Fang Yuan drew milk spring from the Tusita Flower and doused his head.

The fire died, leaving his charred face disfigured and gruesome.

Fang Yuan smirked: "I like the footless bird, do you know why?"

"Why?"

"Because it has only wings and no legs, forcing it to fly. Landing signifies its destruction."

Bai Ning Bing's blue eyes gleamed. With the wind whipping her silver hair, her lips curled: "Hehehe, then let us fly into the sky."

The footless bird vanished into the distant horizon.

*Puff.*

Bai clan leader coughed up a mouthful of blood and collapsed backward, unconscious.

# Chapter 228: Dark Leopard dies for love

After flying most of the day, the footless bird began to descend beneath the dusk sky.

Damaged by extreme aerial maneuvers and the explosion, it could no longer fly.

Under Fang Yuan's careful control, the footless bird crashed into a forest.

"Where is this?" Bai Ning Bing jumped down, surveying her surroundings.

"A purple forest... heading north along our course, this should be near Zi You Mountain," Fang Yuan deduced.

"Zi You Mountain is perilous at night. It is getting dark; we must find shelter quickly."

"Okay." Bai Ning Bing nodded.

After over two hours, they found a cave luckily.

Dry wood crackled in the blazing bonfire while meat stew bubbled in an iron pot, emitting a rich aroma.

As the two devoured the food, their tension gradually eased.

Bai Ning Bing chuckled: "Retribution. You burned those siblings, and now you are burned yourself."

Firelight illuminated Fang Yuan's severely mangled face, making him look hideous enough to terrify any timid girl into screaming.

Fang Yuan merely laughed, unbothered and inwardly pleased.

"Meat Bone Gu could restore your appearance if you slice off the charred flesh and regrow it. Being rank one, you can't activate it. Beg me, and maybe I'll take pity and heal you."

Fang Yuan made a motion of raising his eyebrows, despite his eyebrows already being burnt off.

"Why heal it? This saves me the trouble of disguising myself from Bai clan," Fang Yuan chuckled.

With his right ear shattered beyond healing, losing it was a welcome disguise against pursuit.

"Actually, your blue eyes and silver hair are too conspicuous; you should change them," Fang Yuan remarked, scanning Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing coldly snorted and didn't reply.

"Bai clan will pursue us. If they distribute wanted posters, our situation will worsen."

Bai Ning Bing frowned in thought before agreeing: "Fine, I am tired of this look anyway. A new appearance might be interesting."

Next, the two started to calculate their losses and gains.

Their losses included the Earth Hearing Ear Grass, Chainsaw Golden Centipede, Carapace Gu, Iron Thistles, Stealth Scales Gu, and Footless Bird.

In his aperture lay numerous Bone Spear and Spiral Bone Spear Gu. They had also gained the rank three Flying Bone Shield, Jade Bone Gu, Iron Bone Gu, healing Meat Bone Gu, and several bone recipe books.

Additionally, they had acquired the Clearing Heat Gu from Bai clan.

Most crucial was the Bone Flesh Unity Gu, successfully refined amidst mortal danger.

Compared to the gains, their losses weren't that serious!

"It doesn't matter, I secured everything on my list. Once Bone Flesh Unity Gu displays its effects, it will far outweigh the rest. Losing the Earth Hearing Ear Grass is the only real hassle."

To Fang Yuan, only practical things held true value.

Spiral Bone Spear Gu could replace the lost Chainsaw Golden Centipede, while Canopy Gu and Flying Bone Shield covered defense after losing Iron Thistles and Carapace Gu. However, losing the Earth Hearing Ear Grass left them blind in reconnaissance.

While healing and mobility were now adequately addressed, a glaring gap had opened in their investigative capability.

In life, things often do not go your way.

Night on Zi You Mountain proved far wilder than daytime. Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing took turns keeping watch, neither getting proper rest.

At the crack of dawn, a violent battle erupted near the cave, waking the sleeping Fang Yuan.

This was a fight between two thousand beasts kings!

Two winged black feather python had provoked a dark leopard.

The two clashed with ferocious momentum, trading lethal blows in a deafening frenzy.

On edge, Fang and Bai were effectively trapped inside the cave.

Over time, the dark leopard was gradually overwhelmed and cornered.

Ultimately, the female leopard died from the python's constriction.

The returning male leopard ambushed and slew the python, only to find its mate's cold corpse.

The male leopard paced mournfully around the female, letting out low whimpers—so close, yet forever parted by death.

"Why is it not leaving?" Bai Ning Bing grumbled.

"Mated dark leopards share one heart; neither lives on alone. I'm going back to sleep."

He retreated into the cave depths to sleep while Bai Ning Bing stood watch at the entrance.

The male leopard paced briefly before lying down, gently licking the female's wounds.

Gradually, its bright eyes dimmed and closed forever.

Soon after, a refreshed Fang Yuan stepped out to find Bai Ning Bing leaning against the cave wall, staring blankly at the leopard carcasses.

"How is the harvest?" Fang Yuan asked.

Bai Ning Bing shrugged: "The flying Gu escaped, and whatever remains from battle is useless to us. Haha, if it were not for that, why would someone like you go back to sleep?"

Fang Yuan chuckled: "Though they were thousand beast kings, their Gu weren't extraordinary. But that doesn't mean there's nothing to harvest."

Saying so, Fang Yuan walked towards the dark leopards' corpses.

# Chapter 229: Resting and recovering at the hamlet

Bai Ning Bing watched curiously as Fang Yuan walked over to the carcasses.

He crouched down, feeling around the leopards' ears.

Fang Yuan extracted two delicate purple leaves from the leopards' ears.

This was Breath Concealment Gu.

This rank three grass Gu allowed Gu Masters to conceal their cultivation level.

Fang Yuan didn't have the earth hearing ear grass to scout, but fortunately they obtained two Breath Concealment Gu.

Skirting around Zi You Mountain for two days, they eventually struck a mountain path.

Following the path until evening, they spotted a thin wisp of smoke.

Exchanging glances, they quickened their pace and found a hamlet nestled in a mountain basin.

Short stone walls bordered the hamlet as farmers returned with tools in the dusk.

"Let's go." Fang Yuan walked towards the hamlet.

"Just like this?" Bai Ning Bing was somewhat surprised.

Before they reached the entrance, two similar-looking guards approached: "Distinguished guests, might you be lord Gu Masters?"

Bai Ning Bing kept quiet; by prior agreement, Fang Yuan handled all communication.

Fang Yuan shook his head: "Hello brothers, we are both mortals."

The guards visibly relaxed, heaving sighs of relief upon hearing this.

The younger guard eyed Fang Yuan disdainfully, sneering: "I knew it. How could someone so hideous ever be an esteemed lord Gu Master?"

Bai Ning Bing had dyed her hair black, wearing a straw hat to hide her blue eyes.

Side by side, they looked remarkably like ordinary villagers.

"Mind your tongue," the older guard scolded his partner, then eyed them warily. "Where are you from, and what brings you here?"

"We're from across the mountain, transporting herbs and meat, but ran into a tiger on the road. We ran crazily all the way and only then could we save ourselves. Sigh... we don't dare to go back for the moment, so we came to your hamlet and hope to spend the night here. We will immediately leave tomorrow." Fang Yuan spoke off the top of his head.

The vigilance in the guard's gaze lessened.

"Everyone has it rough these days," the senior guard sighed. "You may enter. If no one takes you in, make do against the outer wall."

Strolling through the hamlet, he stopped before a dilapidated house.

Only an elderly woman lived in the dilapidated house.

The old woman was drawing water from a well outside, clearly straining with the effort.

"Auntie, let me help!" Fang Yuan called, jogging over with an eager, foolish grin.

The old woman started at Fang Yuan's gruesome appearance.

His goofy smile and help hauling water quickly dispelled her guard.

"Young man, you are from out of town?" she asked with a gap-toothed smile.

"Yes, we hope to stay the night. Auntie, I can do your chores."

"Alright," she agreed happily, welcoming the extra manual labor.

Bai Ning Bing watched this scene speechlessly.

"Auntie, let me fetch more water." After dinner, Fang Yuan hauled more water.

Bai Ning Bing rested on the floor, letting days of fatigue melt away.

Sitting cross-legged on the bed, Fang Yuan inspected the Bone Flesh Unity Gu inside his aperture.

Suddenly, Fang Yuan opened his eyes, a sharp light flashing within.

Confirming the refinement succeeded, he murmured, "No issues; it's ready," and summoned the pair of bracelet-like jade Gu.

One bracelet was grass-green and the other blood-red, interlocked together seamlessly.

Accepting the Gu, Bai Ning Bing asked: "What is our next move?"

Fang Yuan chuckled: "I thought you'd never ask."

Fang Yuan did not hide his intent: "Our next destination is Shang Liang Mountain."

"Shang Liang mountain, Shang clan?" Bai Ning Bing's brows slightly furrowed.

Bai Ning Bing hesitated: "After what we did to Bai clan, won't righteous bounties await us? Wouldn't we just be heading straight into a trap by going to Shang clan city?"

Fang Yuan smiled: "If only two places in the Southern Border can accommodate us, Shang Clan City is one. Shang clan may lead the righteous path, but their city is entirely open, serving as the premier black market for demonic cultivators to fence goods. That black market makes Shang clan the wealthiest force in the Southern Border."

Bai Ning Bing pondered: "Rumors say one can buy anything in Shang Clan City. Is that true?"

Fang Yuan shook his head: "Rare goods have endless demand—like a certain Yang Gu, perhaps?"

# Chapter 230: Dual Cultivation

In the dark room, Bai Ning Bing snorted in annoyance.

Fang Yuan shifted topic: "Shang Clan City suits us best right now. Its markets offer suitable Gu. While our current Gu are powerful, they lack synergy. Only with Gu worms that can complement each other, can we exhibit stronger fighting prowess and even fight higher ranks."

Bai Ning Bing was in complete agreement.

Their aims in Shang Clan City were laying low and trading Gu, but Fang Yuan held a third secret goal.

"It's a long journey to Shang Clan City, no need to think about this now. First refine the bone flesh unity Gu," Fang Yuan ended the topic.

She shook her head to calm her mood and began refining the bone flesh unity Gu.

Bai Ning Bing picked the red of the green-and-red Gu pair and poured primeval essence inside.

Fang Yuan erased his initial imprint on the Gu and cooperated with Bai Ning Bing.

The linked jade bracelets dissipated, leaving circular marks on Fang Yuan's and Bai Ning Bing's wrists.

A mysterious connection emerged from both Fang and Bai's hearts.

Through this connection, they could sense each other mutually, as if their flesh and blood were inseparably linked.

Enduring the strange sensation, the two sat cross-legged facing each other on the bed to begin dual cultivation.

They extended their hands, placing their four palms together.

For safety, Fang Yuan, having the weaker cultivation, tested it first.

He extracted some green copper primeval essence and cautiously channeled it into Bai Ning Bing's aperture.

The green and red circular marks suddenly shone brightly.

Upon entering Bai Ning Bing's aperture, the foreign primeval essence immediately shifted to her aura.

"It really worked!" Bai Ning Bing softly exclaimed in delight.

"I transferred ten percent green copper essence, but two-fifths were lost in conversion," Fang Yuan observed.

Bai Ning Bing was not surprised: "The scroll said this Gu has five quality tiers depending on the refiners' bond, from fratricidal to blood kin relation."

She analyzed: "If the siblings had refined it together, their bond would yield 'blood kin relation'. Given our relationship, it would normally be the worst quality 'fratricidal'. These two neutralized each other, giving us the mid-quality 'indissoluble relation.'"

Using his past life's experience, Fang Yuan had altered the recipe at the last moment, achieving the rank three indissoluble relation Gu through good fortune.

"That's it. Your turn now." Fang Yuan said.

Bai Ning Bing transferred ten percent of her snow silver primeval essence into Fang Yuan's aperture.

Now, as she transferred ten percent primeval essence into Fang Yuan's aperture, it caused his aperture to shake before even landing in his sea.

Fang Yuan immediately shouted to stop.

As a mere rank one upper stage Gu Master storing hundreds of bone spear Gu, receiving snow silver essence overwhelmed his aperture.

Bai Ning Bing hesitated; continuing the transfer might shatter Fang Yuan's aperture.

After a moment's thought, she withdrew her primeval essence.

Fang Yuan moved all the bone spear Gu into Bai Ning Bing's aperture before accepting her snow silver primeval essence.

Once inside Fang Yuan's aperture, the snow silver primeval essence shrank and transformed from Bai Ning Bing's aura to his own.

Fang Yuan directed this snow silver primeval essence to surge against his aperture walls.

Yet with a single impact from the snow silver essence, the stone membrane wall shook violently!

# Chapter 231: Addicted to cultivating!

Fang Yuan quickly stopped out of fear.

Fang Yuan reduced the essence per wave and slowed its impact against the walls.

After four hours, this 6% snow silver primeval essence was finally expended.

After one round of cultivation, his rough stone aperture walls began to glimmer like unpolished jade.

"Quick, transfer another 10%." Fang Yuan urged.

As dawn broke and roosters crowed, light filtered through the window, and Fang Yuan slowly opened his eyes.

He looked at his palm and clenched his fists: "Rank one peak stage!"

Even Bai Ning Bing was stunned.

Under the blazing sun, in the fields.

"Auntie, take a rest, I'll help you!" Fang Yuan grabbed the hoe from the old lady's hand.

Unable to match the young man's strength, the old lady beamed happily as her hoe was taken: "Ah, where else could I find such a wonderful lad?"

Since the first dual cultivation, two days had passed.

Relishing the rapid progress from the bone flesh unity Gu, Fang Yuan decided to stay until reaching rank two.

Bai Ning Bing agreed, as becoming a rank two Gu Master would greatly aid their journey.

Having reached rank one peak stage the first night, he spent the following days solidifying his foundation until his aperture walls became crystal clear. At this rate, he would reach rank two in days.

As night approached, they began eating outside the old lady's house.

"You must be hungry, there are still sweet potatoes in the pot, it will definitely fill you up!" The old lady grinned as she scooped two bowls of rice.

"Eat slowly, I have good news for you two," the old lady smiled mysteriously at the table.

# Chapter 232: Intense pain of changing bone

"Auntie, what good news?" Fang Yuan asked with a foolish grin.

Just then, an old man walked inside.

"Young man, this is our village head," the old lady introduced.

Gu Masters could normally sense each other's aura, but with the breath concealment Gu in their ears, the village head detected nothing.

"Sit and chat, there is good news!" The old lady gestured for them to sit. "I told your story to the villagers; our village head is a Gu Master and can help you."

The village head suspected nothing: "I heard you lost your goods to a wild beast. Since you've helped your auntie, I have purple maple leaves. A caravan arrives in a few days; you can sell these purple maple leaves to recover your capital."

"Th-this..." Fang Yuan stammered with joy, tears welling as he choked with emotion. "Honored sir, you are truly a good, great person!"

The old village head patted Fang Yuan's shoulder sympathetically: "Everyone has ups and downs, but I cannot simply give you these leaves without villagers talking. Help plow the eastern fields for seven days until the caravan arrives, and they are yours."

"Are you really going to plow fields for seven days for a cart of those purple leaves?" Bai Ning Bing asked in confusion that night.

"The purple maple leaves aren't my real goal. Didn't you hear? A caravan will pass through here," Fang Yuan replied.

"So what? Didn't you say caravans pass Zi You mountain year-round?"

"Caravans pass every six months or year. I didn't expect one so soon. I inquired discreetly and learned this caravan is traveling east to west. Even if it bypasses Shang Clan City, it will head toward our destination."

Bai Ning Bing understood: "You want to join the caravan and borrow their strength to reach Shang Clan City?"

Fang Yuan chuckled: "We can't just casually join; even mortals need a guarantor. But the village head can resolve that for us."

"Alright. I'll reach rank two initial stage after seven days. It's also time to use the iron bone Gu and jade bone Gu."

Fang Yuan sighed: "It is agonizingly painful. I need to rest my mind before using the iron bone Gu tomorrow night."

The next day, Fang and Bai went to work the eastern fields as agreed.

That night, he sat cross-legged on the bed and channeled primeval essence into the iron bone Gu in his aperture.

A rank one Gu Master could not normally use it, but with the snow silver primeval essence Bai Ning Bing transferred, he was barely able to activate it.

Nearly all the snow silver primeval essence was consumed. The iron bone Gu shone with dark radiance, melted into liquid iron, and surged from his aperture into his skeleton.

A scalding brand seemed to sear his heart, every bone touched by liquid iron burning like coals.

The pain pierced his very soul, distorting Fang Yuan's face.

Sweat dripped from his forehead before drenching his entire body.

Once his skeleton was fully coated in molten iron, he finally relaxed; dizziness instantly struck, blacking out his vision.

Forcing himself to endure, he took several deep breaths and slowly lay down.

"Is it over?" Bai Ning Bing asked from the dark.

"Naturally." Inhaling sharply, Fang Yuan's voice was hoarse but steady. "Go to sleep, we have work tomorrow."

The next day, Fang Yuan remained in bed instead of working.

Consequently, she went to the fields to work alone.

By evening, Fang Yuan could move slightly; unconvinced, Bai Ning Bing used the Jade Bone Gu.

Despite her pride, she groaned in agony, clutching the bedsheets.

Though trembling violently, she persisted to the end; once finished, overwhelming dizziness struck, and she collapsed onto the bed unconscious.

# Chapter 233: A deep misunderstanding

The next day, it was Bai Ning Bing's turn to lie bedridden, in too much pain to move.

Having mostly recovered, Fang Yuan headed off to plow the eastern fields.

The old lady went into the room, asking in concern.

Bai Ning Bing insisted she was fine, merely exhausted from a day of farm work and needing rest.

The old lady smiled knowingly: "Indeed you were *tired*; you two were quite noisy these past two days, I heard everything."

"What?" Bai Ning Bing could not understand it.

"Young lady, stop keeping it from me, I could tell a mile away!" The old lady laughed.

Bai Ning Bing's pupils shrank—was her identity exposed? Killing intent surged, but she could not bring herself to strike.

The old lady patted her hand: "Young lady, no man has hips like yours. No wonder you wear that straw hat. I may be old, but women are far more observant than men."

The old lady lowered her voice: "You two are a couple, right? You've been so loud, you really should restrain yourselves."

Bai Ning Bing froze as though struck by lightning.

"Auntie, it's not what you think," she finally forced out, her expression extremely stiff.

But honestly, the bedsheets were torn by her...

When Fang Yuan returned for lunch, the old lady stopped him: "Lad, youngsters have vigor, but show more care for your wife, understood?"

"Huh?" Fang Yuan's jaw dropped, unable to process what he had just heard.

"Oh... hehe..." He scratched his head sheepishly. "Auntie, you're right, I understand."

At the dinner table, he saw Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing glared at him coldly, practically radiating frost.

Though awkward, Fang Yuan had to admit this misunderstanding helped conceal their identities.

That night, she asked Fang Yuan again when he would return the Yang Gu.

Fang Yuan replied that he would return it once he reached rank three.

Bai Ning Bing snorted; knowing him, she would rather die than trust his words, but she could not take the Yang Gu by force yet.

Determined to obtain a Poison Vow Gu or Seize Gu, Bai Ning Bing grew eager to reach Shang Clan City.

Beyond dual cultivation, a new component was added to Fang Yuan's training.

Namely, using the Crocodile Strength Gu to boost his strength.

Having forged bones tough as iron, his improved foundation now allowed him to add the strength of a crocodile atop his two boars.

His strength continued to rise.

Seven days passed in a flash.

As agreed, Fang Yuan received a cart of purple maple leaves from the village head.

The entire cart wouldn't fetch two primeval stones, but Fang Yuan only needed an excuse to approach the caravan.

The caravan arrived three days later than the old village head expected.

The caravan's size was huge.

Villagers set up temporary stalls at the entrance, while Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing mingled among them with their cart of purple maple leaves.

He was secretly observing the members of the approaching caravan.

This caravan was a mixed coalition of various clans with multiple vice leaders, making it easier for him to blend in.

"Hey, how much for this cart of purple maple leaves?" someone soon came to ask.

"Two and a half primeval stones." Fang Yuan said.

"Two and a half? You might as well rob someone!" The person stared wide-eyed.

"Buy it or leave!" Bai Ning Bing said at the side.

"Hmph!" The person waved his sleeves and walked off.

If they actually sold the cart, they would lack an excuse to join the caravan. Thus, they deliberately drove away three potential buyers.

Before nightfall, Fang Yuan feigned dejection and hauled the cart of leaves back to the old village head's house.

The old village head asked and sighed: "You two, two primeval stones was already high; even one and a half would sell. Why didn't you listen, insisting on two and a half!"

# Chapter 234: Arrest Warrant

Fang Yuan nodded: "We only wanted to recoup costs and care for our parents, but no one bought."

Fang Yuan added: "No matter, Lord Village Head, I've decided to follow the caravan tomorrow. By lowering the price, we'll surely sell it eventually."

"Follow the caravan? Who allowed you to do that?" The old village head stared wide-eyed.

Fang Yuan said matter-of-factly: "I saw many mortals in the caravan. If they can follow, why can't we?"

The old village head sighed: "Those are the Gu Masters' family servants! What if people with ill intentions snuck in?"

"Ah?!" Fang Yuan stood stunned, mouth agape: "Then what do we do? The caravan leaves tomorrow."

The old man sighed deeply: "Never mind, I'll help you through. Tomorrow I'll beg them to let you join; the rest is up to fate."

After a night's rest, the caravan was already loading goods.

"Lord Chen." The old village head bowed respectfully to one of the caravan's vice leaders.

"Oh, Old Zhang, I'm busy here, so make it quick," Chen Gu Master said.

Chen Gu Master turned back: "What did you say? You want me to vouch for two juniors to join the caravan?"

As Chen Gu Master deliberated, a messenger Gu Master came running over.

Holding a stack of papers, he shouted as he ran: "Attention everyone, there is a new arrest warrant!"

"New arrest warrant? From which clan? How much bounty, retrieve it for me." Chen Gu Master was interested.

"Yes, Vice Leader." The messenger quickly handed over a flyer.

Chen Gu Master glanced at it: "Oh, an arrest warrant from Bai clan. A thousand primeval stones just for accurate information? So much!"

"A thousand primeval stones for info, and five to eight thousand for their heads. Bai clan must despise these two demonic rascals to the bone."

Little did he know, those two demonic rascals were right beside him.

"Fine, seeing your hard work over the years, Old Zhang, I'll grant your request," Chen Gu Master said.

"Thank you, Lord! I'll call them over right away," the old village head rejoiced.

Chen Gu Master waved a hand: "No need, I'm busy. Just have them report to Chen Xin."

Chen Xin met the two without suspecting the warrant either.

Fang Yuan was heavily disfigured, while Bai Ning Bing had grown natural in her disguise after days of practice.

Sensing no Gu Master aura from his careless glance, Chen Xin summoned an old attendant to assign them work.

"What are your names?" The old attendant asked.

"My name is Hei Tu, my wife is called Bai Yun." Fang Yuan casually said.

"Female?" The old attendant frowned.

"Women bring trouble; be careful. If anything happens, don't blame me for not warning you!" the old attendant said.

"Understood. This cart is filled with purple maple leaves; my wife will stay by the goods to avoid trouble with others," Fang Yuan replied.

"Hmph, good that you understand."

Assigned to move cargo, the physical labor was effortless, though Bai Ning Bing found feigning breathlessness mentally exhausting.

"Brother Qiang, two newcomers brought their own cargo—an entire cart of purple maple leaves!" a skinny servant reported excitedly.

Squatting on the ground, Brother Qiang squinted: "I see them. Skinny Monkey, go test them."

Under everyone's watchful eyes, Skinny Monkey reluctantly approached Fang Yuan.

"Hey brother, they call me Brother Monkey. Since we're working together, let's look out for each other," Skinny Monkey said.

Fang Yuan gave him a glance, only saying a word: "Scram."

Ignoring him, Fang Yuan continued hauling goods, well acquainted with such customary extortion from his past life.

"Still not scramming? Want me to throw you out?" Fang Yuan gave Skinny Monkey another frigid glare.

Skinny Monkey snorted and backed away without causing a scene.

Such defiance made Brother Qiang suspicious. Did these two have backing to be so arrogant? He decided to investigate their background first.

# Chapter 235: Shang Xin Ci

After traveling all day, the caravan camped in a valley for the night.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing were also among the crowd.

They set up their handcart between a wild herb stall on the left and a milk stall on the right.

Bai Ning Bing looked around with interest: "To think the caravan has a small bazaar like this."

"Demand brings trade, which naturally gives rise to marketplaces," Fang Yuan answered.

She looked at Fang Yuan: "Are you planning on selling these purple maple leaves?"

Fang Yuan nodded slightly: "Now that we're in the caravan, we should dispose of them. Keeping them only invites unwanted greed."

"The caravan's bazaar has two tiers: daily trade among mortals, and a weekly market held exclusively for Gu Masters," Fang Yuan explained.

Bai Ning Bing's eyes brightened: "Joining the Gu Master bazaar would help. We need an investigative Gu for safety."

"I've already planned for it, but it's still too early," Fang Yuan smiled confidently, thinking of an item in his tusita flower.

As they spoke softly, a servant came staggering toward them.

Dressed in bloody rags like a beggar, he approached the neighboring milk stall and gulped: "Brother, could you spare some milk to drink?"

"Go away. Don't hinder my business!" The milk seller impatiently waved his hands.

The servant shuffled helplessly over to Fang and Bai's cart: "Two brothers..."

Before he could finish, Fang Yuan stepped forward and kicked him down, snarling: "Screw off."

The servant collapsed into the black mud, grimacing as the kick aggravated his existing wounds.

Struggling to his feet, he glared with hatred: "Fine, I'll remember this. We're all mortals—everyone has bad days. Hmph..."

Fang Yuan's expression turned cold as he raised his feet again.

*Bam.*

This servant fell to the ground again.

"Say another word, I dare you," Fang Yuan glared down at him fiercely.

Head lowered, the servant climbed up silently without daring to look back, abandoning his begging and walking away.

Watching him leave, Bai Ning Bing asked, puzzled: "Strange, why is there a beggar in the caravan?"

"Normal enough. A Gu Master beat him and denied him food," Fang Yuan shrugged, his cold gaze shifting toward a corner.

"I'll sell these leaves to the next bidder." Just as Fang Yuan considered his next move, the bazaar erupted in an uproar.

Some people excitedly cheered and shouted.

"The kind-hearted lady of Zhang clan has arrived!"

"What's going on?" Bai Ning Bing looked over and saw a green figure enter the bazaar.

Fang Yuan was also bewildered: *What's up with this*?

Most were servants punished by Gu Masters and left without food. Even the man Fang Yuan had kicked was among them, craning his neck eagerly.

"Don't rush, everyone, there's plenty for all," the green-clothed girl said gently.

Her soft, gentle voice was instantly drowned out by the shouting crowd.

"Shut your mouths! Line up one by one. Anyone rushing or shouting will be slashed down!" a thunderous voice roared across the bazaar.

A stern, elderly Gu Master appeared; under his ferocious glare, the noisy crowd fell instantly silent.

Shoving and jostling, the crowd quickly formed a neat, orderly queue.

At the front of the line, the green-clothed girl distributed steamed buns from a basket.

Curious, Bai Ning Bing asked the neighboring stall owner: "Who is she?"

"What, you don't know Lady Zhang Xin Ci? You must be newcomers."

"Zhang Xin Ci?" Fang Yuan frowned deeply. "Tell me everything you know!"

The stall owner answered hurriedly: "Lady Zhang is one of our caravan's vice leaders. She has no cultivation aptitude and is a mortal like us. Yet she has strong clan backing, and that Gu Master is her bodyguard. Lady Zhang brings food almost every evening for starving servants. She comes even in foul weather... Heavens are truly unfair, denying such a person the ability to cultivate."

Bai Ning Bing smiled and nodded toward Fang Yuan: "Truly, all sorts exist in this world."

He was staring fixedly at the green-clothed girl, his brows knit tightly.

Baffled, a familiar figure came to mind: "Isn't this girl Shang Xin Ci?"

Shang Xin Ci was one of Shang clan's young masters.

As a Shang clan young master, Shang Xin Ci had immense fortune.

While Shang clan members were notoriously crafty, Shang Xin Ci was soft-hearted, gentle, and the worst merchant in the clan.

She sympathized with mortals and offered aid, even buying out slave auctions multiple times despite severe rebukes from the clan leader.

Yet through the twists of fate, she ultimately became the Shang Clan Leader!

# Chapter 236: Kindness will be rewarded?

"Why is she here, and why is she called Zhang Xin Ci? In my memories she was a Gu Master—why say she lacks aptitude and is mortal?"

"Is she merely a lookalike? But they look identical! No, wait..."

"Shang Xin Ci was an illegitimate child conceived during the Shang clan leader's travels, bullied from youth after her mother passed. Forced to travel with the caravan as a merchant, she reached Shang Clan City where the leader recognized her as his daughter. Feeling deeply indebted to her, he tolerated and covered up many of her mistakes."

"So this caravan is her first journey as a merchant!"

Realizing this, Fang Yuan's heart beat faster.

Shang Xin Ci was a guaranteed investment—the future Shang clan leader, though no one would believe it now.

Her value began not when she became leader, but the moment the clan leader acknowledged her as a young master.

As a young master, she would receive assets to manage, following Shang clan's tradition of grooming successors.

"This is a gift from heaven!" Fang Yuan sighed as his gaze toward the distant Shang Xin Ci burned like fire.

Tested by a century of upheavals, her kindness, gentleness, and integrity had been renowned throughout the Southern Border in his past life.

Crucially, she had no political foundation.

Unlike other young masters, she would stand alone in Shang Clan City, targeted by rivals scheming to suppress her.

Fang Yuan needed her trade channel, just as she would need his outside help to survive and thrive.

Best of all, she was easy to influence—her youth and kind nature were weaknesses Fang Yuan could readily exploit.

Bai Ning Bing chuckled from the side: "You took a fancy to her, didn't you?"

"What?" Fang Yuan's thoughts paused.

"Don't pretend. As a fellow man, I can tell. She's pretty, but you won't get her unless I kidnap her. In exchange, hand over the Yang Gu first," Bai Ning Bing offered enticingly.

However, Fang Yuan's retort made her seethe: "*You're a man?*"

"You!"

Things were tricky. Fang Yuan had to approach Shang Xin Ci and win her trust before she reached Shang Clan City, leaving little time.

The sun sank behind western peaks as night fell.

The long line dwindled until all the servants received food and dispersed.

"Alright, that's all for today. I'll return tomorr—" Before she finished, a figure leaped in front of her.

What kind of face was this!

Singed eyebrows, patchy hair, and missing an ear—who else could it be but Fang Yuan?

Shang Xin Ci started, while the maids behind her shrieked in fright.

"What are you doing?!" the burly old Gu Master barked immediately.

"Lady Zhang, please buy my goods!" Fang Yuan yelled toward Shang Xin Ci, ignoring the old Gu Master.

Bai Ning Bing stood at a distance, enjoying his performance.

"I have one bun left, please take it," Shang Xin Ci smiled gently, feeling only sympathy for his gruesome injuries.

Fang Yuan threw the bun to the ground: "I don't need this, I sold my house to buy this cart of purple maple leaves! The leaves will soon wither! What meaning will my life have then? Sob sob... If I can't sell it, I'll smash my head and die!"

"You lunatic, you dare frighten our young lady? Screw off this instant!" The old Gu Master barked, stepping forward to shield Shang Xin Ci.

"Lady Zhang, I don't want to live! You are a good person, please buy these purple maple leaves and save me!" Fang Yuan wailed.

Shang Xin Ci's heart softened: "Don't cry. I will buy them. Life is precious. Uncle Zhang Zhu, give him three primeval stones."

"Miss..." The old Gu Master's brows furrowed.

"Thank you, lady! Lady Zhang, you are Hei Tu's savior!" Ecstatic, Fang Yuan repeatedly bowed with tears streaming down his face.

The old Gu Master glanced at the cart behind Fang Yuan, eyelids twitching: "This cart of purple maple leaves is worth two primeval stones at most. Three is too much!"

"Uncle Zhang Zhu..." Shang Xin Ci said with a soft voice.

The old Gu Master sighed: "Miss, three primeval stones is too much for a servant. It will only attract greedy eyes against him. We should only give him two primeval stones, or every peddler will swarm over tomorrow."

"Uncle Zhang Zhu is right, give him two primeval stones then." After pondering briefly, Shang Xin Ci agreed.

Accepting the two primeval stones with trembling hands, Fang Yuan bowed: "Miss Zhang, you are a great person, you'll be rewarded!"

# Chapter 237: Thrashing

Pocketing the two primeval stones, Fang Yuan abandoned the handcart and left the bazaar with Bai Ning Bing.

"The way I see it, we should just capture her directly. Don't forget our current identities if you want to approach her," Bai Ning Bing murmured.

Their Chen Clan laborer cover had become an obstacle to approaching Shang Xin Ci.

Having already planned for this, Fang Yuan smiled at Bai Ning Bing: "You're right. So I'll go thrash people now."

Bai Ning Bing was stumped: "Thrash?"

Inside a wide tent, several servants sat circled around a pot on a coal stove.

"Oh? Speak." Brother Qiang, a muscular servant, raised his eyebrows.

Skinny Monkey added, "A cart of purple maple leaves actually sold for two primeval stones in the bazaar!"

Brother Qiang's eyes immediately brightened.

Skinny Monkey sneered: "That young lady from Zhang Clan—a mortal, living such a good life."

"When I smuggled goods, I sold dirt cheap. Why don't I have that luck?" Brother Qiang fumed.

He said: "We'll discipline them tomorrow. If newcomers don't know the rules, we'll teach them."

Suddenly, the entrance curtain of the tent was lifted open.

Fang Yuan walked inside.

Skinny Monkey leaped up with a sinister grin: "Newbie, scared now? It's too late to apolo—!"

Before he could finish, Fang Yuan calmly raised his foot.

*Bam.*

Skinny Monkey was kicked flying into the coal stove, hot meat stew splashing over him as he screamed.

Furious, the servants sprang up with enraged roars and charged at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan hurled the two primeval stones in his hand straight at them.

"Wait a second, this is... primeval stones?!"

Every one of them pounced for the primeval stones, completely forgetting about Fang Yuan.

"Shut up! Who tripped me just now? Stand up!" Brother Qiang raged, his face covered in dirt.

Suddenly, a hand appeared and pulled him.

Startled and furious, he turned around, only to meet Fang Yuan's ugly face.

He shouted: "You dare grab me? Let go of your dog paw!"

*Bam!*

Fang Yuan waved his fist and punched his face.

Brother Qiang's nose broke, blood gushing out as dizzying stars spun.

Fang Yuan released his grip, and Brother Qiang crumpled limp to the ground.

Snapping out of their daze, the servants brandished their fists and lunged at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan swung a sweeping fist, shattering the nearest servant's teeth and sending him flying.

With a few strikes, Fang Yuan laid out every servant except Skinny Monkey, who was cowering in the corner.

Fang Yuan had already left, leaving the servants collapsed inside the tent, unconscious or groaning in pain.

Fang Yuan walked in between the tents.

The commotion would surely draw attention—exactly what Fang Yuan wanted.

Fang Yuan didn't reply, walking towards the depths of this temporary camp.

He soon reached his destination—a horse carriage where, according to his inquiries, Chen Clan's old steward slept.

Slipping into a deserted dark corner, Fang Yuan drew his dagger and slashed himself.

*Thud thud thud....*

The carriage door was knocked continuously.

With the knocking growing louder, the old steward opened bloodshot eyes: "Who is it?"

"It is me, Old Steward," a voice answered from outside.

Frowning, the old steward recognized the voice as the newcomer from that morning.

"What the hell are you doing disturbing someone's sleep in the middle of the night!?"

# Chapter 238: Lady, save me!

Furious, the old steward decided to teach Fang Yuan an unforgettable lesson.

Bang, he opened the door.

Yet before him stood Fang Yuan, battered and covered in blood.

Startled, the old steward asked, "What happened to you? A beast pack raid?"

"No, Old Steward! Those thieves beat me and stole my primeval stones! You must seek justice for me!" Fang Yuan cried, wiping away tears.

The old steward stormed into the servants' tent and hauled them outside. The servants emerged, finding Fang Yuan standing nearby, battered and stained with blood.

"Old Steward, they are the ones who stole my primeval stones!" Fang Yuan pointed at them with righteous indignation.

"Brat, you dare slander us!"

Slap!

The old steward glared: "You lot are out of control, daring to snatch a workmate's primeval stones!"

The old steward roared, "You took his stones and claim you're falsely accused? Are these two stones his? Speak!"

"Yes, yes."

The old steward rebuked them: "Never mind snatching stones, you dared use knives! Who used the knife—"

"Using knife?"

"We didn't!"

"Bullshit! Where did his wounds come from if you didn't use knives? Did he cut himself?"

"Get back to the tent. I'll settle accounts with you tomorrow." The old steward huffed, his anger spent.

Daring not disobey, the servants retreated, glaring angrily at Fang Yuan.

Returning the primeval stones to Fang Yuan, the old steward snapped, "You have your primeval stones back, don't lose them again. I won't give a damn next time!"

Weeping with gratitude, Fang Yuan pleaded: "Old Steward, please safeguard these two stones for me."

The old steward was thrilled. If wild beasts devoured Fang Yuan on the road, the stones would be his!

"Alright, I will take it upon myself to safeguard them for you." The old steward said.

"Thank you, Old Steward, you truly are a good person!" Fang Yuan bowed repeatedly in profuse gratitude.

"Alright, go rest." The old steward waved his hand and walked away.

After everyone left, Fang Yuan's expression turned back to normal.
