"The wounds on your body are still bleeding, aren't you going to treat them?" Bai Ning Bing came over.

"I still have use for them, let's go." Fang Yuan smiled.

… Inside a tent, oil lamps were lit.

Holding a book, Shang Xin Ci slightly furrowed her brows: "Xiao Die, did you hear that? Is someone crying for help?"

Shang Xin Ci's bodyguard Zhang Zhu walked out and saw Fang Yuan: "Who is crying so late at night? Why is it you again!"

Having no good impression of Fang Yuan, Zhang Zhu shouted: "Get lost, or I'll kill you right this moment!"

"Uncle Zhang Zhu..." Shang Xin Ci and her servant girl emerged.

Fang Yuan ignored them and looked only at Shang Xin Ci: "Lady Zhang, my life has been harmed by you and only you can save me!"

"Harmed by me? What do you mean? Uncle Zhang Zhu, please heal him first," Shang Xin Ci said.

Zhang Zhu sent a white ball of light into Fang Yuan, stopping the bleeding and closing his wounds.

Fang Yuan said: "It's because of those two primeval stones. The others grew envious and robbed me. I couldn't fight back, so I asked the old steward for justice. They returned the stones, but threatened they'd make my life worse than death!"

Fang Yuan continued: "After discussing it with my companion, only Lady Zhang can save us. Otherwise, we will be killed by them, two lives lost!"

"It won't go as far as killing…" Shang Xin Ci comforted Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan cut in: "Lady, they'll scheme during beast attacks to have me eaten. I beg you to shelter us and save our lives!"

Shang Xin Ci hesitated, but couldn't bring herself to reject him: "Alright, I will temporarily take you in."

# Chapter 239: Fei Hou mountain

"Miss, you can't." Zhang Zhu immediately said.

"Miss, our group holds the least power here. Offending Chen Clan over two insignificant servants isn't worth it," Zhang Zhu persuaded.

"This..." Shang Xin Ci was placed in a dilemma and couldn't decide.

"What difficulty is there? Lady Zhang, I am only a hired hand of Chen family, not their bound servant. If you feel troubled, I have a solution. Tell Chen Clan we offended you and you are detaining us for punishment. They won't fight you over two mortals."

"That is a good idea!" Shang Xin Ci's gaze brightened.

"Miss...." Zhang Zhu gave a helpless sigh, knowing he couldn't persuade her anymore.

Scrambling up with a stick, Fang Yuan bowed to Shang Xin Ci: "Miss Zhang, you are a good person. I will definitely repay you in the future!"

Shang Xin Ci smiled: "I only help where I can. Xiao Die, arrange a tent for them tonight."

The two laid down in the dark tent.

Fang Yuan clasped hands with Bai Ning Bing: "Everything will be fine, Bai Yun."

Rolling her eyes, Bai Ning Bing quietly transferred snow silver primeval essence into his palm.

"Sleep early. Luckily we met such a good person like Lady Zhang." Closing his eyes, Fang Yuan began cultivating secretly.

With red steel primeval essence filling his sea, Bai Ning Bing's snow silver primeval essence accelerated his cultivation greatly.

The snow silver essence refined his aperture, accelerating his cultivation, while the breath concealing Gu prevented his aura from leaking.

Once the snow silver primeval essence was spent, Fang Yuan immediately activated the crocodile strength Gu.

New strength permanently fused into his body, his iron skeleton supporting his growing might.

The night passed quietly.

Feigning difficulty, he said, "Brother Zhang, frankly, taking them might leave our Chen Clan short on manpower. We can't make Gu Masters haul cargo, can we? Let me call my steward over. We will temporarily keep them here and hand them over to your Zhang Clan once we get new workers in the next village."

After negotiating with the Chen Clan, Zhang Zhu had the pair transferred to Shang Xin Ci's camp. In the coming dozen or so days, Fang and Bai worked by day and cultivated by night.

The caravan left Zi You Mountain far behind and entered the Fei Hou Mountain region.

He gained half a crocodile's strength, though lacking milk spring caused many bone spear Gu to starve to death.

Purchasing new Gu worms in Shang Clan City would require immense wealth, but using the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus risked exposure.

Hence, he needed to get Shang Xin Ci in his grasp even more.

Fei Hou Mountain was covered in thick fog, navigated by the caravan's investigative Gu Masters.

Suddenly, a clamor broke out ahead and the caravan ground to a halt.

"Monkey groups are blocking our path!"

Fei Hou Mountain was ruled by innumerable monkeys that routinely blocked and robbed passing caravans. Anyone with experience found nothing surprising about this.

"These damn monkeys appeared again to block our path. You all know the rules. Anyone who breaks them and implicates us will face my Jia clan's wrath!" the caravan leader shouted coldly.

# Chapter 240: Arm Wrestling

Elephant-sized bandit monkeys possessed immense strength, their arms twice as thick as legs and tails capable of pulverizing rock.

This bandit monkey group's king suddenly opened its large mouth and howled loudly.

Its howling soundwaves engulfed the surroundings, scattering the wind, clouds, and dense white fog.

As visibility cleared, over a thousand bandit monkeys were seen surrounding the passageway.

Ahead, the massive monkey king sat on a stone bench beside a tank-sized wine jar emitting rich fragrance.

Caravan leader Jia Long narrowed his eyes at the monkey king and ordered, "Jia Yong, go."

"Yes, chief." Jia Yong stood up.

Though tall and fat with a bulging belly, he was remarkably robust.

Having used a tortoise strength Gu, Jia Yong permanently possessed the strength of a tortoise.

With a solemn expression, Jia Yong rolled up his sleeves and stood before the monkey king.

The enormous monkey king was a head taller than Jia Yong even while seated.

The monkey king howled, and several bandit monkeys carried over a stone table.

Jia Yong swallowed hard and sat down, resting his right elbow on the table with forearm raised.

The monkey king extended its left hand, and their palms gripped tightly.

Beside the table, an elderly female bandit monkey suddenly shouted.

At the signal, Jia Yong and the monkey king exerted force, beginning this unusual contest of strength.

Bandit monkeys respected strength, using arm wrestling for recreation and to resolve disputes.

If the caravan won, they passed toll-free; if they lost, the monkeys took goods.

Sitting opposite the monkey king, Jia Yong flushed red and grimaced, exerting all his strength.

Yet he could not resist; the monkey king slammed his arm flat against the table.

The monkey king stood up, excitedly pounding his chest.

Jia Long showed no expression, and just raised his hand.

As Jia clan's troops moved forward, monkeys swarmed to wantonly take goods from the wagons.

"Where is Jia Ping?" Jia Long shouted with a heavy voice.

Jia Ping stepped out slowly. In stark contrast to Jia Yong, he was stick-thin and appeared extremely frail.

Though frail-looking, Jia Ping possessed the strength of two bears from tendon coiling Gu.

Jia Ping sat down and stretched his arm.

Though his arm was thin, Jia Ping quickly defeated the monkey king.

Jia Long chuckled and waved for the troop to proceed.

"Everyone, I shall go ahead." Jia Long cupped his hands to the others as Jia clan's wagons passed the checkpoint.

Gu Masters with ox, tiger, elephant, python, or horse strength stepped up to compete, with varying wins and losses.

Once most had passed the checkpoint, it was finally Zhang clan's turn.

Zhang Zhu looked troubled; as a healing Gu Master, he lacked physical strength.

Arm wrestling allowed only raw strength; anyone caught cheating with Gu worms would be mobbed.

Zhang clan brought no other Gu Masters besides rank three Zhang Zhu, making them the weakest group in the caravan.

Noticing his expression, the perceptive Shang Xin Ci softly consoled him, "Uncle Zhang Zhu, do not worry too much. They are only goods; what matters is our safety."

"*Sigh*, Miss, these monkeys require a contest. We can lose, but not be belittled. I will go!"

"Wait!" Just then, Fang Yuan stepped out from the crowd.

"Lady Zhang, you are my benefactor. Let me go," he said to Shang Xin Ci, cupping his hands.

"You?" Servant girl Xiao Die rolled her eyes. "You're not a Gu Master—don't cause trouble in this crisis!"

Shang Xin Ci smiled: "Hei Tu, I appreciate the thought, but this is no joke. The monkey king is immensely strong—several Gu Masters broke their arms!"

"Lady Zhang, I've had extraordinary strength since childhood. I must go!" Fang Yuan insisted.

"Hei Tu!" Shang Xin Ci wanted to stop him, but was stopped by Zhang Zhu.

"Miss, he is no fool and has some confidence. Sometimes we must trust others," Zhang Zhu urged.

"Eh, look, Zhang Clan actually sent out a servant!"

Fang Yuan's figure soon attracted the attention of the others.

# Chapter 241: Huang Jin mountain

Fang Yuan walked slowly to the stone table and sat down.

The monkey king gazed at him, then stretched its arm.

Under countless gazes, their palms clasped together and began the contest.

The monkey king strained, but Fang Yuan's arm remained like steel, refusing to budge.

The monkey king's pupils shrank in shock; this was the strongest human it had ever encountered!

Fang Yuan laughed inwardly: *I could defeat this monkey king with just two boars of strength, let alone now with half a crocodile's strength added.*

The monkey king had already fought many rounds and lacked stamina, ensuring Fang Yuan an absolute victory.

"Just as a human punch is weaker than a kick, one cannot normally utilize all their strength. Though I have the strength of two boars, half a crocodile, and myself, my arm wrestling cannot output that full sum. Of course, concentrating all one's strength into a single movement was possible with that legendary Gu worm..."

Not wanting to make it obvious, he feigned a strained expression and let his arm tremble during the deadlock.

Slowly, his arm pushed down on monkey king's.

When the contest ended, almost everyone was stumped.

"He actually won!"

Worry faded from Shang Xin Ci's face, replaced by a smile.

"Let's go." Zhang Zhu waved the troops forward with a complex look in his eyes.

Fang Yuan won two rounds straight, then intentionally lost the third to maintain his disguise and let the monkeys take some goods.

Even so, Fang Yuan's performance made everyone view him in a new light.

"Lady Zhang, I have finished my mission." He cupped his hands to Shang Xin Ci.

Shang Xin Ci regarded him gently: "My mother taught me never to judge by appearances, Hei Tu. Please accept these one hundred and fifty primeval stones in thanks."

"One hundred and fifty primeval stones?" Xiao Die gasped. "Miss, why give him so much!?"

Stepping back, Fang Yuan righteously declined: "Lady, I acted to repay your kindness, not for primeval stones. Please take them back; I cannot accept this."

Shang Xin Ci insisted, "This is not a reward, but a token of my gratitude."

With a solemn, righteous expression, Fang Yuan said, "Not for one hundred and fifty, nor even one thousand primeval stones. Lady Zhang, I may be a mortal, but please do not humiliate me!"

"This..." Helpless against his words, Shang Xin Ci could only retract the stones.

Zhang Zhu remained silent, his gaze turning more complicated.

"A lifesaving debt is hard to repay. Allow me to offer my strength," Fang Yuan cupped his hands.

Numerous monkey groups occupied bandit monkey mountain, frequently establishing checkpoints along the trade route.

Fang Yuan repeatedly competed, deliberately staging a mix of wins and losses.

The caravan made halting progress, taking over twenty days to traverse the tall bandit monkey mountain.

Fang Yuan became famous, and many clans sent servants to recruit him.

Though they offered attractive terms, Fang Yuan rejected every recruitment attempt and stayed with Zhang clan.

Fang Yuan sensed that Zhang Zhu was already suspecting him.

Privately, Bai Ning Bing warned him: "Refusing that money was a mistake. We should pause cultivation to avoid Zhang Zhu's investigation."

Fang Yuan rejected the suggestion and continued cultivating every night.

Bai Ning Bing still cooperated, indifferent to exposure and actually eager to see Fang Yuan fail.

Snow silver primeval essence fueled Fang Yuan's rapid cultivation progress.

The night they left Fei Hou mountain, Fang Yuan broke through from rank two initial stage to middle stage.

By the time the caravan reached Huang Jin mountain, Fang Yuan finished using the crocodile strength Gu, permanently gaining the strength of a crocodile.

Both clans welcomed the caravan, but gave an ultimatum: trading with one barred visiting the other.

After discussing their needs, the caravan coalition split in two, each group visiting a different clan.

After this was settled, Zhang Zhu approached Shang Xin Ci privately: "After days of investigation, Hei Tu and Bai Yun are deeply suspicious. I suggest we expel them!"

# Chapter 242: Common understanding between smart people

"Suspicious?" Shang Xin Ci's gaze flickered under her thick eyelashes.

Zhang Zhu nodded solemnly: "I have doubted them since Fei Hou mountain. Miss gave them a hundred and fifty primeval stones, yet they remained completely unmoved by such a fortune. That is highly suspicious."

"Remember when he begged for help that night? How could someone with Hei Tu's immense strength be beaten into that state? Furthermore, I've observed that his cross-dressing companion is actually a woman!"

Shang Xin Ci smiled: "*Two fists cannot rival four hands*; his injuries were understandable. In truth, I noticed every suspicious point you mentioned."

Zhang Zhu smiled wryly: "Nothing escapes you, Miss. But why keep them around?"

"Because I felt no malice from them," Shang Xin Ci said. "Why take such a risk at Fei Hou mountain if not to help me?"

"If they harbored ill intent, they would have stayed hidden as bystanders or accepted the hundred and fifty primeval stones, yet they did neither. When Hei Tu spoke of repaying my kindness, his sincerity was unmistakable. He truly wished to repay the favor," Shang Xin Ci said.

"Even so, what is their motive? Perhaps they are plotting something... wait, they might be bandit scouts infiltrating the caravan to coordinate a robbery with demonic Gu Masters!"

"That makes no sense," Shang Xin Ci shook her head. "If they were accomplices, they would have stayed low rather than exposing themselves at Fei Hou mountain. Since they want to hide their identities, we should help them..."

"Uncle Zhang Zhu," Shang Xin Ci added, "if we are raided, do not fight to protect the goods. Losing cargo is not a fatal issue. My mother's dying wish was for me to deliver a token to someone in Shang clan city, though she said we could live off these goods if we were rejected."

Moving through the crowd, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing bought cow and goat milk from several Jin clansmen.

"Aren't you afraid buying so recklessly will expose us?" Bai Ning Bing questioned.

"Any disguise is eventually exposed, but you have a glaring flaw," Fang Yuan said, glancing at her.

Fang Yuan continued, "Revealing select flaws puts others at ease, making them believe they've seen through us and remain in control."

Bai Ning Bing understood: "So you're waiting for them to catch on before reacting?"

Yet three days passed without the response or probing Fang Yuan expected.

"Clever and bold—she likely held Zhang Zhu back. Being too smart can also be troublesome," Fang Yuan sighed.

"In that case, I'll take the initiative." Fang Yuan went to find Shang Xin Ci.

"You want a partnership with me?" Inside the tent, Shang Xin Ci and Zhang Zhu looked startled by Fang Yuan's proposal.

"Lady Zhang, to be frank, we need primeval stones, and I have some merchant experience. I wish to borrow a batch of goods and split the profits evenly—what do you think?" Fang Yuan bowed slightly, poised and respectful.

"You lack a single stone, yet want to borrow the hen that lays golden eggs? How arrogant!" Zhang Zhu said coldly. "Why would you guarantee a profit, and why should our Zhang clan lend you goods?"

"Business naturally has gains and losses; I cannot guarantee profits. As for your second question, I believe Lady Zhang is benevolent enough to lend them to me. My only reason is this intuition. If my intuition is mistaken, then pretend I never asked," Fang Yuan replied with a smile.

Yet Shang Xin Ci saw in him a confident, decisive composure and calculating foresight—a charisma that pierced through his ugly appearance.

"Without you, not even a quarter of our goods would remain after Fei Hou mountain. Since you have this plan, I entrust these goods to you," she said.

Fang Yuan looked briefly dazed before bowing to express his thanks.

# Chapter 243: Benefits sent towards oneself

"With so much cargo, it won't be cheap—at least fifty thousand primeval stones." The Jin clan Gu Master eyed Fang Yuan suspiciously.

After securing Shang Xin Ci's approval, Fang Yuan approached several Jin clan Gu Masters, this being the sixth.

"I lack primeval stones," Fang Yuan shook his head, "but I can trade my goods for yours."

"Hei Tu, what have you done!" Once the trade concluded, Xiao Die rushed over furiously.

Fang Yuan's expression chilled: "Your Missy lent all these goods to me, making them mine. Is there a problem with me handling my own goods?"

Fang Yuan had spent a century trading in his past life, mastering every merchant trade.

"Shang Liang mountain is still far. With proper management, I can increase these goods' value at least seven- to eightfold!"

The night before departure, however, a Jin clan Gu Master secretly approached him.

"We want to make a secret deal, are you interested?" This Gu Master had traded with Fang Yuan earlier.

"Someone wants to sell Jinzan grass?" Fang Yuan was startled, almost suspecting he had misheard.

To Jin clan, Jinzan grass was an irreplaceable war resource, essential for refining golden silkworm Gu in large numbers. Yet someone now wanted to sell it?

In Fang Yuan's memories, mass-producing the rank three golden silkworm Gu surged Jin clan's strength, letting them eliminate Huang clan and rule the area.

"Huang clan still exists, so Jin clan hasn't perfected the recipe yet, but they must have a lead."

He probed, "I'm almost done trading. Jinzan grass is rare, but few people need such an unpopular material..."

The offered price was lower than the cultivation cost of Jinzan grass; selling it was an outright loss.

Fang Yuan shook his head. "The price is too low. Frankly, your eagerness makes me suspicious."

The Jin clan Gu Master exploded, "You drove the price down, and now you say it's too low?!"

Fang Yuan shrugged. "As you said, this private deal leaves no evidence. What if the goods are fake? The caravan departs tomorrow; I'd have no recourse."

"Your concern is fair..." The Gu Master's anger subsided. "The goods are definitely real. Truth be told, our young master is selling them secretly."

Jin clan was cultivating three acres of Jinzan grass to refine the golden silkworm Gu recipe.

To avoid alerting Huang clan, only higher-ups knew, while the young master assumed the grass was planted merely for amusement.

Dangerous as this windfall was, how could he refuse when it was handed to him?

Claiming this profit would fund his Gu purchases and impress Shang Xin Ci.

What did the seller's trouble matter? Fang Yuan only considered swallowing this windfall cleanly.

# Chapter 244: Almost blackmail

"Miss, I just checked—Hei Tu traded almost all our goods, and three carts are filled with Jinzan grass!"

"Jinzan grass?" Shang Xin Ci's long brows slightly furrowed.

Watching from the side, Zhang Zhu sighed. "Miss, Xiao Die is right. We lent him goods to test him, but seeing the results, why let him squander them? We trust your ability, Miss, but cutting losses now will ease our struggles once we reach Shang Clan City."

"Excuse me, are you Lady Zhang Xin Ci?" An anxious Gu Master hurried toward her.

"I am the clan leader's guard. Our clan leader is fond of Jinzan grass and raised some for pleasure. However, the young master secretly dug it up and sold it. The clan leader has confined him and wishes to repurchase the grass. I sincerely ask that you sell it back to us."

Shang Xin Ci nodded. "I understand your clan leader's passion. We will return the Jinzan grass without a single stem missing."

She added, "A subordinate of mine handled the transaction. I will summon him."

Fang Yuan soon appeared and cupped his fists. "I handled the Jinzan grass deal. I hear your clan leader wants to buy it back?"

The guard snorted. "Indeed. Mortal, our benevolent clan leader offers three thousand primeval stones for your three carts of Jinzan grass."

Fang Yuan shook his head. "Jinzan grass is precious. Three thousand primeval stones hardly seems sincere, does it?"

"You!" The guard ground his teeth and raised five fingers. "Fine, five thousand primeval stones!"

The guard's expression turned cold. "Mortal, this grass belongs to our clan. Without proof of purchase, I could claim you stole it!"

Fang Yuan laughed. "It was a mutual trade with your young master. If you insist on claiming theft, there's nothing I can do. Jin clan is mighty enough to bully the weak and seize our goods. Go ahead and take them—though I know I wasn't the only buyer. Others bought a share too—will Jin clan rob them as well?"

"Hmph, seven thousand primeval stones! Mortal, hand over all the Jinzan grass you bought," the guard shouted.

"Hei Tu, just sell it. Business requires amiability," Shang Xin Ci urged, feeling the pressure.

"Since Miss has spoken..." Fang Yuan nodded, then pivoted, "I'll yield a step. Eight thousand primeval stones, and all my Jinzan grass is yours."

Fang Yuan smiled. "Business is business. If you cannot decide, shall I speak with your clan leader directly?"

"No need!" The guard waved him off. "Hand over the goods. Taking advantage of our plight—I'll remember this!"

The two finished the transaction in moments.

Bought for under five hundred primeval stones, the Jinzan grass was resold overnight for eight thousand!

"Offending Jin clan for eight thousand primeval stones isn't worth it," Zhang Zhu warned with a frown. "Never take such risks again."

Fang Yuan smiled at Shang Xin Ci. "Per our agreement, half of the eight thousand is yours. Please safeguard my half as well."

"Is it settled?" Jin clan leader asked from a hill, watching the departing caravan.

A nearby elder reported, "Yes, Clan Leader, we bought back all the Jinzan grass. But Zhang clan was truly despicable for extorting us."

The clan leader chuckled. "Eight thousand primeval stones is trivial. That Zhang clan lady is clever, pushing a mortal forward to test the waters and profit."

"Clan Leader, what if Zhang clan knows our secret and extorted us deliberately?"

"Haha, don't be paranoid. If they knew its importance, why settle for a mere eight thousand? They would hoard it all or refuse to sell. Still, have Gu Masters shadow them until they leave Huang Jin mountain. If anyone secretly contacts Huang clan, execute them on the spot!"

Fang Yuan looked back at Huang Jin mountain, his lips curling into a smile.

In a nearby carriage, Shang Xin Ci parted the curtain and watched Fang Yuan's back, lost in thought.

# Chapter 245: Rank Two Upper Stage

The caravan crossed mountains and valleys as they traveled west.

As the journey continued, Fang Yuan bought and sold shrewdly, reaping massive profits with every transaction.

Such repeated success inevitably drew attention.

Fang Yuan credited everything to Shang Xin Ci, sparking rumors of her business acumen.

Four villages later, the caravan neared Xiao Yue mountain.

That night, the caravan camped near a cliff.

"We've entered Xiao Yue mountain. Ahead lie wild valleys teeming with beasts and Gu. Stay vigilant," Jia clan's leader instructed.

Shang Xin Ci's eyes shone with bright light.

This was the caravan's most perilous stretch. Beyond it lay Xue Lei mountain and several more clan villages before Shang Clan City on Shang Liang mountain.

Zhang Zhu approached. "Miss, dinner is ready. Shall we invite Hei Tu and Bai Yun to join us again?"

"Of course," Shang Xin Ci nodded. "I want to ask him about trading during dinner."

This time, however, Fang Yuan declined the banquet invitation.

He deliberately accepted only one in three invitations.

Fang Yuan closed the tent flap; Bai Ning Bing was already sitting on a bed.

"Let's begin." Fang Yuan sat on the bed, back turned to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing pressed her palms to Fang Yuan's back, channeling ten percent of her snow silver primeval essence into him.

Bone Flesh Unity Gu glowed green and red, converting the primeval essence before six percent entered Fang Yuan's aperture.

Fang Yuan focused inward, using the essence to nurture his aperture walls.

Light rippled over the aperture walls as the essence condensed them toward rank two upper stage.

Time passed and it was already late at night.

*Howl—— !*

"Judging by the noise, that's quite a large wolf pack," Bai Ning Bing chuckled.

"This is Xiao Yue mountain—some say every wolf in the Southern Border gathers here. Under a full moon, their synchronized howls can shake the whole mountain. Running into a grey wolf pack on our very first night is truly bad luck." Fang Yuan opened his eyes slightly while continuing to cultivate his aperture.

Within minutes, wolves breached the camp's outer perimeter and poured inside.

"Do we go out?" Bai Ning Bing asked.

"For what? What could you do? Remember your identity," Fang Yuan replied nonchalantly after a pause.

"The wolves are charging right here—your new goods might get ruined," Bai Ning Bing teased gloatingly.

"Let them be ruined." Fang Yuan closed his eyes again.

Soon, Xiao Die called from outside, "Hei Tu, Hei Tu! Are you two inside?"

"What's the matter?" Bai Ning Bing asked.

"Grey wolves breached the camp! Miss wants you over by Lord Zhang Zhu for safety!" Xiao Die urged.

"No need to trouble Miss if it's under control. My strength is enough to handle a stray wolf or two," Fang Yuan replied.

The grey wolves attacked for fifteen minutes before retreating.

This assault inflicted the caravan's heaviest losses since its departure.

Three Gu Masters were killed, dozens injured, and cargo suffered heavy damage—especially Zhang clan's.

When dawn arrived and losses were tallied, he offered a wry smile, entirely unbothered.

Overnight, he had broken through to rank two upper stage in mere months.

# Chapter 246: Exposed

Morning light fell upon the ruined camp as the caravan cleaned the battlefield and set off with heavy hearts, yet this wolf attack was merely the beginning.

Days later, grey wolf packs struck again.

Though this assault was even larger, the caravan's thorough precautions kept their losses lower than the first attack.

Three days later, lightning wolves attacked with frenzy and bold leaders, slaying fifteen Gu Masters. The battle left corpses strewn everywhere, with a wounded frenzy lightning wolf fleeing alongside the survivors.

Realizing the grave danger, the caravan leaders resolved that evening to accelerate their pace and exit Xiao Yue mountain immediately.

Even so, wolf packs attacked relentlessly over the next fifteen days.

Everyone breathed a sigh of relief upon finally clearing Xiao Yue mountain.

Subsequent travel through Bai Hu mountain brought relentless beast attacks that further decimated the caravan. The caravan had now dwindled to half its original size.

Yet tempered by this brutal culling, the survivors displayed the hallmarks of an elite group.

Yet the caravan seemed cursed, suffering ceaseless attacks from beasts, insect swarms, and wild Gu worms on the road ahead.

As their numbers shrank steadily, merchants stopped caring about profit and loss, realizing their lives were on the line.

They willingly abandoned large amounts of goods to increase their marching speed.

A laden handcart became bogged in mud, the pulling ostrich straining in vain.

Just then, two hands grabbed the cart from behind and hoisted the stuck wheel from the mud.

He casually clapped his hands; the thousands of kilograms of cargo seemed weightless to him.

Yet despite freeing the cart from the mud, the wheels remained mysteriously jammed.

Beside him, Bai Ning Bing bent down to examine the wheel.

"What is this?" She rubbed the axle, eyes narrowing in suspicion.

Something hidden inside the axle was grinding down into fine gray powder as the wheel turned.

Bai Ning Bing rubbed some of the powder between her fingers; it turned into grease.

"I put this oil powder on the wheels to lubricate them." Fang Yuan wiped the grease from her hands.

He crouched down and checked the wheels; they moved smoothly again.

"Let's go." He patted Bai Ning Bing's shoulder.

The two continued to walk.

Bai Ning Bing slowed as doubts in her heart thickened into an undispellable fog.

"When did Fang Yuan get this oil powder? Why didn't I know... from the start, Huang Jin Mountain, or Xiao Yue Mountain? Strange... He doesn't care about the caravan—he didn't even frown at such huge losses. Why bother oiling the handcart? Strange, strange!"

Lowering her head, Bai Ning Bing concealed the shock in her blue eyes.

The sun sank in the west as stars began appearing across the sky.

The caravan halted near a beach to camp for the night.

Just as they were half-done pitching camp, cold jade owlcats appeared.

People cursed and scrambled, but tempered by prior hardships, quickly formed three tight defensive lines.

The cold jade owlcats had swift leopard-like bodies and owl faces, their enormous eyes glowing with eerie green light.

The owlcat king screeched, and the packs charged the camp like a tide.

As Fang Yuan moved a large trunk, Bai Ning Bing walked over and lifted one side.

Feigning to help, she leaned close to his ear and gritted her teeth: "You bastard, you lured these cold jade owlcats here, didn't you?"

Fang Yuan looked surprised: "Why do you say such a thing?"

"Stop pretending. That powder was suspicious—a guy like you wouldn't kindly care about such details!"

"Hahaha, you finally discovered it." Fang Yuan didn't deny.

The constant attacks along the way were entirely Fang Yuan's doing.

They moved the trunk while everyone around them shouted and fought, oblivious to their whispering.

After a moment of silence, Bai Ning Bing asked, "Why do this?"

"Haha." Fang Yuan laughed, "Guess."

Bai Ning Bing felt a violent urge to pummel him.

# Chapter 247: White Feather Flying Elephant

Being sharp and perceptive, she quickly deduced: "What does Shang Xin Ci have that makes Fang Yuan exert so much effort?"

She dismissed the thought he coveted her beauty; knowing Fang Yuan, only immense profit could drive him to such effort.

Yet over these days, Bai Ning Bing had learned Shang Xin Ci's background: an ostracized mortal of Zhang clan with no cultivation talent.

Quickening their pace, Bai Ning Bing lowered her voice: "Messing around like this, aren't you afraid of exposure? If anyone finds out, heheh, they'll fight you to the death!"

"Then did they find out?" Fang Yuan rebuked.

To deflect suspicion, Fang Yuan had sacrificed most of his goods during the first attack, leaving Zhang clan to suffer the heaviest losses.

Now Bai Ning Bing realized Fang Yuan had kept his most valuable goods all along; his actual losses were far less than they seemed.

Bai Ning Bing felt indignant: "This guy even hid the truth from me!"

As if reading her thoughts, Fang Yuan chuckled: "To deceive the enemy, one must first deceive one's own. I didn't hide it maliciously—you had a purpose to serve."

"Oh, what purpose?" Bai Ning Bing naturally asked.

"As an alert. You are closest to me; once you notice something amiss, others soon will."

"But, it was coincidence today that I..."

Fang Yuan shook his head: "Coincidence reflects inevitability. Regardless, the time has come."

Bai Ning Bing's eyes shone: "What will you do?"

"Leader Jia, why do you assign Chen clan to the frontline every time? What are your motives?!"

"Vice Leader Chen, I have been fair and impartial. Your Chen clan is the strongest; now that we are in this together, the stronger must put in more effort and bear greater responsibility."

A few days later, the demoralized caravan reached Xiang Ya Mountain.

Xiang Ya Mountain reached into the clouds, blanketed in rainforests below and snowy peaks above, home to massive elephant herds.

"Is it snowing?" Someone looked up, seeing white particles floating down from above.

"Damn it, this isn't snow, it's feathers!" Someone shouted.

Fierce winds howled as white feathers drifted everywhere like an oncoming blizzard.

*Angggggg......*

Hundreds of elephants trumpeted together, treading on air as they charged the caravan below.

These white feather flying elephants were covered in white plumage with thick, three-meter curved tusks; their charging momentum was virtually unstoppable.

Caught off guard, the caravan lost hundreds in a single charge as carriages shattered, pack beasts stampeded, and servants were trampled to paste.

"All Gu Masters, gather here!" Jia Long shouted amid the crowd.

Yet as soon as a dozen gathered, the elephants swooped down again, scattering them.

Seeing counterattack was hopeless, Jia Long sighed and shouted: "Everyone, quickly flee into the surrounding rainforests!"

Crashing into the rainforest, the flying elephants felled trees instantly and trampled countless fleeing survivors.

*Angggg!*

A flying elephant targeted Shang Xin Ci, descending like a comet.

"Miss, run! I'll draw it away!" At the crucial moment, Zhang Zhu shot a flash of red light at the elephant.

Enraged, the flying elephant changed direction toward Zhang Zhu.

As a healing Gu Master with weak combat ability, Zhang Zhu fled wretchedly through the rainforest.

Zhang Zhu lunged forward as the elephant crashed behind him, splintering trees in its wake.

In that life-or-death moment, Zhang Zhu activated his defensive Gu, shrouding himself in golden light.

The golden light shattered and he coughed up blood, sent flying into the distance.

With a thunderous crash, the elephant slammed into the cliff face, driving its tusks deep into rock.

The flying elephant cried out, thrashing its head and pulling backward with its four legs.

Zhang Zhu struggled to his feet in a cold sweat, knowing a split-second slower would have meant death.

"I must reach Miss!" He grew anxious—even as a Gu Master he barely survived, so mortal Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die were in far greater peril.

While the elephant struggled to free its tusks, Zhang Zhu sprinted back the way he had come.

Where they had parted, Shang Xin Ci was already gone.

As Zhang Zhu hesitated, a Gu Master sprinted toward him with three flying elephants in pursuit.

"Save me!" He shouted.

"Damn it," Zhang Zhu cursed, recognizing Chen clan's young Gu Master, Chen Xin.

Anxious for his lady's safety, Zhang Zhu paid Chen Xin no heed and fled.

Seeing Zhang Zhu, Chen Xin pursued him desperately like a drowning man clutching driftwood.

# Chapter 248: A peace of mind as long as one does what he can

Shaking off their pursuers, Zhang Zhu and Chen Xin hit a cliffside dead end, cornered by two flying elephants.

The cliff vibrated with the two elephants' roars as they thrashed their heads, sending rock fragments flying and widening the holes around their tusks.

Shocked and knowing the two elephants would soon break free, Zhang Zhu propped himself up desperately.

He had barely stood when a rush of wind made him shudder!

*Pew!*

A spiral white bone spear pierced his back, bursting through his chest and pinning into the ground.

"Who is it?" He wanted to turn his head, to see who had *backstabbed* him.

*Pew*, another bone spear was fired!

The spear entered his brain and exited through his mouth, planting into the ground.

At a hidden corner, Fang Yuan observed from afar.

From intelligence gathered over the past days, Fang Yuan knew Zhang Zhu had to be eliminated.

A white feather flying elephant freed its tusks and charged Zhang Zhu's corpse, crushing bones and trampling it into meat paste.

Seeing Zhang Zhu confirmed dead and trampled by the elephant, Fang Yuan looked away—he didn't even need to clean up the scene.

Suddenly under the pile of rocks, a head emerged.

"Thank god I had the bury Gu..." Chen Xin dug himself out, breathing raggedly in a cold sweat of lingering fear.

"Things are getting chaotic—someone actually assassinated Zhang Zhu." Seeing Zhang Zhu's unrecognizable, mangled corpse, Chen Xin gulped and fled frantically.

Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die supported each other out of the rainforest.

Corpses lined the bloody path, amidst broken carriage wheels, dead ostriches, and carcasses of black skin fat beetles and winged snakes.

Jia Long's face was grim; less than a tenth of the caravan survived, and most were crippled.

Shang Xin Ci found Bai Ning Bing and thanked her: "Bai Yun, thank goodness you're safe. Thank you for luring that flying elephant away earlier."

Distrusting Bai Ning Bing, Fang Yuan had eliminated Zhang Zhu personally while ordering her to protect Shang Xin Ci.

"It's nothing; I repay kindness. Lady Zhang, what saved you was your own past goodwill," Bai Ning Bing said.

No longer disguising her voice, she spoke in her natural cold, indifferent, and clearly female tone, taking Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die by surprise.

"Bai Yun, did you see Uncle Zhang Zhu?" Shang Xin Ci asked anxiously. "I couldn't find him anywhere."

Bai Ning Bing sighed—with Fang Yuan's return, Zhang Zhu was certainly dead.

"Don't worry, lady. Zhang Zhu is a Gu Master and can protect himself; he might already be returning," she consoled.

Jia Long shouted from high ground: "The scent of blood will draw more beasts! Salvage what goods you can and prepare to move out immediately. Discard anything too heavy. We must leave this area in thirty minutes!"

Severely injured servants unable to move begged desperately for help.

Few received aid; unable to work, the injured and crippled were heartlessly abandoned as burdens.

Shang Xin Ci stopped, lips trembling and face pale as her eyes darted around frantically.

"Lady Zhang, move. Now is not the time for kindness." Fang Yuan took her arm, pulling her forward.

"Trust me, things will improve," Fang Yuan said gently.

After thirty steps, her breathing steadied; after fifty, she walked firmly without Fang Yuan's support.

"Thank you." She said to Fang Yuan.

On the slope, she gradually stopped her steps, looking back.

"This is the most difficult road I have ever walked," she sighed painfully, face pale but voice remarkably soft and gentle.

Fang Yuan smiled slightly: so this was Shang Xin Ci, truly one destined to shake the Southern Border.

Fang Yuan chuckled softly, gazing deeply at her: "Lady Zhang, if you are so compassionate, why didn't you save those abandoned?"

Shang Xin Ci smiled bitterly: "If I could, I would, but no matter how hard I try, I cannot save them."

Fang Yuan laughed: "Blind kindness is foolish, but doing what you can earns my respect. Lady Zhang, life is full of hardships, and the road may be dirty and arduous; but doing all you can brings peace of mind."

Shang Xin Ci looked at Fang Yuan, eyes glowing with light.

She had long suspected Fang and Bai were no ordinary mortals, and his words finally confirmed it.

That they stayed by her side in such peril showed that despite their mystery, they possessed pure, righteous natures with true kindness in their hearts.

"Thank you."

Bai Ning Bing could not help but roll her eyes.

If Shang Xin Ci knew Fang Yuan had singlehandedly engineered this entire disaster, how would she react?

# Chapter 249: A droplet received in need will be repaid with a whole spring!

"The casualty report is out: only a hundred and thirty-seven people remain—seventy-eight Gu Masters and fifty-nine mortals," a vice leader reported to the group.

Originally over a thousand strong, the caravan was reduced to this pathetic remnant. Though seventy-eight Gu Masters seemed substantial, only twelve were rank three, twenty-eight were rank two, and the remaining thirty-eight were rank one.

This count included all injured and disabled Gu Masters; in truth, not even half retained their fighting strength.

The reporting vice leader then changed the topic: "The only fortune is that many goods remain in the caravan. Distributing these ownerless goods will yield enough primeval stones to recoup most of our losses."

As glances were exchanged, Vice Leader Chen Shuang Jin coughed: "Equal distribution isn't proper. In the battle, my Chen clan sacrificed the most and killed the most white feather flying elephants. I demand at least thirty percent of these ownerless goods!"

Only Shang Xin Ci was sitting silently in the group.

"Everyone," Shang Xin Ci said, "our priority is surviving the next beast horde, not dividing goods. We are all in the same boat. Our individual strength is limited, so we must cooperate to have a chance at survival."

"I propose we contribute all useful goods to the collective effort. Zhang clan will lead by example—I surrender all my goods without compensation."

Many were left tongue-tied; Chen Shuang Jin, Jia Long, and others showed conflicted expressions.

"I am tired and hope everyone can reach a consensus soon. I will take my leave." Shang Xin Ci nodded and exited the tent.

Returning to her tent, she found Xiao Die curled in a corner, weeping with bloodshot eyes.

"Missy, I'm scared. Lord Zhang Zhu hasn't returned yet, it can't be, it can't be..." Xiao Die buried her head in Shang Xin Ci's chest, weeping bitterly.

"Accept the truth, Xiao Die. From now on, we can only rely on ourselves—crying solves nothing!"

"Without Uncle Zhang Zhu, we are just mortals, Xiao Die—but mortals have strengths too!" Shang Xin Ci's gaze flashed with resolute light.

"Listen properly, we are in danger now—people can be more dreadful than wild beasts. Bring all our savings, and let's find Hei Tu and Bai Yun."

"Okay. I will do as you say missy!"

Inside the tent, Fang Yuan and Shang Xin Ci sat cross-legged opposite each other.

Fang Yuan stared thoughtfully at the girl before him: "You gave away all our goods for free? To those insatiable hyenas?"

"Yes, that is so." Shang Xin Ci calmly admitted.

Fang Yuan's lips curled, his gaze towards Shang Xin Ci filled with admiration.

"I came to apologize," Shang Xin Ci bowed. "Those goods were rightfully yours, yet I surrendered them without asking. Please accept these primeval stones in return."

Shang Xin Ci brought forward the two wooden trunks.

Without opening them, Fang Yuan knew the trunks were filled with primeval stones.

Most of it was his own earnings, along with Shang Xin Ci's fifty-percent share.

Their gazes met, gradually arriving at a tacit understanding.

Fang Yuan smiled, breaking the silence: "Lady Zhang, take these primeval stones back."

To her understanding, refusing the primeval stones meant he refused to help.

With no one else to turn to, Shang Xin Ci smiled bitterly: "I understand. But these primeval stones are my gift to you. A mortal holding treasure invites calamity; if you refuse, I may have to donate them too."

Fang Yuan laughed: "When Bai Yun and I joined the caravan, wounded and weak, even servants coveted our goods. You sheltered us and gave us time to recuperate; that favor saved our lives."

"I always repay kindness and avenge enmity. Though you are a mortal, I acknowledge and admire you. Meeting among such a vast population was fate. *A droplet of water in need is repaid with a spring*, let alone saving a life. Take back the primeval stones—I will protect you with all my strength!"

Her eyelashes shook and her eyes reddened, turning tearful.

Tears blurred Fang Yuan in her vision, but in her heart, his image became incomparably clear.

# Chapter 250: Truly Demonic Gu Masters!

Fang Yuan turned to what truly mattered.

"Bai Yun and I are demonic Gu Masters," Fang Yuan said.

Shang Xin Ci nodded without surprise; Zhang Zhu had already warned her to stay on guard.

Fang Yuan's voice turned cold: "Demonic path cultivators are vicious and merciless. Bai Yun and I are no exception; we have taken lives."

"Once I repay your favor, I will leave quietly so no one associates you with demonic cultivators. But before that, Bai Yun and I will show up as Zhang Clan's Gu Masters, I hope that you can clarify our identities." Fang Yuan indifferently said.

Shang Xin Ci said resolutely: "You are frank and principled, unlike hypocritical righteous Gu Masters. Receiving your protection is my good fortune."

Fang Yuan laughed, eyeing her deeply. "As long as you don't regret it later."

"Is Zhang Xin Ci in this tent?" came an arrogant young voice.

"Important discussion? Haha, all your Zhang Clan goods were donated—what is there left to discuss?" the man sneered.

"Lord Gu Master... aahhh!" Xiao Die screamed, collapsing to the ground with a thud.

"Get lost! A lowly slave dares block Ou Fei's path?!"

The tent flap was flung open, revealing a devious-looking young Gu Master before them.

"Zhang Xin Ci!" The young Gu Master's gaze fixed on her, filled with unconcealed lust.

Ou Fei laughed loudly: "Your protector is dead, Xin Ci. Rely on me, and you'll live safely. No need to thank me for my kindness, pitiful girl—just follow me."

Saying so, he walked forward to grab Shang Xin Ci.

"Missy, you can't go with him!" Xiao Die ran in, spreading her arms to block Ou Fei.

Ou Fei became furious, and immediately slapped Xiao Die.

"Lowly servant, if you want to die, I'll grant your wish!" Enraged, Ou Fei raised his hand to strike down.

The next moment, a sturdy hand shot out and fiercely seized his arm.

"Who is it?!" Ou Fei was startled, but looking at his captor, he saw only an ugly servant.

He tried pulling back, but Fang Yuan's arm held like iron pincers, completely unmoving.

"Dog slave, release me!" Just as Ou Fei tried to activate his primeval essence, Fang Yuan smiled.

His face was covered in burn scars and missing an ear; smiling now, he looked terrifying.

Ou Fei's heart seized just as Fang Yuan released his grip.

Raising his leg, he kicked hard into Ou Fei's stomach.

*Bang.*

Struck by irresistible force, Ou Fei flew out of the tent like tattered rags, skidding thirty feet.

The surrounding people stopped to watch the show.

Through the flap torn by Ou Fei's exit, Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die saw him lying motionless on the ground.

Ou Fei lay dazed for several seconds before the searing pain in his stomach stoked fierce fury and hatred.

"You lowly mortal, you're courting death!" Ou Fei growled in fury, scrambling to his feet.

"Go and die!" He crazily charged towards Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan stepped forward steadily, shielding the two girls with his head held high.

Drawing near, Ou Fei leaped twenty feet into the air before diving down at Fang Yuan.

"I'll smash you into meat paste!" he shouted, thrusting out both palms.

His palms swelled threefold under his Gu's power, crashing down on Fang Yuan with howling force.

Fang Yuan only scoffed, before lightly flicking his forefinger.

A spiral bone spear suddenly shot out towards the sky.

"What?!" Panicking in shock, Ou Fei tried frantically to dodge.

The bone spear pierced his defense, skewering his shoulder to the scapula as blood sprayed out.

The agony halted his dive, sending him crashing to the ground in a sorry state.

Without a word, Fang Yuan charged forward and smashed his right fist toward Ou Fei.

*Bang!*

The strength of two boars and a crocodile burst forth.

The immense strength shattered Ou Fei's palms and defense, slamming into his face.

His face caved in as the blow sent him flying; crashing down like tattered rags, he was already stone dead.

"Oh my god, he killed him!"

"Gu... he's a Gu Master!!" Xiao Die's eyes went wide as saucers.

Dumbfounded with shock, Shang Xin Ci recalled Fang Yuan's words as she looked at his back.

"Those from the demonic path are vicious and merciless. Bai Yun and I are no exception; we have taken lives."

They were truly demonic Gu Masters!

# Chapter 251: The son’s wrongdoings is the father’s failure to teach!

Ou Fei's corpse lay sprawled like a dead dog—how could it not draw attention?

"He is the killer!" Someone cautiously pointed at Fang Yuan.

With arms crossed and head held high, Fang Yuan stood indifferently in place, ignoring the surrounding chatter as if it did not exist.

"He is no servant—I saw him shoot a white spear and kill Ou Fei in one punch! It scared the soul out of me!" an eyewitness Gu Master said.

"A white spear?!" Chen Xin's heart leapt at the words.

Having witnessed Zhang Zhu's death by a spiral bone spear, Chen Xin immediately connected the two upon hearing 'white spear'.

"What is going on? What happened here?!" Jia Long's displeased voice barked.

"Someone was killed... wait, isn't this Vice Leader Ou's son?"

"Ahh! My son!! What happened to you?!" A figure rushed to Ou Fei's corpse, suddenly slowing to an abrupt halt.

This was Ou Clan's Ou Yang Gong—short, thin, and possessing an aquiline nose.

Ou Fei's face was smashed flat, blood and brains pooling together on the ground, his life force completely extinguished.

Tears streaming down his face, Ou Yang Gong stared at the corpse: "Who killed my son so viciously?! I will tear him to pieces!!"

As if in answer, every gaze turned toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan sneered calmly: "Of course it's me. *Are you blind?* I've stood here so long and you didn't notice?"

Jia Long and other vice leaders also couldn't help frowning.

Fang Yuan's fearless manner and servant clothes confused the leaders, causing them to hesitate on the sidelines.

"You are the killer!" Ou Yang Gong glared with razor-sharp eyes, yet despite burning with hatred, he did not make a move.

In truth, Ou Fei's appearance had pleased Fang Yuan—he could make an example of him to awe the crowd with sheer force.

Yet Ou Fei alone was not enough. But if he added Ou Yang Gong...

Fang Yuan's lips curved into an evil smile: "Old man, you arrived just in time. Your son disrespected my clan's lady, and my anger has yet to dissipate! Your son committed a crime because you failed to discipline him. As the root cause, pay with your life!"

With that, he dashed forward, closing the distance to Ou Yang Gong in the blink of an eye.

Unable to contain his rage any longer, he struck back fiercely at Fang Yuan.

*Bang*, the two collided against each other.

Fang Yuan was knocked back five or six steps to diffuse the force. His light armor flickered before stabilizing, the canopy Gu in his aperture turning listless.

Ou Yang Gong was hurled back, spitting blood as he crashed to the ground, face paper-pale.

"You are courting death!" Recovering from the clash, Ou Yang Gong's fury exploded, longing to tear Fang Yuan to pieces.

"Rascal, I'll teach you the difference between rank two and rank three!" Ou Yang Gong roared, charging forward.

Maintaining Canopy Gu alongside bone spears, Blood Moon Gu, and Jumping Grass, Fang Yuan charged at Ou Yang Gong.

Ou Yang Gong was rank three initial stage while Fang Yuan was only rank two upper stage.

With snow silver primeval essence from Bai Ning Bing and mostly rank three Gu, his combat strength surpassed Ou Yang Gong's.

After five rounds of fighting, Fang Yuan maintained complete dominance.

Ou Yang Gong felt immense grievance; he had expected to crush Fang Yuan easily, never imagining this opponent was a wolf in sheep's clothing!

"Both of you, stop! There must be a misunderstanding here!" Seeing Ou Yang Gong about to be killed, Caravan Leader Jia Long could no longer stand by and swiftly intervened to halt the battle.

Drenched in sweat and terror, a pale Ou Yang Gong felt immense relief at Jia Long's words.

He immediately pulled back from Fang Yuan, moving toward Jia Long.

Seeing he could not catch Ou Yang Gong in time, Fang Yuan halted and laughed aloud: "Brother Jia Long arrived right on time, let us use bone spears to kill him together!"

Jia Long was bewildered, having never spoken a single word to Fang Yuan before.

Ou Yang Gong panicked, unable to tell if Fang Yuan was bluffing or allied with Jia Long.

With his life at stake, Ou Yang Gong dared not take the risk; he veered off and fled southeast, away from both men.

"Calm down." Jia Long stopped immediately to avoid suspicion.

Fang Yuan laughed loudly and chased.

Ou Yang Gong realized the trick, but before he could react, Bai Ning Bing struck from the crowd.

Three spiral bone spears shot out in rapid succession.

His weakened defense blocked two spears, but the third pierced straight through his skull from behind.

Ou Yang Gong died!

His death turned everyone pale with fright, sparking a massive uproar.

This was a caravan vice leader—a rank three Gu Master!

"Let's combine our strength and capture them!!"

"Who dares capture me?!" Tossing aside her straw hat, Bai Ning Bing's silver hair flowed as her cold gaze swept the crowd, unleashing her snow silver primeval essence.

"Ahh, it is snow silver primeval essence!"

"She is actually a rank three peak stage Gu Master!"

In the caravan, only Jia Long was rank three upper stage; the others were merely initial or middle stage.

"Come if you want to die, hahaha." Fang Yuan stood beside Bai Ning Bing, toying with a thread of snow silver primeval essence drawn from his aperture.

Oh god! Another rank three peak stage!

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing gazed coldly at everyone. The camp fell into complete silence...

# Chapter 252: The only thing that matters is strength

Over a hundred people encircled the two dead bodies and the two standing figures.

Having charged out aggressively, they were stunned by Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing's revealed cultivation, caught unable to advance or retreat.

With Ou Yang Gong and Ou Fei dead, the caravan still had eleven rank three, twenty-seven rank two, and thirty-eight rank one Gu Masters.

Their combined strength far exceeded Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing's.

The caravan was merely a temporary coalition of scheming clans cooperating only when advantageous.

No single clan could defeat two rank three peak experts alone, and even combined, the caravan would suffer severe losses.

Moreover, amid frequent beast attacks, everyone in the caravan struggled to protect themselves, let alone care about others.

Fang Yuan smiled faintly; their reactions were within his expectations. Now, it was up to her.

Though things had escalated swiftly without prior discussion, Fang Yuan trusted Shang Xin Ci's intelligence to seize the opportunity.

Sure enough, Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die stepped out of the tent.

"Caravan leaders, these two are my Zhang Clan's hidden elders: Lord Hei Tu and Lord Bai Yun," Shang Xin Ci introduced them.

Shang Xin Ci faintly smiled: "The two lords were on a secret clan mission and coincidentally entered this caravan on the way."

Yet regardless of suspicion, Fang and Bai's displayed strength left only one way to conclude the matter.

"Hahahah, so that was it! Everything was a misunderstanding!" Jia Long laughed, wearing a warm smile.

Cupping his hands toward Fang and Bai, he praised sincerely: "I never expected such great heroes in our midst. That Ou father and son were indeed too unreasonable and their conduct questionable. I persuaded them many times, but they refused to listen. Their execution today is well-deserved."

The vice leaders echoed with beaming smiles, shamelessly twisting logic to agree.

The killers became righteous champions while the victims became villains, leaving the remaining Ou Clan members completely ignored.

"We pay respects to Lord Hei Tu and Lord Bai Yun." Over twenty people immediately bowed to Fang and Bai.

"Our caravan has suffered heavy losses and faces crisis; having both of you is our fortune. Please, join us in the tent to discuss our situation," Jia Long invited sincerely.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing glanced at each other before Fang Yuan turned to Shang Xin Ci: "Xin Ci, what is your opinion?"

Shang Xin Ci shook her head gently. "I am tired; it is better for the caravan leaders to discuss it together."

Fang Yuan inwardly praised her intelligence; taking a step back now created opportunities for greater advances later.

He nodded in agreement: "Our Zhang Clan has donated all our goods, so we have no interest in interfering."

Fang and Bai departed imposingly without giving Jia Long an opening, leaving the scene for others to handle.

The three remaining Ou Clan Gu Masters ran over in weeping humiliation, carrying away the corpses.

The crowd dispersed. Back in the tent, the leaders lost their composure; hypocritical smiles gave way to solemnity, bewilderment, and worry.

Jia Long sat in the main seat and asked gloomily, "What do you make of these two sudden Gu Masters?"

"Hidden elders is merely that Zhang girl's claim. From their conduct, they are very likely demonic Gu Masters."

Jia Long nodded. "Indeed. Zhang Clan has no hidden elders, but these two are vicious rank three peak experts..."

"The crucial question is how to use them to our benefit. My invitation just now to recruit them ended in failure," Jia Long sighed.

"We must proceed carefully. Demonic Gu Masters are unyielding; external pressure alone won't work—we must apply pressure from both inside and out."

"My suggestion is simple: give them a share of the goods. When the time comes, they will naturally step forward to protect their property."

Jia Long nodded. "A great suggestion! Demonic Gu Masters crave resources, and we can simply take them from Ou Clan's share."

With the Ou father and son dead, the few remaining members posed no threat, yet their goods were quite substantial...

"Uncle... I have an important matter to report!" Having watched the tent closely, Chen Xin approached Chen Clan's leader, Chen Shuang Jin, as soon as the meeting ended.

Returning to their tent, Chen Xin confirmed no one was eavesdropping before asking, "Uncle, remember when I reported how Zhang Zhu was murdered?"

Chen Shuang Jin nodded gravely. "Zhang Zhu was killed with bone spears. And today, Hei Tu and Bai Yun used bone spears as well!"

"Exactly! Those two harbor sinister motives and are utterly ruthless. We must expose their crime and combine our strength to eliminate the danger early," Chen Xin urged agitatedly.

"Testimony alone is useless without material evidence," Chen Shuang Jin scoffed. "And even with evidence, what good is it without strength? We all saw them kill the Ou father and son, yet divided by clan self-interest, who would unite to fight them?"

"Do you think they don't know about you? They simply ignored you because you pose no threat. Stop being naive. In this cold world, the only thing that matters is strength!" Chen Shuang Jin sighed deeply.

Chen Xin stood dazed, clenching his fists as his uncle's words struck his young heart, rendering him speechless.

# Chapter 253: Only the dead are clean

The caravan moved along the mountain path with a solemn atmosphere.

Among the crowd, Shang Xin Ci looked with complex emotions at the dozen baggage toads beside her.

Though Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing had skipped the discussion, the caravan leaders still sent her abundant goods.

"All of this because of strength..." Shang Xin Ci sighed.

When Zhang Zhu was around, the leaders were indifferent; now, they were polite to the point of fawning over her.

"All these changes are due to them." Shang Xin Ci cast a complex gaze toward Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing nearby.

While she found security in Fang and Bai, her kind nature also felt fear at how effortlessly they took lives.

"Hahaha, seems we really frightened that girl," Bai Ning Bing laughed softly beside Fang Yuan, sensing Shang Xin Ci's gaze.

Seven to eight days had passed since they killed Ou Clan's father and son.

Steeped in righteous ideology, the master and servant struggled to accept Fang and Bai's ruthless demonic nature.

Under external pressure, they would have to compromise to survive. After a few more beast attacks, any lingering estrangement would naturally fade.

"The problem now is him." Fang Yuan looked at Chen Xin.

This young Gu Master had fled with Zhang Zhu; Fang Yuan thought the white feather flying elephants had crushed him to paste, yet he had survived.

Gu Masters possessing unusual Gu worms could never be underestimated, regardless of low cultivation.

Lacking investigative Gu, Fang Yuan had overlooked Chen Xin. Though unsure what the boy witnessed, he prepared countermeasures regardless.

Thus, after killing Zhang Zhu, he intentionally displayed overwhelming strength so that any potential witness would be terrified into hiding.

Even if exposed, Fang Yuan had many ways to strike back; caravan leader Jia Long was one such pawn.

As Jia Fu's subordinate, Jia Long would assist Fang Yuan once shown his elder token.

Fang Yuan's first consideration was the worst outcome.

If terrible luck left ironclad evidence of Zhang Zhu's murder and Shang Xin Ci realized the truth, she would deeply despise him as an ungrateful traitor.

Simple, kill Shang Xin Ci.

As a mortal whose identity remained unknown to Shang Clan's leader, killing her secretly would bring zero repercussions.

As it stood, Zhang Zhu was dead and a trail remained, but Shang Xin Ci was still in the dark.

Fang Yuan was certain of this, as the young girl's emotions were completely transparent to him.

"There is a crocodile-elephant group ahead!"

"Correct. Everyone disperse, enter the rainforests!"

The Gu Masters issued the sensible command, and the high-strung crowd quickly dispersed in all directions.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing charged straight into the rainforest, protecting Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die.

He was not surprised at the crocodile-elephant group's arrival because it was his plan.

The crocodile-elephant group trampled through, bringing blood-curdling screams and crashing trees.

Fang Yuan carefully guided his group through the rainforest, but the beasts were so numerous that they still encountered one.

Yak-sized and agile, crocodile-elephants were covered in scales far sturdier than white feather flying elephants, with long reptilian tails trailing behind them.

"No need to worry." Fang Yuan indifferently said, before charging towards the crocodile-elephant.

Man and beast collided with a loud crash.

Fang Yuan stepped back twice, his light armor flickering as the beast's shattered skull sent it crashing dead against a tree.

Backed by snow silver essence and the strength of two boars and a crocodile, he handled ordinary beasts with ease.

Fang Yuan's direct, unrestrained power left a profound impression on the master and servant.

Two hours later, the crocodile-elephant group departed, and the caravan emerged from the rainforest.

Several days later, they exited the Xiang Ya mountain region and headed toward Mu Bei mountain.

With Fang and Bai guarding them closely through repeated attacks, the master and servant's attitude steadily shifted.

Shang Xin Ci grew closer to Fang Yuan, chatting cheerfully without avoiding his gaze, while Xiao Die came to revere both him and Bai Ning Bing.

Though demonic cultivators, Fang and Bai took no advantage and merely returned kindness—a demeanor rare even among the righteous path.

Several days later, the caravan entered Mu Bei mountain region.

Zombies started appearing.

Mu Bei mountain once bore a different name; over a century ago, a large clan resided here.

He eventually returned as a rank five expert with a zombie army, slaughtering the clan and raising their corpses.

After finishing his vengeance, he erected a massive tombstone in the clan's ruins.

Named Mu Bei mountain ever since, roaming zombies preyed on travelers, spreading ptomaine to produce new undead.

The caravan camped at the base of Mu Bei mountain for the night.

Under the bright starry sky, Fang Yuan gazed at dark Mu Bei mountain, eyes thoughtful.

"It is time. These people are no longer valuable; time to settle these troubles."

Chen Xin had to die, but eliminating him alone risked complications, as Fang Yuan did not know who else knew.

Yet Fang Yuan had no need to find out; in his plan, Chen Xin and everyone else would die.

Only the dead are clean.

# Chapter 254:  Assault of the zombie army

Hiding behind bushes at the mountain's base, Ding Hao looked down at the caravan, licking his lips excitedly.

"According to the cave's instructions, once I kill a thousand people and amass a zombie army, I can go to Zhao Ze mountain to become master's formal disciple!"

Originally a villager, Ding Hao had been recruited as a laborer by a passing caravan.

When zombies swarmed the caravan on Mu Bei mountain, he and dozens of servants were abandoned as cannon fodder.

He entered this cave and discovered a demonic inheritance.

After eight years of focused cultivation, he reached rank three realm, the requirement to enter the final secret chamber.

The final chamber's stone slab was inscribed by the Second Zombie King, promising official discipleship at Zhao Ze mountain to whoever passed the final test.

The test required killing a thousand people and raising an army of white-hair, black-hair, and green-hair zombies.

The victims yielded new zombies, constantly increasing his strength to slaughter even more.

The caravan at the mountain base was his final stepping stone to success—and the perfect prey!

Night fell, and dark clouds veiled the moon.

"Kill." Ding Hao's eyes brightened as he commanded. The arranged zombie army moved stealthily toward the camp from all directions.

A few hundred white-hair zombies hopped toward the camp, with stronger black-hair zombies mixed in the horde.

"There is a zombie group moving towards the camp!"

Jia Long and several vice leaders climbed the wooden wall and observed the situation with grave expressions.

No longer inexperienced, he used the white-hair zombies as cannon fodder to deplete the Gu Masters' primeval essence.

With too few Gu Masters against seemingly endless white-hair zombies, the horde endured the pressure and reached the wooden wall.

"It is your turn, my babies." With a thought, black-hair zombies charged from the rear onto the battlefield.

Ding Hao sent over fifty black-hair zombies charging together, smashing through a gap in the camp's defensive wall like dried twigs.

A black-hair zombie swung its arms, sending a rank one Gu Master flying.

He opened his eyes; a stalwart figure shielded him, while the black-hair zombie lay collapsed in the distance.

Its chest had caved in from Fang Yuan's punch, but unkilled, it hopped up and charged at him.

Clad in light armor, Fang Yuan grabbed both its incoming arms and pulled fiercely.

The black-hair zombie was immediately torn apart into two halves.

Fang Yuan casually tossed the corpse halves aside; even a myriad beast king blue-hair zombie would die from such injuries.

# Chapter 255: Ding Hao, get out here!

By now, the wooden walls had mostly collapsed as hordes of zombies surged into camp, battling the Gu Masters.

Seeing this, Fang Yuan was certain Ding Hao was manipulating this zombie army.

Fang Yuan recalled that the Second Zombie King's inheritance had been claimed by Ding Hao, who became his loyal eldest disciple.

"I thought of searching for you, but delivering yourself saves me plenty of time."

Fang Yuan had planned to use Ding Hao to slaughter the caravan. Though he had methods to lure beast groups, controlling their scale was difficult.

"Where is he hiding?" Amidst blazing fire, Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes and surveyed his surroundings.

"He needs high ground to command the zombies, and his hiding spot must allow an easy escape..."

With hundreds of corpses littering the ground and suffering catastrophic losses, the Gu Masters retreated to the camp's center.

"There are too many zombies; our defense will fall sooner or later."

"We need to break the siege!"

"Better to reinforce our defense and await dawn. Sunlight greatly weakens zombies; they'll retreat on their own without us lifting a finger."

Watching from the sidelines, Fang Yuan suddenly stood up: "Stop shouting!"

"Anyone wanting to defend is an idiot! Why stay if we can break out?" he scolded fiercely, projecting immense authority.

Then Fang Yuan sneered: "Shut up! You fools wanting to break out are even stupider!"

"Then what do you suggest, Sir Hei Tu?" Jia Long asked darkly, his gaze unfriendly.

"Hmph, whether defending or breaking out, we must first assess the situation. Do you even know the scale of this zombie horde? If a large group hides in the darkness, wouldn't breaking out walk right into a trap? And if this is all of them while you insist on defending instead of escaping, the scent of blood will draw more zombies to slaughter you." Fang Yuan spoke so vehemently that spit flew.

Fang Yuan organized three breakout groups to probe the perimeter and rule out potential locations.

"Only that high slope remains. Ding Hao is definitely hiding there." Fang Yuan sneered inwardly, continuing to organize breakout attempts.

"These people never give up." Ding Hao's brow furrowed, then relaxed. "Haha, you might break through an ordinary horde, but too bad, I am controlling this one..."

He mobilized zombies from the darkness to fill gaps while deploying heavy forces to several key areas.

"Cut the nonsense, success is in sight! I'll lead this time—Bai Yun, stand guard here." Fang Yuan aggressively pressed for a breakout.

Leading eight Gu Masters, Fang Yuan combined immense strength, snow silver essence, and Canopy Gu to tear through the encirclement.

"Damn, that side is somewhat thin..." Ding Hao couldn't prevent Fang Yuan from breaking through.

He never expected Fang Yuan to choose this dead end; having shifted zombies elsewhere to fill gaps from previous breakout attempts, this side had the fewest zombies.

Though five Gu Masters died in the process, Fang Yuan and two others successfully broke through.

"Don't think you've succeeded!" Ding Hao gritted his teeth and mobilized zombies to plug the gap.

White-hair zombies resealed Fang Yuan's breach before the camp could react, while five black-hair zombies pounced on his group.

"Follow me!" Fang Yuan shouted, charging toward Ding Hao's hiding spot.

"Running straight toward me? Hahaha, even heaven wants you dead..." Ding Hao sneered proudly at the two zombies beside him.

These hulking green-hair zombies were stronger than black-hair zombies, exerting power equivalent to a thousand beast king at night!

"Go." With a thought, the two green-hair zombies charged out.

Fang Yuan clashed head-on with one; despite possessing the strength of two boars and a crocodile, the impact knocked both back several steps.

"Let me help." Fang Yuan activated jumping grass and lept towards the battle.

The two Gu Masters rejoiced and moved to thank him, but two spiral bone spears suddenly shot toward them.

*Psh Psh...* two piercing sounds determined the two Gu Masters' deaths.

"Eh?" Watching the battle, Ding Hao was filled with confusion at this sudden turn.

Only to hear him shout: "Ding Hao, get out here!"

# Chapter 256: So you are eldest senior brother

*Who is this? He discovered me and knows my name! Impossible—I've been so careful all these years. How could he discover me?*

Turmoil churned in Ding Hao's mind; the green-hair zombies, along with the black- and white-hair zombies around the camp, froze in place.

Seeing the green-hair zombies freeze, Fang Yuan knew he had half-succeeded. He shouted: "Ding Hao, come out or you will regret it!"

Hidden in the bushes, Ding Hao gritted his teeth and had the green-hair zombies surround Fang Yuan: "Who are you? How do you know my name?"

Fang Yuan snorted: "My master, the Second Zombie King, told me."

"Who on earth are you?" Ding Hao anxiously asked.

Fang Yuan sneered: "I am Second Zombie King's eldest disciple, Hei Tu. Ding Hao, call back these green-hair zombies, or else..."

"You're eldest senior brother? But how did Lord Benefactor know my name?" Suspicious yet hesitant, Ding Hao recalled the two green-hair zombies to his side.

Fang Yuan's face hardened as he scolded: "Who is your eldest senior brother? Master hasn't accepted you yet—how dare you claim to be his disciple?"

Fang Yuan's tone softened: "You talk to the stone slab all day—how could Master not know your name?"

In his previous life, Ding Hao had personally recounted this habit to him.

Ding Hao licked his dry lips, almost convinced but still harboring doubts.

Bowing deeply with cupped hands, he said, "Lord Hei Tu, if you are Master's disciple, please show me our lineage's hallmark: the Zombie Heart Gu."

Fang Yuan had the Charred Thunder Potato Mother Gu, but lacked Zombie Heart Gu.

Unfazed by the request, Fang Yuan barked: "Cut the crap! I'm on a secret mission from Master and had to disguise myself in the caravan—how could I carry Zombie Heart Gu? Zombie Heart Gu feeds on zombies; how could I obtain them in plain sight?"

"Do you think I can't kill you?" Fang Yuan promptly displayed his snow silver primeval essence.

Rank three peak stage cultivation flared; Ding Hao's pupils shrank, being only at rank three middle stage.

Fang Yuan continued: "Controlling a zombie army demands intense concentration. Without a strong soul and mind, our control over their quantity and quality remains mediocre. Though you have two green-hair zombies, controlling them leaves flaws in your defense. Killing you would be effortless."

"However..." Fang Yuan pivoted, "Master told me to test your qualifications for his tutelage. Yet before I even sought you out, you came at me with killing intent! Hmph."

Ding Hao cupped his hands self-deprecatingly: "Eldest senior brother, knowing the Zombie Heart Gu's food and our weakness proves your identity beyond doubt."

Ding Hao bowed submissively: "Forgive me, eldest senior brother! I only attacked to harvest corpses. Had I known, I would have welcomed you down the mountain. I am devoted to serving Master—heaven can attest to my loyalty!"

"Yet you dared attack the caravan, sabotaging my secret mission!" Fang Yuan said coldly. "Do you know how much effort second senior sister and I spent infiltrating it?"

"Eldest senior brother, it wasn't intentional..." Ding Hao's excitement plummeted. "Second senior sister is here too? I... I'll recall the horde right now!"

"What?" Fang Yuan rapped Ding Hao on the head. "Is your brain full of paste? If the zombies suddenly retreat without cause, they'll suspect me! Idiot!"

Fang Yuan pointed at him: "You've made a grave error, but since it was unintentional, obey my orders to redeem yourself!"

Before giving instructions, Fang Yuan asked: "Tell me the truth: how many white-, black-, and green-hair zombies do you have? Any blue-hair zombies?"

Ding Hao looked ashamed: "I'm incompetent; despite living on Mu Bei mountain for nearly ten years, I haven't raised a single blue-hair zombie. I have only three green-hair zombies—one guarding my cave and two here—along with over a hundred and twenty black-hair zombies and nearly four thousand white-hair zombies."

Fang Yuan had anticipated him having no blue-hair zombies and was relieved to confirm it.

Hearing this dispelled Ding Hao's doubts. He looked at Fang Yuan in awe: "Eldest senior brother, you have two blue-hair zombies? I am full of admiration!"

Fang Yuan clapped his shoulder, feigning modest restraint: "Raising a blue-hair zombie can be difficult or easy, depending on how you look at it. If so, it becomes at least a green-hair. Nurtured with fresh blood for a year or two, it will evolve into a blue-hair."

"What's done is done. Cooperate with me; perform well, and I will approve you from my end," Fang Yuan said.

Ding Hao's eyes lit up: "Command me, eldest senior brother, I'll do my utmost!"

Fang Yuan gave detailed instructions before charging back to the camp.

With Ding Hao's covert cooperation, Fang Yuan rejoined Shang Xin Ci without incident.

"I've burdened you, benefactor!" Shang Xin Ci said with guilt.

"No matter. You are only part of the reason; Bai Yun is here too." Fang Yuan waved dismissively, his deliberate words moving Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die deeply.

Bai Ning Bing eyed Fang Yuan suspiciously; a subtle wink told her he was plotting something.

# Chapter 257: A spear from behind

The zombie horde's attacks gradually slowed, grinding the battle to a standstill.

Two hours later, Ding Hao followed Fang Yuan's instructions and ramped up the zombie assault, shattering the precarious defensive line.

Only around thirty people remained in the caravan.

"Abandon these mortals, they only slow us down!" Jia Long shouted.

Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die became pale.

"Don't worry, I am here." Fang Yuan defended them.

As they shakily neared a breakout through the encirclement, two green-hair zombies appeared.

"Fall back," Fang Yuan whispered, pulling Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die with him.

As Fang Yuan retreated with Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die, Bai Ning Bing gritted her teeth at the clear path ahead before turning to follow.

The remaining Gu Masters fell into chaotic combat with the two green-hair zombies.

After retreating dozens of steps, Fang Yuan launched another breakout attempt.

Fang Yuan charged fiercely through the blocking white-hair zombies, slaughtering them wherever he passed with overwhelming power.

Bai Ning Bing was dumbstruck, was this Fang Yuan's true strength?

"These white-hair zombies are too weak..." Attacking them, she immediately sensed something amiss.

Fang Yuan, however, was hurling curses inwardly right now.

He had told Ding Hao to make the zombies look formidable while fighting weakly for realism. What awful performance was this?

"I have no choice..." Narrowing his eyes, Fang Yuan deactivated Canopy Gu and deliberately charged into an attacking white-hair zombie.

His flesh was torn open, Fang Yuan got injured.

"Good, this is how it should be!" Fang Yuan thought with satisfaction. He couldn't waste this prime opportunity to win Shang Xin Ci's trust.

"Damn it!" Bai Ning Bing cursed and rushed to Fang Yuan, abandoning Shang Xin Ci. He carried the Yang Gu—her key to regaining manhood—and could not be lost.

"What are you doing? Go back and protect them!" Fang Yuan hissed as Bai Ning Bing reached him.

Bai Ning Bing's blue eyes widened as she realized Fang Yuan took the hit intentionally—this bastard!

"Don't go overboard," Bai Ning Bing warned with twitching eyes before retreating.

Seeing Fang Yuan injured, a terrified Ding Hao dared not obstruct their path.

Fang Yuan charged forward, leading Shang Xin Ci and the group out of the encirclement.

"Hei Tu, are you alright? That wound is so deep, all because of me!" Shang Xin Ci cried, eyes tearing up.

"So much black blood—Lord Hei Tu, you've been poisoned!" Xiao Die said with deep concern.

Fang Yuan puffed his chest out fearlessly: "Just a minor wound. The corpse poison is tricky, but I can detoxify it with Clearing Heat Gu. Don't worry! Hahaha..."

"Save us!" Trapped deep in the horde, the caravan survivors cried out for help upon seeing Fang Yuan's breakout.

Fang Yuan's eyes flickered silently. Shang Xin Ci grabbed his arm: "You're injured, don't go! Remember what you told me? Only do what you're capable of."

Fang Yuan chuckled, patting her hand: "Don't worry, I won't go. There's no love or hatred between us. Let's leave!"

Fang Yuan's eyes flashed as he shouted back: "Hold on! Once I escort them to safety, I will return to help you!"
