Noticing the pair's puzzled stares, Fang Yuan chuckled bitterly: "I'm giving them hope to spark a miracle. *Sigh*, this is all I can do."

Fang Yuan's stature rose immensely in the two girls' hearts.

Fang Yuan and the group ran along the mountain passage.

As planned, Ding Hao had arranged a batch of zombies here to block the path.

Reaching the arranged area, Fang Yuan signaled Bai Ning Bing with a glance and held his ground: "Go ahead first! I'll hold off these zombies and catch up."

The master and servant hesitated, but urged on by Bai Ning Bing, they hurried along the path and out of sight.

Fang Yuan feigned a struggle with the zombies, then activated Jumping Grass at the right moment to leap over them, landing beside Ding Hao.

"Eldest senior brother," Ding Hao bowed, pale-faced and drenched in sweat.

"Eldest senior brother, how is your injury? It wasn't intentional! I have healing Gu here," Ding Hao babbled in panic.

Fang Yuan clapped his shoulder: "You did well. Don't worry about my injury—I took it intentionally. Now put in some effort and wipe them out!"

The zombie horde submerged the three. Fang Yuan nodded: "Did anyone escape?"

"No, none at all. Following eldest senior brother's instructions, I stationed many zombies around the perimeter," Ding Hao answered quickly.

"Good, well done. Clean up the battlefield and remember to leave no tracks."

"Yes, eldest senior brother."

Ding Hao offered the collected stones: "Eldest senior brother, please take these."

Fang Yuan glanced over the roughly thirteen thousand primeval stones and stored them into his tusita flower without hesitation.

"How are your gains?" Fang Yuan patted Ding Hao's shoulder, asking warmly.

"You are not too shabby either, your commanding ability is quite good. But looking at your face, you have used your brain too much, your mind is haggard and your soul is wounded. You need to recuperate for a while. Taking good care of our soul is very important to our lineage. If our soul is not strong, our mind will be weak and commanding the zombies will be more difficult." Fang Yuan smiled, speaking gently.

"I must leave now, but you have my approval. Rest well and await my return; I will take you to pay respects to master. Right, where is your cave?"

"It is halfway up the mountain beside a unique pentagram-shaped dark pond—very easy to find," Ding Hao answered happily. "Eldest senior brother, you really won't visit my cave with me?"

"I cannot; time is tight, and I must report the secret mission to master. Not good—someone feigned death and escaped!"

"Where!" Shaken, Ding Hao immediately turned to look.

Ding Hao collapsed as a thick spiral bone spear pierced his skull, blood and brain matter slowly oozing from the spiral hole.

The smile on Fang Yuan's face vanished into indifference. Bending down, he grabbed Ding Hao's leg and dragged him toward the ruined camp.

Flames still blazed, while the masterless zombies began hopping aimlessly.

He threw Ding Hao into the blazing fire, quietly watching the corpse turn to ash as the Gu worms upon it wailed mournfully before burning to death.

Once Shang Xin Ci was recognized by the clan leader, Shang clan would investigate her thoroughly, making this battle of utmost importance.

Though Ding Hao was a useful chess piece, eliminating him and forfeiting his Gu worms was safer than risking exposure during Shang clan's investigation.

Only after Ding Hao's corpse burned to ashes did Fang Yuan slowly depart.

# Chapter 258: Separation as planned

"How could anything happen to him? Don't worry." Bai Ning Bing leaned against a withered tree, wondering what Fang Yuan was plotting.

Just as the three decided to return and assist him, Fang Yuan appeared along the mountain path.

Shang Xin Ci breathed a sigh of relief, though her heart pounded as Fang Yuan approached: "Brother Hei Tu, you are injured again!"

Fang Yuan, having deliberately added injuries to himself, drew a cold breath: "Right after you left, a green-hair zombie appeared. Fortunately I ran fast, but we should leave immediately—it might still be pursuing me."

Hearing that, the three dared not linger and hastened their journey that very night.

Wanted by Bai clan, Fang and Bai bypassed Zhao clan. The four pressed on relentlessly past Xue Lei mountain, crossing Qian Ku mountain, Ju Ren mountain, Lu Zao mountain, and three others—traversing six great mountains in total.

During this time, Fang Yuan advanced from rank two middle stage to peak stage.

Nearing Shang Liang mountain, the mountain path broadened and traffic thickened.

Finally, on this day, Shang Liang mountain appeared in their sight.

"We will part ways upon reaching Shang Liang mountain. Lady Zhang, I have repaid your favor," Fang Yuan said suddenly.

"Let's go. It isn't completely safe yet; we will part at the city gate." Fang Yuan led the way.

"Wait." Shang Xin Ci summoned her courage and suddenly spoke.

"We will meet again someday. Benefactor, you may feel you have repaid me, but by saving me repeatedly, your favor is as deep as the sea and heavy as a mountain. Benefactor, you said a drop received in need is repaid with a whole river. I will definitely repay you!" The young girl's resolute tone revealed her firm heart.

Fang Yuan smiled at her, then turned and disappeared into the crowd.

"Strange, you have protected Shang Xin Ci all the way, why are you intentionally parting ways now that we reached Shang Liang mountain? If not, you will just be shooting yourself in the foot, it would really be a great joke."

Bai Ning Bing coldly reminded Fang Yuan from the side.

Fang Yuan ignored her, watching Shang Xin Ci and Xiao Die walk through the city gate before looking away.

According to his previous life's memories, Shang clan leader would detect his bloodline in Shang Xin Ci once she entered Shang clan city. To confirm it, the tyrannical leader would place her entire caravan under house arrest for interrogation and investigation.

This is why Fang Yuan needed to part ways with Shang Xin Ci.

Fang Yuan possessed many Gu in his aperture that could never be exposed—especially the Spring Autumn Cicada. An aperture search would bring immense trouble.

In an ordinary clan, Fang Yuan would have no worries since their strongest leaders reached only rank four. Shang clan leader, however, possessed rank five cultivation—and was not even the highest power here. Shang clan possessed rank six supreme elders; if they searched his aperture, the Spring Autumn Cicada would be discovered even if hidden deep within.

Parting also made him appear completely selfless, reinforcing the impression of merely repaying a favor and securing Shang Xin Ci's trust for their future reunion.

The fastest way to forge an unbreakable bond with Shang Xin Ci was sexual intimacy.

Fang Yuan sensed her fondness; taking her chastity would make the future Shang clan leader fall deeply in love with him.

Though ugly, the charisma Fang Yuan radiated had already moved Shang Xin Ci's heart.

He knew Shang Xin Ci would not refuse him.

But this way absolutely could not be carried out!

Doing so would make him Shang clan's son-in-law, binding him to their chariot. Fang Yuan would lose his freedom, have his every move watched, and even be forced to suppress his cultivation.

Rapid cultivation growth would expose the bone flesh unity Gu, causing massive upheaval. Its value was so immense that even Shang clan could not monopolize it.

If the Spring Autumn Cicada were exposed as well, reclusive rank six and even rank seven Gu Masters would emerge. Rank six brought a qualitative leap in power, allowing each to rival an entire clan.

Had he bedded Shang Xin Ci, her confirmed identity would bring the clan searching for him. As a demonic Gu Master of unknown background, severe investigation and interrogation would be inevitable.

Fleeing would mean facing Shang clan's pursuit and arrest warrant—a completely different threat from Bai clan's pursuit.

While Bai clan was weakened and desperate, Shang clan was an overlord of the Southern Border.

In that case, Fang Yuan's only option would be escaping the Southern Border entirely.

Occupied daily with clan affairs, Shang clan leader had little time to cultivate. Even so, with domineering talent and comprehension, he had reached rank five upper stage, just one step from peak stage.

The scarlet flames slowly dispersed and shrank, returning to long crimson hair upon Shang Yan Fei's head.

Suddenly, Shang Yan Fei opened his blood-red eyes, his gaze piercing the darkness like lightning.

"A new bloodline originating from mine... where did this come from?"

# Chapter 259: Reunion of Father and Daughter

Following the bloodline resonance, Shang Yan Fei's sharp senses locked onto Shang Xin Ci.

Two girls stopped in front of a stall.

"Missy, this hairpin is beautiful!" Xiao Die picked up a jade hairpin, holding it against Shang Xin Ci's hair.

Suddenly sensing something in her heart, Shang Xin Ci turned around and immediately locked eyes with Shang Yan Fei.

Without a word spoken, the moment Shang Xin Ci saw the middle-aged man, she knew his identity.

He was her father!

"So this is why Mother told me to come to Shang clan city before dying!" Realization dawned, tears streaming down her face.

The moment Shang Yan Fei saw Shang Xin Ci, he thought of her mother and realized her identity.

On a distant drizzling afternoon, beside willows swaying by a lotus pond, young master Shang Yan Fei had sheltered in a shabby hut and met Zhang clan's young lady fleeing the rain.

Between ambition and love, duty and promise, Shang Yan Fei chose power over all else.

He defeated his siblings to ascend the throne of Shang clan leader, reached rank five, married countless beauties, and fathered many children. Yet because Zhang clan and Shang clan were generational enemies, he could never look for her.

Taking a light step, he vanished and reappeared directly before Shang Xin Ci.

"You... what is your name?" Shang Yan Fei spoke with difficulty, his magnetic voice filled with warmth.

It was this man who hurt her mother's heart.

It was this man and yet he... he is... my father.

Emotions surged within her, forming a turbulent vortex that threatened to overwhelm her consciousness.

She fainted.

"Missy!" Snapping out of her daze from Shang Yan Fei's presence, Xiao Die screamed.

Shang Yan Fei was faster, catching Shang Xin Ci in his arms.

"Who dares violate city rules and use Gu? Do you want to end up in prison?" City guards rushed over, cursing.

"You... you are... Shang clan's..." Xiao Die stammered, and was speechless.

Shang Yan Fei grabbed Xiao Die's arm; a blood colored flame burst forth and the three disappeared.

"Queue up and enter one by one! Ten primeval stones entry fee. Misusing Gu inside the city is forbidden—violators will be imprisoned for at least seven days!" the gate guards shouted.

Moving with the queue toward the city gate, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing indeed saw Bai clan's arrest warrant for them.

"Bai Clan..." Fang Yuan inwardly snorted.

"Here are twenty primeval stones," Fang Yuan said calmly, handing over a pouch.

The guards confirmed the amount, and immediately let them pass.

Passing through the city gate, a wide street opened before them.

Built with black-and-white tiles, vermilion gates, and grand pillars, the famed Shang clan city restaurant wafted with aromas of fine wine and savory dishes.

Exhausted and hungry from their arduous journey, the two entered the restaurant.

When they reached the stairs, the waiter stopped, a young and sweet looking girl walked over and spoke softly: "Lords, could I ask which floor you want to go? Four Seasons Restaurant has five floors, first floor hall is for mortals; second floor provides for rank one Gu Masters; third floor provides for rank two Gu Masters, there is a twenty percent discount for them; fourth floor provides for rank three Gu Masters and has fifty percent discount; fifth floor provides for rank four Gu Masters and the services are free of charge."

# Chapter 260: Each with their own schemes

Soon, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing were seated at an elegant fourth-floor table, served a feast of fragrant, visually appealing delicacies.

"After traveling together so long, this is the first time you've been so generous," she remarked sarcastically between bites.

Fang Yuan chuckled without answering, knowing Bai Ning Bing's suspicion and impatience.

He had painstakingly protected Shang Xin Ci only to deliberately separate from her upon arrival—an action Bai Ning Bing could not fathom.

Fang Yuan was now at rank two peak stage, one step from rank three; their agreed deadline regarding rank three was rapidly approaching.

Free from Bai clan's hunting and beast hordes, the two shared a table in apparent amiability, yet underlying tension brought them to the brink of turning on each other.

How to deal with Bai Ning Bing?

Though Fang Yuan held the advantage with the Yang Gu, Bai Ning Bing also held leverage over him.

Traveling with him, she had witnessed the Bai Gu mountain inheritance and watched him lure beast hordes against the caravan.

"If I finish off Bai Ning Bing, I will not have to worry about Shang Xin Ci, but because my cultivation is insufficient, I missed the best chance to kill off Bai Ning Bing during the caravan journey. Back then I needed her strength to ensure my survival. With her exceptional battle instincts and newfound experience, she is even harder to deal with now..."

"Leaving Qing Mao mountain behind, we finally have time to rest. I must reclaim the Yang Gu to restore my male body! Snatching it directly has little chance of success unless I can kill Fang Yuan instantly. Together with his scheming, devious mind, unrestrained by morals, he can truly commit any heinous sin."

"Most importantly, I have not discovered his true trump card. But his cultivation still depends on me, and knowing so many secrets, maybe I can force him to compromise. Using the poison vow Gu, I could make an unbreakable agreement, or use plunder Gu, seize Gu, petty thief Gu, and others to steal the Yang Gu..."

Most importantly, they held each other's weaknesses. Failing to win in one strike and allowing the other to recover would ruin them both!

Finding no solution, the two looked up simultaneously, staring at each other.

Their gaze met for an instant, before turning away.

On the streets, Fang Yuan heard people discussing.

"Do you know? At the southern gate, Shang clan leader appeared!"

Fang Yuan entered from the eastern gate while Shang Xin Ci entered from the south. By the time rumors reached the east, the truth was already distorted beyond recognition.

He chuckled secretly; Shang Xin Ci's situation was proceeding just as he had expected from his memories.

Next, he would wait for the fruits to mature, and land on himself.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing walked up the path together.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing came to a huge cave.

"Entering the inner city costs a hundred primeval stones per person," the guarding Gu Master requested.

# Chapter 261: Shang Clan Inner City

Fang Yuan paid two hundred primeval stones, and entered the inner city with Bai Ning Bing.

The first inner city, the central inner city, was Shang clan's political heart and military base.

The second inner city was the clan city, where only Shang clansmen could stay.

The third inner city was an elegant, high-class zone with fresh air.

The fourth inner city was the middle-class zone, while the fifth was low-class.

The two continued deeper, and got to the city gate.

"Without a token, entering the third inner city costs six hundred primeval stones each." The guard was already rank three.

Fang Yuan paid the primeval stones and reached the third area.

This was Fang Yuan's destination.

"Tong Ou shop." Fang Yuan looked at the plaque before walking in.

"Are our esteemed guests here to buy or sell?" the elderly rank two Gu Master asked with a smile, cupping his fists.

"Both buying and selling," Fang Yuan answered, raising his cup.

The elderly laughed as two flashes of light flew from his body, hovering before Fang and Bai.

Fang Yuan browsed the inventory, lacking the funds to purchase what he needed.

Fang Yuan waved his hand: "No rush, I have Gu worms to sell."

Saying so, he summoned a bone spear Gu.

The elder examined the bone spear Gu with a flicker of surprise.

Created uniquely by Grey Bone Scholar, this Gu had never entered the market.

Fang Yuan nodded and sipped tea: "Indeed. This is the bone spear Gu, a unique lineage. Try it and you'll see."

Testing it, the elder remarked: "Though hard to nurture, its attack power and uniqueness make it worth the price."

The elderly stretched out his palm, showing a number.

# Chapter 262: Unable to sell

Three hundred ten?

Three hundred ten was quite fair; the Tong Ou shop elder had not intentionally suppressed the price.

Within a few sentences, the elder was forced to raise the price by ten primeval stones.

One bone spear Gu, three hundred and twenty primeval stones.

"Alright, we shall deal at this price." Fang Yuan waved his hand, releasing a light from his aperture.

Fifty-six bone spear Gu floated before the startled elder.

"Fifty-six Gu totals seventeen thousand nine hundred and twenty primeval stones. I'll have servants fetch the stones at once," the elder said, storing the Gu into his aperture.

"No rush, look at this." Fang Yuan smiled, taking out a spiral bone spear Gu.

"This rank two Gu resembles the bone spear Gu..." The elder showed astonishment.

"Indeed, refining the bone spear Gu creates this spiral bone spear Gu. Its spiral force grants formidable penetration," Fang Yuan explained smoothly.

Testing and verifying Fang Yuan's words, the elder offered seven hundred eighty primeval stones.

After some bargaining, Fang Yuan raised the price to eight hundred per Gu.

He had twenty well-kept spiral bone spear Gu in his aperture.

Thus, he sold them all for sixteen thousand primeval stones.

"Now look at this Gu—seven thousand primeval stones," Fang Yuan offered, bringing out the bone spike Gu.

Holding the bone spike Gu, the elder dared not test it, laughing bitterly: "This rank three Gu harms user and enemy alike; spikes piercing one's own skin must hurt terribly. It requires a healing Gu as well. Seven thousand is too high; six thousand five hundred is just right..."

Fang Yuan presented his bone path Gu, negotiating their prices with the elder. The elderly bowed respectfully: "In all my years of business, I have never seen such interconnected Gu with a clear advancement path. May I ask, are they from a single inheritance?"

Fang Yuan nodded: "Any intelligent person can tell, indeed, this was obtained through a lucky opportunity, and I inherited an inheritance, getting these Gu worms."

The elder was overjoyed: "Then you have the refinement recipes! Would you sell them to our shop as well?"

"Everything below rank six has a price. But how many primeval stones can you offer?" Fang Yuan asked.

The elderly raised two fingers: "Two hundred thousand!"

Fang Yuan smirked: "Two hundred thousand? You dare offer that?"

Flushing at the low offer, the elder quickly said: "Three hundred thousand!"

Fang Yuan did not speak, but turned to leave.

The elderly gritted his teeth: "Five hundred thousand!"

"Now that's more bearable. I'll sell it for six hundred eighty thousand," Fang Yuan said, sipping tea.

The elder replied bitterly: "Five hundred thousand is my limit. We could simply hire a recipe master to reverse-engineer them."

Fang Yuan shook his head: "At least six hundred fifty thousand, or no deal. There are other shops in Shang clan city."

"Most shops in Shang clan city are controlled by our young master. If you cannot sell here, nowhere else will buy either. Refuse the recipe, and you won't be able to sell these Gu either." The elder cupped his fists, his tone carrying a veiled threat.

"Oh? We'll see about that." Fang Yuan stored his Gu worms and left.

After they left, the elder entered a secret room and summoned a Gu with his primeval essence.

"What is it?" asked Shang Ya Zi, an eighteen-year-old young master whose dissipation had left him pale and gaunt.

The elderly spoke about Fang Yuan's matter.

Shang Ya Zi beamed: "Excellent! With this inheritance deal, I will pass this year's evaluation!"

"I will do my best, but I only manage Tong Ou shop; as for the others..."

"I'll arrange it. If they want to sell Gu, they must bow to me!" Shang Ya Zi snorted in disdain.

After three shops, Fang Yuan still failed to sell his Gu worms.

"Haha, to think you'd see this day. It really is their territory," Bai Ning Bing mocked Fang Yuan relentlessly.

Fang Yuan remained calm: "Shang Yan Fei only has ten young masters. Annual evaluations eliminate the worst-performing one to make room for others."

Realization struck Bai Ning Bing: "So this young master is either vying for young clan leader or defending his title, or he wouldn't cause such a commotion."

Fang Yuan laughed: "Fighting is forbidden in the third inner city. Shang clan's young masters face even stricter restrictions. With evaluations approaching, their every move is monitored. We're in no rush; let's rest first."

At the same time, in the first inner city.

"Did you investigate clearly?" Shang Yan Fei stood at the window, staring at the garden.

"She is indeed your daughter, Clan Leader. Her identity has been verified, with no soul possession. Oddly, we found no other survivors from her caravan," reported external elder Wei Yang.

Shang Yan Fei sighed inwardly: "Having let down her mother, I cannot fail my daughter!"

Shang Xin Ci sat staring blankly as steam rose from her tea.

She never imagined her father was the Shang clan leader, an overlord of the southern border!

A knock sounded at the door: "Ci Er, can I come in?"

# Chapter 263: Meeting

Shang Yan Fei showed a sincere smile as he entered the house.

Sitting beside the table, Shang Yan Fei spoke gently: "Ci Er, how do you feel now?"

Shang Xin Ci stepped back and bowed: "Lord Clan Leader, please do not worry. I have fully recovered."

"Tell me, how were your past few years?" Shang Yan Fei looked at Shang Xin Ci gently.

"How did you get here from Zhang clan? It is a huge distance away."

"Sir, if Lords Hei Tu and Bai Yun hadn't aided us, our caravan never would have made it..." Xiao Die blurted out.

Memorizing the names, Shang Yan Fei smiled: "From now on, you will stay here safely. Feel free to stroll or shop when bored. I will send a servant to guide you. Rest well for now; I shall leave first."

Stepping out of the room, Shang Yan Fei's smile vanished completely.

"Greetings, Lord Clan Leader," a young female Gu Master bowed respectfully.

"Tian Lan, look after Missy Ci Er to the best of your ability from now on," Shang Yan Fei said coldly.

"Gather intelligence through her outspoken maid. Find out exactly how they reached Shang Liang mountain."

Loyal and capable, Tian Lan completed her mission in just three days.

"Hei Tu and Bai Yun? Demonic Gu Masters..." Shang Yan Fei mused. "I must investigate them thoroughly before rewarding them."

Shang Yan Fei summoned Wei Yang: "Find these two demonic Gu Masters, Hei Tu and Bai Yun."

Losing patience, Shang Ya Zi brought his subordinates to Nan Qiu garden.

When Shang Ya Zi arrived, Fang and Bai were playing chess beside the lotus pond.

Shang Ya Zi snorted tauntingly: "You two certainly have leisure. Why haven't you visited other shops these past days?"

"Why bother? Those who want to buy will find their way here, won't they?" Fang Yuan chuckled lightly.

Suppressing his emotions, Shang Ya Zi raised his head arrogantly: "Seeing your plight, I'll offer five hundred thousand. Happy now?"

"Five hundred thousand primeval stones? No deal. At least six hundred fifty," Fang Yuan glanced at him before returning to the chessboard.

Shang Ya Zi narrowed his eyes, stepping forward threateningly: "Insolent demonic Gu Masters! I know you snatched this inheritance from Bai clan! You even killed Bai clan's two young masters! Tie clan is already on Shang Liang mountain searching for you. What do you think happens if I expose you to them?"

"Oh? Go tell them then." Fang Yuan laughed heartily.

"You!" Shang Ya Zi pointed furiously: "Five hundred thousand is my final offer. In my territory, learn your place!"

Just then, a light descended from the sky, revealing a Gu Master.

Wei Yang bowed slightly to Fang and Bai: "Sir Hei Tu, Lady Bai Yun, by our clan leader's orders, I invite you both to the second inner city for a talk."

"Wh-what? Father wants to meet them personally?!" Struck as if by lightning, Shang Ya Zi stared in utter terror.

# Chapter 264: Absolutely terrible!

"Impossible! They're just wanted demonic Gu Masters, stray dogs! Why would Father invite them?"

"How could backgroundless rogues have ties to Father? If they expose this, my position is finished! Kill them? No, that's courting death. I can't act in the third inner city, especially with Father watching. Worse, both bastards are rank three peak stage—how can I do anything?"

"Shang clan leader wants to see us? What for?" Fang Yuan timely showed his doubt.

Suspecting this was Fang Yuan's trick, Bai Ning Bing glanced at him.

Wei Yang laughed: "Rest assured, this is a friendly invitation—Lord Clan Leader is simply interested in you two."

"It is our honor. We shall go immediately," Fang Yuan said, tossing his chess piece aside and standing up.

Growing more frantic, Shang Ya Zi blurted: "Six hundred fifty thousand primeval stones—let's make the deal!"

Fang Yuan smiled at him: "Sorry, I don't intend to sell the recipe!"

Shang Ya Zi turned deathly pale: "I was rude, please don't take offense! We can discuss the price properly after you return!"

*Who would discuss anything with you? But placating him now prevents desperate actions and trouble.*

Fang Yuan nodded: "If young master is sincere, I look forward to it."

Wei Yang led Fang and Bai to a private residence.

"Esteemed guests, please wait here. Lord Clan Leader is handling matters and will arrive soon. Please do not wander outside."

The two waited six full hours without seeing a soul—there was not even tea served.

"Hmph, what is this Shang clan leader doing? Making us wait, yet nowhere in sight. How arrogant!" Fang Yuan paced angrily.

"Can you sit down? You're making me dizzy. Why not cultivate peacefully?" Bai Ning Bing frowned at his uncharacteristic behavior.

Fang Yuan snorted: "Left alone here, I cannot rest easy. Let's go out and inspect the situation!"

Inside a study, colorful smoke rose before Shang Yan Fei, displaying Fang and Bai in real time.

"Wei Yang, what do you think of these two?" Shang Yan Fei asked his trusted aide.

Wei Yang murmured: "They are indeed demonic Gu Masters. They are young yet already rank three peak stage, showing extraordinary talent. Hei Tu is frank and rash, while Bai Yun seems shrewd and calculating. Both names are likely fake."

"Mm, good analysis. Attend to them over the next few days, test their abilities, and watch them closely." Shang Yan Fei dispersed the smoke.

As the two hesitated over going out, Wei Yang appeared.

"Esteemed guests, lord clan leader is too busy to step away, so we must cancel this meeting." He offered an apparently sincere apology.

"What? We come and go at your whim?" Fang Yuan fumed.

Wei Yang smiled: "Lord Clan Leader asked me to host you in apology. I've arranged a banquet at Shi Tian restaurant; please join me."

Fang Yuan glanced at Bai Ning Bing and said: "Perfect, we're hungry anyway. Let's eat first."

Fang Yuan ate and drank boisterously, his earlier resentment seemingly vanished.

After the banquet, Wei Yang escorted them to Nan Qiu garden.

Wei Yang said in parting: "Rest well tonight. Tomorrow I'll take you sightseeing around Shang clan city."

# Chapter 265: Broadsword of Light vs Bone Spear

On the third day, Wei Yang returned to host them.

"This is the battle zone, boasting over eighty stages for testing Gu, sparring, and multi-opponent battles."

"Big brother Wei, what brings you here?" A young man in the crowd spotted Wei Yang and walked briskly over.

"Little Xiao, I heard you fought your way into the third inner city. Well done!" Wei Yang patted his shoulder encouragingly.

Wei Yang explained: "The battle zone is how Shang clan recruits external talent. I held the stage for eighteen rounds before the clan leader recruited me."

"Being here brings back memories. Guests, care for a spar with me?" Wei Yang proposed.

"Alright, let me experience it." Expecting Shang clan's probing, Fang Yuan went along with it.

Little Xiao asked: "Brother Wei, what field do you want, forest, mountain, wilderness, or desert, lake?"

Bai Ning Bing felt an urge to test herself: "Then we'll choose the flagstone stage."

To maintain privacy, Wei Yang used his elder authority to seal the stage from spectators.

Wei Yang stepped forward: "Since this is a casual spar, we'll skip formalities. Please take the first move."

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing exchanged glances and struck, flanking Wei Yang from left and right.

"Close combat?" Wei Yang stood still as golden light enveloped him in protective battle armor.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing activated spiral bone spear Gu and charged.

Testing the spiral bone spear Gu's power, Wei Yang took three hits without dodging.

"The spiral force penetrates well." Unflustered, Wei Yang channeled essence to restore his light armor.

"My turn!" he shouted, grasping the empty air to manifest two brilliantly shining broadswords of light.

Gripping both broadswords, Wei Yang swept his gaze and charged at Fang Yuan.

*Broadsword light Gu is among the strongest rank three attack Gu...* Fang Yuan's eyes flashed.

Jumping grass propelled Fang Yuan forward at high speed.

White armor enveloped Fang Yuan, while three white bone shields orbited his body continuously.

"Good timing!" Wei Yang shouted, slashing both broadswords down toward Fang Yuan.

Without dodging, Fang Yuan met the blow with his right fist.

The light broadswords crashed down, automatically blocked by the white bone shields.

The blades cleaved the bone shields in half and struck his white canopy armor.

Simultaneously, Fang Yuan's fist struck Wei Yang's stomach.

Though failing to pierce the golden armor, the immense force sent Wei Yang flying.

Bai Ning Bing seized the moment, waving her hand to launch multiple spiral bone spears at Wei Yang.

The strike was ruthless and lethal, aimed straight for Wei Yang's life.

# Chapter 266: My name is Fang Zheng

Sent flying by Fang Yuan's punch, Wei Yang faced incoming spiral bone spears before even steadying himself.

Wei Yang challenging them both had provoked Bai Ning Bing's pride.

Trailing behind the bone spears, Bai Ning Bing dashed forward and launched a fierce kick.

"Hehehe." At the final moment, Wei Yang laughed.

Just as the bone spears were about to hit, Wei Yang flashed white and vanished.

Carried by her momentum, Bai Ning Bing's kick shattered the flagstone with a bang, radiating spiderweb cracks.

Rainbow light tore through the air, dispersing to reveal Wei Yang.

In an instant, he covered over fifty meters across half the stage, pulling far away from them.

Seeing such speed, Bai Ning Bing halted.

"This rank three rainbow light Gu surpasses many rank four Gu in speed—so fast the user struggles to react. Without thought-accelerating Gu, you overshoot before you can even react."

"Its second weakness is high primeval essence consumption. As for the third, guess for yourselves."

"Is it that you cannot use strength Gu to raise your physical strength?" Bai Ning Bing answered immediately.

Wei Yang looked amazed: "Sharp. If a Gu Master increases physical strength, utilizing rainbow light Gu becomes difficult. Essence expenditure spikes drastically, or worse, light transformation fails and destroys the Gu."

Yet upon hearing his praise, Bai Ning Bing's gaze turned icy with killing intent: "Let me warn you, do not call me Miss!"

"Eh?" Wei Yang froze. *What did I say wrong? Is calling someone 'Miss' forbidden? What a strange taboo.*

Wei Yang apologized: "Pardon the offense, Lady Bai Yun..."

Veins popped on Bai Ning Bing's forehead: "Don't use that either!"

"Oh, then... Sir Bai Yun?" Wei Yang carefully probed, watching her expression.

Bai Ning Bing's face finally returned to normal.

Smiling, Wei Yang continued: "Then sirs, it is my turn to attack."

"Give me everything you've got!" Fang Yuan roared, thumping his chest and startling Bai Ning Bing beside him.

"Sir Hei Tu is truly bold. Take my strike!" Wei Yang commended, turning into white light and hurtling toward them.

Before they could blink, the rainbow light darted between them and landed behind their backs.

The white light dispersed, and turned back into Wei Yang.

Dual broadswords in hand, he slashed toward Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Sensing her skin tighten, Bai Ning Bing knew her ice muscles could not endure the blade and retreated immediately.

Fang Yuan growled and punched forward, activating canopy Gu and flying bone shield Gu without dodging.

After several exchanges, Wei Yang stopped: "If you hope to outlast my essence consumption, you will be disappointed. My light source Gu halves the primeval essence cost of all light-type Gu."

Fang Yuan waved his hand: "No more, I can't win. Brother Wei, we concede."

Wei Yang laughed heartily, cupping his fists: "It has been an honor."

Seeing the battle end, Little Xiao hurried over excitedly: "Big brother Wei is impressive, but sirs lasting this long is equally amazing! You may not know, but brother Wei has superior battle skills even though he is rank three peak stage. He is Lord Shang Yan Fei's third general, called the White Light Swordsman. If it was me, I would not even be able to exchange twenty blows with him."

"A loss is a loss. Wei Yang, once I acquire suitable Gu, I'll challenge you again." Bai Ning Bing's face was icy; having never suffered such a defeat, she was in a foul mood.

Fang Yuan chimed in: "Brother Wei held back. Once I buy better Gu, let's spar again—next time, don't hold back!"

"Hahaha, Brother Hei Tu speaks his mind! Next time I'll go all out." Wei Yang patted Fang Yuan's shoulder.

"That's the spirit! I concede fair and square—let me treat you to wine. Brother Xiao, come along and tell me Wei's heroic tales so I know how to beat him next time!" Fang Yuan laughed.

"Alright!" Little Xiao laughed. "Sparring brings us closer! I know a cheap, newly opened place with great food. Guests shouldn't pay—tonight is on me. Big brother Wei, please join us!"

Fang Yuan grinned: "Your treat this time! I'm broke right now, but once I sell my recipe, I'll treat you properly."

Sharing wine in the room, they exchanged names and grew closer.

Little Xiao was Xiao Yan, a rank three fire-path Gu Master who had fought his way into the third inner city battle zone.

"Hei Tu and Bai Yun are aliases. I am Gu Yue Fang Zheng, and she is Bai Ning Bing," Fang Yuan revealed, feigning drunkenness.

With Bai clan's warrant active, Shang clan would uncover them anyway. Disclosing them now projected an open conscience.

*Surname Gu Yue...* Wei Yang's eyes flashed. This vital clue meant he had successfully fulfilled the clan leader's assignment.

# Chapter 267: Greatly underestimated Fang Yuan’s shamelessness

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng?" Listening to Wei Yang's report, Shang Yan Fei frowned slightly.

"Gu Yue clan, one of Qing Mao Mountain's three ancient clans. Qing Mao Mountain..." Shang Yan Fei's gaze sharpened as he recalled intelligence from six months ago.

Qing Mao Mountain had suffered a mysterious overnight catastrophe, transforming from a lush mountain into a frozen wasteland.

Receiving that report, Shang Yan Fei had immediately deduced the involvement of the Northern Dark Ice Soul Physique, one of the ten extreme physiques.

"In that case, Gu Yue Fang Zheng's background makes sense. The investigators sent to Mu Bei Mountain also reported nothing suspicious."

"Only one question remains: why hide their identities within the caravan?"

"Right, you sparred with them, how were their skills?" Shang Yan Fei asked.

Wei Yang reported solemnly: "Geniuses! Given time, they will certainly surpass me."

Shang Yan Fei smiled: "A-grade aptitude only reflects talent, not everything. You are living proof: with B-grade aptitude, you became my external elder. Besides, how many A-grade geniuses have fallen by your hand?"

"Individually, they differ. Gu Yue Fang Zheng is straightforward, charging with peerless bravery and a lofty demeanor. As for that Bai Ning Bing, she is quick-witted with a sharp gaze and only moves when she is sure of it, she had been constantly trying to find my weakness during the battle. Moreover, Fang Zheng conceded calmly once I revealed my light source Gu—he is magnanimous, far beyond ordinary men."

"Lord clan leader, they are *a land tiger and a sea dragon*. One Yang, one Yin; one domineering, one scheming—twin stars amplifying each other. Recruiting them would be a massive boon to Shang clan!"

Shang Yan Fei was moved.

"Wei Yang, I trust your judgment after all these years. Yet do not undervalue yourself; they may not necessarily surpass you in the future. You are held back only by aptitude; otherwise, you would shine even brighter. Rest assured, if a reform Gu appears, I will secure it for you."

"I never mistreat loyal followers. Continue hosting them for a few days to gauge their view of Shang clan and assess recruitment prospects."

"Yes, clan leader!"

In a blink of an eye, three days passed.

In the lounge, Fang Yuan and Shang Ya Zi was sitting opposite each other.

Fang Yuan leisurely tasted the tea while Shang Ya Zi's expression looked rather unsightly.

"I have visited you sincerely these past few days, yet you keep raising the price by tens of thousands daily—from six hundred and fifty thousand up to eight hundred thousand! Are you trifling with me?" Shang Ya Zi ground out, suffocating with frustration.

*I still don't know their relationship with Lord Father! Wei Yang has escorted them everywhere—and as the Third General, he is Father's most trusted aide! *His actions reflect Lord Father's will! Why is Father granting such deferential treatment to two nobodies from nowhere?*

Setting down his teacup, Fang Yuan smiled: "Everything has its price. To others, this is merely an inheritance, but to you, young master, it is the final hope of keeping your status."

"Hehe, if priced cheaply, how could it match your final hope or the prestigious rank of Shang clan young master? After all, only young masters can vie for young clan leader."

Shang Ya Zi's eyes shook with anger.

Restraining his fury for his position's sake, Shang Ya Zi said: "Spending eight hundred thousand is a total loss that will drag down my evaluation. The evaluation elders aren't stupid! I simply can't buy it at this price."

Fang Yuan smiled, having expected this: "Therefore, I have a solution. I will openly sell it to you for six hundred and fifty thousand, but you will actually give me eight hundred thousand. You will maintain your young master's position and I will be getting a reasonable price, it is a complete win-win situation."

Shang Ya Zi's expression changed, staring with wide eyes: "You want me to falsify accounts? Impossible! If discovered, my young master status will be revoked and I'll be severely punished."

Fang Yuan looked nonchalant: "Who said anything about false accounts? I merely sell you the recipe, and admiring my character, you gift me primeval stones. There is no connection between the two!"

Shang Ya Zi stared at Fang Yuan, stupefied and speechless.

He had thought Fang Yuan shameless, but had greatly underestimated his shamelessness!

# Chapter 268: Sudden wealth

Two days later, a haggard Shang Ya Zi visited Fang Yuan again.

"I agree, we'll do as you asked!" He gritted his teeth, finally relenting after long hesitation.

"Alright, take a look at the agreement." Fang Yuan brought over a proposal.

Shang Ya Zi glanced at it, slamming the table in furious rage: "Nine hundred and fifty thousand? You raised the price again! Last time, you said eight hundred thousand, but it has only been a few days and you raised it by a hundred and fifty thousand?! Do you think I'm made of gold? Scoundrel! You think my primeval stones fall from the sky?!"

Fang Yuan smiled calmly: "It has been three days; naturally the price increased. You know why."

Veins bulging on his forehead, Shang Ya Zi jumped up: "I don't have that much money! Demanding nine hundred and fifty thousand for this inheritance—*you are like a lion opening its huge mouth*!"

"Calm down, anger harms the health. This isn't a tiny inheritance; it concerns your position. Every year a young master is eliminated—how many crave this spot?" Fang Yuan said casually.

At the mention of his position, Shang Ya Zi's rage dissipated.

Seeing nine hundred and fifty thousand exceeded his limit, Fang Yuan relented: "Alright, alright, how about nine hundred thousand primeval stones? I'll concede that much."

After a solemn moment, he nodded: "Nine hundred thousand then. But I don't trust a paper agreement—we must swear with the poison vow Gu!"

"Why, are you afraid? Without the poison vow Gu, how do I know you won't run off? I won't budge on this!" Shang Ya Zi's attitude was firm.

"In that case, I'll go first." Fang Yuan extended his left hand.

Only then did Shang Ya Zi smile, summoning the poison vow Gu.

Poison vow Gu was a finger-sized, purplish-red worm with a vicious mouth; it was a rank three consumable Gu.

It flew to Fang Yuan's left index finger and bit down.

Enduring the pain, Fang Yuan read the proposal's terms. As it absorbed his heart blood, the poison vow Gu nearly doubled in size.

Soon after, the poison vow Gu flew to Shang Ya Zi's finger and sucked blood.

Gripping the paper, Shang Ya Zi read the terms with a trembling voice, and the poison vow Gu doubled in size again.

*Bam.*

The poison vow Gu stuffed with blood, suddenly exploded.

Spots of light rained down upon Fang Yuan and Shang Ya Zi, fusing into their bodies.

He looked at Fang Yuan: "Hehe, we've sworn. If you violate the agreement, you will turn into a puddle of blood and die."

Fang Yuan's expression remained unchanged: "Where are my primeval stones?"

Shang Ya Zi shrugged: "Don't worry, why would I violate the agreement, here!"

He tossed out a Gu.

This palm-sized Gu resembled a semitransparent crystal ball containing a cloudy figure, as if sealing numerous clouds.

"There are eight hundred and seventy thousand primeval stones here. I'll give you the remaining thirty thousand once gathered." Shang Ya Zi reluctantly handed over the primeval elder Gu.

After Fang Yuan received it, Shang Ya Zi cooperated in refining it.

As the primeval elder Gu changed ownership, the cloudy old man inside shifted his gaze from Shang Ya Zi to smile at Fang Yuan.

"I'll deliver the final thirty thousand in three days. Only we two may know this. Don't seek loopholes." Shang Ya Zi got up and left.

"There are no loopholes in the agreement; you examined it yourself, didn't you?" Fang Yuan said plainly.

Three days later, Shang Ya Zi gathered and delivered the thirty thousand primeval stones.

At the same time, Fang Yuan handed over the recipe, withholding only the most valuable bone flesh unity Gu.

Fang Yuan then sold him the bone spear Gu, spiral bone spear Gu, and bone spike Gu.

Per their original agreement, this yielded forty thousand six hundred and twenty primeval stones.

After this transaction, Fang Yuan's assets rose to nine hundred thirty-four thousand and twenty primeval stones, totaling about nine hundred and forty-five thousand with his savings.

Fang Yuan stored most primeval stones in the primeval elder Gu, keeping some on himself and in the tusita flower for safety.

"In my previous life at this time, I was still slogging in a caravan with barely sixty primeval stones. Now, I am nearly a millionaire."

In a flash, three days passed.

Once Shang clan's investigations ended, Wei Yang brought Shang Yan Fei's invitation: "Esteemed guests, the clan leader has hosted a clan banquet and specifically ordered me to invite you both."

"Clan banquet? The moment is finally here." Fang Yuan thought.

# Chapter 269: Clan Banquet

Shang Yan Fei hosted the clan banquet in a spacious courtyard.

Three seats were already occupied.

"Big brother, do you know why father summoned us?" Shang Chao Feng asked vaguely, popping a red fruit into his mouth.

The eldest, Shang Qiu Niu, sat resting with closed eyes. He opened them slightly, answering in a deep voice: "Father recently exited closed cultivation and misses us. What's strange about holding a banquet?"

"Big brother may be right, but when has father ever held a casual banquet? Look, aren't there extra seats today?" Shang Pu Lao interjected from the side.

Managing the brothels made Shang Pu Lao quick to hear rumors. He was about to speak when his ears twitched: "Someone has arrived."

The gate creaked open and three people entered.

Wei Yang led the way, followed by Fang and Bai.

"This private residence is where the clan leader stayed when suppressed by an alliance of rival young masters, voluntarily stepping down to become an ordinary clansman. After becoming clan leader, he maintained this place as a reminder for himself and future generations. Clan banquets are always held here."

Shang Qiu Niu, Shang Chao Feng, and Shang Pu Lao rose and cupped their hands: "Our greetings, Elder Wei Yang."

"Greetings, young masters. These two are esteemed guests invited by the clan leader," Wei Yang replied calmly.

"Sirs, please take a seat." Wei Yang led Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing to their seats.

Wei Yang smiled: "Allow me to introduce them. This is the eldest son, Shang Qiu Niu, and the fourth son, Shang Chao Feng. And this is Shang Pu Lao, who manages the red light zone's brothels."

"Qiu Niu greets both esteemed guests." Shang Qiu Niu took the lead in cupping his hands.

"The young masters are too polite. I am Hei Tu and this is my companion Bai Yun," Fang Yuan introduced.

Soon, a blood-colored flame flickered on the main seat, revealing Shang Yan Fei.

Shang Yan Fei wore a white robe with gold-inlaid cuffs. Scarlet hair flowed wildly to his waist, complementing his handsome face with a unique charisma.

"Your children greet Lord Father." The young masters rose and knelt on one knee in unison.

"Take your seats." Shang Yan Fei leaned back into his wide chair and casually waved his hand.

Immediately, seven colored beautiful lights sprinkled down like rain.

The lights settled onto the tables, transforming into fine dishes whose fragrance filled the courtyard.

Shang Yan Fei presented the dishes and addressed his sons: "We have two esteemed guests today; toast them properly. Qiu Niu, as the eldest, set an example."

Shang Qiu Niu rose, raising his cup with a deep voice: "Qiu Niu toasts the two esteemed guests."

With that, he swigged down the glass of wine.

Fang Yuan drained his wine while Bai Ning Bing drank water.

Amidst the toasts, the courtyard gate opened and a girl walked in: "Father, I am sorry I am late."

After everyone finished toasting, Shang Yan Fei raised his cup to Fang and Bai: "Thank you for protecting Xin Ci all the way—that was the only reason I gained a daughter."

Once he said so, everyone present was stunned.

# Chapter 270: Reward by Shang Yan Fei

"Daughter?"

The young masters looked at one another in astonishment.

Shang Ya Zi realized at once, patting his own forehead.

*So this was it! This was why Father treated them with courtesy!*

With the mysterious veil lifted, Shang Ya Zi glared at Fang Yuan with concealed hatred.

Fang Yuan cooperatively acted stunned, muttering with a bewildered gaze: "Don't tell me?"

"That's right," Shang Yan Fei nodded. "Lady Zhang is my daughter; once she entered the city, I detected her bloodline and reunited with her."

"Zhang Xin Ci!" Bai Ning Bing immediately followed.

Shang Yan Fei laughed heartily: "Hehehe, she is no longer Zhang, but Shang. You saved my precious daughter's life and are benefactors of Shang clan; you deserve our toast."

"Brother Hei Tu, Sister Bai Yun, why are you here?" Shang Xin Ci stared unblinkingly at Fang Yuan, her eyes shining brightly.

"Ci Er, didn't Father say I'd give you a surprise?" Shang Yan Fei laughed gently.

Shang Xin Ci bowed: "Thank you, Father. This is the happiest day I've had since coming to Shang clan city."

Shang Xin Ci turned anxiously to Fang Yuan: "Brother Hei Tu, I didn't know either. Mother sent me here, and I only learned the truth from Father."

The words struck Bai Ning Bing's heart like lightning.

Her blue eyes narrowed into slits, filled with confusion: *Even Shang Xin Ci didn't know her own identity? How does Fang Yuan know everything?*

"So that's it; I wondered why Shang clan treated me so well..." Fang Yuan sighed, slowly shaking his head. "To think you were the clan leader's daughter!"

"Brother Hei Tu, can you forgive me?" Shang Xin Ci gripped her hand, asking nervously.

Fang Yuan laughed: "Why would I blame you? The unknowing are innocent. In truth, I've lied to you as well."

"What?" Shang Xin Ci froze.

Fang Yuan rubbed his nose: "Hei Tu, Bai Yun are just fake names."

Shang Xin Ci smiled gently: "I guessed that long ago."

Fang Yuan feigned hesitation, then cupped his fists: "I am ashamed—my real name is Gu Yue Fang Zheng, and this is Bai Ning Bing."

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng, Fang Zheng..." Shang Xin Ci repeated the name, memorizing it to heart.

Shang Yan Fei's smile widened, appreciating Fang Yuan's honesty.

"This is a joyful occasion—you have all gained a blood-related sister." Shang Yan Fei looked at his children, well aware of their thoughts.

Shang Yan Fei turned back to Fang and Bai.

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng, Bai Ning Bing, you escorted my daughter here. State any request, and as clan leader, I will satisfy it."

Fang Yuan shook his head: "Lord Clan Leader, we protected Lady Shang Xin Ci only to repay her prior kindness. The debt is settled; we owe each other nothing."

Seeing Fang Yuan refuse the reward, Shang Xin Ci said anxiously to Shang Yan Fei: "Father, Brother Hei Tu lost many assets during the caravan trip too."

He nodded: "Since both of you are Gu Masters, I shall gift a million primeval stones to express my thanks."

Fang Yuan shook his head again: "Truthfully, I risked my life to claim an inheritance. After selling the recipe, I no longer lack primeval stones."

"Oh, is that so." Shang Yan Fei looked meaningfully at Shang Ya Zi.

Shang Ya Zi's heart nearly leapt out in fear, his mouth going dry as cold sweat broke out on his forehead.

# Chapter 271: Purple Thorn Token (Two-in-one chapter)

Seeing Fang Yuan reject the primeval stones, Shang Yan Fei offered another solution: "Bai clan's arrest warrant for you is clearly a misunderstanding. I can explain and have it removed. What do you think?"

Fang Yuan shook his head: "Our conflict with Bai clan stems from an inheritance. We seized it and killed their young masters. One day, I will seek revenge. I repay kindness with gratitude and grudges with revenge. *A drop of kindness repaid with a whole spring; a spark of hatred burning an entire forest!*"

As he spoke, Fang Yuan did not hide his overwhelming killing intent.

"There is no need to remove the warrant. I need it to spur myself to grow stronger. Thank you, Lord Clan Leader." Fang Yuan cupped his fists.

"Then tell me what reward you want. Shang clan always rewards those it chooses—that is our rule. Even if you throw the reward away, that is your business." Shang Yan Fei frowned, feigning displeasure.

Fang Yuan looked around and laughed: "What if I truly don't want it?"

Shang Yan Fei spoke calmly, but his gaze was determined: "That is not an option."

Wei Yang laughed, easing the atmosphere: "Brother Fang Zheng was disfigured by injuries—why not have a doctor restore his appearance?"

"A good idea. Wei Yang, summon Doctor Su Shou here," Shang Yan Fei nodded.

He looked at Bai Ning Bing: "Come along and get a full-body checkup as well."

Bai Ning Bing snorted, knowing her own condition, but agreed since she knew Fang Yuan had other plans.

The two left the courtyard and entered the house.

Doctor Su Shou, a veiled, slim woman in white, was drinking tea at her seat.

As a rank five healing Gu Master with a complicated past with Shang Yan Fei, she held a unique position in Shang clan city.

"My mood is great today." Setting her cup down, she looked at Fang Yuan: "You seek treatment? Go bathe first."

She pointed at Bai Ning Bing: "Especially you, young lady. What is that ugly, dirty stuff on your face? Clean it before returning."

Wei Yang explained: "Doctor Su Shou requires every patient to bathe and change into white robes before treatment. Please follow me."

Entering the inner room, they found two wooden bathtubs prepared.

Bai Ning Bing frowned, snapping: "Get out, I will wash myself."

"I'll keep mine, brother Wei, please go out first. Her loss if she cannot enjoy it." Fang Yuan laughed.

Fang Yuan stripped off his clothes and stepped into the tub.

Bai Ning Bing stood before her tub, hesitating motionless.

Relaxing in the tub, Fang Yuan said casually: "Bai Ning Bing, our identities are exposed and we have no need to hide in Shang clan city. Do you dare not show your face?"

Fang Yuan continued: "Doctor Su Shou is one of Southern Border's four great doctors. You can ask her about the Yin Yang rotation Gu later."

Yin Yang rotation Gu!

This Fang Yuan, was like an unknown abyss!

Back at the main room, Fang Yuan found Doctor Su Shou.

Without wasting time, she placed her palm on Fang Yuan's shoulder.

Pure white light surged out like water, enveloping Fang Yuan's body.

His charred skin rapidly healed as flesh began regrowing on the right side of his head.

Fresh skin quickly formed, and new eyebrows sprouted from his healed pores.

Within fifteen minutes, his injuries were healed, his appearance restored, and his right ear fully regrown.

Doctor Su Shou withdrew her hand: "Now you look decent. Leave, and take your companion along. Hmph—eh..."

Just then, the door opened and Bai Ning Bing stepped out.

Clad in a white robe, she stood unmasked in her true appearance. With icy muscles, jade bones, sky-blue eyes, a flushed face, and lingering bath steam, even Doctor Su Shou was stunned by her ethereal beauty.

Doctor Su Shou's attitude softened completely as she praised: "Little sister looks incredible; even I was almost charmed."

Bai Ning Bing shook her head: "I don't need healing. I only want to know about the Yin Yang rotation Gu."

"I will answer whatever little sister asks," Doctor Su Shou said gently, then turned coldly to Fang Yuan: "As for you, why are you still here? Get out!"

The moment he got out, he saw Wei Yang.

"Brother Fang Zheng?" Wei Yang look at him hesitantly.

Fang Yuan nodded gratefully: "Thank you, brother, for standing guard all this time."

Wei Yang laughed bitterly: "Since you call me brother, I must lecture you. Why refuse Lord Clan Leader's reward? You have your principles, but Shang clan has its customs. Lord Clan Leader holds no malice toward you—this is a good thing!"

Wei Yang smiled more bitterly: "Medical treatment alone isn't a reward. Without a proper reward, outsiders will mock us and damage Shang clan's reputation. If our young masters run into trouble later, who would help them? You must accept a reward no matter what."

Observing Fang Yuan's deepening frown, Wei Yang urged: "I don't know what to say. You're doing your utmost to refuse what others would die for! Brother Fang Zheng, *an arm cannot overpower a thigh*. If you truly don't want it, accept it first and give it to Shang Xin Ci later. Isn't that a solution?"

Fang Yuan pondered, then said: "That solves the dilemma. But Brother Wei, what should I ask for?"

Wei Yang immediately replied: "Oh course it is a token!"

Fang Yuan chuckled inwardly—this was exactly what he wanted. Shang Yan Fei had deliberately withheld mentioning tokens in hopes of recruiting him.

"Token?" Fang Yuan showed a confused expression.

"High-grade tokens are crucial in Shang clan city. Without one, even money is often useless here," Wei Yang advised earnestly.

Fang Yuan nodded: "Although I don't get it, since brother Wei said so, I shall request for a token."

Back in the courtyard, Fang Yuan said: "After Brother Wei's advice, I realized the importance of tokens and would ask Lord Clan Leader for two."

Shang Yan Fei's eyes flashed. He had withheld the offer with an agenda, only for Wei Yang's well-meaning interference to spoil it.

Since they saved his biological daughter, the tokens could not be low-grade.

Shang Yan Fei laughed heartily and waved his hand: "Very well, I shall grant each of you a purple thorn token in gratitude."

Of Shang clan city's nine token tiers, the purple thorn token was the highest, granting authority rivaling half a clan elder.

Even Fang Yuan had not expected a purple thorn token. He had underestimated Shang Yan Fei's magnanimity.

Shang Yan Fei took out a palm-sized token of cercis wood. The front bore 'Shang Clan', while the back showed a map of Shang Liang Mountain.

Shang Yan Fei summoned another Gu: "This is Shang clan's special token Gu. It requires your blood."

The mosquito-like Gu flew to Fang Yuan's arm, drew a drop of blood, and landed back on the token.

With a crisp sound, the token Gu burst into blood and merged into the token.

The token appeared unchanged, but upon entering Fang Yuan's hands, its surface glowed with a watery purple light.

As Fang Yuan obtained the purple thorn token, the young masters' gazes shifted completely.

A purple thorn token could only be bestowed by the clan leader or ten elders in agreement. Only two hundred existed in the world.

"Your companion?" Shang Yan Fei took out another token.

"Still with Doctor Su Shou, I'll fetch her now." As Wei Yang rose, Bai Ning Bing reappeared before everyone.

Her expression was cold and solemn. Doctor Su Shou had confirmed that regaining her male body required the corresponding Yang Gu.

Seeing her appearance, many young masters fell into a daze.

# Chapter 272: New Alliance Agreements

The clan banquet lasted for an hour before ending.

"Can I still call you Brother Hei Tu?" Shang Xin Ci asked with glimmering eyes as they parted.

"Whichever you like." Fang Yuan smiled warmly.

Shang Xin Ci murmured: "It is wonderful your injuries have healed, yet I feel we've drifted apart. Are you staying at Nan Qiu Garden?"

Sensing her feelings, Fang Yuan invited: "Yes, Nan Qiu Garden. Visit whenever you are free. Shang clan city is vast, we can explore it together."

As the banquet dispersed, the two returned to Nan Qiu Garden.

Bai Ning Bing stared at Fang Yuan: "I must admit, I underestimated you. Much as I want to chop you to pieces, I admire your tactics."

She paused, then added: "Let's cut to the chase: under what conditions will you return the Yang Gu?"

Fang Yuan chuckled. Bai Ning Bing initiating the talk proved she had lost her calm—Shang Xin Ci's revelation had shaken her, creating an opportunity.

Fang Yuan said: "Assist me to rank five peak stage, and I will return the Yang Gu."

"Screw you!" Bai Ning Bing snorted derisively. "You think I'm an idiot like Shang Ya Zi? Rank five peak stage—the nerve of you!"

"Don't be ungrateful, Bai Ning Bing. Without my Yin Gu, you'd have died on Qing Mao Mountain long ago," Fang Yuan countered coldly.

Bai Ning Bing retorted sharply: "Hmph, if I hadn't blocked First Generation Gu Yue's strike, you would have died first!"

A moment later, Bai Ning Bing broke the silence: "What are your next plans?"

"We will stay in Shang clan city for a few years to assemble matched sets of Gu worms. Only a complete set unlocks our true strength," Fang Yuan replied.

Bai Ning Bing snorted: "Rank four peak stage at most. For my efforts on Bai Gu Mountain, you must give me half the primeval stones."

Fang Yuan nodded: "Okay, let's hi-five as a promise."

Bai Ning Bing glared at him furiously: "You think I'm a fool? High five?! We use poison vow Gu—understand?!"

Fang Yuan nodded: "No problem, poison vow Gu it is."

Fang Yuan added: "Future gains will be split thirty-seventy in my favor. For any Gu, I have first pick and will compensate you with primeval stones. This is my baseline—take it or leave it."

"So be it." Bai Ning Bing agreed immediately.

After purchasing a poison vow Gu and a primeval elder Gu, they established their new vow. Per their agreement, Fang Yuan transferred half of his nine hundred-odd thousand primeval stones into Bai Ning Bing's primeval elder Gu.

"Now, will you finally help me break through to rank three?" Fang Yuan asked solemnly.

"Sure, I'll help you advance to rank three today," she agreed.

# Chapter 273: Advance to Rank Three

Inside the room, Bai Ning Bing pressed her palms against Fang Yuan's back, channeling primeval essence into his aperture through the Bone Flesh Unity Gu.

Once converted by the Bone Flesh Unity Gu, the essence became Fang Yuan's to command.

Fang Yuan directed the abundant snow-silver primeval essence to batter his aperture walls.

At rank two peak stage, his primeval essence was deep red, while his aperture walls were transparent crystal.

Under the barrage of snow-silver essence, the tough crystal walls began cracking.

The crystal wall shattered, sending fragments plunging into the primeval sea.

A brand new white light wall, replaced the crystal wall.

Concurrently, a wisp of light-silver primeval essence rose from the sea depths.

Fang Yuan had finally broken through rank two peak stage to reach rank three.

"In under a year since leaving Qing Mao Mountain, I've regained rank three. With A-grade aptitude, my prospects are bright," Fang Yuan exulted, clenching his fist.

Examining his aperture, he evaluated his Gu worms. "Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus must be kept, though I lack recipes to advance it. It works for now, but by rank four its utility will dwindle."

"Blood Skull Gu is useless to me now; nurturing descendants takes too much time and effort. First Generation Gu Yue only did so out of desperation. I can change out this Gu; it is an inheritance of the blood sea ancestor. Perhaps I could trade it in Shang clan's treasure realm... I have to plan this carefully."

"Spring Autumn Cicada, Primeval Elder Gu, and Bone Flesh Unity Gu must stay. Blood Moon Gu is easy to feed but lacks power and synergy. Tusita Flower should also be replaced for better options. Breath Concealment Gu can be replaced, and Jumping Grass must be removed—it was only an emergency backup."

"Besides these, I need strength, investigative, and movement Gu. With our situation stable and Shang clan's trust earned, I can explore the gambling dens."

His Gu had been gathered piecemeal, lacking synergy and comprehensive coverage, which severely hampered his combat power.

Shang clan city offered him time to grow. He had to assemble a cohesive Gu set within three years and maximize his cultivation.

Afterward, the Three Kings Inheritance would open, followed by the righteous-demonic war at Yi Tian Mountain across Southern Border. Without sufficient strength in that clash of titans, one would merely be cannon fodder. If qualified to participate, his rebirth advantage would yield immense rewards.

"This subordinate greets Lord Clan Leader!" Wei Yang knelt in the study.

Shang Yan Fei stopped writing, looking up: "Wei Yang, there is only the two of us here, do not be restrained."

"Thank you, Clan Leader." Wei Yang took a seat nearby.

Shang Yan Fei shifted the topic: "I initially assumed Bai Ning Bing was a woman disguised as a man, given how many clans favor male leaders. Yet Doctor Su Shou told me she inquired about the Yin Yang rotation Gu, hinting at a deeper story. Still, whether male or female is an unimportant detail. What matters is whether she and Fang Zheng will serve our Shang clan."

Wei Yang was enlightened: "This subordinate understands."

Snatching the inheritance from Bai clan and safely escorting Shang Xin Ci proved Fang and Bai possessed both brains and brawn.

With their fine aptitude, reports confirmed they were rank three.

Yet Shang Yan Fei wanted to recruit them not for himself, but for Shang Xin Ci.

Opening his eyes, he vanished from the study in a pillar of flame.

When he reappeared, he was at a huge walkway.

Before long, he reached the end of the walkway.

A giant red door was in front of him.

Over thirty meters tall, the door matched the passageway's height. It bore no handle, only the carving of a colossal human face.

Standing before the red door, Shang Yan Fei said exasperatedly: "Living treasure door, quit playing asleep. You pull this trick every time."

The door's carved face snapped open its eyes and grumbled: "Sigh, Little Fei Fei, you've grown up and aren't cute anymore!"

Shang Yan Fei's eyelid twitched: "Cut the nonsense. I'm here to exchange for treasure."

# Chapter 274: Living Treasure Door

"Exchange treasure?" The face teased: "Little Fei Fei, taking advantage of me again? As the treasure realm guardian, I won't help you steal from within."

"Oh, then let's do that." The living treasure door's face turned solemn: "I can exchange treasures, but I have a condition..."

"Ah, looking at your expression, you've guessed it! That's right, I want you to dig my nose!" the living treasure door screamed excitedly.

Veins popped on Shang Yan Fei's forehead: "Scoundrel, you're trying this again?"

"My nose is really itchy, I can't breathe properly. Having no hands, I cannot dig it myself like you humans. I am so pitiful. Ah... Little Fei Fei, my good friend, please dig my nose. As a reward, I will give you my treasured booger." The living treasure door sang out poetically.

"Alright, stop chanting, I'll do it, okay?" Shang Yan Fei shouted, completely helpless inside.

After all, the treasure realm was left by Shang clan's ancestors, the work of a Gu Immortal beyond rank five.

His expression freezing as his eye twitched, Shang Yan Fei extended his right hand.

On his right palm was a tiny, blood-red palm-shaped tattoo.

Activating his primeval essence, the blood tattoo flew out and turned into a giant, bright-red hand.

Rank five — Blood Handprint Gu!

Whatever this palm struck would turn into a puddle of blood. Shang Yan Fei used it to dominate Southern Border and establish his fierce reputation.

The bright-red palm formed a fist, extended its index finger, and entered the living treasure door's nostril.

"Ah... ah... ahhh... CHOO!" The living treasure door sneezed loudly.

Shang Yan Fei collected the expelled treasures. Shang Yan Fei sighed: "Can we get to business now?"

"Of course! What does Little Fei Fei want?" The living treasure door's mood had improved significantly.

"I recall there's still a rank five man triumphing heaven Gu. I want to exchange for it," Shang Yan Fei replied.

The living treasure door looked curious: "Man triumphing heaven Gu can defy heaven and change fate, forcefully awakening an aperture for mortals without cultivation talent. If you want to exchange for this Gu, you must offer one of even greater value to the treasure realm."

This Gu was like a baby's palm, tender, plump, and crystal clear, with red veins emitting a ghostly aura.

Seeing this Gu, the living treasure door exclaimed: "Eh, giving up the blood handprint Gu? Little Fei Fei, isn't this one of your best Gu? Weren't you collecting the Blood Sea inheritances? Have you given up your childhood aspiration?"

Shang Yan Fei sighed helplessly: "Blood Sea inheritances are countless, yet the true ones are hidden among them, making the difficulty immense. Even with Shang clan's resources, after all these years I've only obtained two. I want the man triumphing heaven Gu, but unable to part with my other precious Gu, I must temporarily sacrifice the blood handprint Gu. Someday, I may redeem it."

"Okay then, since you've made your decision." Living treasure door opened its mouth, and swallowed the blood handprint Gu.""

With a loud sound, the blood handprint Gu entered the treasure realm.

Next, it regurgitated, and opened its mouth, throwing up one Gu.

Resembling a thousand-year ginseng with many roots and a dim yellow body, the Gu's upper half looked like a torso while the lower half diverged like legs.

This was the man triumphing heaven Gu.

"Refine it quickly! Once outside the treasure realm, this Gu will regain its wild nature in three breaths, making it far harder to refine," the living treasure door urged.

Shang Yan Fei nodded, and activated his purple crystal primeval essence, instantly refining this man triumphing heaven Gu.

"Hehehe, with this Gu, I can awaken Ci Er's aperture! Now I just need a few more Gu to raise her aptitude and aid her cultivation. Then I can recruit Fang and Bai as her wings. Ci Er, I will ensure your happiness, but in this world, strength is the prerequisite to all happiness!" Shang Yan Fei stored the man triumphing heaven Gu, emotions surging.

At another feast filled with fine wine and delicious food, the atmosphere was lively.

"Brother Wei, Brother Xiao Yan, come, another glass!" Fang Yuan stood and shouted, raising his cup, while Bai Ning Bing ate emotionlessly.

"To think Brother Fang Zheng saved Shang clan leader's precious daughter. *Good people truly get rewarded*. A purple thorn token, hehe, I'm extremely envious." Cheerful and honest, Xiao Yan didn't hide his envy at all.

Wei Yang put down his glass: "What are the two of you going to do from now on?"

Fang Yuan answered: "We plan to stay in Shang clan city a while to sell our useless Gu, then buy a set of complementary Gu worms."

"Excellent, that is the proper thing to do." Wei Yang gave a thumbs-up, secretly pleased by their plan to stay.

"If you don't mind, tell us your plans. Big Brother Wei is knowledgeable and can give great advice—he recommended my fire Gu path," Xiao Yan added from the side.

# Chapter 275: Strength Path and Ice Path

"Please advise me brother Wei." At the feast, Fang Yuan cupped his fists.

"Among the five mortal ranks, ranks one and two are beginners, while ranks four and five are high-level forces. Rank three connects the two. As full-fledged Gu Masters, this is the crucial stage where you choose your path and build your foundation."

"At rank three, a Gu Master's strength is formidable, often making them clan elders. At this stage, one must choose their direction and build a set of Gu worms along that path. For a future in Gu refinement, you can adapt existing recipes into new ones. Those who plot can gather thought-assisting Gu, like lightning thoughts Gu or immortal showing the way Gu."

Wei Yang paused and looked at Fang and Bai: "Have you two thought about your advancement direction?"

"Attack," Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing answered simultaneously without hesitation.

He continued: "Then next, you must select Gu worms to form a set, chaining into a unified system."

"Since you both chose attack, an attack-type Gu will naturally be your core, supported by your other Gu worms. After that, fire source Gu to reduce primeval essence expenditure, and lastly fire discharge Gu for movement," Wei Yang said, patting Xiao Yan's shoulder.

Wei Yang turned to Fang Yuan: "Your core Gu is the entire set's foundation. Have you decided which attack-type Gu to use?"

Fang Yuan clenched his fist: "I've already used many Gu to raise my strength. Strength is my advantage; I want to continue along it and crush foes with overwhelming force."

Bai Ning Bing named a Gu: "Frost demon Gu."

Wei Yang frowned upon hearing this: "Strength and ice paths... Ice path's advantage lies in defense. Frost demon Gu, snow fairy Gu, and ice crystal Gu are the three most famous transformation-type Gu. Frost demon Gu and snow fairy Gu suit women, while ice crystal Gu is better suited for men."

"As for Fang Zheng's strength path..." Wei Yang shook his head. "I'm not trying to discourage you, but that won't work. The strength path is the most common choice, but why? Because it's cheap and affordable. Most strength-path Gu are cheap, but because of this, its Gu Masters remain ordinary and mediocre."

"Honestly, I tried the strength path before, but later wasted many primeval stones purging the added strength from my body. Demonic Gu Masters only use it as a stepping stone due to lack of resources. Brother Fang Zheng, you have a purple thorn token and hundreds of thousands of primeval stones. You don't need this narrow path—the strength path makes success hard to achieve!"

Hearing Wei Yang's words, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing exchanged glances and nodded.

"Brother Wei makes sense, but I still choose the strength path," Fang Yuan cupped his fists.

"Yes, I've decided to forgo frost demon Gu and choose ice crystal Gu!" Bai Ning Bing declared beside him.

"Huh..." Wei Yang's smile froze on his face.

"Hey, did you even listen to what he said?" Xiao Yan shouted.

Bai Ning Bing smiled as determination shone across her eyes.

*I chose ice crystal Gu precisely because it's suited for men, heh.*

Fang Yuan rubbed his nose: "I appreciate Brother Wei's advice, but I still prefer the strength path. Others may fail, but if I succeed, wouldn't that prove my superiority? Hahaha, accomplishment only comes from facing difficulty. I was hesitant before, but hearing Brother Wei's words, I've definitely made up my mind!"

Choosing the strength path was part of Fang Yuan's plans involving the Three Kings Inheritance years later.

"Say no more, Brother Wei. Paths are forged by man, and my decision is final. Hahaha, let's keep drinking!" Fang Yuan's determination forced Wei Yang to swallow his words.

After the banquet, once Wei Yang and Xiao Yan departed, Fang and Bai headed to the Gu shop.

Presenting their purple thorn token, Bai Ning Bing spent twenty-eight thousand primeval stones on the rank three ice crystal Gu.

Fang Yuan chose a rank two brown bear innate strength Gu, spending four thousand one hundred primeval stones.

Outside the shop, Fang Yuan frowned: "You really intend to make the ice crystal Gu your vital Gu?"

Bai Ning Bing shook her head grimly: "I don't intend to advance to rank four. Just yesterday, my aptitude rose another one percent."

Fang Yuan paused: "Then you can't survive without my Yang Gu. Hehehe, maybe check Feng Yu Manor. They specialize in selling information and might have what you need."

"You're not coming with me?" Bai Ning Bing paused, looking at Fang Yuan.

"I'm heading to the gambling den," Fang Yuan chuckled.

"Alright."

Seeing Bai Ning Bing walk away, Fang Yuan's smile vanished.

Returning to the shop, he asked: "Shopkeeper, do you have these items I forgot to buy?"

He gave a list.

Not daring to keep a purple thorn token holder waiting, the shopkeeper quickly checked the list and nodded: "Yes, esteemed guest, we have them all."

"Now, let's see that sealed legendary Gu fossil." Guided by memory, Fang Yuan entered an extravagant gambling den.

# Chapter 276: Stellar Stone

It was a black rock.

This was a stellar stone.

Unlike the surrounding stellar stones, this one was cubic, resembling a brick.

Yet this rock, buried in a pile, held the legendary Gu that would later turn an unknown lucky person into an envied, renowned strength-path Gu Master.

Observing the surroundings, Fang Yuan instantly understood why no one cared about this rock.

"Hehehe, who could guess that the stellar stone propping up this counter leg hides a legendary Gu worm?" Fang Yuan chuckled inwardly.

A down-and-out Gu Master, came here, and tripped at this counter.

Thus, the Gu Master bought the rock and dissected it publicly.

And then, the legendary Gu appeared in this world.

"Dear customer, is this your first time here?" A clerk approached seeing Fang Yuan's dazed look, unaware he was eyeing the rock from the corner of his eye.

Fang Yuan rubbed his nose: "Yeah, I suppose." Excluding his past life, this was his second time in a gambling den.

The clerk's smile broadened: "Please come inside! Honestly, rocks here are the cheapest, but cheap goods aren't quality. What could you get from them? Allow me to introduce our better stock, customer."

"Oh? Go ahead." Fang Yuan raised a brow and followed the clerk inside.

"Customer, do you know how gambling rocks form?" the clerk asked, glancing back as he led the way.

Before Fang Yuan could reply, the clerk answered himself: "As the saying goes, *Man is the spirit of all living beings, Gu is the essence of heaven and earth*. Humans possess great inspiration to innovate. With supreme intelligence, humans can create new Gu worms not found in nature. Meanwhile, Gu have little intelligence, but their tiny bodies contain law fragments of the Great Dao."

"Heaven and earth always leave a chance of survival; injured or starving Gu often hibernate. Over time, rock layers accumulate around them, growing thicker until they gain substantial mass. People harvest these fossils—that is the origin of the gambling rocks you see before you."

The clerk said: "Since you wish to gamble, customer, you shouldn't be rash. Though a gambling den, we don't scam clients; we want you gambling informed and with peace of mind. Here, we sell old man stones, pushing cart stones, stellar stones, and cold ice stones. Each type is divided into five grades by quality, priced from over a hundred thousand down to a few primeval stones."

"The dissection method must be appropriate: magnetism for stellar stones, acid for old man stones."

"Any Gu inside a gambling rock is extremely weak. Using the wrong excavation method would deal a fatal blow, and it'd be too late then."

"Customer, if you choose high or super-grade rocks, dissection is free. For low or medium-grade, you must pay yourself. For scrap grade, hiring a master is a waste, as his fee far exceeds the rock's price."

After browsing, Fang Yuan selected several low-grade rocks: a few old man stones, pushing cart stones, and stellar stones.

Seeing this meager selection after so long, the clerk asked listlessly: "Customer, do you have a token? Yellow pear tokens and above receive discounts. Please show it if you have one."

Moments later, his jaw dropped and his eyes nearly popped out in shock.

"Purple thorn token? Oh my god!"

# Chapter 277: Vigilance and Endurance

Recovering his senses after a long while, he said respectfully: "Esteemed guest, forgive my blindness! Please wait while I fetch the shopkeeper."

The shopkeeper, a plump middle-aged man, rushed in flustered upon hearing a purple thorn token holder had arrived.

Pointing at his chosen rocks, Fang Yuan said indifferently: "I've selected these rocks; I'll trouble you to dissect them."

Looking at the rocks, the shopkeeper was stupefied—how could they all be low-grade?

The shopkeeper bowed: "Purple thorn token holders receive free rock dissection. With your discount, these rocks total six hundred and fifty primeval stones."

"I know the rules—pay first." Fang Yuan nodded, taking primeval stones from the primeval elder Gu.

The shopkeeper swiftly took the stones and told the clerk: "Quickly summon Masters Duan, Huang, Zhang, Zhao, and Ma."

He then turned towards Fang Yuan: "Esteemed guest, rock dissection counter is inside, let's wait there."

The five masters hurried from the courtyard onto the platform.

Accompanied by the shopkeeper, Fang Yuan stood near the counter with the crowd, watching from a distance to keep a low profile.

Solemnly placing the rocks onto the stone platform, the five masters summoned their respective Gu worms and began dissecting.
