The results flabbergasted everyone. The low-grade rocks were either solid or hollow inside, containing not even a dead Gu.

The shopkeeper paled. Normally delighted when gamblers found nothing, he now desperately wished he could stuff Gu worms into the rocks himself.

Fang Yuan smiled: "No matter. My luck is poor today, so I will stop here and return another time."

The shopkeeper and clerk respectfully escorted Fang Yuan to the door.

Fang Yuan's purpose in coming here today was only to probe.

Having located his target, he had achieved his goal. But he could not take the stellar stone directly; he needed an indirect approach.

Lacking dissection Gu himself, he had to rely on the den's masters to retrieve the Gu within the stellar stone.

Having them dissect it publicly would cause a commotion, while using his purple thorn token for a private dissection would only arouse suspicion.

Obtaining a legendary Gu right from the start would seem far too lucky.

Given Shang clan's vast reach, Shang Yan Fei's investigation likely uncovered Fang Yuan's earlier success winning the mudskin toad in Jia clan's caravan.

With rock gambling's enormous profits, if Shang Yan Fei suspected Fang Yuan could inspect rocks, he would surely be tempted to act.

Fang Yuan had no such ability, but certain Gu on him could never be exposed.

Especially Spring Autumn Cicada.

On Shang Liang Mountain, Shang Yan Fei was not the main threat—the rank six Gu Masters were.

"I will stroll the rock gambling zone for a few days and buy rocks to establish an impression."

Fang Yuan schemed as he walked back to Nan Qiu garden.

Bai Ning Bing was already back from Feng Yu Manor.

"I have been waiting." She handed the ice crystal Gu to Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan used Spring Autumn Cicada's aura to refine it, then tossed it back.

Bai Ning Bing helped him cultivate while he helped her refine Gu—one of their poison vow terms.

Bai Ning Bing took the Gu and entered a secret room to refine it.

Fang Yuan went to another secret room and began refining.

The name of the Gu — Eating one's words.

# Chapter 278: What naivety!

Fang Yuan sat cross-legged on his mat, beginning his refinement.

He had purchased materials for the eating one's words Gu, mixing in extraneous items to conceal the recipe.

Injecting light silver primeval essence, Fang Yuan caused the smelly fart Gu to swell and float.

"Rice bag, go." At Fang Yuan's command, a seed flew from his aperture.

The seed took root, sprouting dark green vines from the rotting mud as his primeval sea depleted.

Leaving them inside, Fang Yuan poured rice grains into the mud, lowered the smelly fart Gu, and activated both Gu simultaneously.

The smelly fart Gu glowed orange while the rice bag grass shone green, eventually fusing together.

The fused light dimmed as it sank into the urn.

The urn vibrated violently, then quieted as a new Gu flew out—yellow-brown like mud, exuding a slight fecal stench.

This was the defecate Gu.

Next, Fang Yuan took out the big strength Gu.

Fusing the big strength Gu and defecate Gu, he successfully refined the big strength defecate Gu.

Next, Fang Yuan took out the lion roar Gu.

Fang Yuan used both the lion roar Gu and big strength defecate Gu.

A bizarre scene unfolded: the phantom lion head opened its maw and began eating the big strength defecate Gu.

After devouring a third, the lion head stalled, on the verge of collapse.

Fang Yuan then summoned a purchased paper crane Gu.

The light blue paper crane flew toward the lion phantom and was swallowed.

Swallowing the paper crane Gu, the lion phantom stabilized.

Fang Yuan willed it to resume eating the big strength defecate Gu.

Fang Yuan then used the third pink paper crane Gu.

Upon swallowing it, the lion phantom shrank by half, its white form glowing light blue, dark green, and pink as it chanted: "Eat shit, eat shit..."

This was the eating one's words Gu!

However, the eating one's words Gu would self-destruct after only ten breaths.

Wasting no time, Fang Yuan summoned the Gu to his side.

Bathed in tricolor light, Fang Yuan's body began emitting faint purple smoke.

As the lion roared, the swirling smoke condensed into phantom images of two poison vow Gu within two breaths.

At Fang Yuan's command, the lion swallowed one. Chewed up, the poison vow phantom gave an indignant cry and vanished.

The lion then swallowed the second poison vow Gu, eliminating it completely and leaving no trace of purple smoke.

Moments later, the lion phantom burst into a tricolor rain of light, illuminating the secret room.

Standing amid the sparkling light, Fang Yuan smiled coldly.

No one could blame them; in his previous life, eating one's words Gu was only invented two centuries later.

With five centuries of future knowledge, bypassing the poison vow Gu was simple.

Freed from restraint, Fang Yuan felt relaxed. Crucially, having removed only his side of the vows, Shang Ya Zi and Bai Ning Bing remained bound.

Fang Yuan now held complete advantage over both of them.

"Narrow-minded Shang Ya Zi will definitely seek revenge after being extorted. But I have plans for that; I'll let him play around for now. As for Bai Ning Bing, now dancing in the palm of my hand, I can make good use of you for now—no need to fall out yet."

"After giving away half my stones, buying Gu, hosting the banquet, renting Nan Qiu garden, gambling, and feeding Gu, I only have four hundred and twenty thousand left."

To assemble a full set of Gu, his current wealth was far from enough.

"Planning to stay two or three years, four hundred and twenty thousand won't last even one. Higher ranks require vastly more resources, plus the gambling den expenses—I must find a way to earn money."

Combining past and present experiences, Fang Yuan already had an idea to earn primeval stones.

That would be the battle stage.

# Chapter 279: Attacking just like that?!

Wei Yang was distressed, nursing a headache for days.

Shang Yan Fei wanted to recruit Fang and Bai, but despite Wei Yang's probing, neither showed any interest.

"Cultivation relies entirely on resources! Easy for righteous Gu Masters, but for demonic ones... hehe."

Wei Yang was once a demonic Gu Master, fighting his way out of the battle stage.

"Once Fang and Bai realize their individual limits, Shang clan can recruit them. But I cannot let Fang Zheng squander his youth and talent on the wrong path!"

To Wei Yang, Fang Yuan choosing the strength path was a huge mistake.

Wei Yang had advised Fang Yuan repeatedly, only to be firmly rebuffed each time.

This day, he came again.

"Brother Wei, you want me to enter the battle stage?" Fang Yuan feigned confusion.

"Yes! I cultivated step by step through the battle stage, and I want to share that experience with you," Wei Yang said sincerely.

Raising three fingers, he continued: "The battle stage offers three major benefits."

"First, fortune. Every victory earns primeval stones, scaling with the size of the audience."

"Second, Gu worms. The victor is permitted to claim a Gu from the loser."

"Third, combat experience. Battles hone tactics and test your Gu worms' synergy, helping you make adjustments."

Fang Yuan understood Wei Yang's underlying motive immediately.

Having failed to dissuade him directly from the strength path, Wei Yang wanted the battle stage's harsh reality to expose its shortcomings and force him to switch.

"My choice of the strength path was carefully planned for the future—how could I change it? Still, the battle stage fits my plans..."

Thinking so, Fang Yuan nodded, agreeing with Wei Yang.

Delighted, Wei Yang said: "Our previous fight was informal. Shang clan's battle stage has an established system—you must register to participate. Follow me!"

Thus, the two came to fifth inner city's battle zone.

Far more crowded than the third inner city, the bustling streets and clamor assaulted them with an intense atmosphere.

Disguised, Wei Yang led Fang Yuan, explaining: "You need thirty net victories in the fifth inner city to advance to the fourth, and eighty more to reach the third. Net victories mean total wins minus losses—ten wins and two losses equal eight net victories."

"The stages have three tiers: low in fifth, middle in fourth, and high in third inner city. As a newcomer with zero wins, you start in fifth."

Wei Yang led him into a grand hall.

Inside, numerous Gu Masters were registering for matches or checking match details.

Registering under Gu Yue Fang Zheng, Fang Yuan paid five hundred primeval stones to receive a vine information Gu.

"The battle stage has few rules and great freedom. Simply register when you want to fight, and Shang clan will assign an opponent. They may be stronger or weaker. You can also challenge specific opponents if they agree, plus you get one mandatory challenge per month that cannot be refused."

"However, you can only issue one challenge daily, and you must fight at least once every ten days, or lose a win. Accumulating five total losses revokes your qualification, requiring re-registration. Interested in giving it a try?" Wei Yang smiled.

Fang Yuan nodded.

At the efficient VIP reception, Fang Yuan was swiftly assigned a match: "1:30 PM, stage five, grassland terrain."

Only seven minutes remained before the match.

Wei Yang brought Fang Yuan to stage five, where his opponent already waited.

His opponent was a tall, skinny youth of plain looks wearing a green shirt.

Fang Yuan stepped onto stage five, a medium-sized arena more than twice the size of their previous flagstone stage.

*Clang!*

The bells rang loudly, signifying the start of the battle.

"I am Tang Qing," the polite young man said, cupping his fists toward Fang Yuan.

Jumping grass.

Fang Yuan willed, stepping hard and bursting forth.

"What the f*ck, attacking just like that?!" Taken aback, Tang Qing never expected this youngster to shamelessly launch a sneak attack.

He completely ignored the formalities!

# Chapter 280: Sword Shadow Gu (Two chapters in one)

Fang Yuan moved like lightning, pouncing on Tang Qing.

Wind howled. Tang Qing's vision went dark before Fang Yuan's fist slammed into his chest.

The left side of his chest had caved in two inches, exposing torn flesh and a broken pale rib cage.

Fang Yuan stepped before Tang Qing, raising his leg to stomp harshly between Tang Qing's legs.

Squinting through blurred vision, Tang Qing's heart trembled as Fang Yuan activated canopy Gu, covering himself in white armor.

A rank three Gu Master!

He subconsciously activated his defense Gu, surrounding his body with green wind.

Fang Yuan's heavy kick was meant to smash Tang Qing's groin into paste, but the wind greatly reduced its impact.

Eyes cold, Fang Yuan clenched his left fist and punched.

A fist covered in white light slammed into Tang Qing's face.

Tang Qing's screams abruptly stopped as he fell backward, his head hitting the ground.

The Gu Master hosting the battle rapidly came over.

Seeing Tang Qing's caved-in face and the brain matter and blood oozing from his cracked skull, he abandoned rescue efforts.

Fang Yuan shrugged: "It is just a life, why freak out? By battle stage rules, I killed him, so his possessions are mine, right?"

The host Gu Master snorted: "All his things are yours, but we must retrieve the vine information Gu. Young man, disrespecting life is dangerous!"

Fang Yuan handed over the vine information Gu; the host edited the record and returned it.

Fang Yuan inspected the Gu, and his zero wins record had become one.

Searching Tang Qing's corpse, he obtained four ordinary Gu—three rank two and one rank one—worth around two thousand primeval stones.

Tang Qing had died too abruptly to self-detonate his Gu worms.

"Brother Fang Zheng, try to avoid killing in the future," Wei Yang advised after leaving the battle stage.

"Even though the battle stage allows killing..." Wei Yang shook his head. "There is no need to fight to the death every round. Vast as the battle zone is, you will eventually meet a stronger opponent. We interact often living in Shang clan city. When it is possible to spare a life, one should show mercy."

Fang Yuan frowned slightly: "Sparing someone doesn't mean they will spare me. Demonic Gu Masters I met attack without hesitation. If I don't strike, I will be assaulted. My experience taught me to kill when possible, lest I breed threats. Giving enemies no chance is how I survived until today."

"Little brother Fang Zheng, you mentioned using several Gu to modify your body and increase strength. Exactly how many did you use?"

"Since Brother Wei asks, I have used three: black and white boar Gu, crocodile strength Gu, and the brown bear innate strength Gu I just bought," Fang Yuan smiled lightly.

Wei Yang asked: "You possess the strength of two boars, one crocodile, plus your own. Yet your first punch only dented his chest and broke ribs, and your third merely cracked his skull. What does that say?"

Before Fang Yuan replied, he continued: "Punches and kicks only unleash a fraction of one's strength. Out of two boars and a crocodile's strength, what percentage can you actually use? That is the strength path's greatest weakness! A person's strength is like a full vat; in battle, you can only use a portion of that water."

"Black and white boar Gu, crocodile strength Gu, and brown bear innate strength Gu permanently increase strength, but are expensive. Investing heavily for barely ten percent returns is inefficient. That money would yield far greater results elsewhere."

"I see, Brother Wei is trying to persuade me to abandon the strength path," Fang Yuan smiled, seemingly enlightened.

"I understand that human bodies differ from beasts and can rarely display their true power. But strength path Gu Masters who achieve harmony with their Gu can unleash beast phantoms, displaying the full power of the Gu worm."

"Hehehe, indeed. During my time in the battle stage, I met strength path Gu Masters whose proficiency allowed beast phantoms to appear, unleashing the Gu's full, threatening strength. But that happens far too rarely. Moreover, the attack method is rigid; only specific attack styles summon the beast phantom, making it easy to evade," Wei Yang advised painstakingly.

Fang Yuan pondered; he knew this well, which was precisely why he needed that legendary Gu.

Aloud, he said: "Brother Wei, I appreciate your concern, but I have chosen the strength path and want to see it through."

"Sigh, then do as you wish." Seeing Fang Yuan's obstinacy, Wei Yang could not force him.

Soon, half a month passed.

Inside the gambling den, the shopkeeper bowed beside Fang Yuan, smiling.

"Brother Wei, care to pick a few rocks? We might find something good. My treat!" Fang Yuan smiled.

Wei Yang shook his head: "Today Tang Xiong forcefully challenges you. To think Tang Qing was his brother. Be vigilant, Fang Zheng—Tang Xiong deliberately dropped from the fourth to fifth inner city for revenge. He is somewhat famous, wielding the strength of three bears, and comes seeking vengeance."

"I'll deal with it when it comes. If Brother Wei won't choose, I will. I've already spotted several good ones." Fang Yuan's eyes gleamed as he selected gambling rocks.

The shopkeeper instructed clerks to carefully retrieve the chosen gambling rocks.

"Eh?" Fang Yuan pointed to the counter leg. "Is that supporting rock a stellar stone?"

The stunned shopkeeper quickly replied: "Esteemed customer has sharp eyes. A gambler damaged the counter leg years ago, so I wedged a squarish stellar stone beneath it to fill the gap."

Fang Yuan frowned: "Rocks are meant for gambling! You are burying its potential. I shall take that as my final piece today."

At the dissection counter, young masters were slowly and delicately opening rocks for a middle-aged Gu Master.

Thinking of the legendary Gu he was about to obtain, even spending five hundred thousand was worth it.

Though rank three, this legendary Gu possessed amazing effects and was nearly extinct.

Wei Yang frowned: "Mind the time, we must reach the battle stage soon. Shopkeeper, can they dissect faster?"

"My rocks..." The middle aged Gu Master cried out.

"Li Ran, stop screaming, we'll refund your primeval stones," the shopkeeper shouted.

The middle-aged Gu Master with a messy beard glared indignantly: "What if there was a Gu inside?"

The shopkeeper laughed in disdain, waving him off: "Come on Li Ran, you've gambled for years only picking low-grade rocks. When have you ever gotten anything good? Stop making a din, or forfeit your compensation!"

"*Ptu*, such a big shop bullying customers! Look down on me now, but one day I'll strike it rich!" the middle-aged Gu Master muttered furiously, though he stopped arguing.

"Li Ran..." This name made Fang Yuan recall a certain memory.

Li Ran was the man who obtained the legendary Gu, unless they merely shared a name.

Regardless, Fang Yuan had snatched Li Ran's one chance at success.

The five old masters took the stage, starting to dissect the rocks.

Over ten rocks—scrap, low, and middle grade—were swiftly opened.

"There is a Gu, the sword shadow Gu!"

The old masters cupped their fists at Fang Yuan, sighing in relief.

The shopkeeper ran over joyfully: "Esteemed customer, what a profit! Sword shadow Gu sells for thirty-two thousand primeval stones, yet you spent only eight thousand!"

"Well done, brother. The sword shadow Gu matches my broadsword light Gu in quality. I advise keeping it for yourself," Wei Yang congratulated.

"Haha, light at the end of the tunnel! I told you my luck wasn't bad," Fang Yuan laughed. "Brother Wei, let me buy you drinks after today's match to celebrate."

Wei Yang nodded in agreement, unaware that beneath Fang Yuan's cheerful facade lay complete shock!

The sword shadow Gu was an accident, it was not his goal.

Dissecting the counter's supporting stellar stone revealed no Gu—it was completely useless!

Events had defied Fang Yuan's expectations. The supposedly guaranteed Gu was missing, and the lucky sword shadow Gu could not cover his recent losses.

"Without that legendary Gu, my efforts are in vain. Damn it, how did this happen? Rumors might have errors, but they travel for a reason. Every specific detail matched the rumors, so why..."

"Must I really change my cultivation path? That would severely impact the three kings inheritance!"

"It's almost time, we should head to the battle stage," Wei Yang reminded.

# Chapter 281: Bear Strength Phantom

Stepping onto the battle stage, Fang Yuan cast all stray thoughts from his mind.

Shirtless and barefoot, Tang Xiong stood eight feet tall with broad shoulders, a thick waist, and massive arms.

"Little scoundrel, you killed my brother? Today, I'll send you to join him!" Tang Xiong glared with bloodshot eyes, burning with hatred.

Disguised in the crowd, Wei Yang watched the battle stage intently. A rank two peak stage strength path expert wielding the strength of three bears, Tang Xiong was a formidable opponent.

A clear ring of the bell announced the start of the match.

Tang Xiong roared and charged straight at Fang Yuan like a wild bull.

Fang Yuan smiled coldly and charged head-on to meet him.

They collided violently, the tremendous impact sending both flying backward.

Forced back six steps, Tang Xiong looked shocked. *'How can this kid have such great strength?'*

Fang Yuan fell back three steps as his protective white light armor shook.

Fang Yuan shook his numb arm indifferently; the result was within his expectations.

Fang Yuan possessed two boars and a crocodile's strength, augmented recently by brown bear innate strength Gu, while Tang Xiong only had the strength of two bears.

Grand bear Gu!

Tang Xiong roared as his body swelled, temporarily gaining another bear's strength.

Bear palm Gu!

Yellow light covered his hands and feet, expanding them into massive bear paws.

Leaping with ferocious strength, he swung his right palm down at Fang Yuan.

Calmly, Fang Yuan clenched his left fist and punched directly into the bear palm.

Fang Yuan met offense with offense; fists and palms collided repeatedly with booming impacts, howling with wind.

A fierce bear's roar resounded throughout the battle stage.

A black bear phantom appeared behind Tang Xiong, roaring skyward with a blood-red maw.

Tang Xiong had luckily triggered a beast phantom.

An otherwise ordinary strike now carried the full strength of a bear!

Unable to dodge in time, Fang Yuan raised his arms to block.

Sent flying with a loud crash, his white light armor flickered rapidly and dimmed.

His arms were numb and would not be usable for some time.

Stabilizing himself midair with a twist of his waist, he landed firmly on his feet.

Upon landing, he activated canopy Gu; the white light armor reformed weak and translucent, its defense greatly diminished.

"I have two boars and a crocodile's strength; boar strength favors charges and crocodile strength favors bites, but I haven't acquired bear strength yet. I cannot manifest a beast phantom with fists or palms. Furthermore, canopy Gu defends poorly against melee strikes and will shatter after one or two more hits from the bear phantom."

Spring-like grass grew from his soles, piercing through his boots.

As Tang Xiong charged, Fang Yuan stamped the ground and leaped away like a frog.

At the same time, his left palm swiped at Tang Xiong's direction.

A scarlet moonblade shot from his palm and struck Tang Xiong, making his defensive light flicker.

Though not overwhelmingly powerful, blood moon Gu was still rank three.

Fang Yuan kept throwing scarlet moonblades from afar, refusing to engage Tang Xiong in close combat.

Fang Yuan kited and attacked; Tang Xiong's weak ranged methods posed no threat.

Time passed by, both their primeval essences continually fell.

Fang Yuan's rank three light silver essence easily outlasted Tang Xiong's rank two red steel essence.

After suffering five or six wounds from Fang Yuan's blood moon Gu, Tang Xiong had to surrender as his subpar healing Gu could not stem the bleeding.

"Just wait, one day, I will smack you into minced meat!" Clutching his wounds, Tang Xiong walked off the stage.

Securing his third victory, Fang Yuan claimed the grand bear Gu from Tang Xiong per the rules.

However, Fang Yuan was not happy.

If the legendary Gu wasn't in the counter's stellar stone, where was it?

"Without the legendary Gu, I hold no advantage in the strength path. It thrived in ancient times, but is now a mere shadow of its former glory. To pursue the strength path without the legendary Gu, I would need an ancient strength path inheritance. Unfortunately, I only recall three: one in Eastern Sea and another in Central Continent."

"And Southern Border, which Wu clan has long occupied. Empress Wu Ji herself walked the strength path using that inheritance."

Only one clue remained: Li Ran, the person who found the legendary Gu in his previous life.

# Chapter 282: A flash of inspiration Gu

With his monthly enforced challenge still unused, Fang Yuan visited the secret reception area.

There were three people who were named Li Ran among the ones who had signed up.

One was a rank two gambler over fifty who had lived in fifth inner city squalor for ten years. He had married and had children in Shang clan city, but separated after marital discord.

Investigating secretly, Fang Yuan confirmed this man was the gambler he had seen at the den.

Tailing him for days, Fang Yuan discovered Li Ran lived hand-to-mouth, often unable to afford his next meal.

"This must be the owner from my past life. But with such poor self-control, no wonder he vanished soon after gaining fame."

"He found it in my past life; will he get it again now? I cannot tail him indefinitely. Am I truly not fated to receive this Gu?"

The greatest question was why the legendary Gu was not inside the stellar stone, contradicting the rumors!

Whether due to his suspicious nature or pure instinct, Fang Yuan found Li Ran very strange. He could not pinpoint why, and the feeling was so baseless even he was unsure.

"I am too entrenched and overthinking. At this time, I need a fresh spark to inspire me." Fang Yuan understood his situation.

Thus, he visited a shop: "Shopkeeper, do you have a flash of inspiration Gu?"

Fang Yuan asked several shops before finally finding one that had it, priced at twenty-nine thousand primeval stones.

Fang Yuan gritted his teeth, buying this flash of inspiration Gu.

Activating the Gu, a spiritual light entered his brain, tearing through the layers of confusion.

Fang Yuan's eyes shone brightly, finally realizing what that strange feeling was.

"That's right. Although Li Ran's life is an undisciplined mess, a pattern exists within the chaos. He visits the restaurant every three days, the gambling den twice every seven days, and the brothel every five days. And every ten days, he would go to a restaurant named 'Fu Tai Xiang He'."

If someone were truly unrestrained and aimless, their life would be completely chaotic—how could it follow such a perfect rhythm?

Three days later, in Fu Tai Xiang He restaurant.

"Clerk, the bill." Li Ran looked away from the window, calling loudly.

Li Ran walked downstairs, exited the restaurant, and turned into an alley, gradually vanishing into the shadows.

Observing downstairs, Fang Yuan called the shop assistant and moved to Li Ran's vacated window seat.

"Eh? This is..." Fang Yuan's gaze concentrated on a tofu shop opposite the street.

Fang Yuan recognized them as Li Ran's wife and son!

He closed his eyes, mapping the streets in his mind.

There were better places to peek at Lady An Yu, but to observe this mother and son, this was the only spot!

"If he abandoned them years ago, why secretly watch them? If he feels guilty, why not appear openly? Why remain so secretive?"

He slowly set down his chopsticks and raised his wine glass, the fragrant liquor glimmering inside.

Suddenly, Fang Yuan's black pupils expanded!

"So that's why... I finally understand!"

The mystery spanning his past and present lives was finally solved; all doubts cleared, Fang Yuan found his answer!

At the same time, he had 'found' the legendary Gu!

# Chapter 283: Got it

"Wu Ran, if asked to sacrifice your future for the clan, are you willing?"

"I am willing!"

"I was right about you, Wu Ran. Like your grandfather and father, you are loyal to Wu clan! The clan has a mission for you requiring you to sacrifice your future, reputation, and perhaps your life."

"Lord clan leader, I am willing to sacrifice for the clan!"

In the darkness, Wu Ran opened his eyes.

"*Huff*..." Exhaling slowly, he sat up in bed, muttering: "I dreamed again."

'Li Ran' was his alias; his real name was 'Wu Ran'.

The surname Wu held immense weight in the Southern Border, representing its number one overlord: Wu clan!

Eleven years ago, Wu clan leader Empress Wu Ji granted Wu Ran an audience, assigning him a secret mission—

Making his way into Shang clan's higher up level!

He changed his name and roamed the wilderness for three years before settling in Shang clan city.

Living masked for eight years, he posed as a libertine and low-level Gu Master, revealing nothing even to his beloved wife.

He contacted Wu clan only once every half a year, always through one-sided transmissions to thoroughly conceal all traces.

A year ago, an agent informed him the clan was activating him, filling him with inexpressible excitement!

"After eight years, they have finally activated me; everything will change!" Li Ran exclaimed, summoning a Gu from storage.

The cube-shaped fossil resembled a brick shimmering with starlight on its surface.

A decade of lurking in the city gave him a flawless background to gain Shang clan's trust.

Yet he also needed extraordinary strength to dominate the battle stage so Shang clan would recruit him as an external elder.

Thus, Wu clan leader Empress Wu Ji chose a Gu worm for him personally.

Obtained from an ancient strength path inheritance, it was nearly extinct and exceptionally rare in the current world!

With this Gu, Li Ran could emerge from slumber and dominate the battle stage.

The demonic Gu Master Wei Shen Jing was Empress Wu Ji's subordinate; creating an undetectable fake scrap-grade fossil was effortless for him.

To ensure everyone believed the fossil came from the gambling den, he chose a rock used there as leg support.

Li Ran had contacted Wu clan, asking Wei Shen Jing to create an identical stellar stone.

Having observed the gambling den for months, he planned to swap the leg support rock with his stellar stone undetected.

Once he 'found' the Gu, he would rise as an external elder, complete his mission, and bring his family back to Wu clan.

Tomorrow he would fight on the battle stage, and the day after, bring the stellar stone to the gambling den for his life-changing performance.

"I planned to use that stone as cover, but someone unexpectedly bought it. That idiot, buying a scrap-grade stellar stone... though tomorrow, I must play the idiot too."

Li Ran chuckled, returning the stellar stone to his storage Gu.

With such a belief, he arrived at the battle stage.

From probing to fighting, the two were evenly matched, at a stalemate.

Spotting a weakness in his opponent, he was about to strike.

Suddenly, pain struck his heart and his body stiffened; his opponent's counterattack sent him flying.

Heavily injured and groaning, Li Ran flew back over ten paces before steadying himself.

"My flowerbud Gu! How did I lose connection to it? The stellar stone is inside!!"

At the same time, at Li Ran's residence.

The bed was flipped over, showing a secret compartment.

Fang Yuan stood beside it, holding a flowerbud Gu.

With Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, Fang Yuan instantly refined Li Ran's Gu.

"This is it!" Fang Yuan's heart trembled as he activated primeval essence, drawing a black light from the flowerbud Gu.

The black light settled in Fang Yuan's palm as a stellar stone.

*The legendary Gu, is finally in my hands!!!*

He rejoiced inwardly: "Li Ran truly is a spy! Just like I hid my Liquor worm on Qing Mao mountain, he staged the rock gamble. The rumors were merely his cover story!"

"Today he fights on the battle stage. Whether concealing his strength or not, his past records indicate an evenly matched opponent, making it a tough battle. The legendary Gu's stellar stone is too vital to carry into battle; with his cautious nature, he would never keep it in his storage Gu during a match."

# Chapter 284: One, Two, Five

"I admit defeat!" Li Ran shouted loudly, dropping to one knee.

Spitting a mouthful of blood, Li Ran stood up shakily.

Extremely anxious, Li Ran hurried off the battle stage, feigning dejection.

Hastily bandaging his injuries, he rushed back home.

"Damn it, how can this happen? Why did I lose connection with the flowerbud Gu?"

"Only two possibilities exist: either the flowerbud Gu was destroyed, or someone else refined it! Was I discovered? No, perhaps a burglar simply broke into my house. Damn it, I've lived in Shang clan city for eight years and know every thief; why would anyone rob my house!"

Li Ran hastened his steps; the stellar stone was so vital that even his calm nature could not suppress his anxiety.

He returned to his home feeling uncertainty, the door was closed.

Opening it, he found the room in a mess.

"Eh? This is..." Flipping over his bed, he discovered a Gu left behind.

"A Heart sound Gu... did the thief leave this telepathic Gu specifically for me?!"

Hesitating briefly, Li Ran gritted his teeth and placed the heart sound Gu into his ear like an earplug.

Li Ran activated the heart sound Gu with primeval essence, asking in his mind: "Who are you?"

"That question is not important. What matters is that Li Ran is a fake name, right? Hehehe." Fang Yuan's voice echoed in Li Ran's mind.

Li Ran's pupils shrank, freezing on the spot as if struck by lightning.

Yet as an eight-year veteran spy, he quickly calmed himself and replied to Fang Yuan: "Fake name? What do you mean?"

Fang Yuan chuckled: "Don't bother acting, Li Ran. I am not your enemy. Cooperate, and we can meet."

Fang Yuan's voice instructed: "Now, walk out of your room and turn left outside the door."

"Why should I listen to you?" Li Ran demanded in his mind.

Fang Yuan laughed: "Don't test how much I know. I will count to five—consider well."

"One." Fang Yuan counted.

"Two." Fang Yuan spoke casually.

"Five." Fang Yuan continued to count.

Thrown into chaos, Li Ran cursed: "Damn it, can you even count properly?!"

"Hehehe, it seems you have come to a decision." Fang Yuan laughed.

Taking a deep breath with clenched fists, Li Ran gritted his teeth and left the house.

He walked out of the door onto the busy street and turned left.

Fifty steps later, Fang Yuan instructed: "Turn right, then left at the third junction."

"Don't look around. After eight years of undercover work, do you want it all ruined?"

"Turn around," Fang Yuan added. "Go to the third floor of that restaurant ahead; I will be waiting there."

He entered the restaurant.

The familiar shop assistant called out enthusiastically: "Lord Li Ran, you are here! Please come upstairs!"

"Opposite you, there is a shop selling baked pancakes, go buy a few." Fang Yuan continued.

His eyes twitching, Li Ran obeyed and bought several pancakes.

Returning upstairs, Li Ran found Fang Yuan sitting at his usual spot.

Fang Yuan stretched out his hand, inviting him to sit while transmitting into his mind: "Please take a seat."

Li Ran sat silently before Fang Yuan, staring unblinkingly at him.

In truth, Fang Yuan's youthful appearance gave him a huge shock.

Streetlights illuminated Fang Yuan's ordinary face, yet his dark eyes were deep as an abyss.

# Chapter 285: All-out effort Gu

Sitting in apparent calm, Li Ran harbored deep killing intent while Fang Yuan remained quietly watchful.

"Who exactly are you?" Li Ran asked using the heart sound Gu.

"That is not important," Fang Yuan transmitted, then spoke aloud: "Pass the pancake to me."

Stunned, Li Ran pushed the paper bag toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan took a bite of a pancake, nodding: "Now this is the flavor I wanted, sorry for the trouble."

Snorting inwardly, Li Ran asked: "Aren't you afraid I poisoned it?"

Outwardly, he smiled and said: "No trouble at all. Don't forget our great relationship."

Fang Yuan chuckled aloud, looking pointedly at Li Ran while transmitting: "You think I don't know what Gu you have?"

Seeing Li Ran fall silent with shifting eyes, Fang Yuan knew the warning got through and produced the stellar stone.

Fang Yuan's voice rang in his mind: "Stop pretending, I know the all-out effort Gu is inside this."

"WHAT?!" Li Ran's heart shook as if struck by lightning.

"Wait, only a few people know about the all-out effort Gu. Even I only learned of it after receiving it; how did he find out?"

"Things are complicated now... With no loopholes on my end, this must have leaked from the clan! Wu clan sent me here, but Shang clan could send spies there too. The great clans in the Southern Border constantly infiltrate each other, keeping tabs on one another's affairs."

Overthinking made Li Ran more suspicious, subconsciously imagining Fang Yuan as a terrifyingly powerful enemy.

Fang Yuan toyed with the stellar stone until the moment was ripe, then transmitted: "I want the all-out effort Gu."

"Yet seizing it by force would invite your clan's revenge, which is why I need your cooperation."

Li Ran smiled mockingly: "You want me to cooperate with you? What a joke—on what basis?"

"Because like you, I have people to protect," Fang Yuan transmitted, gazing out the window.

At the same time, he spoke aloud: "I heard that shop sells great tofu."

Li Ran's eyes narrowed ferociously as he screamed in his mind: "Are you threatening me?!"

Fang Yuan shook his head, his gaze clouded with complex emotions that Li Ran instantly caught: sadness, pain, helplessness, and fondness.

"People like us are caught between great forces, hiding our true feelings and facing death daily. We walk this path for our clans, but why should our families bear the risk?"

Li Ran stared at Fang Yuan in disbelief: "You... are a spy too?"

Fang Yuan laughed bitterly: "What do you think?"

Fang Yuan looked at Li Ran: "You have lurked in Shang clan city for eight years. If exposed, your mission will fail. Not only would you die, but the clan would suffer and you would disappoint them."

"If exposed, your wife and five-year-old son will die with you. You are one step from becoming a clan elder, but is that the end? No, as long as Shang clan exists, your clan will need you to keep spying."

"Thus, we should cooperate to ensure our families' safety without betraying our clans. We must survive to serve our clans, and Shang clan is our common target."

"Of course, this cooperation is only in private. If our clans want to wage war, you will be the first person I will eliminate." After pausing briefly, Fang Yuan added.

Li Ran snorted coldly; Fang Yuan's frank declaration only made him more trustworthy.

He fell into deep silence for a long time.

Moments later, he transmitted via heart sound Gu: "How can I believe you?"

Fang Yuan smiled, taking out a poison vow Gu.

Three days later, Fang Yuan unsealed the all-out effort Gu in the gambling den, throwing the entire district into chaos.

"Is that really the all-out effort Gu? That ancient, near-extinct Gu can summon beast phantoms with a hundred percent success rate!"

"You only just found out? I know more—this lucky fellow is Gu Yue Fang Zheng, and he even holds a purple thorn token. A Gu Master named Li Ran chose the fossil, but Fang Zheng picked a fight and demanded three rocks as compensation. That all-out effort Gu was within those fossils."

# Chapter 286: A Small Test Run

"This is the all-out effort Gu?"

In the secret room, Wei Yang held the Gu, eyes gleaming with a strange light.

"That's right, a rank three Gu that summons a beast phantom moving according to my will," Fang Yuan explained.

Wei Yang exhaled deeply and handed the Gu back to Fang Yuan.

"Is it fate? Brother Fang Zheng, are you truly bent on the strength path?" he asked in a final attempt.

Fang Yuan nodded silently, his determined expression saying everything.

"Fate is truly mystical. The all-out effort Gu is the ideal core for your set. Since you are determined, let me offer one final reminder."

Fang Yuan turned solemn: "Please, advise me!"

"Although you have the all-out effort Gu, times have changed. If it is your core, you must ensure its safety. It is only rank three now. If it is not your vital Gu, refining it to rank four or five carries immense risk. If it is destroyed during refinement, your entire set collapses without its core. Thus, my first advice is to make it your vital Gu—Shang clan can help you do so."

"A complete set requires key support Gu alongside the core. Wrong choices will prevent all-out effort Gu from displaying its full power. In ancient times, many Gu complemented it, but most are extinct now. You must explore combinations yourself."

"Another crucial matter: you lack its refinement recipe. Fortunately, most recipes from the Olden Antiquity Era survived in the hands of recipe masters, so you must collect them. Otherwise..."

"You could also explore ancient strength path inheritances. Wu clan found one years ago; finding one yourself would secure your future."

After saying these things, Wei Yang took his leave.

Fang Yuan had long considered Wei Yang's advice and already formed plans.

He already possessed the all-out effort Gu's recipe, obtained from Li Ran.

But this recipe only allowed the rank three all-out effort Gu to reach rank four. Any higher, Fang Yuan needed to find out himself.

"My strength path set should use all-out effort Gu as the core, supported by self-reliance Gu, bitter strength Gu, and a few others for initial success. But to achieve greater heights, I must rely on the three kings inheritance."

"After the three kings inheritance comes the battle at Yi Tian Mountain—without sufficient battle strength, I cannot reap enough benefits. Time is tight..."

Fang Yuan's vital Gu is Spring Autumn Cicada, this cannot be changed.

Even without making it his vital Gu, there were ways to protect all-out effort Gu during refinement.

Second, he could use the undefeated hundred battles Gu to guarantee a hundred percent refinement success rate.

"The three kings inheritance holds an undefeated hundred battles Gu. Obtaining it will guarantee all-out effort Gu's advancement to rank four. As for the rest, I'll find other ways... Now, it's time to replace everything."

Two days later, the Gu worms arrived.

Fang Yuan's battle strength surged, but he paid a huge price.

They were exorbitantly expensive, costing several hundred thousand primeval stones and emptying his reserves.

Another day later, Fang Yuan stepped into the battle stage.

His opponent was none other than Li Ran.

With a twisted expression and burning eyes, Li Ran screamed at Fang Yuan: "Gu Yue Fang Zheng, you despicably stole my opportunity! Today, I will reclaim what is mine!"

"Hmph, you bumped into me and refused to pay compensation in primeval stones. Those fossils settled your debt by mutual consent," Fang Yuan sneered.

"Ahhh!" Li Ran clutched his hair, shouting furiously: "Fang Zheng, you tyrant, I was forced! It was an accidental bump, yet you wouldn't let me go. Today, I demand justice!"

Saying so, Li Ran pounced at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan snorted coldly, seeing him get close, he willed.

Vertical crash Gu.

Wind surged as he stepped forward, charging fifty paces in a flash.

All-out effort Gu.

At Fang Yuan's thought, radiant light manifested a wild boar phantom charging behind him.

"Not good!" Li Ran screamed, dodging.

Fang Yuan swept past; though Li Ran dodged a direct hit, his arm was grazed.

With a sharp crack, his forearm bone shattered, his defensive light vanished, and Li Ran collapsed to the ground, unable to rise.

# Chapter 287: Reputation Gu

Fang Yuan halted after fifty paces and turned to face Li Ran, preparing a second strike.

But Li Ran raised his hand, shouting: "Wait, stop, I admit defeat!"

A loud ring from the bell, and this match was over.

As spectators began dispersing, Fang Yuan turned to leave as well.

"Fang Zheng, wait first." Li Ran suddenly called, stopping him.

Fang Yuan frowned, turning back: "What do you want?"

"Fang Zheng, you owe me, yet you injured me! Repaying kindness with enmity—you must compensate me!" Li Ran shouted.

Fang Yuan shook his head, turning away: "Did I damage your brain too?"

Struggling to his feet, Li Ran shouted: "Fang Zheng, I know your character! You distinguish kindness and hatred clearly: *'a drop received in need repaid with a spring; a spark of hatred answered by burning a forest!'*"

"I did you a favor. If I hadn't chosen that stellar stone, could you have obtained the all-out effort Gu? I understand you. Tyrannical as you are, you cannot sleep if a debt of gratitude remains unpaid. Knowing you owe me a favor, can you sleep tonight?"

"Heh, that's because you've never seen his true colors!" Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly from the crowd.

Suddenly, Fang Yuan stopped in his steps.

Under everyone's gaze, he turned back to Li Ran with a solemn expression.

"Put that way, I do owe you a favor. But you offended me first, and I cannot part with the all-out effort Gu. What do you propose?"

"Though rank three, the all-out effort Gu hails from the Olden Antiquity Era and is priceless. Give me a hundred thousand primeval stones, and your debt is settled!" Li Ran proposed.

Fang Yuan thought about it, shaking his head.

"A hundred thousand is not enough—I'll give you two hundred thousand to set my mind at ease." He summoned the primeval elder Gu and poured out all his primeval stones.

A mound of primeval stones formed on the stage.

"Here is eighty thousand—all I have now. I will pay the rest later!"

Word of Fang Yuan's purple thorn token spread as well, causing ill-intentioned onlookers to vanish immediately.

Regardless, Fang Yuan's reputation for clearly distinguishing kindness and hatred spread widely.

Back at Nan Qiu garden.

"Do you really intend to give Li Ran two hundred thousand stones?" Bai Ning Bing asked suspiciously.

"Of course," Fang Yuan replied, keeping their secret pact hidden: Li Ran provided the recipe while Fang Yuan compensated him two hundred thousand stones.

Bai Ning Bing remained skeptical before sneering: "Two hundred thousand just for reputation—is it worth it?"

Fang Yuan snickered: "Haven't you heard the story of reputation Gu?"

Bai Ning Bing hesitated: "What do you mean?"

"Reputation is a bridge spanning the abyss. It is a travel token far more precious than the purple thorn token, allowing one to move unhindered. Two hundred thousand cannot buy a purple thorn token, yet I used it to obtain this reputation. It is the finest trade in the world. Hahaha!" Fang Yuan laughed.

The story of reputation Gu originated from *The Legends of Ren Zu*...

Once, Verdant Great Sun awoke from a drunken stupor trapped on a lone hill surrounded by a bottomless abyss.

Pale green spiral winds filled the abyss—this was *'ordinary wind'*. It blew dark yellow dust into the air, known as *'mortal dust'*.

He recognized Ordinary Abyss, which no living creature had ever crossed; trapped on this isolated hill, he was doomed.

Fortunately, a forest grew on the hill. Driven by hunger, Verdant Great Sun searched it for fruit. But the forest was bizarre—its marshy black soil reeked pungently.

Starving and exhausted, he collapsed unconscious. In his slumber, he could hear many voices talking.

"Leaving Ordinary Abyss is actually possible—you only need reputation Gu."

"Reputation Gu is hidden under a rock at the forest's center. Too bad he doesn't know, hahaha..."

Hearing this, Verdant Great Sun jolted awake.

This was Xi Yu Forest; what he had thought were rustling leaves were actually the whispers of the trees.

Verdant Great Sun went to the forest center, lifted the rock, and obtained reputation Gu.

Reputation Gu resembled a golden chrysanthemum, radiating a rich fragrance.

Reputation Gu said, "Young man, thank you for rescuing me. To repay you, I will help you cross Ordinary Abyss."

Reputation Gu told Verdant Great Sun the method to use it.

Verdant Great Sun was overjoyed, arriving at ordinary abyss, and placed the reputation Gu in his mouth, shouting as loud as he could...

No sound emerged, yet tremors shook the abyss like an earthquake as fragrance filled the air.

Verdant Great Sun was not surprised, recalling what reputation Gu told him: *Reputation has no voice, but it spreads far and creates tremors.*

A golden bridge formed in midair as he shouted, but it fell far short of reaching the other side.

Too hungry and exhausted, his subsequent attempts yielded diminishing results, dimming his hope of salvation.

Reputation Gu sighed: "Sigh, you have not eaten anything in a while, even though you have a breath left in your stomach, it is too little. From your diaphragm, it has to go through your stomach, chest, throat, and finally your mouth, the journey is too long, we have to reduce it, here, place me between your two buttcheeks."

Verdant Great Sun forced his remaining breath out through that opening.

*Prrrrrrrrt—*

A thunderous blast echoed amidst an unimaginable stench, and the golden bridge expanded majestically to the opposite cliff.

*A shitty reputation compared to good reputation, is always easier to obtain and maintain.*

Verdant Great Sun swiftly crossed the bridge, escaping Ordinary Abyss and saving his life.

# Chapter 288: The Crux of Strength Path Cultivation

Eight days later.

In front of him were a dozen stone pillars.

"Indeed, summoning a beast strength phantom with normal attacks without all-out effort Gu is difficult."

His right palm smacked heavily towards the stone pillar.

A brown bear phantom manifested behind Fang Yuan, raising its right paw and striking down in unison with his palm.

In that instant, Fang Yuan felt immense power surge into his right palm!

The thick stone pillar shattered on impact, crashing down with a loud boom.

The recoil instantly numbed Fang Yuan's right palm.

Having thoroughly refined the brown bear innate strength Gu, he had permanently gained the strength of one bear.

"Normally, I'd need dozens of strikes to break this pillar. With all-out effort Gu, it shattered in a single blow..."

Though a support-type Gu rather than an offensive one, all-out effort Gu produced devastating power when activated.

"Canopy Gu offers solid defense, but it is too rigid. Replacing it with golden shield Gu should yield much better results," Fang Yuan pondered.

The effect of force is mutual.

Though he possessed iron bones, his flesh remained ordinary; without canopy Gu, that recoil would have mangled his muscles.

However, golden shield Gu was temporarily not available.

Fang Yuan also did not have money right now.

Now broke, Fang Yuan was even in debt, still owing Li Ran a hundred and twenty thousand primeval stones under their poison vow.

Fang Yuan knew this was a necessary investment.

The main goal was keeping Li Ran placated, even though Fang Yuan had nullified the vow using eat one's words Gu.

Li Ran was an unexpected boon. Killing him would leave traces, arousing Shang clan's suspicions and disrupting Fang Yuan's plans, so he spared him.

To Fang Yuan, Li Ran was an unwieldy yet useful pawn. In the future, he might even obtain the rank five all-out effort Gu recipe from Wu clan through him.

Vertical crash Gu was a movement Gu Fang Yuan bought to break through by force.

Horizontal charge Gu was similar to vertical crash Gu, except it charged horizontally.

These two Gu turned movement into attacks, allowing him to exert bodily force while charging.

"At rank four, I can fuse these two into charging crash Gu and continue using them."

"The boar phantom sends five stone pillars flying and knocks down eight; the bear sends seven flying and knocks down five; the crocodile sends three flying and knocks down four."

The boar suited long-distance charging, the bear had explosive initial power, while the crocodile was unsuitable for charging.

"All-out effort Gu only activates one phantom at a time, making two boars wasteful. Changing to another beast strength will diversify my attacks."

"With the strength of two boars, a bear, and a crocodile, I've reached my body's limit. It is time to use steel tendon Gu."

Fang Yuan had already bought steel tendon Gu.

Steel tendon and iron bones perfectly complemented each other.

Unlike iron bone Gu, steel tendon Gu required continuous nourishment over time to turn tendons into steel.

Without wasting a second, he began using steel tendon Gu to reform his body.

Three days later, fifth inner city's battle stage.

A burly man in a blue shirt stood before Fang Yuan, breathing heavily with lingering dread.

"I cannot lose! If I defeat him, battle stage rules grant me all-out effort Gu, and I can cultivate the strength path too!"

Thinking this, the burly man growled fiercely and pounced upon Fang Yuan.

Water prison Gu!

He spat an expanding water sphere, trapping Fang Yuan inside.

Fang Yuan coldly snorted and casually punched inside the water.

Boar strength phantom!

The water ball shook thrice and warped, yet held together.

"It's useless. To deal with you, I took a high-interest loan to buy this rank three water prison Gu!" the burly man laughed proudly.

Fang Yuan raised his leg and kicked sideways. Crocodile strength phantom!

Unlike the boar and brown bear, the amphibious crocodile was naturally adept at water.

The water prison was smashed into pieces.

"What?!" The blue shirt burly man was pale with fright.

Vertical crash Gu!

Fang Yuan sent him flying twenty steps, blood spurting as he crashed down.

He struggled violently to get up, but collapsed before he could even rise.

He was dead.

# Chapter 289: Can’t stop me

The hosting Gu Master stepped forward and declared Fang Yuan victorious.

Taking back the vine information Gu from the host, Fang Yuan checked his record: seventeen wins, zero losses.

These victories earned him a large number of primeval stones.

"Li Ran, here is fifty thousand primeval stones handed over in public." Fang Yuan tossed over a money bag.

Li Ran emerged from the crowd, smilingly accepting the primeval stones under countless envious gazes.

"Fang Zheng, you truly keep your word—a hundred and thirty thousand primeval stones in total." Cupping his fists, he left.

Watching Fang Yuan leave, someone in the crowd sneered, "His good days are ending. In five days, Li Hao will forcefully challenge him."

Swimming in his primeval sea, steel tendon Gu absorbed essence and radiated dark light.

Under the dark sheen, his muscles and tendons grew firmer and stronger.

After half a day, Fang Yuan ceased using steel tendon Gu.

Using steel tendon Gu was agony; a painful itch invaded his nerves, testing his endurance.

An ordinary Gu Master would take half a year to complete the steel tendon process.

Fang Yuan planned to condense the process into a single month.

"I must treasure every second to cultivate and increase my strength as fast as possible!"

"I need primeval stones, but if I lose on the battle stage, I forfeit all-out effort Gu or pay a steep price elsewhere!"

"Shang clan city is merely a starting point; how can I fall here? In this life, I must become a Gu Immortal, breaking through to ranks seven, eight, and nine. I will ascend to the peak and reach the supreme realm of immortality beyond rank nine! Longevity is easy to obtain, but no one has ever attained true immortality."

"Immortality is the pinnacle desire of any lifeform. Wealth is mere hoarding; lust, primal instinct; reputation, foul nonsense spewed by others."

"On Earth, death was inevitable, but in this world rich with primeval essence, why not pursue immortality when it is possible?"

"For immortality, I will use and discard all worldly things! Fear cannot stop me—I will charge forward relentlessly! Laziness will not stop me—I will never slack for a moment! Pain, gods, demons, and even heaven and earth cannot stop me!"

Affirming his belief, Fang Yuan's eerie dark eyes burned with a scarlet demonic blaze.

After resting, he activated steel tendon Gu again without hesitation.

If stubbornness is demonic, then Fang Yuan is a demon among demons.

Dying countless times would never shake his resolve; setbacks were merely firewood that made the demonic ambitions in his heart burn even brighter.

# Chapter 290:  Fang Yuan vs Li Hao (1)

"Your opponent today has quite the background," Wei Yang warned, walking toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan nodded.

"Li Hao once reached the fourth inner city as a strength cultivator, but hit a severe bottleneck. Strength cultivators stall in the middle stages. Discouraged, Li Hao abandoned strength cultivation for a support style."

"He struck luck in a gambling den, obtaining the rare position swap Gu. Relying on his experience, he sold all his Gu and bought a mountain back toad at auction."

"Pairing these two Gu, he won every match. He is challenging you because he wants all-out effort Gu, so be careful."

Wei Yang patted Fang Yuan's shoulder and melted into the crowd.

The arena was a vast swamp filled with puddles and foul, rotting black mud.

Li Hao walked onto the stage with a light smile.

But around sixty steps away, he stopped, not advancing further.

Knowing Fang Yuan's vertical crash Gu reached fifty steps, Li Hao kept a safe distance.

"Strength cultivator, hehe..." Li Hao scoffed. "Young man, your winning streak ends here. You are just like my past self. There is no future in the strength path. After I defeat you today, you will understand its futility."

"You talk a lot. Can you shut up?" Fang Yuan folded his arms plainly.

At the chime of the bell, the hosting Gu Master announced the battle's start.

Vertical crash Gu!

Fang Yuan took a big stride, charging forward.

As expected, Fang Yuan charged forty-five steps and stopped.

Fang Yuan immediately turned sideways, facing Li Hao with his profile.

Horizontal charge Gu!

A wild boar phantom surged, carrying Fang Yuan to Li Hao in the blink of an eye.

Paling at the critical moment, Li Hao activated his vital Gu.

Position swap!

In an instant, he swapped with Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan's charge continued forward, while Li Hao was now behind him, widening the gap.

"I was careless!" Sweat poured down Li Hao's forehead.

Using his movement Gu, he floated backward as light as the wind, rapidly pulling away from Fang Yuan.

Simultaneously, he pointed upward as a green light shot from his aperture toward the sky.

Amidst the glaring light, the mountain back toad appeared and descended.

A growing black shadow fell over the ground, engulfing Fang Yuan.

With cooldown passed, Fang Yuan charged outward with vertical crash Gu.

Accelerating rapidly, he quickly cleared the shadow's range.

THe moment he got out, the mountain back toad landed.

With a loud bang, the entire stage trembled as black mud and soil surged like tidal waves.

Black mud splashed over him like a wave, repelled by canopy Gu's white armor.

Horizontal charge Gu.

Angling his body, he charged horizontally toward the mountain back toad.

*Howl!*

A brown bear roared as its phantom appeared howling skyward, exuding a wild aura.

*Bam!*

Fang Yuan slammed into the mountain back toad, making it sway.

Undaunted, Fang Yuan raised both arms and smashed down like a hammer.

Above his head, the boar, brown bear, and crocodile phantoms appeared in succession.

His punches tore through the air, detonating against the toad like firecrackers.

With a cold expression, Fang Yuan unleashed a relentless barrage upon the beast.

Li Hao's expression changed.

However tough the toad was, it could not endure this endless barrage.

*Gua gua gua...*

The mountain back toad croaked as the pain exceeded its limits.

"This cannot continue!" Li Hao's heart jumped as he mounted the mountain back toad.

The mountain back toad counterattacked, stomping through mud to charge Fang Yuan.

Horizontal charge Gu.

Fang Yuan shifted aside, effortlessly dodging the charge.

Getting around the toad's body, and hitting again!

# Chapter 291: Fang Yuan vs Li Hao (2)

*Roar!*

A beast phantom roared as it flashed; croaking in pain, the toad suddenly slammed toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan snorted and dodged.

"This kid is so violent!" Anxious, Li Hao gritted his teeth and ordered the mountain back toad to jump.

"Brat, I'll smash you into minced meat!" Li Hao sneered with murderous intent.

However, mountain back toad's shadow was not over Fang Yuan.

The mountain back toad's massive body began falling heavily toward Li Hao.

Li Hao planned to use position swap Gu to trade places with Fang Yuan, crushing him under the falling toad.

The moment the toad jumped, however, Fang Yuan charged straight toward Li Hao!

Chaining horizontal charge Gu and vertical crash Gu, he quickly reached Li Hao.

"Accursed brat, he saw through my plan!" Li Hao fumed.

Reluctantly, Li Hao activated position swap Gu, swapping positions with Fang Yuan.

*Bam!*

The mountain back toad landed, and Fang Yuan predictably used horizontal charge to dodge.

Tracking their three-breath cooldowns across fifty steps, he always kept a movement Gu ready.

He charged the toad again in an all-out offensive, beast phantoms appearing in succession to empower his fierce strikes.

Stones flew as the mountain back toad fell into danger again, croaking in distress.

"Not good, the toad is coughing blood!" Watching from afar, Li Hao felt a chill run through him.

Position swap Gu!

In a split second, Fang Yuan's vision shifted as Li Hao swapped him far away.

Placing his palms on the toad, he began healing it.

Fang Yuan sneered and charged again.

After several attempts, Li Hao stopped healing, his complexion troubled.

"Although position swap Gu is mystical, its massive primeval essence consumption is a major flaw."

"The greater the distance and stronger the target, the more primeval essence position swap Gu consumes."

"Fang Zheng possesses the strength of two boars, a bear, and a crocodile, drastically increasing Li Hao's swap cost. Combined with healing the toad, even a rank three's essence cannot support such intense usage."

He did not have much primeval essence left now.

Seeing Fang Yuan charge again, Li Hao hesitated but was forced to engage.

Clenching his fists, Fang Yuan bypassed the toad and attacked Li Hao directly.

*Bang bang bang.*

Several moves later, Li Hao could not endure it anymore!

Despite having other Gu, his core lineup was the mountain back toad and position swap Gu.

To reduce position swap Gu's primeval essence consumption, he had completely removed the beast phantoms in his body.

Relying on his former strength cultivation foundation, Li Hao withstood several moves before being forced to swap places with the mountain back toad.

Ignoring Li Hao, Fang Yuan resumed attacking the mountain back toad.

He never thought to outwit it, simply swinging his arms in an all-out assault—beating whoever appeared before him!

Fang Yuan fought to his heart's content, his surging emotions finally pouring out like a torrent.

*"Because hardships strengthen resolve, the strong-minded will not be lured by worldly affairs. Today I step on grass; later I shall step on mountains and rivers!"*

*From today, I will walk on the path of supremacy!*

*Sweep the earth and laugh at the world.*

*Tread the green mountains; step on the blue seas; bind the blue dragon; assault the sky!*

*Bathe in difficulties to sharpen the demonic soul; raise the flag in triumph; defy heaven, defy fate, defy the universe!*

*Bam!*

A ruthless punch collapsed the hill on the toad's back, beating it to death in a pool of blood!

Horizontal charge, vertical crash!

Li Hao flew back dozens of steps before crashing to the ground.

The battle ended abruptly. Fang Yuan stood proudly, his ferocious aura pervading the scene.

# Chapter 292: Law Fragment, Traces of Great Dao

In the secret room, Fang Yuan sat on the cushion, his eyes shut tightly.

Light silver primeval essence continued to pour into the self-reliance Gu.

Self-reliance Gu was arguably the best Gu for strength-cultivating Gu Masters.

The greater a Gu Master's physical strength, the stronger the self-reliance Gu's healing effect, and vice versa.

With the strength of four beasts, his healing from self-reliance Gu already matched flesh-bone Gu, improving further with every added beast.

Now, Fang Yuan was using the self-reliance Gu to recover his injuries.

His ferocious attacks had torn his muscles and tendons, while canopy Gu was battered and needed recovery.

"I've been healing with self-reliance Gu for ten hours now. The steel tendon Gu is primarily what makes recovery so difficult." Fang Yuan understood his situation.

*Man is the spirit of all beings, Gu is the essence of heaven and earth.*

Regardless of size, every Gu worm contained a law fragment of the Great Dao.

Steel tendon Gu imbued his tendons with the Great Dao's natural law, making them tough as steel.

Similarly, the brown bear innate strength Gu, black and white boar Gu, and crocodile strength Gu were the same.

They held Great Dao fragments of strength that formed beast phantoms—physical Dao marks within his body.

"Gu Masters use Gu, but in truth they utilize the Great Dao law fragments within them. Gu are vessels for these fragments, tools of heaven and earth. In refining Gu, Gu Masters fuse and form laws. Nurturing, using, and refining Gu is a true Great Dao of learning from nature, holding the eternal hope of immortality."

"Because hardships strengthen resolve, the strong-minded will not be lured by worldly affairs. Today I step on grass; later I shall step on mountains and rivers!"

In his study, Shang Yan Fei muttered, pondering Fang Yuan's poem with visible interest.

"Hardships forge resolve, immune to worldly allure. What grand aspirations! Especially that last line—does he treat my Shang clan as mere grass?"

"Lord Clan Leader, I believe Fang Zheng was merely referring to his opponent Li Hao as too weak," Wei Yang said, cupping his fists.

Shang Yan Fei rose and walked to the window. "Armies are easily raised, but generals are rare. A pity this general is too arrogant to respect even Shang clan. Yet surviving hardships and gaining rapid cultivation naturally shaped his disposition this way."

With that thought, Shang Yan Fei asked, "I heard Fang Zheng seized Li Ran's opportunity and promised two hundred thousand primeval stones in compensation. How much does he still owe?"

Wei Yang replied, "He has paid one hundred and thirty thousand, with seventy thousand remaining. It should be repaid soon."

Hearing this, Shang Yan Fei's worry eased.

Operating by a code of repaying debts and vengeance, Fang Zheng was dangerous yet offered hope for recruitment to protect Shang Xin Ci.

As Shang Yan Fei predicted, Fang Yuan's path grew easier as opponents forfeited subsequent matches.

Meanwhile, a group of pursuing Bai clan Gu Masters arrived at the foot of Shang clan city.

# Chapter 293: Heaven is blind

"We've finally reached Shang clan city." Staring at the mountain of buildings, Bai Feng sighed.

Arriving at the fifth inner city's battle zone, Bai Zhan Lie, Bai Lian, and the other juniors were awed by the bustling atmosphere.

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng!!!"

Thunderstruck, the Bai clan group halted and stared wide-eyed at the speaking passerby.

Fang Yuan stepped onto the battle stage opposite a burly, moustached middle-aged man who looked fierce and aggressive.

Instead of leaving immediately, Fang Yuan handed forty thousand primeval stones to Li Ran.

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng, we've finally found you!" In the crowd, Bai Zhan Lie gritted his teeth, face contorted with rage.

"Heaven is blind! How can such a vile scoundrel thrive like this?" Bai Feng cried, looking upward.

"He saved Shang Yan Fei's daughter and received a purple thorn token—Shang clan protects him here!" Bai Lian said bitterly.

The murderer of their two young masters stood right before them, yet they were powerless.

"We're not entirely helpless. If he can enter the battle stage, so can we," Tie Dao Ku said in a low voice, eyes sharp as daggers.

"Wait, where is Bai Zhan Lie?" Bai Lian suddenly asked.

Fang Yuan exited the battle stage as the crowd parted for him.

"Though I've killed many Gu Masters, my reputation remains solid. Repaying Li Ran publicly is a great performance. Only thirty thousand of the two hundred thousand remain."

"With twenty-nine wins, one more brings me to the fourth inner city. I can add more beast phantoms, but..."

"An upcoming auction features instant success Gu, but I lack the primeval stones to qualify for entry..."

As Fang Yuan pondered whom to borrow from, a young man blocked his path.

"Gu Yue Fang Zheng, do you recognise me?!" Bai Zhan Lie screamed.

Bai clan and gang arrived, forming a semi circle around Fang Yuan.

# Chapter 294: Crimes have to be punished!

Fang Yuan furrowed his brows slightly and glanced at the group.

"Justice has long arms, Gu Yue Fang Zheng! You slaughtered our two young clan leaders—fleeing to Shang clan city won't save you!" Bai Feng shouted.

"Fang Zheng, your trap killed my Tie clan's young master and clansmen! This blood feud is irreconcilable—I, Tie Dao Ku, will have your head!" Tie Dao Ku roared, pointing at Fang Yuan's face.

"Oh, a Tie clansman? Strange—if I killed your clansmen, how would I not know about it?" Fang Yuan sneered.

"Deny it all you want! You dug those potato pitfalls that blew my Tie clan group to bits!"

"Hehehe." Fang Yuan paused, then chuckled. "So that's what happened. Seems digging those pitfalls was the right call. Thanks for the delightful news."

"So what?" Fang Yuan shrugged mockingly. "I dug those traps for grass skirt monkeys. If your Tie clansmen stepped on them, blame your own blindness. Besides, why were you tailing me in the first place? Courting your own death! Hehehe, good riddance, their deaths are wonderful..."

Tie Dao Ku snapped back, "Fang Zheng, I'll face you on the battle stage! You cannot escape; your heinous crimes will be punished!"

"Oh? You plan to stop me on the battle stage?" A glint of cold light flashed in Fang Yuan's eyes.

Fang Yuan chuckled and addressed Bai Feng: "Bai clan gained the Bai Gu inheritance and still wants to target me? Without me, you wouldn't have it! Hmph, I suppose you don't care to know the whereabouts of your two young clan leaders."

"Fang Zheng, what do you mean?" Bai Feng immediately asked in bewilderment.

"Fang Zheng, if our young clan leaders are alive, this is merely a misunderstanding. Our Bai clan is not unreasonable," Bai Feng coaxed carefully, probing for their whereabouts.

"You want to know? Fine. I'm hungry—treat me to a meal at the third inner city's famous Shi Zi restaurant," Fang Yuan said indifferently.

He chose it for its soundproof private rooms, where confidential business could be conducted without fear of leaks.

"Speak, where are our young clan leaders?" Bai Feng asked impatiently the moment they sat.

"Before I speak, I'd like this Tie clan Gu Master to wait outside," Fang Yuan said, looking at Tie Dao Ku.

Bai Feng hesitated; needing to handle the young clan leaders' fate with care, he looked at Tie Dao Ku.

Tie Dao Ku snorted and stepped outside in annoyance, shutting the door.

"Tell us where they are—don't think of lying! Shang clan city has plenty of Gu worms to verify your words," Bai Feng demanded sternly.

Fang Yuan sipped his wine. "Whereabouts? They're dead, of course. How naive! Since I made an enemy of Bai clan, I naturally eliminated all future threats."

The Bai clansmen stood up furiously, their flaming anger pressed on towards Fang Yuan.

"I truly admire you all. Your spirit spring is drying up and your clan is in crisis, yet you leisurely chase after me. How remarkable."

The words struck like lightning, sending waves of shock through Elder Bai Feng's heart.

He was horrified as he blurted out: "This... how do you know this?!"

"Your two young clan leaders knelt and offered this secret to buy their lives," Fang Yuan fabricated freely.

"Lord clan elder, is what he said about the spirit spring drying up true?"

Bai Lian, Bai Zhan Lie, and the others questioned him urgently, full of dread.

Their questions snapped Bai Feng back to reality, realizing he had compromised himself.

# Chapter 295: Actually, I was a victim too

Bai Feng's gaze flickered darkly as he considered denying it, but Fang Yuan saw right through him.

"Don't bother denying it. As the spirit spring dries, primeval stone production plunges. Bai clan covered it by using reserves or trading to fill the deficit. That leaves traces anyone investigating carefully can uncover." Fang Yuan leaned back calmly.

"Our spirit spring is drying up?" Bai Lian slumped back listlessly, in a daze.

"Surrounding forces already covet your Bai Gu inheritance. Imagine what happens if I leak that your spirit spring is dry?" Fang Yuan chuckled.

Startled into understanding the stakes, Bai Zhan Lie and the others glared at Fang Yuan with fierce killing intent.

"Want to kill me?" Fang Yuan sneered, wagging a finger. "Meeting you alone means I'm fully prepared. In fact, had you not found me, I would have sought you out."

"We need to discuss my silence fee. I want three million primeval stones to keep quiet."

Hearing this, the Bai clansmen sprang to their feet once more.

"That's right, I'm blackmailing you! Disagree, and I leak the secret. Die together with me? Hmph, even if I die, this information will spread worldwide. You will become clan sinners—because of your refusal, surrounding forces will besiege your clan!"

"You what?" Fang Yuan sneered disdainfully at Bai Feng. "What can you do? Kill me? With a purple thorn token in hand, do you dare? I know you're fearless, but your clan comes first. If your family and friends die, it will be all because of you!"

"Ahh—!" Bai Zhan Lie looked up with a roar and slammed his fist down.

His furious punch shattered the banquet table, sending dishes crashing to the ground.

"No need to get angry. I was a victim in all this too," Fang Yuan sighed, his tone relaxing.

"Think about it, the Bai Gu inheritance was originally mine, but Bai clan stole it. You chased Bai Ning Bing and me, forcing us to kill Bai Hua and Bai Sheng to escape. Later, I was badly injured on Zi You mountain, facing death the whole way. Do you think that was easy?"

"Bai clan is too powerful for me to offend. I could only lie low in Shang clan city, yet you still pursue me. What should I do? I am not unreasonable; three million primeval stones is my minimum. In all sincerity, I have only shared this secret with a few people and haven't spread it. I even sent the Tie clansman outside for Bai clan's sake."

"Three million primeval stones, you are demanding an exorbitant price!"

Fang Yuan's expression chilled into a sneer: "It seems you still don't understand the situation. I gave you face, but you refused? Very well."

"Shang clan's Feng Yu Manor will pay handsomely for this information," Fang Yuan smiled sinisterly, turning to leave.

"Fang Zheng, don't go too far!" Bai Zhan Lie roared, stepping forward to glare directly at him.

*Slap!*

Fang Yuan slapped him across the room with immense strength.

"Do you want to be your clan's sinners?" Fang Yuan snorted, his icy words dousing their surging fury.

He glared at them: "Fools! One sentence from me would bring calamity upon Bai clan! *A wise man submits to circumstances*—can't you see reality? Make a move if you want. Come, having Bai clan dragged into death with me wouldn't be bad."

Though anxious, Bai Feng only spoke after five minutes, breaking the silence.

"Three million... is too much. We can't agree, and we don't carry that much!" His voice was hoarse, like a man dying of thirst in a desert.

Fang Yuan laughed: "You don't need to pay in full right away. For now, hand over five hundred thousand."

"Five hundred thousand? Who carries that many primeval stones?!" Bai Feng retorted.

"Borrow, pawn, or sell Gu—I want five hundred thousand primeval stones within a day, and every Bai clansman gone from Shang clan city!"

# Chapter 296: Auction

Outside the secret room, Tie Dao Ku waited with growing suspicion until the door finally opened.

Inside, dishes were scattered across the floor around a shattered banquet table.

Fang Yuan had just bound the Bai clansmen with a poison vow Gu agreement.

"What happened?" Tie Dao Ku was bewildered.

"I'll take my leave first. Take care," Fang Yuan said, exiting the room.

"Fang Zheng, wait for me on the battle stage!" Tie Dao Ku growled.

Bai Feng sighed to Tie Dao Ku: "Brother Tie, our Bai clan must withdraw—we can no longer oppose Fang and Bai."

"Don't worry! He relies on Shang clan, but isn't my Tie clan also a southern border overlord?" Tie Dao Ku urged hastily. Dealing with Fang and Bai needed strength in numbers—if Bai clan left, he would be alone!

"Subduing the enemy without fighting—well played, Fang Zheng! But I will not give up," Tie Dao Ku muttered coldly as Bai clan departed.

He decided to register at the battle zone immediately to hunt Fang Yuan on stage!

"Sir, please hold on." A waiter hurried over with a polite smile: "You haven't paid the bill."

"...what?"

To raise the five hundred thousand in a day, Bai Feng borrowed from loan sharks and pawned his core small tornado Gu.

Bai Zhan Lie exclaimed: "Lord Elder, small tornado Gu is your core Gu—are you really pawning it?"

Bai Feng nodded slowly: "With your willingness to sacrifice, Bai clan will surely flourish. Today's disgrace will be repaid a thousandfold in the future, but for now, we must think of the clan. Since I made this decision, I'll take full responsibility once we return to the clan."

Three days later, in a large scale auction.

"Ladies and gentlemen, before you is a rank three frost breath Gu, capable of exhaling freezing air that inflicts frostbite and slows opponents. Its greatest advantage is consuming merely five percent light snow primeval essence per activation. The starting bid is twenty-three thousand primeval stones!" On the round platform, the host spoke through sound amplify Gu.

Fang Yuan looked calmly down at the circular stage.

He sat in a private room, hidden from others' sight—a benefit of his purple thorn token.

After competitive bidding, Fang Yuan bought it for over one hundred eighty thousand primeval stones.

Next, he spent eighty-three thousand primeval stones to buy a white silver relic Gu and golden shield Gu.

All-out effort Gu was his core, but he still lacked bitter strength Gu—an exceedingly rare and expensive Gu in Shang clan city.

"I obtained the white silver relic Gu, but there's no rush to use it. If I were cultivating alone, using it earlier would be better, but with Bai Ning Bing to rely on, I'll save it for later."

"Li Ran confirmed Bai clan has left Shang clan city. Though I extorted three million from them, they cannot be underestimated. Enduring silently with a sacrificial mindset, no wonder they rose abruptly in my previous life. Under the guise of exploring Bai Gu inheritance, they are frantically searching for the spirit spring on Bai Gu mountain. Their progress is far faster than in my past life; I must remain vigilant."

"Bai clan chased me here; can Tie clan be far behind? Tie clan, demon suppression tower... Tie Dao Ku is no concern for now."

As Fang Yuan planned ahead, outside the entrance of Shi Zi restaurant, a grim-faced Tie Dao Ku stood alongside several other Gu Masters, guarding the entrance.

Shi Zi restaurant's banquet was so expensive that, unable to pay, he had to work off his debt.

"An esteemed Tie clan Gu Master reduced to a doorman! I just hope no acquaintances pass by..." Tie Dao Ku prayed.

"Eh! Isn't this brother Tie Dao Ku? What are you doing here?" A voice arrived.

"Aaaah! Fang Zheng, just wait!" Veins bulged on Tie Dao Ku's forehead as he raged inwardly.

# Chapter 297: Instant Success Gu

In the secret room, Fang Yuan summoned Yin Yang dual cloud Gu. Fang Yuan activated canopy Gu, covering his body in white armor.

Simultaneously, he activated all-out effort Gu and casually stretched his arm; a wild boar phantom appeared behind him, expanding into a giant image.

The lightning bolts shot toward the boar phantom like *sharks smelling blood*.

Instantly, the boar phantom was suppressed to the size of a millstone, trapped by circulating lightning like deep blue chains.

Fang Yuan was using Yin Yang dual cloud Gu to gather lightning and strip away one boar strength.

It had been three days since that auction.

He had also purchased materials and refined eating one's words Gu to remove his poison vow Gu.

Fang Yuan had previously used the black and white boar Gu on Qing Mao mountain for two boars' strength.

Rank three all-out effort Gu could only summon one beast phantom at a time. At rank four, stronger beast phantoms became available, making two boars' strength redundant.

Purging the boar phantom normally required nineteen days, but summoning lightning was the most economical method.

Though expelling the boar phantom took time, Fang Yuan now summoned another Gu.

Resembling a palm-sized dragonfly with three wing pairs, this Gu shone in four brilliant colors.

This was the instant success Gu, a rank four consumable Gu that accelerated time.

Fang Yuan divided his mind three ways: managing Yin Yang dual cloud Gu, regulating primeval essence, and fueling instant success Gu.
