As his primeval essence dropped, Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus bloomed, rapidly replenishing his primeval sea.

Within ten-plus breaths, the wild boar phantom was completely destroyed.

After confirming it, Fang Yuan halted instant success Gu and put away Yin Yang dual cloud Gu.

Even dimmed as much as possible, only two pairs of wings remained as its body slowly faded away.

Once activated, this Gu could not be halted; dimming it merely prolonged its remaining duration.

"Again."

Seizing the remaining time, Fang Yuan next activated galloping horse strength Gu.

This rank three Gu modified a Gu Master's body, granting a horse strength phantom.

Under galloping horse strength Gu's effect, Fang Yuan grew numb as the strength law fragment settled into his body.

Normally, this time-consuming process took two to three months.

Inhaling deeply, he released the instant success Gu's restraints, and dazzling light instantly enveloped him.

Fang Yuan gritted his teeth, drenched in sweat after five minutes.

Replacing a boar's strength, he had gained the strength of a horse!

The instant success Gu's light dimmed again; losing another wing, only one pair and a half remained.

Instant success Gu's great drawback cost him three months of lifespan in that short span!

Green bull labor Gu!

Through sheer determination, Fang Yuan endured the process and gained the strength of a bull!

Instant success Gu now had only two tattered wings left, its body becoming translucent.

Breathing in deeply, he forced focus and activated stone turtle strength Gu.

This time, the process lasted only halfway before the light faded and vanished, the instant success Gu entirely spent.

Instant success cost half a year of lifespan, but he purged the boar phantom and gained the strength of a bull, horse, and half a turtle!

A day later, Fang Yuan fought his thirtieth battle stage match.

Thus, Fang Yuan claimed thirty straight wins in the fifth inner city, advancing directly to the fourth inner city!

"A small matter. Brother Tie, what are your plans now?" his friend smiled.

Tie Dao Ku declared with resolve: "Gu Yue Fang Zheng is my target; I won't stop until he's arrested. I'm entering Shang clan's battle stage!"

"What, Fang Zheng went to fourth inner city? Damn it!"

Tie Dao Ku clenched his fists: "Fang Zheng, think you can escape? If you advanced to fourth inner city, so will I! My first match begins today!"

"Having refined ice crystal Gu as my vital Gu and using vitality Gu to suppress its energy backlash, my core is complete. For close range I have ice edge Gu, but for long range, snowball Gu or icicle Gu?" Bai Ning Bing pondered on her way to the battle stage.

"Eh? It's you!" Seeing Bai Ning Bing take the stage, Tie Dao Ku rejoiced: "Great! Heaven has eyes, giving me you in my first match!"

Hearing this, Bai Ning Bing raised her jewel-blue eyes, asking in her usual cold tone: "Who are you?"

# Chapter 298: Bai Ning Bing vs Tie Dao Ku

But Tie Dao Ku recognised Bai Ning Bing.

He had already memorized their faces from the Bai clan arrest warrant.

The battle began, and Tie Dao Ku charged at Bai Ning Bing without hesitation.

Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly and charged toward him.

Iron hand Gu! Hand blade Gu!

Tie Dao Ku's hands turned into iron, his palm edges forming razor-sharp blades.

Ice edge Gu!

Bai Ning Bing pressed her palms together and pulled them apart, forming a pure ice blade.

With a crisp sound, iron hand blade met frosty iceblade.

Crossing five or six steps past him, Bai Ning Bing twisted and flicked her wrist, shooting three icicles at Tie Dao Ku's back.

Growling, Tie Dao Ku spun around and shattered the icicles with his palms, then charged ferociously at Bai Ning Bing again.

Swift shadow Gu!

Tie Dao Ku accelerated, leaving a trail of shadowy afterimages behind him like a tail.

Tie Dao Ku raised both arms high above his head and chopped downward like an axe toward Bai Ning Bing.

Even with ice muscles jade bones, Bai Ning Bing dared not block it head-on and dodged.

Tie Dao Ku rotated his arms like a windmill, slashing hand blades at her.

Creating another iceblade to dual-wield, Bai Ning Bing retreated, blocking Tie Dao Ku's furious assault.

Ice edge Gu, the advancement of rank two iceblade Gu, was sturdier with a sharper edge.

Tie Dao Ku combined rank three iron hand Gu with hand blade Gu, the core of his Gu set.

Whipping up a whirlwind of steel blades, the older and more experienced Tie Dao Ku unleashed relentless fury, forcing Bai Ning Bing back over a hundred steps.

"This cannot continue... frost breath Gu!" Bai Ning Bing channeled primeval essence into her tongue.

Her cheeks puffed out as she exhaled icy wind.

The icy wind enveloped Tie Dao Ku, frosting his arms and chest.

Assaulted by bitter cold, Tie Dao Ku shivered, slowing his attacks.

Bai Ning Bing leapt back, putting distance between them.

A torrent of icicles and snowballs shot toward Tie Dao Ku, forcefully halting his assault.

"He specializes in close combat and struggles at range. But how could I lose in swordplay? Ice crystal Gu!"

As Bai Ning Bing activated her vital Ice Crystal Gu, the sound of freezing water echoed across her body.

Her flesh and blood transformed into a diamond-like crystal form.

Resembling an ice sculpture, her cold expression turned utterly emotionless, her unblinking eyes coated in transparent frost.

She strode forward, charging at Tie Dao Ku with chilling killing intent.

Bai Ning Bing slashed with her iceblades, while Tie Dao Ku fought more nimbly using his arms as blades.

Having turned into ice crystal, Bai Ning Bing's body was so hard that Tie Dao Ku's strikes left only minor scars.

But gradually, Tie Dao Ku felt increasing pressure.

Abandoning defense to focus purely on attacking, Bai Ning Bing's strikes grew increasingly sharp.

"She's using me to hone her sword technique!" Tie Dao Ku was shocked as her strikes grew more precise and forceful under pressure.

His distraction slowed his movements, giving Bai Ning Bing the opening she needed.

"An opening!" Her eyes flashed brilliantly as her right hand slashed toward Tie Dao Ku's head.

Unable to dodge, Tie Dao Ku defended, confident he would be fine as long as his eyes stayed protected.

The iceblade in Bai Ning Bing's right hand exploded intensely.

Caught off guard, Tie Dao Ku watched the explosion tear through his defenses.

He instinctively shut his eyes as agonizing pain struck him.

"A trap!" Tie Dao Ku didn't panic, swiftly activating Swift Shadow Gu to retreat.

Bai Ning Bing pursued him with relentless slashes.

Retreating, he forced his eyes open to find his left eye blinded, his right heavily injured, and his vision dyed red.

"I admit defeat!" He screamed his throat out.

"The battle is decided, both sides cease!" the hosting Gu Master quickly announced.

His healing Gu could not restore his lost left eye; he could only stabilize and try to preserve his right.

# Chapter 299: Shut up, you fat pig!

"I expended seventy percent of my primeval essence..." Victorious, Bai Ning Bing stepped off the stage.

"That battle consumed a huge amount of primeval essence. In a fierce fight, my Gu set clearly has consumption issues."

"If only I had Fang Yuan's Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus; at rank three, I'd never worry about primeval essence expenditure."

"Without the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus, I can only streamline my Gu."

After careful thought, Bai Ning Bing decided to discard Snowball Gu.

"I'll keep Icicle Gu; it restricts opponents and pairs threateningly with Ice Explosion Gu."

"Ice crystal Gu, Ice edge Gu, Icicle Gu, Ice explosion Gu...can this set defeat Fang Yuan?"

"Fang Yuan cultivates the strength path with All-Out Effort Gu, far more domineering than Tie Dao Ku. Winning won't be easy unless I recreate my killer move."

"Today is Fang Yuan's first battle in the fourth inner city. If I go now, I might still catch it!"

Fang Yuan was locked in a fierce duel with a burly Gu Master—whom the spectators identified as Zhu Ba.

Nearly three meters tall, Zhu Ba was covered in fat, resembling an oversized sumo wrestler.

Fang Yuan circled Zhu Ba, unleashing strikes. Each blow made Zhu Ba's fat ripple, dispersing the force across his body. Then, using a Gu, he gathered that dissipated force and rebounded it back onto Fang Yuan as a counter-shock.

"Fang Zheng, it's useless. I reflect eighty percent of your attack force as counter-shock. Your strikes only injure you," Zhu Ba spoke in a muffled voice.

Fang Yuan stepped back, pausing his assault.

Blood seeped from his lips; Zhu Ba's counter-shocks had jolted his organs, inflicting internal injuries.

The shock bypassed all defense to strike his internal organs.

His internal organs were unreinforced; even with Self-Reliance Gu healing them, the injuries continued to accumulate.

Once he paused, the counter-shock dissipated and his organs healed within several breaths.

Stepping forward firmly, Fang Yuan unleashed meteor-like punches, but Zhu Ba's fat reflected the force back.

"Fang Zheng, you take eighty percent of the force while I suffer only twenty percent. Just admit defeat," Zhu Ba urged.

When blood seeped out again, he paused to heal with Self-Reliance Gu.

With bull, horse, and half a turtle strength, Self-Reliance Gu surpassed Flesh-Bone Gu.

"Fang Zheng, there's no point. Surrender, and I promise not to take your Gu worms," Zhu Ba chuckled.

"Shut up, you fat pig!" Fang Yuan suddenly shouted.

The battle stage went silent.

# Chapter 300: Fang Yuan vs Zhu Ba (1)

"Young man, learn some humility. Behave like this, and you'll hit a wall everywhere," Zhu Ba sighed.

Fang Yuan laughed loudly: "Are you concerned, or just afraid to attack? More likely, you can only passively defend against me. Your ability to send counter-shock force to injure me while defending is really amazing but how many Gu are you controlling to maintain such a defense? At least three? Can't concentrate enough to attack while defending? I've seen through your tricks—do you take me for an idiot like those spectators?!"

Zhu Ba's expression darkened: "Ignorant youth, then I'll teach you a lesson about reality."

His whole body curled into an enormous meatball and rolled towards Fang Yuan.

Meanwhile, a faint black blade aura enveloped the giant sphere.

As the giant ball rolled, stone slabs fractured under its crushing mass and sharp blade aura.

"There it is—Lord Zhu Ba's killer move, rolling blade meat!" the spectators cheered excitedly.

Bai Ning Bing's eyes narrowed: "Rolling Meat Gu paired with Blade Aura Gu? Zhu Ba's massive body amplifies his attack strength!"

Facing Zhu Ba's assault, Fang Yuan smiled with a trace of madness.

Abruptly, he charged straight towards the giant ball!

They collided; the meatball's charge slowed while Fang Yuan was sent flying, crashing heavily onto the ground.

Fang Yuan wiped the blood from his lips and sprang back up.

The giant ball rolled at him again; Fang Yuan laughed aloud and charged back.

Though heavily injured, Self-Reliance Gu granted him potent sustainability.

"Still so stubborn? Then die!" Zhu Ba roared and slammed into Fang Yuan.

The two sides collided like meteors.

This time, Fang Yuan's fist sank deep into the meatball as rampant blade aura slashed him like a dark waterfall.

Fang Yuan was hurled backward as the blade aura completely exhausted itself.

Canopy Gu took heavy damage, its white armor flickering weakly before completely dissipating.

Bathed in blood, Fang Yuan was covered in deep wounds. Yet his steel tendons remained largely intact and his iron bones suffered no damage.

Activating Self-Reliance Gu, his bleeding halted instantly as new flesh sprouted and wounds closed.

Bai Ning Bing's eyes flickered in thought: "Without Canopy Gu, winning with just Self-Reliance Gu's recovery will be tough... eh?"

Right then, golden light flared around Fang Yuan.

The light condensed into armor: Golden Shield Gu!

Golden Shield Gu suited his strength path style even better than Canopy Gu.

"Enough, how long will you keep acting?" Fang Yuan stood, seeing victory in reach.

"Ungrateful brat, I'll show you the true gap between us," Zhu Ba said, rolling toward him.

Fang Yuan suddenly charged horizontally and evaded.

After dashing fifty paces, he swiftly pivoted and charged.

Right before impact, he triggered All-Out Effort Gu as a brown bear phantom flashed.

Under the blow, the meatball's flesh undulated like river waves, rebounding the counter-shock into Fang Yuan.

Knocked back several steps, Fang Yuan immediately charged again.

He circled the meatball, unleashing strikes at varied spots to overwhelm Zhu Ba's defense.

Zhu Ba manipulated his Gu frantically until the meatball failed to reflect the force.

Furious, Zhu Ba reverted to human shape and launched blade aura at Fang Yuan.

Triggering horse strength, his speed surged as he charged fifty paces.

Zhu Ba could only watch his blade aura strike empty air behind Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan's punch detonated the air, shattering stone tiles and shaking the stage.

Zhu Ba's fat trembled violently to disperse the impact and reflect the force.

However, Fang Yuan immediately followed with another punch.

Cumbersome and unagile, Zhu Ba took the ruthless blow squarely on his body.

"I... counter-shock!" Zhu Ba's eyes widened as he strained to manipulate multiple Gu.

Having just expended concentration on Blade Aura Gu, prolonged manipulation left his mind exhausted and in disarray.

The rippling fat across Zhu Ba's body froze.

Still dispersing the force from the previous strike, he had to take this punch head-on.

Dizzying pain overwhelmed him as the force detonated throughout his body.

Fang Yuan's gaze flashed; he had waited long for this moment.

Punches flew like the wind as bear, boar, crocodile, and horse phantoms alternated in midair.

Golden-armored Fang Yuan unleashed an onslaught of unrivaled ferocity.

Under astonished gazes, Zhu Ba retreated, howling in pain under Fang Yuan's beating.

Fang Yuan held total dominance as Zhu Ba's eyes began rolling back.

# Chapter 301: Fang Yuan vs Zhu Ba (2)

Ever the veteran, Zhu Ba shouted, threw his arms out, and tightly hugged Fang Yuan.

Wrapped in suffocating fat like dense cotton, Fang Yuan found himself immobilized from all directions.

He punched and kicked into solid flesh, shattering Zhu Ba's bones and mashing the meat beneath his skin.

Zhu Ba gritted his teeth, stubbornly trapping Fang Yuan. Though beaten nearly senseless, his one clear thought warned that letting this monster loose meant certain doom.

Though squeezed tightly, Fang Yuan snorted fearlessly and activated All-Out Effort Gu.

A green bull phantom appeared in mid air.

With enduring bull strength, Fang Yuan extended his arms and pried off Zhu Ba's tightly coiled limbs.

"I... I admit defeat!" He screamed.

The hosting Gu Master immediately manipulated the battle stage to separate them.

"The battle is decided: Fang Zheng wins, Zhu Ba loses!" the hosting Gu Master announced.

Hearing the chatter, Bai Ning Bing shook her head inwardly: "Zhu Ba is a veteran expert, but unlucky to meet Fang Yuan. His style relies on countering attacks. They countered each other in different ways, but the tougher one won!"

"Good job," Wei Yang laughed, congratulating Fang Yuan below the stage.

Patting Fang Yuan's shoulder, he said, "I failed on the strength path, but I see hope in you. Many eyes are on you now—walk this path without hesitation!"

Having just entered the fourth inner city and defeated Zhu Ba, his overnight fame caught many off guard.

No sooner had the battle ended than multiple forced challenges arrived.

These people were mostly strength path Gu Masters, coming after his all-out effort Gu.

The better he performed, the more valuable All-Out Effort Gu appeared and the more greed it drew. His challengers were all fourth inner city experts.

Time passed as Fang Yuan won repeatedly.

Fourth inner city held many experts, but Fang Yuan's growth was astonishing.

Yet he remained vigilant, never underestimating opponents. With Wei Yang's authority and Li Ran's underground network, Fang Yuan gathered all necessary intel before every battle.

Far from a brainless brute, he accepted only four matches a month. Though forceful challenges were allowed, rules granted challenged Gu Masters eight days of rest after each match to prevent intentional targeting.

Three months passed like this.

This day, in the secret room.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing sat opposite each other.

Bai Ning Bing placed both palms on Fang Yuan's back, injecting snow silver primeval essence.

Converted through the flesh bone unity Gu with slight loss, the primeval essence flowed into Fang Yuan's aperture.

From rank three initial stage, he advanced to middle stage!

This was entirely thanks to the flesh bone unity Gu and Bai Ning Bing. Higher ranks brought wider gaps between small realms; Bai Ning Bing's rank three peak stage cultivation helped Fang Yuan immensely.

Sensing Fang Yuan's shifted aura, Bai Ning Bing knew he had succeeded and retracted her palms.

Fang Yuan turned and asked, "You'll reach the fourth inner city battle stage in a few days. Interested in raising your cultivation to rank four?"

The higher Bai Ning Bing's cultivation, the faster his own progressed.

"No need. If you can fight through at rank three, so can I," Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly, giving Fang Yuan an icy look.

Then she added, "My aptitude rose again to ninety-two percent. At this rate, when are you giving me the Yang Gu?"

"Don't worry, I can protect your life. We used the poison vow Gu before—even if you don't trust me, surely you trust the Gu's power?"

Over these months, Fang Yuan had gained turtle strength and used the rank three bronze skin Gu, adding another defensive layer.

With iron bones, steel tendons, bronze skin, and golden shield Gu, Fang Yuan's defense reached initial success, sufficient against any strong foe even in the third inner city battle stage.

Just as Fang Yuan advanced to middle stage, a group arrived outside Shang clan city.

"Shang clan city... Gu Yue Fang Zheng is inside, huh," Tie Ruo Nan thought, walking ahead of everyone.

"Tie Dao Ku greets young master." Tie Dao Ku rapidly paid his respects.

# Chapter 302: Tie Ruo Nan

"Tie Dao Ku, what happened to your eyes?" Tie Ruo Nan asked in surprise, seeing his black eyepatch.

Looking embarrassed, Tie Dao Ku answered truthfully, "My eyes were injured by Bai Ning Bing."

Precisely because of his letter, Tie Ruo Nan brought a team here personally.

Amid the clan's grief, Tie Xue Leng's daughter, Tie Ruo Nan, stepped forward.

After roaming the Southern Border with her father and returning to the clan, she challenged the Tie clan thirteen halls, triumphing in arena battles to become one of Tie clan's eight young masters.

Though young, she was already rank three upper stage, truly living up to her genius reputation.

Straightening up, he said, "I've reserved a garden estate for young master. Please follow me."

Tie Ruo Nan waved her hand. "No rush. Take me to Nan Qiu garden—your report said Fang and Bai are staying there, correct?"

Decisive though Tie Ruo Nan was, she was denied entry moments later.

"Apologies, my masters are cultivating in their secret rooms," Nan Qiu garden's doorboy said respectfully.

She smiled and handed an invitation to the doorboy. "No matter, give them this. I have ties with your masters; we'll return tonight."

After Tie Ruo Nan was turned away multiple times, just after they left, Fang Yuan emerged from his secret room.

"Tie Ruo Nan?" Hearing the servant's report, Fang Yuan gave a weird look.

He knew she was Tie Xue Leng's daughter—hadn't she died on Qing Mao Mountain?

"To think Tie Ruo Nan survived and even became a Tie clan young master." Holding the three invitations, Fang Yuan frowned deeply.

A Qing Mao Mountain survivor who had interacted with both him and Fang Zheng was a grave threat.

"The Qing Mao Mountain truth isn't exposed—judging by her actions, Tie Ruo Nan knows nothing. Otherwise, she wouldn't have brought just this group to arrest me."

"She sent three invitations, showing clear determination to meet. She likely wants to learn what happened back then, since her father died on Qing Mao Mountain."

"If she has suspicions, how do I get rid of her doubts?"

"Worse, if exposed, how do I salvage it? Shang Yan Fei investigated my ordinary aptitude while only my brother had A grade—how do I explain my changed talent?"

"If it comes to that, I'll sell the blood skull Gu to Shang Yan Fei. He's collecting the Blood Sea true inheritance; with it, he could raise Shang Xin Ci's aptitude. He dotes on her enough to have used Man Triumphing Heaven Gu to change her fate—he won't reject this deal."

Yet unless pushed to the absolute worst situation, Fang Yuan would never sell the blood skull Gu.

Fang Yuan did not know that Fang Zheng was still alive and rapidly growing stronger.

# Chapter 303: I really want to kill her

Tie Ruo Nan had assumed Fang Yuan possessed the Desolate Ancient Moon physique. Having learned much about the flaws of ten extreme physiques in Tie clan, she believed Fang Yuan had died long ago.

On Tie Ruo Nan's fourth visit, she finally met Fang Yuan.

In the living room, only Fang Yuan and Tie Ruo Nan remained, the rest dismissed.

"To think we would meet under such circumstances," Tie Ruo Nan remarked.

Fang Yuan sighed even more emotionally. "The past is the past—let it stay that way. I don't wish to speak of it again."

"No, some things cannot be forgotten. I came regarding what happened back then. You must know how my father died—tell me the truth!"

He sighed: "Every thought of Qing Mao Mountain aches. You lost a father, but I lost my entire clan and family, forced to wander the wilds. Though Shang clan city is good, it remains a foreign land, not home."

Saying so, his eyes turned red.

Seeing Fang Yuan like this, Tie Ruo Nan's steel gaze softened.

"You killed a young master of my clan. If I hadn't intervened, you would already be facing our clan's trial," Tie Ruo Nan changed the subject.

"A life for a life, it is perfectly justified, no?" Tie Ruo Nan's expression turned solemn.

With a cold smile, Fang Yuan replied, "Tie Ruo Nan, we're no longer naive children. Tie clan has no standing here—the only reason you haven't acted is the purple thorn token in my hand, isn't that so?"

Tie Ruo Nan replied frankly, "The purple thorn token is the main reason. But it only protects you in Shang clan city; once you leave, we won't let you go. If you tell me the truth behind what happened back then, I promise that as long as I live, Tie clan will not pursue this matter."

"The truth is nothing much. As Tie clan's young master, can't you already guess?" Fang Yuan lowered his head, watching her secretly.

The girl smiled. "Even if you don't say it, I already have an idea."

Fang Yuan's tone changed: "You know?"

Tie Ruo Nan sighed softly. "The ten extreme physiques are no secret to top-tier clans. Freezing all of Qing Mao Mountain in ice was beyond even a rank five Gu Master. Yet I never expected your brother possessed the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique rather than the Desolate Ancient Moon physique."

"Wait... no wonder the Gu Yue clan leader protected me back then. Did they think my cultivation speed was due to a ten extreme physique?" Everything began to make sense.

"If she believes that, the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique is my perfect cover. She has never doubted my real identity. As long as I don't slip up..."

Thinking of this, Fang Yuan's face showed a complicated and painful expression.

Seeing his expression, Tie Ruo Nan felt confirmed in her guess and softened her tone: "Fang Zheng, I know your pain is complicated. The one who ruined your home and slaughtered your entire clan was your own brother, Fang Yuan..."

Fang Yuan nodded, as tears fell silently.

Seeing this, Tie Ruo Nan could not force him further, remarking dejectedly: "When I rushed back to Qing Mao Mountain and saw it buried in ice, my heart was in turmoil. I know my father was killed by Fang Yuan. Were he still alive, I would avenge my father, but he is already dead..."

Fang Yuan snorted with displeasure. "Fang Yuan was still my brother, and he's dead. What more do you want?"

Tie Ruo Nan's eyes gleamed. "I want to know some things. Back then, my father received a mysterious letter whose contents I know. It claimed Gu Yue village held a Blood Sea inheritance, prompting my injured father to rush to Qing Mao Mountain immediately. Did you and Bai Ning Bing know about it?"

Fang Yuan shook his head: "If I obtained it, I would've used it already. Why would I suffer so much on this journey?"

Tie Ruo Nan stared meaningfully at Fang Yuan. "The Blood Sea inheritance brings endless disasters. The demonic Blood Sea Ancestor left this vile legacy, and ultimately, my father's death was caused by it. Fang Zheng, if you really received this inheritance, please hand it over to me so I can resolve some of my regrets."

Tie Ruo Nan turned solemn. "According to reports, you possessed a Gu radiating a strong blood aura, which you used for ranged attacks. Why did you stop using it after converting to the strength path?"

"You suspect I took the Blood Sea inheritance and concealed it? Hmph, you mean this Gu?"

Fang Yuan willed the blood moon Gu from his aperture and tossed it to Tie Ruo Nan.

"This is my clan's blood moon Gu—didn't you see my brother use it before? During the explosion, our clan leader and Bai clan leader sacrificed their lives to protect Bai Ning Bing and me. The clan leader handed me his remaining Gu; while many starved during our travels, this blood moon Gu survived because it was easy to feed."

Inspecting the blood moon Gu, Tie Ruo Nan visibly relaxed: "So that's it. Your clan leader was courageous and noble, sacrificing himself to protect the bloodline!"

After a long pause, Tie Ruo Nan spoke: "I will bring Tie Dao Ku back. Tie clan will no longer pursue this matter. Rebuilding your clan through Shang clan is a sound path, and I wish you success."

With that, the young girl stood up and left decisively.

"I really want to kill her in advance..." Fang Yuan felt helpless regret.

# Chapter 304: Bitter Strength Gu

The river of time flowed ceaselessly as over a year passed.

"Failed again..." In the eerie darkness, Fang Yuan sighed softly.

Blood seeped from his nose as the failed Gu refinement dealt a soul backlash, leaving him dizzy.

"A year and a half has passed, and I've advanced to the third inner city, yet I still haven't bought bitter strength Gu."

Second, Shang Ya Zi managed the shops and secretly obstructed Fang Yuan from the shadows.

Unable to purchase bitter strength Gu, Fang Yuan could only attempt refining it himself.

Fortunately, continuous victories in the battle stage earned enough resources to sustain the cost of failed Gu refinements.

Fang Yuan meditated until hearing a knock outside, then opened the door to welcome Bai Ning Bing.

Over the past year and a half, Bai Ning Bing had progressed rapidly to the third inner city with a complete set of Gu worms. Matching Fang Yuan with few defeats, they were hailed as the battle stage's two rising stars and drew widespread attention.

Sitting cross-legged on the opposite cushion, Bai Ning Bing pressed her palms to Fang Yuan's back, channeling snow silver primeval essence.

In contrast, Fang Yuan advanced rapidly, now only half a step from rank three upper stage.

"Once I reach upper stage, I'll use the white silver relic Gu to advance straight to rank three peak stage, catching up to Bai Ning Bing and easing Spring Autumn Cicada's pressure on the aperture."

Fang Yuan pondered: "At rank three peak stage, Bai Ning Bing's primeval essence won't help me much. Yet from our long interaction and subtle shifts in her aura, I sense she isn't far from rank four."

Seven days later, Fang Yuan received good news from Wei Yang.

"A rare disaster destroyed Ju clan at Ju Feng Mountain. Ju clan remnants submitted to Shang clan city and are auctioning their collections for livelihood, so an auction will be held soon."

Wei Yang added meaningfully, "Brother Fang, luck is on your side. My investigation found that a bitter strength Gu will be auctioned."

"Crucially, everyone in Shang clan city values this auction, so young master Shang Ya Zi cannot easily play tricks," Wei Yang added.

"Having saved nearly a million primeval stones over this year and a half, I have a great chance of buying the bitter strength Gu."

"What, Ju Clan has a bitter strength Gu?" In a study, Shang Ya Zi slowly furrowed his brows.

"Still, Fang Zheng... I won't let you get the bitter strength Gu so easily. I'll join the auction and make sure you suffer a huge loss!"

# Chapter 305: Auction (1)

Seven days later, the auction was held.

The auction hall was packed with no empty seats, and the private rooms on the upper floors were sold out as well.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing, using their purple thorn tokens, snatched a private room for themselves.

Shang Pu Lao, Shang Suan Ni, and Shang Bi Xi entered, smiling as they talked.

Shang Bi Xi was short and fat, but also a strength path Gu Master whose core turtle tire Gu granted the strength of ten turtles.

"To help Shang Xin Ci ascend, we cannot compete with the three factions. Though she can cultivate, her forcefully awakened aperture is only C grade—she has no foundation at all."

"The annual young master assessment offers Shang Yan Fei's other children a great chance to rise. In my memory, Shang Xin Ci only became a young master after six years. That is too long; to use her, I must elevate her as soon as possible. This year's assessment is our chance," Fang Yuan calculated.

Shang Xin Ci and Shang Chi Wen held hands and spoke as they entered the auction house.

"Brother Hei Tu, Sister Bai Yun," Shang Xin Ci greeted, unable to conceal her joy and excitement.

"Fang Zheng, I came uninvited—are you going to chase me out?" Shang Chi Wen laughed, following Shang Xin Ci inside.

"How could I? Please take a seat," Fang Yuan smiled.

# Chapter 306: Auction (2)

"I checked—the bitter strength Gu is the thirteenth item. But Shang Ya Zi is in the fifth private room and reportedly withdrew all his assets. You must be careful, Brother Fang Zheng," Wei Yang said upon entering, delivering crucial news.

Wei Yang patted Fang Yuan's shoulders, still feeling worried: "Shang Ya Zi is not here for fun, he has at least one million and two hundred thousand primeval stones on hand. Bitter strength Gu is a treasure for strength path Gu Masters. Besides Shang Ya Zi, your main rivals are Shang Bi Xi and Ju Kai Bei. Watch out for them."

"I have some spare funds, use them for now." Wei Yang handed Fang Yuan a primeval elder Gu.

"Say no more, take it. It's a loan, not a gift—I'm your creditor now!" Wei Yang laughed, forcing the primeval elder Gu into Fang Yuan's hands before he could refuse.

Across from them, Shang Xin Ci also produced a primeval elder Gu.

"Brother Hei Tu, this is mine."

"Refusing any further would be hypocritical. I accept, and I will definitely repay you later!" Fang Yuan laughed, cupping his fists as he took the primeval stones.

"Sorry to keep everyone waiting, the auction officially begins now!" the young, beautiful auctioneer announced from the stage through a sound amplify Gu.

A rank four Gu shaped like an ice-blue drain fly, with a long, needle-like mouth.

"When activated, Frost Arrow Gu gathers frost energy to shoot an ice arrow at the enemy. Its greatest advantage is low primeval essence consumption, allowing even rank three Gu Masters to use it. Starting bid: seventy thousand primeval stones!" the auctioneer introduced.

"A hundred and ten thousand. " Bai Ning Bing said.

"A hundred and twenty thousand," Shang Ya Zi called from the fifth private room.

Bai Ning Bing glanced at Fang Yuan: "A hundred and fifty thousand."

"A hundred and fifty thousand for a Frost Arrow Gu... hmph, suffer then!" In the fifth room, Shang Ya Zi sneered and stopped bidding.

"Shang Ya Zi is clearly here with a vengeance," Wei Yang sighed.

# Chapter 307: Auction (3)

"Since the eighth item, Shang Ya Zi has interfered whenever I bid, staying silent when I do nothing. He came purely to oppose me," Fang Yuan noted with glinting eyes.

"Our twelfth item is the Chasing Wind Gu, a rank four movement Gu that grants wind-chasing speed. Starting bid is a hundred and eighty thousand," the auctioneer's clear voice echoed through the hall.

Eventually, Elder Yi Bu Hui bought the Chasing Wind Gu for four hundred and forty thousand.

Chasing Wind Gu contained wind Dao marks; using it alongside his beast phantoms would disrupt and weaken its effect, making it an unsuitable choice for Fang Yuan.

"If this were the rank four Fairy Wind Gu, I would buy it, but it is merely a Chasing Wind Gu."

Fang Yuan possessed a recipe using Fairy Wind Gu, Seven Shining Stars Gu, and other materials to refine Fixed Star Gu—the core ingredient for Fixed Immortal Travel Gu.

Dream Travel, Carefree Travel, Fixed Immortal Travel, Liquor God Travel, these were the four great movement Gu.

All of them were rank six Gu.

Dream Travel allowed one to enter dreams; Carefree Travel excelled at dodging attacks; Fixed Immortal Travel enabled teleportation anywhere. Liquor God Travel was the most unique, first appearing in The Legends of Ren Zu.

Fang Yuan had his previous life's memories, thus he had the carefree travel Gu and fixed immortal travel Gu's recipes. Fang Yuan needed Fixed Immortal Travel Gu most, but with an abysmal refinement success rate and insufficient materials, he had to set it aside for now.

"Alright, for our thirteenth item, it is a... bitter strength Gu!"

"I will have this bitter strength Gu—no one can stop me! Two hundred thousand primeval stones!" Fang Yuan shouted before the auctioneer could even give the starting bid.

Fang Yuan's haste displeased Ju Kai Bei: "Youngsters are so impatient. I bid two hundred and fifty thousand."

"Five hundred thousand!" Fang Yuan reported a frightening price.

"Youngsters sure have vigor," Ju Kai Bei sighed, bowing out. Half a million primeval stones could buy several other useful Gu.

"Hold on, five hundred and ten thousand," Shang Ya Zi called out slowly from his room right before the hammer fell.

"Hmph, you think you can stop me, Shang Ya Zi? Six hundred thousand," Fang Yuan immediately retorted with disdain.

Shang Ya Zi sneered: "I'll play along, Fang Zheng. Six hundred and ten thousand."

"Seven hundred thousand!" Fang Yuan shouted. "Shang Ya Zi, you're a defeated loser who hasn't learned his lesson!"

Shang Ya Zi retorted: "Complete bullshit, we'll see who teaches who a lesson! Seven hundred and ten!!"

Amid the crowd's murmurs and speculation, their bidding war escalated to eight hundred and ten thousand.

"With me here, you are bound to lose, Fang Zheng. Give up!" Shang Ya Zi gloated, raising the bid by ten thousand each time just to infuriate him.

"You think I fear you? What is eight hundred and ten thousand?" Fang Yuan snorted.

"Young master, that is enough, we should stop now," a servant cautiously advised.

Shang Ya Zi hesitated: "I know. Fang Zheng is desperate for it! I'll bid once more after him and stop. That idiot will pay double the market price, and I'll finally vent this anger. Hahaha!"

"This time, I'll let you off. You can have the bitter strength Gu."

Servant: "..."

Shang Ya Zi: "..."

# Chapter 308: Auction (4)

"Haha, Shang Ya Zi's face must be green now!" In the ninth room, Wei Yang laughed uncontrollably. "But Brother Fang Zheng, are you really fine letting the bitter strength Gu go?"

Fang Yuan laughed: "I need the bitter strength Gu, but I'm no fool to pay eight hundred and ten thousand primeval stones. I'd rather refine it myself—that sum affords many attempts."

"Failure rates are high, and it will harm your body, Brother Hei Tu," Shang Xin Ci worried.

Fang Yuan shook his head slowly; he had other plans he could not disclose.

"The thirty-second item—rank four Atmosphere Gu," the auctioneer announced.

After introducing it, the auctioneer declared: "Atmosphere Gu, starting bid two hundred and sixty thousand primeval stones."

"Five hundred thousand!" Fang Yuan shouted.

The crowd was silent.

"Five hundred thousand for an Atmosphere Gu? Trying to con me again, Fang Zheng? Do I look like an idiot!" Shang Ya Zi gnashed his teeth in fury.

Having just spent eight hundred and ten thousand primeval stones, spending another half million would bankrupt him.

"Five hundred thousand, going once... twice... thrice... sold!" the auctioneer announced.

Just as Fang Yuan lost interest, the final forty-ninth item was displayed.

"Our grandest treasure today is neither a Gu worm nor refinement material, but a recipe," the host introduced.

She added: "Because of its extreme value, this recipe has not yet been appraised."

Seeing the crowd's rapt attention, the auctioneer dropped a bombshell: "This recipe is for the Heavenly Essence Treasure Monarch Lotus."

The auctioneer continued: "Since everyone knows of it, I need say no more. This recipe details refining the Heavenly Essence Treasure Monarch Lotus. Starting bid: five hundred thousand!"

"Hold on! Does this recipe require the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus as refinement material?" Fang Yuan shouted at the top of his lungs.

Forced to comply, she admitted: "Though recipe details are normally confidential, in keeping with Shang clan's integrity, it indeed requires Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus as the primary ingredient."

"Six hundred and seventy thousand." Wei Yang placed the final bid, securing the recipe.

---

# Chapter 309: Xin Ci’s aspiration

With Wei Yang securing the Heavenly Essence Treasure Monarch Lotus recipe, the auction concluded.

"It's rare for us all to gather—stay, let me host a feast," Fang Yuan urged.

"Brother Fang Zheng, here is the recipe." After three rounds of wine, Wei Yang handed Fang Yuan a Heart Engraved Gu.

Fang Yuan had spent six hundred and seventy thousand primeval stones on it, having Wei Yang bid to avoid suspicion.

Fang Yuan didn't take the Gu, saying: "Since you've already refined it, Brother Wei Yang, activate it for me so I don't need to refine it again."

"That works." Wei Yang nodded, channeling his primeval essence.

With a soft crackle, the Heart Engraved Gu transformed into pink light that flowed into Fang Yuan's heart and vanished.

Instantly, the refinement recipe for Heavenly Essence Treasure Monarch Lotus surfaced in Fang Yuan's mind.

Fang Yuan felt pleased: "My Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus is only rank three. Useful now, but its value will plummet once I reach rank four. With this recipe, refining the Heavenly Essence Treasure Monarch Lotus in the future will be of tremendous help."

"Though rare and precious, Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus isn't uniquely infamous like Blood Skull Gu. Otherwise, I couldn't have bought this recipe so openly. With my Gu set nearly complete and my cultivation advancing, buying this recipe poses little risk."

"Fang Yuan bought the recipe because he has the lotus—that makes sense. But why give up Bitter Strength Gu for Atmosphere Gu?"

"If only I were a young master. Shang clan young masters can request three Gu worms annually, sourced by the clan free of charge," Shang Xin Ci sighed.

Fang Yuan patted her shoulder with a smile: "No matter. Perhaps Shang Ya Zi will hand the bitter strength Gu over himself."

"Second Brother became a laughingstock after paying eight hundred and ten thousand for it. Expecting him to hand it over willingly is impossible..." Shang Chi Wen shook her head, dismissing it as fantasy.

"Has Brother Hei Tu already devised a plan?" Shang Xin Ci's eyes brightened.

Fang Yuan pointed confidently at Bai Ning Bing: "Everything hinges on Ning Bing."

"Me?" Bai Ning Bing was stupefied.

"You'll find out in two or three days. Please wait until then," Fang Yuan laughed.

He looked solemnly at Shang Xin Ci: "Xin Ci, do you really want to become a young master? You will enter the political whirlpool and lose your freedom. You understand Shang clan's situation better than I do. The young masters' competition is intense and bloody. If you want to become one, you will enter this dangerous conflict."

Under Fang Yuan's gaze, Shang Xin Ci smiled: "Big Brother Hei Tu, do I really have freedom even now? I am completely helpless in this."

"The caravan journey was perilous. I've realized the world is an ocean, and we are small boats upon it. Only with high cultivation and influence can a small boat become large enough to weather hardships and shelter those we care about."

"Great. You have aspiration." Wei Yang laughed.

"Haha, she is right. In that case, we will lend a hand and help you become a Shang clan young master," Fang Yuan laughed in relief.

"Hehehe, this year's young master examination has passed, so Sister Xin Ci must wait until next year. However, the competition is fierce. Father has many children; with only one position available each year, there are hundreds of competitors," Shang Chi Wen chuckled, advising Shang Xin Ci.

"Xin Ci, do not worry. With my help, there's no need to wait for next year—I'll make you a young master this year!" Fang Yuan laughed.

"There is no need for Brother Wei Yang's help. The plan was made two years ago, and it is all thanks to Ning Bing," Fang Yuan smiled.

"Eh?"

Instantly, the gazes of everyone concentrated on Bai Ning Bing.

Becoming the center of attention, Bai Ning Bing maintained an indifferent expression, but inwardly she grew suspicious and cursed secretly.

---

# Chapter 310: You shameless scoundrel!

In the blink of an eye, three to four days passed.

"Bitter strength Gu..." In his study, Shang Ya Zi held the Gu with furrowed brows, fighting an overwhelming urge to crush it to death.

"No, I must salvage my reputation. I have to change father's and the clansmen's impression of me!" Shang Ya Zi gritted his teeth, resolved.

"Fang Zheng, you dared to oppose me and play me for a fool. I'll make you pay a terrible price!" His eyes gleamed with sinister intent as he plotted revenge.

The door opened and the servant knelt in terror: "Young master, there is trouble! A rumor has spread all over Shang clan city. It says you lost Lady An Yu to Fang Zheng, and the grudge led you to target him."

The servant continued: "They also say you made a secret deal with Fang Zheng to pass your yearly assessment, and... faked the accounts."

"What?!" Shang Ya Zi shrieked, jumping from his seat and knocking over the brush and ink on his table.

The servant added anxiously: "The rumor includes the exact transaction time and amounts. The clan's internal affairs hall has been alerted and is sending Gu Masters to investigate!"

"Huh?!"

"I'm finished. Once the fake accounting is exposed, I will lose my young master position. Violating clan rules means even mother cannot protect me, and begging father is useless! Without this position, countless people will devour me."

Nan Qiu garden, the pavilion in the middle of the lake.

Bai Ning Bing played a move, then looked out at the lake: "Shang Ya Zi is so slow-witted. I spread the rumor long ago, why hasn't he come yet?"

"No worries, I instructed the guards to let him in; he will come," Fang Yuan smiled, exuding complete control.

Bai Ning Bing sighed: "Fang Yuan, you exploited a loophole in the poison vow Gu. You planned this two years ago, patiently awaiting the right time."

Fang Yuan played a chess piece and smiled: "Shang Ya Zi is petty and will definitely seek revenge. Why leave such a threat around? I didn't use it earlier because Shang Xin Ci had just arrived and lacked a foundation. Replacing Shang Ya Zi with her benefits both of us."

"Fang Zheng, get out here now!" Shang Ya Zi's furious screams finally arrived.

"Fang Zheng, you still have the mood to play chess? Do you know how far the rumors have spread? Did you do this!" Shang Ya Zi shouted furiously, pointing at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan turned and spoke plainly: "If I did it, would I still be alive? Shang Ya Zi, have you forgotten we took a poison vow together?"

Shang Ya Zi snorted, his anger cooling slightly. Fang Yuan was right; had he leaked it, he would be dead. Sitting there unharmed meant he wasn't the culprit.

"Although I didn't spread the rumor, Bai Ning Bing did."

"It was you! You bitch, I'll kill you!" he screamed, glaring at Bai Ning Bing with bloodshot eyes like a ravenous beast.

"Oh? You want to strike here? Attack me?" Bai Ning Bing slowly stood, her stunning face icy and tone full of disdain: "I am rank three peak stage, undefeated on the battle stage, and hold a purple thorn token. Do you really want to fight me to the death?"

"Fang Zheng, you went back on your word! How does she know about our deal? Wait, if you broke it, why are you still alive? Did you disable the poison vow Gu?" Shang Ya Zi looked at Fang Yuan in uncertainty.

"No, no," Fang Yuan shook his head. "The poison vow stated we couldn't divulge information to an 'unknowing third party." But before using the Gu, I had already told Bai Ning Bing. Thus, she was a 'knowing third party.' The rumor was spread by her, not me. I haven't violated the poison vow at all.""

"Fang Zheng, you shameless scoundrel! How dare you deceive me like this! You are despicable scum!"

---

# Chapter 311: Actually, I am a kind person

Shang Ya Zi breathed raggedly, growing angrier with every curse.

"Ah!" he howled, unable to suppress his rage, and flipped the stone table.

Chess pieces flew as the table nearly hit Fang Yuan's leg, but he dodged in time.

Shang Ya Zi stumbled back three steps, dizzy as two streams of blood leaked from his nose.

"Calm down, Shang Ya Zi. The poison vow forbids harming me. Thank me for dodging—had the table hit my leg, your backlash would be far worse," Fang Yuan laughed lightly.

Bai Ning Bing frowned: "Shut up, or I'll throw you out. You're a young master now, but not for long. The internal affairs hall is investigating; your days are numbered."

Seeing Shang Ya Zi's turmoil, Fang Yuan said: "Shang Ya Zi, I am actually a kind person; I always repay kindness with gratitude and hatred with revenge. If you hadn't repeatedly targeted me and snatched the bitter strength Gu at the auction, why would I target you?"

"I admit the poison vow had a loophole. But in two years here, did I ever use it? If you hadn't gone overboard, why would things come to this? You caused this yourself. I let you in to make it clear: Shang clan is powerful, but you are merely a young master—I don't fear opposing you. Leave, I do not wish to make your life harder."

"Shang Ya Zi, why are you still here? Forget it, I won't bully a pitiful soul like you. Let's go, Bai Ning Bing—Ju Kai Bei and Yan Tu are fighting today. To dominate the battle stage, we must face them; this is a good chance to scout them," Fang Yuan signaled to Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing stood and feigned leaving; after two steps, Shang Ya Zi spoke.

"Please stay here." His voice was coarse.

He summoned the bitter strength Gu, holding it out with trembling hands.

"Fang Zheng, I was wrong earlier. Take the bitter strength Gu as an apology." Though deeply reluctant at first, as he spoke, he felt a huge burden lift from his shoulders.

"This is the bitter strength Gu?" Bai Ning Bing stopped, glancing back.

Fang Yuan laughed: "Shang Ya Zi, do you think I cannot survive without the bitter strength Gu? Though hard to refine, with perseverance I would obtain it eventually. Do you think you can provoke me at will and reconcile when convenient? Fighting and making peace whenever you please—is the world so easy? I, Gu Yue Fang Zheng, have my dignity—are you trying to humiliate me?"

Shang Ya Zi added anxiously: "I swear I won't cause trouble. We can be allies. Aside from bitter strength Gu, I can offer further compensation. Primeval stones, Gu worms, refinement materials—I manage the shops and can give them to you."

"Is that so..." Fang Yuan's expression relaxed.

"I'll keep the bitter strength Gu while I consider it." In the end, Fang Yuan made no decision.

"Brother Fang Zheng, I am completely sincere. Please don't take too long—the investigation is happening in days. Without my position, I cannot compensate you."

Shang Ya Zi left relieved. Watching him go, Fang Yuan told Bai Ning Bing: "All right, you can report the case to the internal affairs hall now."

Bai Ning Bing nodded, thinking: *Tonight, I must reread the vow I made with Fang Yuan word for word!*

A few days later.

Shang Yan Fei placed his paperwork down expressionlessly.

The report from internal affairs hall detailed Shang Ya Zi's fake accounting.

"Revoke his young master status and banish him to the slave capture group for three years." Shang Yan Fei raised his brush and wrote Shang Ya Zi's punishment.

"Fang Zheng, Bai Ning Bing..." Shang Yan Fei's eyes gleamed brightly.

A bright red paper crane Gu flew out.

"Scheming against my son... as his father, I must avenge him. This is your punishment, but also a test," Shang Yan Fei muttered.

The swift paper crane Gu flew into a secret room.

"Clan leader wants us to forcefully challenge Fang Zheng and Bai Ning Bing?"

One tall as a tower and the other thin as a matchstick, they looked at the letter and each other wordlessly.

They were rank four Gu Masters Ju Kai Bei and Yan Tu!

Both were rank four initial stage, dominating the battle stage as rivals who swore never to leave until defeating the other.

In truth, they were Shang Yan Fei's secret pawns.

Through them, Shang Yan Fei controlled the battle stage!

---

# Chapter 312: Rank Three Peak Stage once again

Nan Qiu garden, secret room.

Cross-legged on mats, Bai Ning Bing pressed her palms to Fang Yuan's back, transferring snow silver primeval essence.

Fang Yuan guided the surging snow silver essence, supplemented by blossom silver, to continually cleanse his aperture walls.

The rippling water membrane aperture wall shuddered under the cleansing. As quantitative change led to qualitative change, light froze and settled into a solid stone membrane.

With the stone membrane formed, Fang Yuan advanced to rank three upper stage.

Cultivation complete, Bai Ning Bing withdrew her hands, complex emotions stirring in her blue eyes.

"This guy is a treacherous fox. Just because I haven't found a flaw in the poison vow doesn't mean none exists." Bai Ning Bing remained silent, thoughts racing.

"Don't worry, my cooperation is sincere. There are no issues with the poison vow," Fang Yuan said, as if reading her mind.

"Hmph." Having her thoughts read, Bai Ning Bing's eyes chilled as she sneered, "I hope so."

Just yesterday, Ju Kai Bei and Yan Tu had simultaneously issued forced challenges to Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing.

Behind him, Bai Ning Bing stood up.

"Tomorrow is your match with Yan Tu. Do you need my heavenly essence treasure lotus? By our vow, I will do my best to help you," Fang Yuan spoke slowly from the darkness.

"No need." Bai Ning Bing coldly replied.

"So what if he's rank four? You aren't the only one who improved these past two years." Bai Ning Bing turned and left.

After she left, the corners of Fang Yuan's lips curled into a smile.

"Rejecting the heavenly essence treasure lotus... self-doubt has grown in her heart. She may not even realize this insecurity yet. Bai Ning Bing, you are still too inexperienced..."

He took out white silver relic Gu.

Four hours later, Fang Yuan leapt from rank three upper stage to rank three peak stage.

"Since rebirth, I've reached rank three peak stage once again!" Fang Yuan's eyes shone as he clenched his fists, joy surging in his heart.

After recuperating for over two years, its condition was rapidly recovering.

Its recovery naturally caused great pressure on Fang Yuan's aperture.

"I cannot relax yet. Spring Autumn Cicada's recovery is accelerating; my cultivation speed must surpass it, or disaster will repeat."

Time quietly passed and soon it was the next day.

The audience eagerly awaited the battle between Bai Ning Bing and Yan Tu.

On stage, Bai Ning Bing and Yan Tu stood opposite each other.

Yan Tu was an old man with long, unkempt hair.

"Hehehe, little girl, you have such smooth skin, you're really beautiful," Yan Tu rasped, sizing up Bai Ning Bing.

White-robed with blue eyes and silver hair, Bai Ning Bing frowned as killing intent filled her. "Old geezer, stop your shamelessness."

"Little girl, you have no manners." Yan Tu laughed dryly, rubbing his fingers together. "It seems I must teach you to respect your seniors."

"Pointless banter." Bai Ning Bing's expression was cool and gaze icy as she stared at Yan Tu, every movement brimming with fighting spirit.

The sound of the bell signalled the start of the match.

Almost simultaneously, Bai Ning Bing and Yan Tu made their moves.

Four white icicles and three fire pellets shot toward each other.

An icicle and fire pellet collided in midair; the icicle melted, but the weakened pellet continued toward Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing's icicle Gu was rank two, whereas Yan Tu's fire pellet Gu was rank three.

She leaped agilely, dodging the fire pellets.

Three icicles flew towards Yan Tu.

Yan Tu laughed dryly, stepping forward and twisting his body into a bizarre posture to dodge the icicles before breaking into a run.

Pointing his index finger repeatedly, he unleashed a dense barrage of fire pellets toward Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly; refusing to yield, she stomped repeatedly and retaliated with icicles.

For a moment, the two clashed across the vast grassland, ceaselessly dodging and trading icicles and fire pellets.

# Chapter 313: Bai Ning Bing vs Yan Tu

Among the crowd, Fang Yuan watched the match calmly.

Wei Yang and Shang Xin Ci stood beside him.

"Bai Ning Bing is fighting forcefully. Even with icicle Gu, firing so many will quickly deplete her primeval essence," Wei Yang worried.

"There's a difference between rank three and rank four primeval essence. But Yan Tu is also burning essence with fire pellet Gu, and he only has B grade aptitude. Bai Ning Bing is relying on her A grade essence recovery speed to contend with his rank four cultivation." Fang Yuan saw through her tactic at a glance.

As they spoke, the situation on the stage suddenly shifted.

"Little girl, you sure like hopping around." Yan Tu sneered, raising his right hand to claw at Bai Ning Bing from dozens of steps away.

Flames roared as a giant fiery claw instantly formed and flew toward Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing immediately activated her movement Gu to pull away.

However, Yan Tu cackled and triggered his own movement Gu, quickly closing in.

The fiery claw repeatedly descended, swiping at her.

The fiery claw grazed past Bai Ning Bing's shoulder and hit the grassland, igniting a raging fire that quickly spread.

Regaining her footing, Bai Ning Bing fired frost arrow Gu from her pinky.

The arrow struck the fiery claw, damping its raging flames.

She activated icicle Gu while secretly triggering ice explosion Gu.

The icicles exploded, extinguishing the fiery claw.

Seizing the opening, Bai Ning Bing formed twin ice blades and charged, slashing at Yan Tu.

"Good blades!" Yan Tu smiled strangely and snorted, sending two fiery snakes flying from his nostrils.

One fiery snake intercepted her twin blades, while the other slithered around to attack her from behind.

Bai Ning Bing looked solemn, her icy gaze focused. The immense pressure from the fiery snakes only stoked her fighting spirit further.

Fiery snake Gu was rank four; pincered by two rank four constructs, Bai Ning Bing gradually fell into a disadvantage.

"Those fiery snakes restrict her options. Whenever they take damage, Yan Tu simply expends primeval essence to heal them. She can't keep this up," Wei Yang said with furrowed brows.

She used frost breath Gu and frost arrow Gu one after another.

The continuous frost aura slowed and restrained the fiery snakes' offense.

However, this barrage sharply increased her primeval essence expenditure.

"Little girl, stop struggling," Yan Tu laughed, firing off fire pellets.

Some pellets forced Bai Ning Bing to waste energy dodging, while others merged into the fiery snakes, reinvigorating them.

Bai Ning Bing's dark blue eyes flashed with resolve.

Transforming into a humanoid ice crystal, her silver hair froze rigid while frost sharpened and chilled her twin blades.

"Going all out now, little girl?" Yan Tu spoke, but his smile soon froze.

Encased in ice crystal with attack and defense united, Bai Ning Bing charged fearlessly through the fiery snakes straight at Yan Tu.

Yan Tu immediately mobilized a fiery snake to block her.

Bai Ning Bing breathed out blue frost.

The fiery snake immediately shrunk from this frost breath aura.

Bai Ning Bing pressed her charge. "Old man Yan Tu, fight me if you have the guts!"

Yan Tu turned grave; lacking close-combat skills, engaging her awe-inspiring ice form up close would spell trouble.

Bai Ning Bing's heart sank.

In ice crystal form, her movement Gu was less effective than before.

She fired repeated frost arrows, hoping their chill would slow him down.

The frost arrows failed to hit Yan Tu, only allowing the gap between them to widen.

Ice crystal Gu could not be maintained long-term, or the user would permanently turn into an ice sculpture.

Thus, Bai Ning Bing used vitality Gu alongside it to maintain her life force.

However, even vitality Gu could only prolong her transformation time.

Yan Tu knew this well from his investigations; dragging out the match would guarantee his victory.

Bai Ning Bing stopped.

"I can't hit this old guy. Only that move can change the situation now."

Her eyes flashed with a trace of hesitation.

# Chapter 314: Victory and Loss (Double chapter)

Bai Ning Bing hesitated only a moment before her blue eyes shone with resolve.

"Ice blade storm, reappear once more." Bai Ning Bing sighed softly.

She raised her arms, sharp ice blades extending from her limbs.

Vitality Gu circulated warm currents through her internal organs, preserving her life force against total crystallization.

Swirl Gu activated, sending Bai Ning Bing rotating rapidly.

Tornado Gu whipped up an expanding whirlwind around her.

Ice edge Gu, ice crystal Gu, swirl Gu, and tornado Gu—Bai Ning Bing was managing four Gu at once.

Bai Ning Bing exhaled and frost aura instantly pervaded her surroundings.

The frost aura mixed into the tornado, changing it into an icy tornado.

At this moment, Bai Ning Bing was doing five tasks at once!

Such was the world-defying talent of the ten extreme physiques, surpassing even Fang Yuan in multitasking.

The two fiery snakes mindlessly charged the tornado, their heads and bodies severely shredded by the icy storm.

The fiery snakes hurriedly retreated.

The burning patches of grassland were already being snuffed out by the pervasive frost.

Yan Tu's eyes narrowed at the sight, and he recalled the fiery snakes into his nose.

He had seen her ice blade storm before, but its power had now jumped to a whole new level.

"Ordinary moves will only waste primeval essence. I'll meet her killer move with my own!" Yan Tu resolved.

Yan Tu spurted out torrents of fuel oil.

Fuel oil cascaded onto the grassland in surging waves, submerging the battlefield within seconds.

The fiery snakes landed, instantly igniting blazing flames.

The flames spread swiftly, turning the entire stage into a sea of fire.

The two fiery snakes swam joyfully through the flames, circling the ice blade storm without rushing to attack.

"I'm not done yet—fiery claw Gu!" Yan Tu shouted.

He unleashed all four of his fiery claw Gu at once.

Instead of charging the storm, the four fiery claws flew toward the snakes and merged with them.

Instantly, both fiery snakes grew pairs of claws.

Snakes with claws were no longer snakes but dragons!

Rearing their heads with soundless hisses, the fiery dragons pounced from both sides.

The fiery dragons struck the icy storm, tearing with their claws and coiling their bodies around it.

Eventually, the battered fiery dragons could endure no more and retreated.

Submerging into the sea of fire, they absorbed flames and instantly recovered their power.

"The storm's wind flow was severed. Bai Ning Bing must start kiting!" Wei Yang said gravely.

Bai Ning Bing was wise enough to know she shouldn't fight head-on without moving, yet she remained stationary.

"It seems, this killer move is still imperfect..." Fang Yuan inwardly speculated.

Right now, Bai Ning Bing was powerless to dictate her movement!

Lacking forward momentum, the ice blade storm could only spin in place—an awkward limitation.

Multitasking five Gu had pushed her mind to its absolute limit, leaving no energy to adjust.

Fortunately, Yan Tu had still not discovered this.

Nevertheless, both of their primeval essence reserves were rapidly dwindling.

As time ticked by, the ice blade storm slowed and its wind flow weakened.

Yet the fiery dragons remained fierce, sustained by the sea of fire and Yan Tu's yellow gold primeval essence.

Had she accepted Fang Yuan's heavenly essence treasure lotus, she would have had the strength to fight on.

"It's over," Yan Tu laughed, willing the two fiery dragons to merge into a single colossal beast.

Opening its maw, the fiery dragon slashed at the ice blade storm with all four claws.

But suddenly, the storm exploded.

Bai Ning Bing furiously unleashed frost breath Gu, freezing all four fiery claws!

The ice blade storm violently expanded, swallowing most of the fiery dragon's body.

"What?!" Yan Tu paled in shock, realizing her earlier weakness was merely a feint.

He immediately willed the fiery dragon to retreat.

Severely wounded, the dragon lost two claws and shrunk to less than half its size.

The ice blade storm suddenly dispersed completely.

Bai Ning Bing dismissed her ice crystal form, returning to mortal flesh with an empty aperture.

"I admit defeat." She coldly announced.

"She exhausted her last primeval essence on that final explosion to heavily damage Yan Tu," Fang Yuan realized.

Realizing this, Yan Tu raged, but a formless force suppressed him from above.

The hosting Gu Master stepped onto the ruined stage and announced the result.

By rule, the winner had the right to demand a Gu from the loser.

"I want your ice crystal Gu," Yan Tu demanded venomously.

"Take it." Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly, surrendering the ice crystal Gu.

As ice crystal Gu was her vital Gu, blood seeped from her facial orifices upon losing it.

Yan Tu looked sour; claiming her rank three Gu could not cover his losses.

"A pity. Had Bai Ning Bing listened to my advice and chosen frost demon Gu back then, things would be much better," Wei Yang lamented.

Ice crystal Gu suited men, whereas frost demon Gu suited women.

"Losing ice crystal Gu might be her chance to switch to frost demon Gu," Wei Yang said expectantly.

# Chapter 315: Hard Qi Gu

Fang Yuan's match with Ju Kai Bei was set seven days later, drawing immense attention after Bai Ning Bing's performance.

Bai Ning Bing, however, went missing.

"Did something happen to her?" Shang Xin Ci was worried.

"Don't worry, I know her. She's prideful, so after such a defeat, she needs time alone," Fang Yuan comforted Shang Xin Ci.

Shang Xin Ci said with concern, "Can you not fight? Ju Kai Bei is as renowned as Yan Tu. Ju Kai Bei is a strength path Gu Master. If Brother Hei Tu loses all-out effort Gu..."

Fang Yuan smiled. "Which is why I cannot lose. I'll enter seclusion for a few days to refine a Gu. Head back first."

Ju Kai Bei was a rank four initial stage Gu Master on par with Yan Tu; Fang Yuan estimated only a thirty percent chance of victory.

"If this refinement succeeds, my chances rise to sixty percent! I can only hope it works."

Fang Yuan entered the secret room and began refining.

First inner city.

Shang Yan Fei quietly watched churning colorful smoke replay the match between Bai Ning Bing and Yan Tu.

"Fang Zheng is next. What surprise will he bring? Hmm, I heard rumors of a heavenly essence treasure lotus..."

"Fang Zheng cannot unleash the lotus's true worth. It would serve Xin Ci far better."

After pondering upright for a moment, Shang Yan Fei dispatched a paper crane secret letter.

Ju Kai Bei and Yan Tu soon responded, gathering in the secret room.

"Master sent another secret letter!" Yan Tu glanced at the unfolded letter and handed it over. "It's for you."

Ju Kai Bei muttered, "Master suspects Fang Yuan holds a heavenly essence treasure lotus and wants me to demand it if I win, forbidding me from choosing all-out effort Gu."

He had intended to claim the legendary all-out effort Gu, an irresistible prize for a strength path master.

"Master cherishes talent and wishes to protect Fang Zheng's growth," Yan Tu analyzed.

Yan Tu advised, "Brother Ju, mind your mindset. As Shang clan's hidden elders, we must obey the clan leader."

Yan Tu patted his shoulder. "Fang Zheng is as renowned as Bai Ning Bing. They arrived and live together; their relationship is certainly unusual. Known as the battle stage's twin stars or the black and white demons, Fang Zheng won't be weaker than her. Brother Ju, your strongest moment is also your weakest—be careful."

Ju Kai Bei proudly smiled: "Brother, guess what this is."

He took out a Gu from his aperture.

"Eh, hard qi Gu!" Yan Tu gasped in delight. "Wonderful, Brother Ju! Your defense drops when using your killer move, so this rare Gu will patch your flaw. Nurture it well."

Ju Kai Bei nodded and put away the hard qi Gu, sighing: "A pity it is only hard qi Gu and not strength qi Gu. With strength qi Gu, my battle strength would qualitatively transform and surge two to threefold..."

Yan Tu laughed: "Ancient qi path has disappeared, and strength qi Gu is extinct. Be content with hard qi Gu."

# Chapter 316: Absolute Victory, Demonic Heart

Seven days passed, and the battle stage hosted its largest match in a long time.

Fang Yuan walked slowly into the battle stage, looking calm.

This was a super-size battle stage, black stone forest terrain.

Thick, sturdy black stone pillars stood throughout the forest terrain.

"This is the final battle." Fang Yuan sighed in his heart.

With the three kings inheritance starting, no time remained.

Winning this was crucial to helping Shang Xin Ci become a young master.

When Shang Xin Ci became a young master, Fang Yuan would leave Shang clan city.

Ju Kai Bei arrived on the stage too.

Though rank three peak stage, breath concealment Gu made him appear middle stage.

Refining that Gu had raised his winning chances to sixty percent; Fang Yuan was confident.

Five hundred years of experience had forged this mindset in Fang Yuan.

*Winner takes all, climbing over corpses to the apex! I may let the world down, but justice, integrity, fame, fortune, beauty, hatred, and kindness will never obstruct me—they are merely tools to use.*

Bai Ning Bing pursued victory, but Fang Yuan possessed a heart of absolute victory!

As the bell rang, the battle began.

Light burst from Fang Yuan's eyes as he injected primeval essence, summoning a horse phantom behind him.

Empowered by horse strength, Fang Yuan activated vertical crash Gu.

Upon reaching Ju Kai Bei, the horse phantom vanished, replaced by a boar phantom.

Horse strength accelerated his charge while boar strength enhanced impact upon arrival.

Seeing Fang Yuan charge, Ju Kai Bei opened his mouth expressionlessly.

Amid his clean white teeth, two fangs rapidly lengthened, protruding like elephant tusks.

No blood flowed; instead, pure white armor formed at the contact point.

"Good timing." Ju Kai Bei did not dodge, stepping forward with bent waist to take Fang Yuan's charge head-on.

A loud crashing sound, and the two's shoulders collided.

Fang Yuan retreated five steps, while Ju Kai Bei flew backward.

"Weird, why does Fang Zheng have rank three peak stage cultivation?!"

Fang Yuan's attack broke breath concealment Gu's effect, instantly exposing his rank three peak stage aura to the crowd.

Ju Kai Bei rose from the ground largely unharmed, with only his shoulder armor cracked and broken.

Ivory armor Gu was rank four, but required time to grow its rigid plating.

Fang Yuan had not even used golden shield Gu; bronze skin, iron bones, and steel tendons alone gave him extraordinary defense.

"This Ju Kai Bei has immense strength and defense, the strongest foe I've faced since rebirth." Fang Yuan's gaze turned dark and eerie as he pounced again.

The two traded punches blow for blow, with Ju Kai Bei at a disadvantage.

Fang Yuan gave him no room to grow, repeatedly shattering the ivory armor with iron fists before it could fully form.

Yet Ju Kai Bei's strength grew; though initially suppressed, he gradually matched Fang Yuan.

Unsurprising, as Fang Yuan already knew from intel that Ju Kai Bei possessed rank four habitual strength Gu.

Fang Yuan punched Ju Kai Bei's chest while Ju Kai Bei chopped a palm into Fang Yuan's shoulder.

Fang Yuan's fist went numb, whereas Ju Kai Bei's ivory armor merely showed a few cracks.

With continued movement, Ju Kai Bei's ivory armor thickened steadily, turning his defense monstrously tough.

# Chapter 317: Fang Yuan vs Ju Kai Bei (1)

Fang Yuan and Ju Kai Bei fought head-on, exchanging moves after moves.

A dragon-elephant phantom suddenly rose behind Ju Kai Bei with tremendous might.

Ju Kai Bei possessed three dragon-elephant huge strength beast phantoms.

Fang Yuan's pupils shrank, and he swiftly raised both arms in defense.

Ju Kai Bei's bowl-size fist ruthlessly struck Fang Yuan's arms.

A massive force erupted; Ju Kai Bei merely stepped back, while Fang Yuan was sent flying.

Fang Yuan flew back nearly thirty steps, crashing through two stone pillars before stopping.

Fang Yuan stood unharmed, protected by iron bones, steel tendons, and bronze skin.

Expressionless, Fang Yuan charged at Ju Kai Bei once more.

Snow silver primeval essence continually poured into all-out effort Gu.

Boar, brown bear, crocodile, green bull, horse, stone turtle, white elephant, and black python—eight beast phantoms flashed in succession.

With dragon-elephant strength spent, Ju Kai Bei's power reset, allowing Fang Yuan to seize the initiative.

However, every time dragon-elephant phantom appeared, it would send Fang Yuan flying.

As injuries mounted, Fang Yuan's bronze skin lacerated under Ju Kai Bei's immense strength.

Fang Yuan flashed behind Ju Kai Bei and struck.

Unable to block in time, Ju Kai Bei spun and swept his arms.

With an elephant trumpet, the dragon-elephant phantom appeared and thoroughly suppressed the brown bear phantom.

Fang Yuan was instead sent flying. This was the eighth time.

Two dragon-elephant phantoms manifested simultaneously behind Ju Kai Bei.

His twin fists struck Fang Yuan's chest ruthlessly, blasting him far away.

Fang Yuan coughed up a mouthful of blood midair, his vision blurring.

He smashed through five stone pillars and hit the ground, veiled in rising dust.

Soon, spectators saw a dark figure slowly rising from within the smoke.

"Three fractured ribs and internal bleeding. What a powerful strike..."

Intense pain assaulted Fang Yuan, yet the corners of his lips curved into a smile.

"Only defeating powerful foes is truly interesting! Hahaha!" Head held high, Fang Yuan laughed unrestrainedly.

Indifferent until now, Ju Kai Bei's expression changed for the first time upon hearing Fang Yuan's crazed laughter!

"This kid..." Ju Kai Bei's expression turned grim, feeling both familiarity and strangeness from that laughter.

This was the aura of the demonic path!

It held razor-sharp killing intent and a crazed desire to trample all creation.

The laughter seemed to drag Ju Kai Bei back into his past.

Endless slaughter, walking alone in darkness—a demonic existence worse than death.

Sick of that life, Ju Kai Bei had fled ashore to Shang clan from that bitter sea.

Though safely ashore, Fang Yuan's laughter rolled like a tide, dragging his gaze back toward that bitter sea.

This laughter evoked a trace of fear in Ju Kai Bei!

Ju Kai Bei's face twisted sinisterly, deep hatred toward Fang Yuan welling from his heart.

Ju Kai Bei took the initiative, charging like a tiger.

Dragon-elephant phantom appeared again.

Fang Yuan met it head-on as the stone turtle phantom soared behind him.

The two fiercely collided.

Ju Kai Bei retreated five steps in shock, while Fang Yuan did not budge an inch.

Gritting his teeth, Ju Kai Bei lunged forward again.

With a loud crash, he was sent flying like a sandbag before hitting the ground.

Struggling to his feet, he stared fixedly above Fang Yuan.

In the air, brown bear phantom and boar phantom slowly disappeared.

"Using two beast phantom at once!"

# Chapter 318: Fang Yuan vs Ju Kai Bei (2)

"What Gu allows him to summon two beast phantoms at once?" Ju Kai Bei's expression turned grave as he stared at Fang Yuan, pupils contracting: "Come!"

Expressionless with eyes dark as miasma, Fang Yuan charged at Ju Kai Bei.

Simultaneously, horse and boar phantoms appeared above his head.

Boar strength enhanced impact while horse strength boosted speed.

This time, he dodged of his own accord and brushed past Fang Yuan.

Brushing past, Fang Yuan cancelled all-out effort Gu, checking his momentum against a pillar.

As the black stone pillar collapsed, Fang Yuan's momentum stopped.

Fang Yuan pivoted with horizontal charge Gu as bear and elephant phantoms surged.

Caught unprepared at point-blank range, Ju Kai Bei took the violent hit and was sent flying.

Ju Kai Bei rose to resist, but Fang Yuan's twin phantoms erupted like a rampaging lion, their ferocious roar making spectators' hearts palpitate.

Ju Kai Bei's towering frame smashed through several thick stone pillars like a burlap sack amidst rising dust.

Spurting another mouthful of blood, he struggled to rise, but Fang Yuan was already upon him without pause.

Fang Yuan charged with twin beast phantoms.

"Damn it!" Lacking time to dodge, Ju Kai Bei gritted his teeth and raised his massive arms to block.

Two dragon-elephant phantoms rose behind him, overshadowing Fang Yuan's attack.

Fang Yuan slammed into Ju Kai Bei; Ju Kai Bei merely budged back, while Fang Yuan was hurled away.

Two beast phantoms could overpower one dragon-elephant, but would lose against two.

"Finally, two dragon-elephants..." Ju Kai Bei exhaled turbid air, shaken by Fang Yuan's violent strength and only regaining his footing with two dragon-elephants.

Habitual strength Gu summoned phantoms unpredictably, resetting his accumulated power each time a dragon-elephant was unleashed.

Ju Kai Bei refocused, lunging forward with dragon travel tiger steps Gu.

Fang Yuan coughed up blood from the blow, his iron-hard arm bones fracturing.

Despite the intense pain, Fang Yuan grinned and laughed.

Brown bear, white elephant, and green bull—three phantoms erupted together!

Ju Kai Bei was blasted flying, a gaping hole torn through his ivory chest armor. Spurting blood, Ju Kai Bei smashed through black stone pillars and crashed into the dirt, rising hurriedly through gritted teeth.

Ju Kai Bei stared fixedly at Fang Yuan, gritting out: "Bitter strength Gu!"

Rank four bitter strength Gu was the ideal match for strength path Gu Masters; the heavier their injuries and pain, the greater the strength they unleashed.

"To think he has bitter strength Gu! Didn't Shang Ya Zi buy one at the auction? Is this that same Gu?" Yan Tu deduced.

"If it isn't Shang Ya Zi's, Fang Zheng toyed with him at the auction. But if it is the one Shang Ya Zi bought, the implications are terrifying! Shang Ya Zi was recently ousted as young master over fake accounts involving Fang Zheng. This bitter strength Gu might be Fang Zheng's spoils of victory! The kid is far from simple—Brother Ju, be careful..."

Ju Kai Bei felt deeply unsettled, his resolve waning. The more Fang Yuan fought and the heavier his injuries, the stronger he became. Crucially, Fang Yuan possessed eight beast phantoms; if unleashed together, even Ju Kai Bei's three dragon-elephants would be overpowered.

Worse for Ju Kai Bei's morale, he knew Fang Yuan possessed self-reliance Gu.

All-out effort, bitter strength, and self-reliance Gu formed a rock-solid foundation.

Fang Yuan's planned Gu set had achieved initial form; its remarkable synergy was already giving rank four Ju Kai Bei endless trouble.

# Chapter 319: Fang Yuan vs Ju Kai Bei (3)

"Even with bitter strength Gu, he can unleash at most eight beast phantoms. Bitter strength Gu cannot raise strength indefinitely."

"Shang clan leader forbade taking all-out effort Gu, so I can only choose this bitter strength Gu!" Ju Kai Bei's eyes brightened at the thought.

"I haven't lost yet! At rank four initial stage, I hold the advantage in primeval essence, plus I have my killer move."

The two began exchanging moves again.

Ju Kai Bei's ivory armor repeatedly shattered and reformed as he coughed up blood from time to time.

Fang Yuan fared no better; restraining self-reliance Gu left him bloodied with fractured bones.

The heavier his injuries, the stronger he became, soon unleashing five beast phantoms.

Ju Kai Bei fell into dire straits, suppressed almost to the point of being unable to lift his head.

"Junior, you're too arrogant!" Ju Kai Bei roared, sweeping his arms.

Three dragon-elephants!

He erupted suddenly as huge dragon-elephant phantoms manifested behind him.

Fang Yuan was sent flying.

Fang Yuan's arms were completely fractured by the impact; his left arm bent at a right angle with bone protruding through bloody flesh.

Gritting his teeth against the pain, Fang Yuan activated self-reliance Gu.

He first healed his right arm, then snapped his left arm back into place and set the broken bones with self-reliance Gu.

While healing, Fang Yuan multitasked by evading Ju Kai Bei's strikes with horizontal charge Gu and vertical crash Gu.

Maximizing the stone forest terrain, Fang Yuan stayed out of reach despite Ju Kai Bei's superior speed.

Once healed, Fang Yuan wheeled around and counterattacked.

As time passed, the battle grew more frantic; Fang Yuan's phantoms steadily climbed from five to six, seven, and finally eight!

Yet Ju Kai Bei held his ground, his dragon-type Gu resonating similar Dao marks to trigger dragon-elephant phantoms more frequently.

Even Fang Yuan's iron body strained under eight beast phantoms; sweat poured like rain as his muscles ached with numbness.

Summoning eight phantoms simultaneously meant his injuries were perilous.

Fang Yuan was walking a tightrope; a hit from Ju Kai Bei's three dragon-elephants could prove fatal!

From start till now, it had already been over two hours.

"Why has his primeval essence not been exhausted?"

Even the rank four strength path Gu Master Ju Kai Bei had little primeval essence left.

"Could he really have a heavenly essence treasure lotus?" Ju Kai Bei recalled Shang Yan Fei's secret letter.

"If Fang Zheng really has it, his luck is simply heaven-defying!" Ju Kai Bei gritted his teeth, his fighting spirit dampening as his expected advantage vanished.

"I can't lose! I must use my killer move!" Ju Kai Bei resolved.

Dragon travel tiger steps Gu!

His sudden retreat immediately drew Fang Yuan's attention.

Dragon travel tiger steps far surpassed horizontal charge and vertical crash Gu. Pursuing him across this terrain was disadvantageous.

Extremely nervous, he explained hurriedly: "Ju Kai Bei's killer move has a flaw. If Fang Zheng exploits it, he could clinch victory! If not, Ju Kai Bei will win."
