Body, heart, and mind combined; essence, qi, and spirit merged!

Ju Kai Bei's physique tripled in size, a white aura enveloping him as his domineering presence surged.

This was his killer move — *Djinn transformation!* It tripled the Gu Master's strength and mental energy.

His three dragon-elephant strength tripled into nine dragon-elephant strength!

Nine dragon-elephant phantoms would crush the eight beast phantoms, handing Fang Yuan certain defeat.

However, the ivory armor covering his body shattered under his increased size.

"He is completely defenseless now. His strongest moment is also his weakest! Seize this chance!" Wei Yang mumbled with clenched fists, eyes wide.

As if hearing Wei Yang, Fang Yuan moved to attack, but suddenly stopped.

The broken ivory armor began regrowing, tightly covering Ju Kai Bei's body once more.

Fang Yuan's inaction caught Ju Kai Bei off guard after preparing to counter an assault.

Because, just earlier, he had activated hard qi Gu.

Hard qi Gu enveloped him in formless air. Though appearing defenseless, his defense was as solid as a boulder.

Had Fang Yuan attacked, he would have met Ju Kai Bei's counterattack and likely lost.

Ju Kai Bei had even slowed his armor's regeneration to bait Fang Yuan, who merely watched.

"Did he notice? Impossible. Hard qi Gu is formless, and I have never revealed it."

"Hmph, since you won't attack, I will." Bright light flashed in Ju Kai Bei's eyes.

Djinn transformation was formed from three rank four Gu, rapidly draining both his yellow gold primeval essence and mental energy.

Dragon travel tiger steps!

Ju Kai Bei charged at Fang Yuan.

Horizontal charge, vertical crash!

Fang Yuan retreated and avoided the attack.

Was Fang Yuan's sudden retreat an attempt to stall for time?

"My stride is three times longer, and dragon travel tiger steps makes me even faster!" Ju Kai Bei scoffed inwardly.

With almost all black stone pillars destroyed and djinn transformation crushing everything in its path, the terrain was completely flat to Ju Kai Bei.

# Chapter 320: Tear through the sky

Severely injured, taking a hit from Ju Kai Bei's nine dragon-elephant strength would be fatal for Fang Yuan.

Being slower than Ju Kai Bei, Fang Yuan's only way out was a strong long-range attack.

"Fang Zheng, you lost." Ju Kai Bei strode forward, knowing Fang Yuan's weak blood moon Gu could not stop him.

Yet Fang Yuan's lips curled into a faint smile.

His dark eyes gleamed coldly — it was time!

Eight phantoms instantly appeared behind him.

He activated his Gu worm and pointed forward.

Over forty percent of his aperture's snow silver primeval essence vanished instantly!

A mystical change occurred: the beast phantoms manifested from Dao marks solidified from illusory to real.

Roaring loudly, boar, brown bear, crocodile, green bull, horse, stone turtle, white elephant, and black python descended, charging Ju Kai Bei.

The boar charged, bear smacked, crocodile bit, bull gored, horse stomped, turtle suppressed, elephant struck with tusks, and python coiled!

Amid rising smoke and flying rocks, Ju Kai Bei was surrounded and pummeled by the eight phantoms!

Unable to withstand the assault, his ivory armor shattered into flying fragments while cracks spread across the rest.

A moment later, the eight phantoms became illusory again and vanished.

As smoke cleared, Ju Kai Bei knelt on one knee, clutching his head and panting heavily.

"It's finally over?" He lowered his arms, staring at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan held two primeval stones, rapidly recovering his primeval essence.

With A-grade aptitude, the heavenly essence treasure lotus, and dual-absorbing primeval stones, his primeval essence had already recovered to seventy percent.

He unclenched his teeth, spitting out blood and broken teeth.

"Had I not desperately activated ivory armor Gu, I might have fainted..." Ju Kai Bei felt lingering fear, having nearly exhausted his yellow gold primeval essence.

Ju Kai Bei had over ten broken bones; with primeval essence running low, he could only heal the most crucial injuries.

Even so, Fang Yuan had taken a risk: absorbing primeval stones while controlling the beast phantoms had temporarily reduced his precision over his Gu.

"Was that... strength qi Gu?" Ju Kai Bei slowly stood up, his hoarse voice tinged with bitterness.

Glad for the pause to absorb primeval essence, Fang Yuan replied: "You guessed right, it is strength qi Gu."

Beast phantoms were immaterial traces of the Great Dao, unable to attack directly.

However, strength qi Gu allowed a Gu Master to create formless *Qi of Strength.*

Attaching to this qi of strength, the beast phantoms could leave Fang Yuan's body to attack directly.

Once the qi vanished, the phantoms dissipated and returned to his body.

"There is no need to continue. I surrender." Realizing Fang Yuan possessed strength qi Gu, Ju Kai Bei conceded.

With this Gu, Fang Yuan gained a powerful long-range attack.

Dominant in both melee and ranged combat — this was the true ancient strength path!

Ju Kai Bei relaxed his guard after activating djinn transformation, an opening Fang Yuan exploited to severely injure him.

The hosting Gu Master stepped forward to announce the result.

# Chapter 321: Shang Clan City Uproar

Regarding strength qi Gu's origin, Fang Yuan spread rumors directing suspicion toward the Gu Yue clan.

"A title? Many call him and Bai Ning Bing the Black and White Demons, but even more call Fang Zheng the Little Beast King."

Controlling eight beast phantoms with a ferocious, head-on fighting style, the title Little Beast King suited Fang Yuan perfectly.

Bai Ning Bing was also discussed: "Bai Ning Bing is formidable; after losing to Yan Tu, she broke through to rank four initial stage in her next match! Reaching rank four at her age is monstrous talent. Given her close ties with Fang Zheng, she might forfeit to help him."

Yet at the peak of the fervor, Fang Yuan abruptly announced his retirement from the battle stage.

Sensing something amiss, Shang Yan Fei quickly settled the matter.

Though he secretly controlled the battle stage through Ju Kai Bei and Yan Tu, it could never be exposed.

A few days later, Fang Yuan publicly announced he would assist Shang Xin Ci in claiming a young master position.

Immediately, everyone's attention shifted from the battle stage to the young master competition.

With Shang Ya Zi stripped of his position over fake accounts, a vacancy had opened.

Shortly after, Bai Ning Bing also retired from the battle stage.

Backed by two experts, Shang Xin Ci became the second-favorite contender for the young master seat.

Before her, Shang Yi Fan was considered the top contender for young master.

Shang Yi Fan was rather short, with an aquiline nose, narrow eyes, and a sharp gaze.

"Shang Xin Ci..." Sitting in his study, he muttered with an uncertain gaze.

It was none other than Shang Ya Zi.

"Brother Yi Fan, I came to help you. With Fang and Bai, Shang Xin Ci is your biggest rival. Beware of Fang Zheng especially; he is scheming and devious, and caused my downfall!"

# Chapter 322: Competitive Pressure

Shang Ya Zi said: "I lost, but not completely. Father exiled me to the slave capturing group for three years, but I will return. Before that, I have things for you."

Saying so, Shang Ya Zi handed documents to Shang Yi Fan.

Shang Yi Fan examined them, finding records of Shang Ya Zi's secret assets, spies, and trusted aides.

"Brother Yi Fan, you are the most likely among us to become young master. I am lending you these resources to secure victory. Use them freely, and return them when I come back in three years," Shang Ya Zi sighed.

Shang Yi Fan stood and cupped his fists: "I won't forget your help, brother Ya Zi. Once I become young master, I will repay you tenfold."

After their conversation, Shang Yi Fan saw Shang Ya Zi out.

Seeing he had left, Shang Yi Fan's smile turned cold.

"Wanting me to preserve your forces? I'll use this chance to absorb all your assets—*borrowing without returning*."

Yet thinking of Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing darkened his expression.

Shang Yi Fan analyzed to himself: "Shang Xin Ci's greatest advantage is father's affection, but under open competition governed by clan rules, father cannot cheat blatantly."

"Even with their help, so what? The young master position is mine!" Shang Yi Fan licked his lips, his spirits lifting at an idea.

At Nan Qiu garden, Wei Yang explained to Shang Xin Ci, Fang, and Bai: "Shang clan's rules on successors are extremely rigid."

"To become young master, Lady Xin Ci must pass the traditional Shang clan test, which is exclusively focused on trading."

"Each competitor receives a start-up of a hundred thousand primeval stones. After three months, whoever earns the most wins."

"Then, how much do we need to earn to win this competition?" Fang Yuan asked.

"Normally, three hundred thousand eliminates most competitors, while six hundred thousand gives a high chance of winning. Seven to eight hundred thousand is historically a first-class result. But..." Wei Yang paused.

"This time, a competitor named Shang Yi Fan is backed by a major faction through his mother, Lord clan leader's cousin, making him the favored contender. With his mother's secret aid, he will reach at least six hundred thousand. Lady Xin Ci will need even higher earnings to beat him."

Shang Xin Ci was quiet for a moment before asking: "What is the best result in Shang clan history?"

"Wei Yang was stunned: "Of course it exceeded eight hundred thousand, reaching nine hundred thousand. But very little people in history managed this, the total is less than twenty. Our young clan leader Shang Tuo Hai caused a commotion with eight hundred and ninety thousand, but your father reached nine hundred and twenty thousand, entering Shang clan records.""

Wei Yang paused again: "The all-time record is one million one hundred and ten thousand by Shang Gui Cai, a monstrous talent who unfortunately possessed a ten extreme physique..."

"Nine hundred and twenty thousand, one million one hundred and ten thousand..." Shang Xin Ci's eyes shone as she clenched her fists.

Blessed with sharp business acumen, she was a willing competitor rather than merely being pushed by Fang Yuan.

Wei Yang advised them: "All eyes are on this competition, so do not attempt shortcuts. Cheaters have never succeeded in the past."

Bai Ning Bing frowned: "Sextupling our capital in three months is easier said than done."

Shang Xin Ci smiled with bright, confident eyes: "I have a plan that will surely yield six hundred thousand if executed."

"Oh, what plan?" Bai Ning Bing asked.

Looking at Fang Yuan, Shang Xin Ci shared her idea: "Watching brother Hei Tu's matches inspired me — we can trade in intelligence."

# Chapter 323: Start of Three Kings Inheritance

"Yes, an intelligence business. Shang clan's battle stage hosts over a hundred battles daily, offering immense opportunity. We can collect and sell intel on the participating Gu Masters. We could even hire prominent figures for pre-match predictions and post-match analyses," Shang Xin Ci explained with a smile.

"Brilliant!" Bai Ning Bing cheered.

Even Fang Yuan looked at her in surprise.

He had utilized this concept at Wu clan's grander battle stage in his previous life, causing a huge sensation and earning massive daily profits.

While Fang Yuan drew on memories from Earth, Shang Xin Ci conceiving the idea natively demonstrated her extraordinary talent in commerce.

Although this idea was good, Fang Yuan shook his head.

Shang Xin Ci asked: "Does brother Hei Tu think this is bad?"

"The idea is good, but unfeasible. Xin Ci, do you trust me?" Fang Yuan looked at her deeply.

"Brother Hei Tu saved my life, of course I trust you."

As an insider, she knew full well that Fang Yuan was the sole cause of Shang Xin Ci's misery.

Fang Yuan nodded: "Then trust me. In twenty days, we will triple your primeval stones."

"Really?" Shang Xin Ci said curiously, "Then I will follow your lead."

*"What scheme is he cooking up now? Who is his next victim?"* Bai Ning Bing eyed Fang Yuan, wondering inwardly.

San Cha mountain lay between Zuo clan's Leng Zhan mountain and Che clan's Fei Lai mountain.

Over the past century, both expanding clans fought to occupy San Cha mountain.

During the latest clash between the two clans, the entire San Cha Mountain trembled violently, sending rocks cascading down!

Amid shocked cries, three giant pillars of yellow, blue, and red light erupted from San Cha Mountain's three peaks, shooting high above the clouds.

Then, a voice echoed to everyone on San Cha Mountain—

"Three Kings Inheritance opens today, regardless of righteous or demonic, only fate matters."

*Three Kings inheritance! All three kings were rank five Gu Masters—this is truly their inheritance!*

Three days later, in Shang clan city.

Placing a document lightly on the table, brilliance shone across Fang Yuan's eyes.

"Three kings inheritance has finally opened..."

The date matched his previous life, proving that although rebirth could alter history, major events were not easily swayed.

"With the Three Kings inheritance open, time is short. Once Shang Xin Ci becomes young master, I must set off."

Unlike the single Flower Wine and White Bone inheritances he claimed before, this was three collective inheritances in one place.

Obtaining all three would multiply Fang Yuan's strength, supply most of the rank four Gu he needed, and free him from resource worries until rank five.

However, this demonic inheritance was perilous, requiring six months of bloody sacrifices before anyone reached the end in his past life.

"News of the Three Kings inheritance cannot be hidden; how could those towering light pillars be concealed?"

Fang Yuan knew the pillars were the entrances, but entering required specific conditions.

"Now, I can finally hoard dog enslavement Gu, paper crane Gu, and exploding egg Gu," Fang Yuan planned.

Five days later, Fang Yuan bought massive quantities of the three Gu, spending Shang Xin Ci's hundred thousand primeval stones plus three hundred thousand received from Bai clan.

Of the three million primeval stones Fang Yuan had extorted from Bai clan, nearly half was already paid.

With Bai Ning Bing's funds added, Fang Yuan's purchasing plan succeeded completely.

# Chapter 324: Earning a fortune

"Shang Xin Ci used all her funds on these three Gu worms?" In his study, Shang Yi Fan clutched a report.

"Dog enslavement Gu, paper crane Gu, exploding egg Gu—what use do they have?" Shang Yi Fan frowned, finding no explanation.

"Shang Xin Ci gave the entire hundred thousand to Fang Zheng. Reckless buying like this only harms them. Did I overestimate them?"

Yet Shang Yi Fan's unease lingered. He felt he was missing something, sensing a deep scheme behind this nonsensical bulk purchase.

Just then, the old steward called from outside: "Young master, something major has happened!"

"Come in, Old Zhang," Shang Yi Fan urged quickly.

Old Zhang entered, looking grim: "Young master Yi Fan, bad news from San Cha Mountain—someone discovered how to enter the inheritance."

Intrigued, Shang Yi Fan asked, "What is needed to enter the Three Kings inheritance? Wait, don't tell me..."

Old Zhang gave a bitter laugh: "Entering requires three specific Gu. The yellow pillar for King Quan's inheritance requires a rank one dog enslavement Gu. To enter the blue pillar for King Xin's inheritance requires a paper crane Gu. To enter the red pillar for King Bao's inheritance requires an exploding egg Gu."

"How can this be?" Shang Yi Fan screeched.

He finally understood why Fang and Bai had stockpiled these three Gu.

News of the Three Kings inheritance spread throughout the Southern Border, drawing countless Gu Masters eager for a share.

In Shang clan city, Shang Xin Ci held them all, guaranteeing immense profit from selling them.

"Where did they get such information? And so precise!"

"No, that is no longer important. More importantly, how can I stop them!"

Shang Xin Ci had spent far beyond a hundred thousand primeval stones, but this broke no rules—it was a loophole at most.

"Young master Yi Fan, this deal will at least triple their capital. We cannot use dirty tricks; we must focus on our own business. With Madam's influence, you will reach at least six hundred thousand in three months. Young master, we can still win," Old Zhang explained.

"Yes, we can only hope so..." Shang Yi Fan exhaled deeply, though his worry remained.

Three kings, they referred to King Quan, King Xin, and King Bao.

Their origins were traced back to three hundred years ago.

The three kings came from Wang clan. Wang clan was wiped out by mortal enemy Wu clan, which missed three children.

Among the three children, the eldest was eight-year-old future King Quan, the youngest was five-year-old King Bao, and the six-year-old girl was the future King Xin.

While risking their lives gathering herbs on the mountain to survive, the three stumbled into an ancient inheritance ground.

Far from ordinary, it had been left by a mysterious rank six Gu Immortal.

Their fates transformed, the three each received a portion of the rank six Gu Immortal's inheritance and cultivated diligently for nearly a century.

As news of the entry requirements spread, people across Shang clan city went wild.

"I want dog enslavement Gu, paper crane Gu, and exploding egg Gu!!"

"Why are these Gu so expensive? Prices have tripled!"

"Tsk, get lost if you're poor, there are lots of people waiting to buy."

Countless people cursed the unscrupulous merchant under their breath, yet painfully shelled out their hard-earned money.

# Chapter 325: Immortal’s Blessed Land

"Over these past ten days, we averaged twenty thousand a day. The initial hundred thousand has tripled to three hundred thousand!" Shang Xin Ci said with delight, holding the ledger.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing also profited heavily from their own investments, with Fang Yuan buying two more primeval elder Gu to reach 2.4 million in assets.

"Brother Hei Tu, thank you so much. With this three hundred thousand, I can better execute my plans," Shang Xin Ci smiled.

Fang Yuan waved his hand: "No need. Xin Ci, your idea is great, but difficult to execute."

He continued, "Selling battle stage intel is unlike hoarding Gu in bulk. Without a leader, competitors couldn't interfere and had to watch us reap the profit."

"An intel business is different. It demands not just resources, but ample manpower and a vast network, otherwise competitors will suppress and sabotage you. With our weak foundation, we cannot afford failure; our investment must succeed. We must prepare thoroughly."

Shang Xin Ci nodded solemnly: "Brother Hei Tu, you're right. But having lived in Shang clan city only a few years, I have only Xiao Die and Xiao Lan, and virtually no network."

Fang Yuan chuckled: "Don't underestimate yourself, Xin Ci. Shang Chi Wen, who oversees the battle stage, shares a good bond with you."

"We get along well as girls, and an intel business boosts her revenue, so I doubt she will object," Shang Xin Ci analyzed.

"Xin Ci, first you need loyal subordinates, but above all, you need a capable manager. He must be a dependable rank three Gu Master, experienced and skilled in commerce. I have already identified a candidate: Zhou Quan, a former shopkeeper under Shang Ya Zi," Fang Yuan laid out.

Shang Xin Ci was dejected: "Zhou Quan? He was once Zhou clan's rank four leader, but fell to rank three. He is exceedingly proud. Even Shang Ya Zi couldn't win his loyalty. Such a person, I fear..."

Fang Yuan waved his hand: "How do you know without trying? Have confidence, Xin Ci; you possess a unique charisma. In the future, many will willingly serve and fight for you. Zhou Quan is tough and you might fail, but showing genuine sincerity will surely pay off."

Not longer after Shang Xin Ci left, Wei Yang arrived at Nan Qiu garden.

Receiving him at the lake pavilion, Fang Yuan offered, "I know why Brother Wei has come. Please sit."

Wei Yang laughed: "Brother Fang Zheng is sharp. Please, enlighten me."

"My intel came from Bai Gu Mountain, where Grey Bone Scholar had explored the Three Kings inheritance grounds. In truth, the site is an ancient Gu Master's blessed land," Fang Yuan answered with half-truths.

His information had not come from Bai Gu Mountain, but the blessed land detail was genuine, confirmed in his past life.

"Blessed land?" A brilliant light flashed in Wei Yang's eyes.

"Brother Fang Zheng, are you serious?" Wei Yang asked anxiously.

"Calm down, Brother Wei. Though left by a rank six Gu Immortal, this blessed land has long degenerated and sits on the brink of collapse. Long ago, the Three Kings occupied it and stripped its resources. Building their inheritances inside placed even greater strain on the blessed land. Eventually, it will collapse into nothingness," Fang Yuan said slowly.

"This was all in Grey Bone Scholar's notes, but for safety, I destroyed the documents and cannot show them," Fang Yuan smiled faintly.

"Brother, this matter is huge. I trust you, but I must report to Lord Clan Leader for a decision," Wei Yang said solemnly.

Fang Yuan nodded understandingly: "If Shang clan sends an exploration team, I have some intel that may help."

"Haha, I won't let you lose out. Name a price." Managing Feng Yu Manor, Wei Yang oversaw city intelligence.

Fang Yuan thought about it, and said four hundred thousand.

Wei Yang frowned: "Four hundred thousand is too low for rank six intel, but since it is unconfirmed, I'll pay that first and compensate you later."

"I am assured by brother Wei's words." Fang Yuan laughed.

Taking the intel seriously, Shang Yan Fei heavily invested by sending three teams to San Cha Mountain, each comprising at least three elders.

After getting the funds, Fang Yuan approached Li Ran.

Li Ran was a Wu clan spy in the third inner city battle stage; no one knew his secret identity except Fang Yuan.

Meeting Li Ran in secret, Fang Yuan opened directly: "I have intel on the Three Kings inheritance that Wu clan would surely want."

# Chapter 326: Nine Eyes Liquor Worm

As days passed, news of the Three Kings inheritance sparked widespread unrest at San Cha Mountain.

"It is still too early for San Cha Mountain. Better to let others clear the obstacles while I conserve strength and refine Gu, then seize the rewards when ready," Fang Yuan planned.

The Gu he intended to refine was none other than the liquor worm!

Rank four nine eyes liquor worm could purify yellow gold primeval essence by a small realm.

Bone flesh unity Gu had made earlier liquor worms redundant, but circumstances had changed.

Bai Ning Bing was now rank four while Fang Yuan was rank three peak stage; once he reached rank four initial stage, their cultivation would match.

Bone flesh unity Gu's assistance would sharply diminish, making a rank four nine eyes liquor worm vital to boosting his cultivation speed.

Refine the nine eyes liquor worm!

While Shang Xin Ci began recruiting Zhou Quan, Fang Yuan entered closed-door cultivation to refine Gu.

Refining it required two seven fragrances liquor worms and eyeballs from nine different hundred beast kings.

Fortunately, fusing the two seven fragrances liquor worms went smoothly, yielding a nine eyes liquor worm three days later.

"I have the nine eyes liquor worm now." After inspecting the Gu, Fang Yuan stored it in his aperture with satisfaction.

Still at rank three peak stage, he could not use the nine eyes liquor worm until reaching rank four.

"Once I reach rank four initial stage and use this nine eyes liquor worm, I will obtain yellow gold middle stage essence. For the first time, my primeval essence quality will surpass Bai Ning Bing's." Fang Yuan sensed he was close to rank four.

Though bound by a 'vow', he never forgot the threat Bai Ning Bing posed.

Shang Xin Ci predictably faced setbacks and slow progress recruiting Zhou Quan. Arrogant and aloof, he showed no willingness to negotiate.

After refining the nine eyes liquor worm, Fang Yuan devoted all his energy to cultivating.

With Bai Ning Bing continuously supplying yellow gold primeval essence, his cultivation accelerated toward rank four.

However, Spring Autumn Cicada was also recovering faster, placing increasingly noticeable pressure on his rank three aperture.

On this day, Shang clan city welcomed the Auspicious Festival.

Yet the festival's joyous atmosphere failed to reach Shang Xin Ci.

Frowning slightly, the young girl sat at a table, looking melancholically at several invitation cards.

These invitation cards all came from the nine young masters.

"Big Brother Hei Tu, what should I do?" Caught in a dilemma, Shang Xin Ci looked subconsciously to Fang Yuan for guidance.

"Xin Ci, our sales have tripled your hundred thousand capital, making you a favorite in the young master competition. Naturally, they invited you."

Fang Yuan smiled: "However, we can only accept one. On the surface, it seems a harmonious banquet, but it is actually a political test of allegiance."

"Should I choose Shang Chi Wen's invitation? She oversees the battle stage and would greatly aid our future plans," Shang Xin Ci suggested.

Fang Yuan shook his head: "Real power lies with Shang Qiu Niu. As the eldest son, he holds the greatest advantage. The second faction is the prominent alliance of Shang Pu Lao, Shang Suan Ni, and Shang Bi Xi. The third faction consists of Shang Chao Feng and Shang Fu Xi, who are biding their time. Among the rest, Shang Chi Wen lacks ambition, while Shang Pi Xiu is too playful..."

"With your weak foundation, you must choose from among these three factions to gain real backing."

She chose the third faction, Shang Chao Feng's invitation.

"The first and second factions are locked in rivalry; choosing either will offend the other. It is better to join the third faction, entering the political whirlpool while staying on the sidelines for now," Shang Xin Ci explained to Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan was pleased, yet felt a touch of irony.

In his past life, Shang Chao Feng and Shang Xin Ci fiercely contested the clan leader position. In this life, the two arch-enemies were walking together.

# Chapter 327: Beautiful and Talented, Xin Ci’s Brilliance

Shortly after, Shang Yi Fan learned that Shang Xin Ci had accepted Shang Chao Feng's banquet invitation.

Shang Yi Fan was secretly delighted: "Shang Chao Feng and Shang Fu Xi are the third faction and have always been suppressed. How could they match Eldest Brother Qiu Niu? My chances of becoming young master are even higher!"

However, Old Steward Zhang shook his head gravely: "Young Master Qiu Niu is locked in battle with Pu Lao and cannot afford another enemy."

"So even after joining Shang Qiu Niu's faction, I still can't touch her?" Shang Yi Fan's expression darkened.

"We simply cannot borrow his strength," Old Steward Zhang stroked his grizzled beard. "However, Shang Xin Ci faces major issues of her own, namely a severe lack of capable manpower. She has been trying to recruit Zhou Quan—an impossible feat."

The old steward sneered: "Zhou Quan was once a clan leader, proud and tenfold more capable than me. Why would such a man submit to an inexperienced girl? I have already secretly mobilized people to create all kinds of rumors and slanders. Zhou Quan cares for his reputation very much, and the rumors have already prevented him from joining Shang Xin Ci. Shang Xin Ci is attempting this in secret, but failing will damage her prestige, put her at an early disadvantage, and make her a laughingstock. Hehehe..."

Meanwhile, Shang Chao Feng, Shang Xin Ci, Fang Yuan, and Bai Ning Bing were also in discussion.

Shang Chao Feng was somewhat surprised that Shang Xin Ci had joined him.

During their discussion, Shang Chao Feng sincerely pledged his full support for her in the young master competition.

"Sister Xin Ci, you've earned three hundred thousand primeval stones selling Gu worms and currently lead, but that is far from enough. What do you plan to do next?"

Shang Chao Feng added, "Speak freely, and I will aid you to the best of my ability."

She replied honestly, "To be frank with Big Brother Chao Feng, I am considering an information-gathering business."

"Yes." Shang Xin Ci started to explain her plan in detail.

Upon hearing it, Shang Chao Feng's expression changed, and he shook his head: "An information business regarding the battle stage? Unsuitable, completely unsuitable."

Shang Chao Feng sighed: "Demonic Gu Masters can become external elders here, making the battle stage vital. This unique policy has recruited many talents for us. Among Shang clan's five great elders, three are external elders."

"However, this policy is a double-edged sword. External elders form our clan's leadership, and any compromised individual gaining entry spells disaster. Thus, our leadership attaches the utmost importance to the battle stage."

"A young master once opened a betting house for battle stage matches, earning five hundred thousand primeval stones in just two days. Yet on the third day, the house was shut down, and he was demoted and exiled. It serves as a stern warning not to tamper with the battle stage."

"Xin Ci, your idea is novel and well-conceived, and would yield massive daily profits. But the risks are too great—do not gamble with your future. I manage the Gu battle zone, and your thirteenth brother oversees the auction houses; joining us in these fields will let you counter Shang Yi Fan directly," Shang Chao Feng urged gently.

After pondering deeply, Shang Xin Ci smiled: "Big Brother Chao Feng, I view the battle stage differently. This is well worth doing!"

"A betting house invited match manipulation, wrecking the external elder policy. Profiteering at the clan's expense must be eliminated for the battle stage to flourish."

"However, my information business is completely different. I know Shang clan's premier intelligence organization, Feng Yu Manor, secretly investigates demonic Gu Masters at the battle stage. They must verify whether these cultivators harbor ill intent, yet they never investigate openly. Why?"

"Because most demonic Gu Masters are deeply suspicious and paranoid. Open investigations upon arriving in Shang clan city would provoke resentment. To attract top talent, the clan cannot investigate publicly. Yet leaving them uninvestigated is equally unacceptable."

"That is where my business comes in. I will investigate each Gu Master's general strength, Gu worms, and battle record, selling this information. Meanwhile, I will invite authoritative experts to predict key match outcomes and create rankings—invaluable intelligence for competing Gu Masters. Many seek external elder status or greater fame, and will desire to see their names ranked."

"This information will avoid sensitive secrets, summarizing and comparing only demonstrated strength. For demonic Gu Masters on the battle stage, it will be immensely helpful. At the same time, it tightens Shang clan's grip on the battle stage. Father will surely welcome it, so why would he suppress it?"

Shang Chao Feng applauded: "Brilliant analysis! Sister Xin Ci, your vision is truly admirable. I will fully support you in this. Since you lack manpower, take as many people from my side as you need!"

Shang Xin Ci shook her head: "Thank you, Big Brother Chao Feng, but I have already chosen my manpower."

He urged: "Sister Xin Ci, Zhou Quan is excessively proud. Even Shang Qiu Niu was rejected and scolded: *'An inexperienced junior dares recruit a hundred-year-old man like me?'*"

"Even if you recruit him, what can one man do? You still lack capable, loyal middle and lower-level subordinates. Building such a force takes time. Without trustworthy subordinates, Shang Yi Fan could easily sabotage your business and steal your gains."

"Young Master Chao Feng need not worry. I already have a plan," Fang Yuan interjected.

# Chapter 328: Eating will be eating

Parting with Shang Chao Feng, the trio stepped onto the streets.

"Counting the days, we've been in Shang clan city for nearly three years," Shang Xin Ci remarked emotionally.

"So much has happened," she sighed. "I never would have imagined I was the Shang clan leader's daughter."

Shang Xin Ci looked at Fang Yuan with a bright smile: "Without Brother Hei Tu, I never would have made it this far."

"Indeed, I never expected your father to be the renowned Shang Yan Fei! But saving you was fate. *People come and go*—that is the way of the world," Fang Yuan replied, gazing ahead.

Shang Xin Ci caught his meaning, her expression shifting: "Brother Hei Tu, are you leaving Shang clan city?"

"Yes. Soon, Bai Ning Bing and I must leave Shang clan city for San Cha Mountain," Fang Yuan said.

"Do not worry. Before leaving, I will secure your position as a young master." Fang Yuan chuckled. "Come, let us recruit your subordinates and lay the foundation for your future force today."

"What, brother Hei Tu, you have ideal candidates already?" Shang Xin Ci asked in surprise.

"Just follow me." Fang Yuan walked ahead, leading the way.

Behind the stall, there was a young man lying down.

Dressed in rags, the young man leaned against the wall with half-closed, dazed eyes and a sickly pallor, looking drunken and utterly defeated.

"Brother, what are you buying... oh, Lord Fang Zheng!" Sensing company, the young man opened his eyes, flustered and shocked.

"Here are ten primeval stones. I'm buying everything; now scram." Fang Yuan tossed the pouch over.

Fang Yuan slapped him hard, sending him sprawling to the ground.

Clutching his face in terror, the young man glanced at Fang Yuan, but shrank from those abyssal pupils and scrambled silently out of the alley.

"Xin Ci, as a demonic Gu Master, I favor a straightforward approach," Fang Yuan explained casually in a self-righteous tone.

Having driven the vendor away, Fang Yuan stooped and picked out a token from the stall.

The dirty, broken black steel token bore weathered carvings that were illegible without the missing half.

But Fang Yuan knew this word was 'Fan'.

Upon recovering, the demonic Gu Master had forged a black steel token engraved with 'Fan' to repay her kindness.

He split the token, giving half to the girl and keeping the other.

# Chapter 329: Three brothers of Xiong clan

As it turned out, the demonic Gu Master had descendants. Before his arrest by Tie clan, he instructed them to repay that debt of gratitude if ever possible.

Now in possession of the half-token, Fang Yuan led Bai Ning Bing and Shang Xin Ci straight to them.

The three brothers had set up a stall outside their home, enjoying the festive atmosphere.

"Ah? Lord Fang Zheng! And Lord Bai Ning Bing." Seeing them, the three scrambled to their feet.

"I came looking for the three of you today," Fang Yuan said, observing them emotionlessly.

These three brothers all had the surname Xiong.

The eldest, Xiong Tu, was an earth path rank three upper stage Gu Master.

The second, Xiong Huo, was a fire path rank three middle stage Gu Master.

The youngest, Xiong Feng, was a wind path rank three initial stage Gu Master.

Hearing Fang Yuan's words, the three brothers became uneasy.

Xiong Tu cupped his fists respectfully: "Lord Fang Zheng, may I know what this is about? If we have unwittingly offended you, I apologize."

Fang Yuan frowned slightly: "You know I left the battle stage to support Shang Xin Ci's young master campaign. We lack manpower, and I want all three of you to join us."

Then he added: "I will not force you. Join if you are willing; if not, so be it."

As the youngest and boldest, Xiong Feng ventured carefully: "Lord Fang Zheng, truth be told, we are accustomed to freedom and wish to avoid the young master competition. So..."

"Oh?" Fang Yuan raised an eyebrow, eyes flashing cold: "You three are bold enough to refuse my invitation?"

Xiong Tu hurriedly cupped his fists: "Not at all! Please don't misunderstand, Lord Fang Zheng—your invitation is our honor. My brother was simply flustered and spoke out of turn. He meant to say that we would gladly serve Lady Shang Xin Ci."

"Hehe, then I am reassured. Since they are so eager, accept them, Xin Ci," Fang Yuan said, turning to her.

Though sharing their apprehension, Shang Xin Ci trusted Fang Yuan. Stepping forward, she said, "Please do your best from now on."

"Ah, I almost forgot—take a look." Fang Yuan sobered up, tossing the half-token to Xiong Tu.

Under Bai Ning Bing and Shang Xin Ci's curious gazes, Xiong Tu retrieved the matching half-token.

The two halves joined seamlessly, and an illusory flame ignited across the token.

"This is real!" The three Xiong brothers stared wide-eyed.

"Xiong brothers, did you think I came without reason? Why hesitate?" Fang Yuan timed his words well.

"Grandfather said no matter who holds the token, even an enemy, we must repay the kindness from back then!"

"From now on, Lady Shang Xin Ci is our master. But only for twenty years—sufficient to repay the debt."

All three dropped to one knee before Shang Xin Ci, cupping their fists.

"Xiong Tu, Xiong Huo, and Xiong Feng greet Lady Xin Ci!" they shouted, their tone transformed with drive and excitement.

"Brother Hei Tu, what is happening?" Realizing they were sincerely submitting, Shang Xin Ci was deeply curious.

Fang Yuan snickered. "It's a long story for next time. Now, let's recruit more people."

The gang of six arrived at the slave market.

Having planned and investigated beforehand, Fang Yuan led the way directly to a cage.

"Lady Xin Ci, Lord Fang Zheng, Lord Bai Ning Bing, are you here to buy slaves? If so, I advise against buying from this cage," a managing Gu Master hurried over.

"Lady Xin Ci might not know, but these people are from Wei clan. After a recent coup where the leader was overthrown by his younger brother, this caged faction of the former leader is being sold off by the new clan head," the Gu Master explained.

Shang Yan Fei reportedly backed Wei clan's upheaval, creating complex political ties between the two clans.

But Fang Yuan quickly said: "We are buying these people."

# Chapter 330: Wei De Xin

"This... Lord Fang Zheng, are you certain about buying them?" the managing Gu Master hesitated.

"Why, are you doubting my decision?" Fang Yuan gave him a plain glance.

The Gu Master chuckled. "There are numerous procedures; you will have to wait until tomorrow..."

Fang Yuan raised his leg and kicked.

He flew over thirty paces, crashed into a bystander, and collapsed unconscious, vomiting blood.

The commotion drew instant attention, and three groups of Gu Masters quickly surrounded them in the heavily guarded market.

"It's Fang Zheng!" Recognizing him, the agitated group immediately deflated.

The leader's expression softened politely. "Lord Fang Zheng, even esteemed guests cannot injure clansmen here. According to the rules..."

"According to the rules, I'm fined forty-nine primeval stones," Fang Yuan interjected.

Tossing over a pouch, Fang Yuan waved his hand. "Fifty primeval stones inside. Keep the change."

With the first Gu Master beaten unconscious, the market quickly sent a replacement to attend to them.

We are buying everyone in this cage." Fang Yuan pointed at the cage and said."

In less than fifteen minutes, all the procedures were done.

"This is the rank three poison scorpion Gu, Lord Fang Zheng." The Gu Master handed him the worm.

Fang Yuan planned to refine the poison scorpion Gu first before transferring it to Shang Xin Ci, as doing it herself was too difficult.

"What? Someone bought the Wei clan slaves? I told you to watch them! We were supposed to wait until I settled things with Shang Qiu Niu and buy them once it cooled down!"

"Sigh... these Wei clansmen once managed the clan with high cultivation and experience. With them, I could build half a Wei clan. Above all, their first lady, Wei De Xin, excels at training guards. The clan leader's famous guards thwarted numerous assassinations and once held off five rank three Gu Masters simultaneously. Without that internal rebellion, they never would have fallen!"

Morning of the next day.

Over thirty Wei clansmen assembled in Nan Qiu garden's square.

Fang Yuan had already helped Shang Xin Ci refine the poison scorpion Gu, supplementing it with other Gu worms to help her manage the demanding rank three Gu.

"From today on, Shang Xin Ci is your master. Greet her!" Fang Yuan handed her the poison scorpion Gu before them.

The Wei clansmen slowly knelt before Shang Xin Ci, their listless voices filled with numbness and helplessness, like lifeless puppets.

"Wei De Xin, come out. I want a private word with you," Fang Yuan said, pointing at a woman.

With a darkened expression, Fang Yuan stepped forward and slapped her.

"It seems you haven't grasped reality. Though I spent heavily to buy you, I don't mind killing a few for amusement. Even if I do whatever I please to your clan leader's wife, what could you do?" Fang Yuan sneered, his voice icy cold.

"Everyone stand down," Wei De Xin said, stepping forward.

She bowed to Fang Yuan: "What does Lord Fang Zheng need from this lowly servant?"

Fang Yuan snorted, raking his fierce gaze over her. "Lady Wei, you talk too much. Follow me and obey."

Helpless as meat on a chopping block, Wei De Xin gritted her teeth and followed him to the lake pavilion.

Yet Fang Yuan's next words rattled her: "Lady Wei, I am very interested in you."

Wei De Xin quickly knelt. "This lowly servant is honored to draw Lord Fang Zheng's gaze, but I dare not offer my soiled body to your esteemed self."

Fang Yuan laughed. "Do not misunderstand, Wei De Xin. I want your talent to train loyal female guards for Shang Xin Ci. You must also motivate your clansmen to work hard."

Hearing this, Wei De Xin breathed a sigh of relief and promised, "Yes, this servant shall obey Lord's orders."

Fang Yuan chuckled. "I know you are pregnant, seeking to preserve your husband's bloodline and contact your brother Wei Shen Jing."

At his words, Wei De Xin's face blanched and her body trembled.

# Chapter 331: Advancement to Rank Four

In Fang Yuan's previous life, Wei De Xin had helped Shang Pu Lao surpass Shang Qiu Niu to become the frontrunner for young clan leader.

Now that her outstanding talent was in his hands, controlling Wei De Xin and making her serve Shang Xin Ci was simple for Fang Yuan.

"Wei De Xin, do not fear. As long as you serve Shang Xin Ci wholeheartedly, I will not harm you, and may even help raise your child safely. Do not contact Wei Shen Jing; Wu clan has recruited him and watches him constantly. Verify it yourself if you doubt me."

"Now Shang Xin Ci has the Wei clansmen, the Xiong brothers, and her maidservants to form a reliable foundation."

"Her faction's framework is established and sufficient for the young master competition. But if we recruit Zhou Quan as steward, the young master position will be secured without doubt! Wei De Xin, the Xiong brothers, Xiao Die, and Xiao Lan will be generals, while Zhou Quan acts as commander."

Subduing Zhou Quan would not be easy, especially after vivid rumors spread of Shang Xin Ci failing to recruit him over a dozen times.

Though the timing was not yet ripe, he already had a plan.

Several nights later, within a secret room of Nan Qiu garden.

Guided by his will, peak stage rank three primeval essence slammed against his aperture walls.

Cultivating with Bai Ning Bing via the bone flesh unity Gu, Fang Yuan made rapid daily progress.

Snow silver primeval essence assaulted the rank four barrier, draining his sea without triggering a breakthrough.

Just as Fang Yuan viewed his emptied aperture, a waterfall of yellow gold primeval essence poured down.

It was external support channeled by Bai Ning Bing through the bone flesh unity Gu!

Directed by his will, the surging gold essence formed a tidal wave and slammed into the aperture walls.

With a faint sound, fine cracks spread across the aperture walls.

The fractures widened until the crystal walls collapsed, dissolving into the primeval sea.

A thin light membrane replaced the crystal walls, illuminating the aperture.

Fang Yuan advanced into rank four realm!

"Finally, rank four!" Fang Yuan opened his eyes, a sharp gleam flashing across the dark room.

"Reaching rank four this quickly was possible by compounding advantages through past-life experience and Spring Autumn Cicada..."

"Yet despite reaching rank four, Spring Autumn Cicada's recovery is accelerating, exerting ever greater pressure on my aperture."

"Rank four lets me wield new Gu. Next is charging crash Gu, but first, business to handle..."

Fourth inner city.

"This is the manor Zhou Quan manages?" Fang Yuan asked indifferently, standing with hands behind his back.

Behind him stood Bai Ning Bing, Xiao Die, the Xiong brothers, and Wei De Xin's clansmen.

"Lord, if you wish to see my master, present your visiting letter. He is currently taking an afternoon nap," the doorboy said arrogantly.

"Afternoon nap? Visiting letter? Heh." Fang Yuan's face turned icy as he waved his hand. "Smash the door!"

Fang Yuan's group barged in, smashing everything and beating the staff.

# Chapter 332: Zhou Quan’s shock

"What do we do, Master?" the steward rushed to Zhou Quan in a panic.

"Fang Zheng is completely lawless, using violence in Shang clan city! Wait... this rank four aura... good heavens, he's already rank four at his age?!"

Zhou Quan told his steward, "Pack quickly, we are retreating to the shop to avoid them."

"Lord, we searched everywhere but couldn't find Zhou Quan," Xiong Tu reported.

"Mm, he must have fled through the back door to hide in his shop. It is all as expected; I deliberately left the back exit open," Fang Yuan chuckled.

Following Fang Yuan's orders, Wei De Xin circulated the news, making the incident a city-wide sensation.

"Master, Fang Zheng is here again!" the old steward reported in terror.

"Don't worry. This shop belongs to Shang clan; he wouldn't dare barge in," Zhou Quan stroked his beard, soothing his steward.

Before the watching crowd, Fang Yuan kicked the shop door off its hinges and strode inside.

"This is a Shang clan shop! Forcible entry violates Shang clan city rules!" the guard leader shouted, sweating profusely.

Bai Ning Bing stood outside, radiating rank four aura as she eyed the leader coldly. "We are entering. Can you stop us?"

Gulping, he replied, "Even so, it is our duty. Lord Fang Zheng's actions violate city rules..."

"We know—a fine, right?" Bai Ning Bing raised a brow and tossed him a bag of primeval stones.

"Five hundred primeval stones inside; keep the change as an advance." Bai Ning Bing strode inside.

"Fang Zheng, what are you doing? Forcibly storming a Shang clan shop—do you still wish to remain in this city?" Zhou Quan demanded grimly, invoking Shang clan's name to suppress him.

Fang Yuan sneered. "Old man, why hide here? Let's cut to the chase: you have two choices—submit or die. Choose."

Zhou Quan squeezed out a smile, his pride refusing to yield: "Young man, rein in your arrogance. You wish to harm me? Look where we are standing!"

"You cannot see I am nothing like Shang Xin Ci. I'll give you a chance to understand me."

Relying on rich experience, Zhou Quan sprang up and engaged Fang Yuan in battle.

The brawl devastated half the shop. When the dust cleared, Zhou Quan lay sprawled on the ground, broken-boned, bleeding, and unable to move.

Fang Yuan stood dominantly, his foot planted atop Zhou Quan's head.

"Lord Fang Zheng, what have you done?!" three Shang clan elders arrived, shouting.

"Are you blind? Can't you see I'm teaching someone a lesson?" Fang Yuan rolled his eyes and shouted back.

"Lord Fang Zheng, the matter is grave—do you understand the consequences?" the elders demanded.

Under everyone's gaze, Fang Yuan laughed. "Of course I do. I also know the consequences of murder!"

Saying so, he took out the purple thorn token.

"Elders, if I kill here and disrupt peace, I forfeit this token, correct?" Fang Yuan asked.

Exerting strength, he crushed the purple thorn token into powder.

As token fragments rained onto the floor and struck his face, Zhou Quan's smile froze.

*"Fang Zheng destroyed his purple thorn token just to take my life... is he insane?!"*

# Chapter 333: Leaving Shang Clan City

"Was it worth going so far just for me?" Feeling Fang Yuan's sheer resolve, Zhou Quan nearly wept.

"Rejecting Shang Xin Ci damaged her reputation, so Fang Zheng resolved to eliminate me! Shang Yi Fan misled me..."

"Zhou Quan, you cannot understand circumstances. I wasted a purple thorn token to kill you; feel honored. Now die." Fang Yuan applied pressure.

"Brother Hei Tu, have mercy on him." Shang Xin Ci rushed over.

"Xin Ci, you found out after all. I know you cherish talent, but you needn't plead for him. Such a person deserves death." Fang Yuan replied coldly, easing the pressure on his leg.

She continued: "Sir Zhou Quan has always borne heavy burdens for his noble aspiration of rebuilding Zhou clan. Before his wife passed away, she urged him to rebuild their home. All these years, he has labored under this grief..."

"Is that so?" Fang Yuan's expression shifted as he eased his foot.

*"When did I ever tell her that?"* Zhou Quan was baffled; he hadn't even been present when his wife died.

Yet he immediately realized that Shang Xin Ci and Fang Yuan were putting on an act.

"What a brilliant scheme... I lost to these juniors today," Zhou Quan gritted his teeth, sighing.

"So that was it. Assisting Xin Ci does not conflict with rebuilding your home; your aspirations impress me. Yet dying is easy; enduring humiliation to live on and press forward for your ideals is true courage," Fang Yuan declared loudly.

Taking the cue, the old man sighed: "Every generation breeds new talent. Hearing your words, I am truly enlightened!"

Enduring the pain, Zhou Quan slowly rose and bowed to Shang Xin Ci: "Zhou Quan greets Lady Xin Ci."

"What? Zhou Quan acknowledged that girl as his master?" In the study, Shang Yi Fan froze in shock.

"It really happened," Old Zhang sighed. "Shang Xin Ci is young and naturally lacks such ability. But she has Fang Zheng and Bai Ning Bing. Frankly, I underestimated Fang Zheng. Beneath that straightforward facade lies a schemer who deliberately escalated the matter to force Zhou Quan's submission."

"Had Zhou Quan refused, Fang Zheng would have killed him on the spot. Now the entire shop district is abuzz with rumors that Zhou Quan endured humiliation solely to rebuild his clan. Moved by Fang Zheng's words, he submitted to Shang Xin Ci, who cherishes talent. Her reputation has reached an all-time high!"

With Shang Xin Ci leading, Fang and Bai guarding, Zhou Quan assisting, and Wei De Xin and the Xiong brothers cooperating, the battle stage intel business was officially established.

When clan higher-ups moved to shut down the intel business, Shang Yan Fei stepped in to fully back his daughter.

Several months later, Shang Yi Fan and his mother's forces lost terribly to Shang Xin Ci.

Shang Xin Ci became one of the ten young masters succeeding Shang Ya Zi.

"Brother Hei Tu, are you in such a rush?" Shang Xin Ci walked outside the city, bidding farewell.

"You are already a young master; with your talent, you will thrive here. Xin Ci, all banquets must end. We will meet again—do not be sad."

Fang Yuan consoled her, then added: "Before leaving, let me remind you: keep a broad vision. Above the ten young masters sits young clan leader Shang Tuo Hai. Above him are Shang clan's five elders, your father Shang Yan Fei, and the supreme elder..."

"Rest assured, brother. Back then, Shang Tuo Hai had favorable conditions when rising to young clan leader, as did the other young masters. Lacking both, I can only invest in people—only talent will let me compete. Brother, if you ever need anything, tell me; I will do everything to help you!" Brilliance shone in Shang Xin Ci's eyes.

"Alright, until next time." Fang Yuan gave her a deep look, then turned to leave.

"Brother Hei Tu, San Cha Mountain is perilous, please take care!" Shang Xin Ci's eyes were tearful as she prayed for him in her heart.

# Chapter 334: Immortal Crane Sect, Fang Zheng

Surrounded by Eastern Sea, Western Desert, Northern Plains, and Southern Border lay the central region: Central Continent.

Central Continent boasted rich primeval energy, countless sects, and abundant resources, its overall strength surpassing all four other regions.

In southern Central Continent, amidst tens of thousands of hills, a grand mountain pierced the clouds.

This was Immortal Crane Sect.

One of the top ten great sects, it possessed immense power across Central Continent.

A crucial battle was currently underway in Immortal Crane Sect's sparring arena.

With a sharp gaze and grim expression, Fang Zheng fought evenly against Sun Yuan Hua.

Over the years, he had grown taller and broader, becoming much more mature and composed.

Suddenly, Fang Zheng unleashed a jade-green wind, forcing Sun Yuan Hua back.

"Senior Brother Sun, just admit defeat," he said with complete confidence.

"Little Junior Brother, show me your trump card," Sun Yuan Hua laughed with equal confidence.

"Alright then." Fang Zheng let out a resounding whistle, and distant crane cries answered.

"An ironbeak flying crane group!"

"Impossible! Fang Zheng reached rank four middle stage, but how can he control nearly ten thousand cranes?"

Sun Yuan Hua's eyes hardened: "Impressive, Junior Brother. But I haven't lost yet—I have a crane group too! Come forth!"

In stark contrast to Fang Zheng's massive flock, there were only a few hundred.

"Be careful, those are illusion lightning cranes. Ordinary ironbeak flying cranes are no match for them," Lord Sky Crane warned.

"Understood, Master!" Fang Zheng replied, eyes shining as he commanded his ironbeak cranes against the illusion lightning flock.

Activating a Gu in his aperture, he transformed into a flash of lightning, closing in on Fang Zheng.

Losing the initiative, Fang Zheng was suppressed, forced to defend with all his strength.

Though few in number, the illusion lightning cranes charged in unison, shredding many ironbeak flying cranes.

"Hold on! Divert your focus to guide the ironbeak cranes—doing so will be a huge breakthrough for your future!"

"Junior brother Fang Zheng, it is still too early for you to defeat me." He sneered.

Hearing those words triggered Fang Zheng's deepest memories.

Back on Qing Mao Mountain, he had clashed with his brother Fang Yuan.

*"I must avenge my clan, my uncle and aunt, the clan leader, and Lord Qing Shu!"*

Enduring the splitting headache, he split his concentration!

The ironbeak cranes surged forward like a beast opening its maw, devouring the illusion lightning flock.

Defeating Sun Yuan Hua, Fang Zheng took first place in the eight-year middle examination and became an elite disciple of Immortal Crane Sect!

# Chapter 335: Gu Immortal Bai Hu’s Inheritance

After the examination, Fang Zheng stood before the sect leader. "Fang Zheng, you are intelligent and hardworking. At rank four, you qualify as an elder, but having joined recently, you must complete missions to prove your loyalty. Work hard, win the grand examination, and become a legacy disciple," the Immortal Crane Sect leader said from his grand throne, looking down at Fang Zheng kneeling below.

"I have a sect mission for you and several elite disciples. Return and review the details," the sect leader said, sending a bookworm flying over.

Fang Zheng received it and took his leave.

Fang Zheng slept for two full days until knocking woke him.

Opening the door, he found a group of elite disciples outside.

"Fang Zheng, have you heard of the shocking event at Tian Ti Mountain months ago?" an elite disciple asked.

"Tian Ti Mountain?" Fang Zheng nodded. "Fairy Bai Hu's inheritance appeared there—the owner of Hu Immortal blessed land. Its appearance drew many Gu Immortals, as the inheritor will gain her blessed land. Right now, Tian Ti Mountain is surrounded by Gu Immortals."

"Our mission is to head to Tian Ti Mountain and try to obtain this inheritance. The sects' Gu Immortals agreed not to intervene, sending elite disciples to compete instead."

"If any of us inherits it, we will soar to success and have a great chance of becoming a Gu Immortal. Disciples from all top ten sects will be there. We must stay united!"

Huo Tan mountain in southern border is a dormant volcano.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing had been traveling through Huo Tan Mountain for several days.

Two bald demonic Gu Masters lurked there—elder Jiao Huang and middle-aged Meng Tu, a famous assassination duo.

"Killing these two earns us ten thousand primeval stones and a yellow pair token from Shang clan. How could we pass up such a deal?" Meng Tu licked his lips excitedly.

"They are rank four while we are rank three. The reward is rich, but the risk is high..."

"Brother Jiao Huang, don't let their cultivation scare you. They are merely rank four initial stage, and most of their Gu are still rank three. Besides, we've killed rank four Gu Masters before—remember Xiao Fu Lu?" Meng Tu encouraged.

At the mention of Xiao Fu Lu, Jiao Huang frowned nervously: "How many times must I say it? Never speak of Xiao Fu Lu. He was the grandson of Xiao clan's supreme elder—killing him offended a Gu Immortal. Keep your mouth shut!"

"Hmph, so what if he is a Gu Immortal? Haven't we lived free and safe all these years?" Meng Tu pouted indifferently: "Gu Immortals are not omnipotent, not to mention these two newly advanced rank four Gu Masters. Brother, don't forget that we've spent several days preparing that trap for them. Hehehe!"

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing stopped walking.

The soil parted as lava crocodiles crawled out from underground.

"A lava crocodile group, led by a thousand beast king!" Bai Ning Bing focused on the group's leader.

Facing the danger, Fang Yuan laughed: "Just some crocodiles. Bai Ning Bing, work with me and kill this crocodile king!"

"Okay," Bai Ning Bing replied flatly, battle intent blazing in her eyes.

# Chapter 336: Hero saves the beauty

The battle concluded; Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing stood atop Huo Tan Mountain, surrounded by lava crocodile corpses.

Quickly clearing the battlefield, Fang and Bai resumed their journey.

Hiding motionless in the distance, the two assassins wore deathly pale expressions like zombies.

"Those two are monsters," Jiao Huang sighed deeply. "*Comparing oneself to others only brings anger*. We are no match for them."

"Brother Jiao Huang, San Cha Mountain is packed with experts. They will inevitably clash with others there. We can wait for an opportunity and strike when they run into trouble!"

Jiao Huang patted Meng Tu's shoulder: "You make sense. Let's head to San Cha Mountain!"

"That group of lava crocodiles was suspicious," Fang Yuan pondered along the way.

The ambush was too coincidental. While Bai Ning Bing noticed nothing, Fang Yuan scented a conspiracy.

"Someone deliberately laid this trap to deal with me. But who? Wu clan, Bai clan, or Shang clan?" Fang Yuan wondered.

"As for Bai clan? I know their spirit spring is drying up and extorted three million primeval stones from them. How could they not hate me?"

"Shang clan is also possible. I've offended Shang Ya Zi, Shang Yi Fan, and even Shang Pu Lao by purchasing those Wei clansmen. During the young master competition, I backed Shang Xin Ci; eliminating me would significantly weaken her."

"No matter, I'll deal with whatever comes." Fang Yuan shook his head, clearing away the scattered thoughts.

Central Continent.

Fang Zheng and the Immortal Crane Sect elite disciples rode flying cranes toward Tian Ti Mountain.

Guided by Lord Sky Crane's soul within a spirit incubating flea, he commanded ten thousand cranes.

"It's time to land and feed the cranes." Fang Zheng stamped his foot, guiding the crane flock down through the clouds.

"A battle is taking place below!" an elite disciple shouted as they descended.

Four demonic Gu Masters laughed sinisterly, closing in on three surrounded female Gu Masters.

"*Tsk*, the four great obscenities," an elite disciple said in disgust, recognizing them.

"Look, they've cornered Fairy Bi Xia of Heavenly Lotus Sect!" a sharp-eyed disciple shouted.

"Hmph, demonic scoundrels deserve death!" Fang Zheng's gaze chilled as he ordered the crane group to charge.

"Damn it," Fairy Bi Xia gritted her teeth, too severely injured to break out.

"Who goes there?" the four great obscenities shouted, looking up.

"Immortal Crane Sect elite disciple, Fang Zheng!" Fang Zheng roared like thunder from atop the ironbeak crane king.

Ten thousand ironbeak flying cranes swept forward, charging at the four obscenities.

Outnumbered, the four immediately turned and fled.

"Are you alright?" Fang Zheng dismounted his crane and asked Fairy Bi Xia softly.

"Thank you, Young Master Fang Zheng!" Fairy Bi Xia stammered, blushing with admiration.

# Chapter 337: Leaving somebody in the lurch

"Leave your primeval stones, strip, and kowtow thrice, and I shall spare your lives!"

A disheveled rank three demonic Gu Master leaped out, blocking their way.

From the darkness, many demonic Gu Masters watched with sinister smiles.

Fang Yuan responded directly by summoning his beast phantom.

As he attacked, breath concealment Gu lost effect, fully revealing his rank four aura.

He tried to flee, but Bai Ning Bing struck simultaneously, keeping him in check.

Struck directly by the beast phantom, old monster Hei Shi turned into minced meat.

"I remember now, they are the black and white twin demons! Fang Zheng and Bai Ning Bing, demonic path geniuses! They made names on Shang clan city's battle stage—especially Fang Zheng, the little beast king who practically dominated it. Why are they here?"

"Hmph!" Fang Yuan willed another beast phantom to smash a nearby shadow.

With a loud boom, the demonic Gu Master hiding in the shadow exploded into meat paste.

Trees shook as countless hidden watchers scattered in terror.

"Monsters are fighting everywhere before we even reach San Cha mountain. The situation ahead must be chaotic," Bai Ning Bing noted.

The three kings inheritance on San Cha mountain had drawn countless experts across Southern Border.

The righteous and demonic paths clashed for spots to enter the inheritance.

"Kill until they tremble in fear, and none will dare provoke us," Fang Yuan smiled coldly.

"Hiding our rank four aura makes this fun; we'll leave impressions they remember for life," Bai Ning Bing agreed.

"The three kings inheritance has the Gu I need to complete my strength path set and reach rank five."

"The Hu Immortal blessed land in Central Continent has likely opened. A rank six inheritance far surpasses the three kings, but I'm not there. No, even if I were, I'm not a disciple of the ten great sects and cannot participate at Tian Ti mountain. A pity..."

Fang Yuan gazed toward Central Continent before moving forward.

Traversing mountains, the two charged ahead, killing any demonic or righteous Gu Masters they met.

Eventually, they reached the foot of San Cha mountain.

Sounds of a fight suddenly came from ahead.

"Don't come near, don't come!" a terrified female Gu Master gasped, her ragged clothes revealing fair shoulders and thighs.

"Save me! I have thirty thousand primeval stones!" the female Gu Master cried desperately.

"This girl is the Ten Tyrants' prey. Wait until our boss arrives."

Hearing the Ten Tyrants' name, several righteous Gu Masters shrank back in fear.

Only Fang and Bai stood on their spots, watching indifferently.

"Lords, please have mercy and save me!" Eyes shining with hope, the weeping beauty ran to Fang Yuan, pleading.

"We are Nan mountain's Ten Tyrants—eh?!"

"Black and white twin demons!"

The demonic Gu Masters halted, staring at Fang and Bai in sheer terror.

"Lord twin demons, please save me!" Seeing a lifeline, the female Gu Master knelt before Fang and Bai, imploring them.

Under everyone's gazes, Fang Yuan took a light step back.

"Why would I save you? Do you think I am some kind-hearted hero?" Fang Yuan looked down coldly.

Fang Yuan sneered at the Ten Tyrants: "Continue. Put on a good show for me."

# Chapter 338: Hu Mei Er

Hearing Fang Yuan, several Ten Tyrants members relaxed their expressions.

One of them pounced, pinning the female Gu Master to the ground.

"We thank the twin demons for standing aside; we'll report your courtesy to our boss," a member smiled respectfully.

Suddenly, pink light erupted from the female Gu Master's body.

Caught off guard, the Ten Tyrants members were blasted away, crashing unconscious.

Her disguise vanished, revealing overwhelming rank four strength and an alluring, seductive figure.

Only Fang Yuan remained impassive, as though he had known all along.

"Little Beast King, abandoning a maiden—are you even a man?" She pouted resentfully.

Fang Yuan remained emotionless, his tone bone-chilling: "Hu Mei Er, did you think such acting could fool me?"

"What an honor that you know my name! But before I acted, how did you know?" she pouted.

Fang Yuan laughed: "I was only guessing. Right or wrong, I lost nothing."

Demonic Gu Master though she was, Fang Yuan's callous words chilled Hu Mei Er's heart.

"Hahaha, Fang Zheng, you are truly heartless... But don't be smug. All of San Cha mountain now knows two demonic path geniuses are here. Righteous and demonic experts alike are eager to test you. You've offended many people; I advise you to stop, or you'll lose your lives ascending the mountain." Hu Mei Er laughed.

"So you're the first to test us? Offending people is the one thing I don't fear. What if I offend everyone alive?" Fang Yuan replied.

Hu Mei Er listened, her eyes glowing with excitement.

Covering her mouth, she laughed: "What courage! Looking down on my beauty offends me. Only women and villains are hard to please— and I am both. Let's see who laughs last—I hope you leave this mountain alive!"

With a sharp glare, Hu Mei Er turned and left immediately.

"Hu Mei Er..." Fang Yuan laughed coldly in his heart.

In his previous life, Hu Mei Er had sown discord and instigated conflicts across Southern Border.

Cultivating enchantment path, she manipulated demonic experts—even rank five masters—into her puppets.

She had significant backing as the granddaughter of Granny Mei Hua.

Granny Mei Hua was a rank six Gu Immortal occupying Southern Border's Mei Hua blessed land.

"She harbors a secret and is beginning to recruit people with enchantment path. To think she'd target me this time."

"Centuries of experience eliminated my mortal desires, but enchantment path is tricky. She will surely incite trouble against me. She holds Gu from Granny Mei Hua; killing her requires preparation. Let's see which fools she sends."

Central continent, Tian Ti mountain.

Riding flying cranes, Fang Zheng's group traveled day and night before arriving.

Before them, many elite disciples from the other ten sects had already arrived.

"Bi Xia, you're here! I begged Master to let me come just to see you," a young man hurried over.

Bi Xia was speaking and laughing with Fang Zheng, but upon seeing him, her smile vanished.

# Chapter 339: Trouble

"Oh, Sir Wei... thanks to Young Hero Fang Zheng, I am safe and sound," Fairy Bi Xia greeted, avoiding Wei Wu Shang's gaze.

"Young hero Fang?" Wei Wu Shang squinted at Fang Zheng standing beside her.

"Greetings, I am Immortal Crane Sect's Fang Zheng," Fang Zheng smiled warmly, cupping his fists.

"Young Hero Fang Zheng placed first among Immortal Crane Sect's elite, commanding ten thousand cranes to bring us here."

Gritting his teeth, Wei Wu Shang challenged: "Immortal Crane Sect's elite? Let us see your crane manipulation in a spar."

Wei Wu Shang strode forward, eyes brimming with overbearing pressure as he stared at Fang Zheng.

Fang Zheng was stunned, sensing the absurd animosity directed at him.

Lord Sky Crane laughed: "A beauty's gratitude brings endless trouble, disciple! Saving Bi Xia earned you enemies. Wei Wu Shang is only your first challenger."

"Huh? Likes me?" Fang Zheng shouted in his mind.

Thinking of Shen Cui, Fang Yuan's heart sank: "The girl I liked is dead. Although Gu Yue village was destroyed, the Gu Yue bloodline still exists!"

"Hahaha, foolish disciple, Wei Wu Shang won't let you off regardless. When you saved Fairy Bi Xia, didn't you foresee this?" Lord Sky Crane chuckled.

"I don't want to spar. Wei Wu Shang is Heaven's Envy Sect's rising star healing master. Even if I win, exhausting myself will hurt my chances for the Gu Immortal inheritance."

Lord Sky Crane laughed: "Fight him! The Hu Immortal inheritance tests willpower rather than battle strength. With my assistance, you hold the advantage!"

Lord Sky Crane paused, then continued: "You're too young to understand reputation. Sometimes it is more useful than strength. Seize this rare chance to defeat these challengers and build your fame. Use the flying cranes without fearing losses. Our sect has plenty of cranes; even if they all perish, we can replenish your flock back home."

Hearing his master, Fang Zheng sighed to Wei Wu Shang: "If you wish to battle, so be it."

"Excellent! Please." Sneering, Wei Wu Shang stomped and shot airborne like lightning.

Not backing down, Fang Zheng stepped onto a flying crane as ten thousand cranes cried out, hailing their king.

"Battle!" Wei Wu Shang activated his Gu worms, his body flashing and vanishing midair.

An instant later, space tore open as he crossed several hundred steps, appearing directly before Fang Zheng.

"Space piercing Gu?!" Even Lord Sky Crane was shocked.

An intense battle unfolded.

At the same time, on San Cha mountain.

In a certain cave, the Ten Tyrants gathered.

"So Hu Mei Er used you to test the black and white demons?!" muttered the Ten Tyrants' leader, Tyrant Heng Mei.

"Right, Boss! That demoness tricked us and injured Seventh and Tenth Brother. You must avenge us!" shouted the second brother.

"Good, as long as they aren't dead! You brainless brutes, control your lust—do you think this is Nan mountain?!"

"Kong Ri Tian, Long Qing Tian, Wu Shen Tong, or Yi Chong could crush you like ants! Mind yourselves on San Cha mountain!"

"Don't provoke Hu Mei Er. That seductress is deeply connected with Lin San Chi, Li Xian, and even Mo Wu Tian."

Sensing the moment, Tyrant Heng Mei shifted tone: "No need to worry; rank four and five Gu Masters here are keeping each other in check, so none dare act rashly. We, the Ten Tyrants of Nan mountain, cannot swallow this insult. Come, accompany me to find the black and white demons."

"Hmph, what do you know!" Tyrant Heng Mei chided. "The black and white demons are young. Spared you means they respect my name and showed restraint. But youngsters prize 'face'. If I put aside my ego to visit them personally, it grants them full respect. When I propose allying to deal with Hu Mei Er, they'll certainly agree. Hehehe..."

Laughing loudly, Tyrant Heng Mei waved his hand: "Brothers, come with me to visit the black and white demons!"

Fang and Bai looked up at San Cha mountain's peak.

Entrances to the three kings inheritance lay above the three peaks.

Periodically, the mountain peaks shot out three pillars of light, allowing people to enter.

Once entrant quotas were met, the light pillars faded and the entrances vanished.

Failed Gu Masters were expelled periodically, while many died within the inheritance tests.

"Eight days remain until the next opening. Let's settle there," Fang Yuan indicated a halfway cave.

# Chapter 340: Beaten to a pulp

Stronger Gu Masters claimed caves closer to the peak for prime inheritance access.

The cave Fang Yuan selected lay halfway up the mountain; traces around it clearly indicated an occupant.

Sure enough, as they approached, a loud voice boomed from inside: "This is Mang Kuang's territory! Get lost, or die violently!"

"Who the hell is Mang Kuang? I like this place—get lost," Fang Yuan laughed.

"Two blind fools... eh?!"

Mang Kuang charged out cursing, but seeing Fang and Bai, his jaw dropped in terror.

"Are you... the black and white twin demons?"

"What do you think?" Bai Ning Bing laughed faintly, blue eyes flashing with killing intent.

"It is my greatest honor that both lords fancy my cave!"

"Sensible. Now get lost," Fang Yuan waved his hand.

"Yes, yes!" As if pardoned, Mang Kuang slipped away and fled at full speed.

Having driven Mang Kuang away, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing entered the cave.

"San Cha mountain is dangerous; we'll take turns keeping watch over these eight days," Fang Yuan instructed.

"Naturally." Bai Ning Bing nodded.

"Hu Mei Er will send trouble, but fools delivering themselves will only help establish our dominance."

Just as Fang Yuan finished speaking, a voice called from outside.

"Are the black and white twin demons inside? I am Tyrant Heng Mei, and I've come specifically to pay my respects."

"Tyrant Heng Mei? The Ten Tyrants' boss? I heard he's a ruthless strength path cultivator who terrorizes Nan mountain and eats children." Bai Ning Bing glanced at Fang Yuan.

The two walked out, observing the eight people standing outside.

In front stood a robust man with a bare, hair-covered chest, exuding the dense aura of a rank four middle stage Gu Master.

Yet this savage demonic figure was smiling politely, cupping his hands like an elegant black bear.

"Tyrant Heng Mei, good timing—I was just about to visit you," Fang Yuan responded.

"You are a strength path Gu Master? Let us fight to the death—I shall be number one on the strength path! Die!"

With that, Fang Yuan activated his Gu and charged straight at Tyrant Heng Mei.

All-out effort Gu !

A brown bear phantom appeared from behind him.

Furious, Tyrant Heng Mei did not dodge, raising his arms to block head-on.

Activating violent strength Gu, his body expanded, strength surging sharply.

Fang Yuan slammed into Tyrant Heng Mei, knocking him back five steps while rebounding from the terrifying force.

"Hmph! Little Beast King, I gave you face, but you refuse it!" Tyrant Heng Mei shouted, though he refrained from attacking.

"Why so cautious? Bai Ning Bing, handle the others—Tyrant Heng Mei is mine!" Fang Yuan charged again.

"You have five minutes. If you can't finish him by then, I'll take over." Bai Ning Bing raised a brow, blue eyes flashing sharply.

Fight!

Two sides collided.

Fang Yuan fought Tyrant Heng Mei while Bai Ning Bing suppressed the remaining Ten Tyrants.

With bitter strength Gu, Fang Yuan only grew stronger as injuries mounted, terrifying Heng Mei.

When Fang Yuan unleashed six beast phantoms at once, Tyrant Heng Mei turned pale.

"How is he so strong?! Superior Gu, vast battle experience, ruthless skill—he is simply a hundred-year-old monster!"

"No wonder he acts so overbearingly. With such strength, even I would act fearlessly. I've failed badly—I must retreat!"

Fang Yuan's ferocious strikes surged like tidal waves, throwing Tyrant Heng Mei's blood and energy into complete disorder.

Tyrant Heng Mei fell into a passive defense, while Fang Yuan fought without restraint, his attacks surging like endless tidal waves.

"Fang Zheng, don't go too far!" Spitting blood with shattered limbs, Tyrant Heng Mei tried to flee, but Bai Ning Bing had already slaughtered the rest and blocked him.

Fang Yuan pointed, and six massive beast phantoms solidified, crashing from the sky to engulf Tyrant Heng Mei.

A loud sound echoed and dust scattered everywhere.

Tyrant Heng Mei was crushed to pulp, organs and bones splattering everywhere.

# Chapter 341: What ferocity and wickedness!

Tian Ti Mountain was Central Continent's foremost mountain, known as the land of inheritances and the mountain of sages.

This year, Gu Immortals sealed Tian Ti Mountain for the Hu Immortal blessed land opening, staging a contest for their sects' juniors.

At the foot of Tian Ti Mountain, an intense battle among the youths was nearing its end.

Ten thousand cranes circled as Wei Wu Shang panted within the encirclement in ragged clothes.

"No, I haven't lost yet! I still have my trump card!" Refusing to concede defeat, Wei Wu Shang rallied.

However, Fang Zheng looked like he was facing a great enemy.

Lord Sky Crane warned: "Defend with all your might! This kid has injury wind Gu—it looks harmless, but is terrifying. This is his trump card!"

As the injury wind blew, Fang Zheng abandoned attacking and defended with all his might.

Wherever the seemingly gentle wind blew, ironbeak flying cranes shrieked and plummeted with broken wings.

As the injury wind struck Fang Zheng, the defensive light around him flickered continuously.

After a brief stalemate, Wei Wu Shang sighed inwardly and ceased activating injury wind Gu.

"I lost." Wei Wu Shang's gaze was dark.

With his primeval essence exhausted, he could no longer fight against Fang Zheng's crane flock.

"Brother Wei's methods have broadened my horizons. My essence is also nearly spent—shall we call this a draw?" Fang Zheng offered.

"What?" Wei Wu Shang was stunned.

Given a way out, Wei Wu Shang cupped his fists: "Brother Fang is a true young hero; I am full of admiration. Like Brother Fang said, we will call this a draw."

Wei Wu Shang secretly transmitted: "Fang Zheng, I will repay your leniency. But Fairy Bi Xia is my love; I will spar with you again!"

Fang Zheng smiled and nodded outwardly, but felt a headache within.

Wei Wu Shang continued transmitting: "Fang Zheng, be careful. Fairy Bi Xia has many admirers beyond me. Even with your strength, you still have four competitors. Chen Da Jiang, Gu Ting, Tang Ru Qi, and Zhao Shu Ye. Since you're close with Bi Xia, they will certainly come for you. Don't lose."

Sure enough, three days later, Ancient Soul Sect disciples arrived at Tian Ti Mountain. Led by Gu Ting, who immediately challenged Fang Zheng upon learning of his connection to Fairy Bi Xia.

To avoid trouble, Fang Zheng chose to decline the fight.

On the eighth day, Ancient Soul Sect blocked Fang Zheng's cave, cursing incessantly.

When they insulted Immortal Crane Sect, Fang Zheng sighed helplessly and walked out of the cave.

Lord Sky Crane laughed: "Do you understand now, disciple? Blindly enduring only invites bullying. The more cowardly you appear, the more you will be bullied. Defeat Gu Ting, silence them, and build your reputation!"

"I truly understand now," Fang Zheng realized. "I must fight!"

*Gu Ting, come, let's fight!*

Around the time of Fang Zheng's fight with Gu Ting, a battle concluded far away on Southern Border's San Cha Mountain.

Fang Yuan stood proudly on the battlefield while his opponent, Fei Li, knelt on the ground kowtowing for mercy.

"Lord Fang Zheng, please be magnanimous and spare me!" Fei Li begged bitterly, kowtowing repeatedly.

A once-vigorous rank four strength path Gu Master, Fei Li was now soaked in blood with broken limbs, cutting a wretched figure.

"Hand over your expend strength Gu first, and I will consider it," Fang Yuan said coldly.

Fei Li hesitated before handing over expend strength Gu.

This vital, core Gu forced enemies to expend twice as much stamina with every movement.

Surrendering his vital Gu, Fei Li suffered a severe backlash, vomiting mouthfuls of blood.

Accepting the expend strength Gu, Fang Yuan's eyes flashed: "I've considered it—expend strength Gu isn't enough to buy your life."

With a thought from Fang Yuan, beast phantoms pounced forward and shredded Fei Li into minced meat.

"Pauper," Fang Yuan sneered, looking down at the mangled corpse.
