Fang Yuan laughed: "Tell Sky Tiger Xue San Si that as a fellow strength cultivator, I will visit in three days to spar!"

# Chapter 342: Biao

"What, the Little Beast King is challenging me?" Xue San Si refused to believe the news at first.

She had never met or offended the Black and White Demons; why trouble her?

But soon, she investigated, and realized this was true!

"What is the Little Beast King thinking? The three kings inheritance opens in a few days, yet he picks a fight with me? What did I do to provoke him?!"

With her godfather in closed cultivation refining Gu, Fang Yuan's public challenge left Xue San Si no choice.

To defend her reputation, Xue San Si accepted Fang Yuan's challenge.

They chose a wide valley as the battlefield.

Three days later, Xue San Si saw Fang Yuan for the first time.

To her surprise, Fang Yuan was not the savage brute she had imagined. Instead, he appeared refined and gentlemanly, his deep, dark eyes utterly tranquil.

"Sky Tiger, exiled after offending a clan elder, you risked your life hunting beast kings to become an expert. Then you returned and slaughtered the elders who framed you. Frankly, I admire that quite a bit," Fang Yuan said.

Amid the onlookers' chatter, Xue San Si replied: "I'm flattered. Little Beast King, the name of the Black and White Demons is renowned here as well."

Fang Yuan smiled: "We both cultivate the strength path. My fights with Tyrant Heng Mei and Fei Li provided great experience. But such a sparring method is boring to me now. This time, why don't we change the sparring method."

Xue San Si raised her brows: "Oh? What do you propose? I'm listening."

"Simple. I will take your hits, then strike back. Since you're a woman, I'll let you strike three times before I retaliate once," Fang Yuan smiled.

"Spar like that?" Xue San Si's eyes brightened, intrigued by the proposal.

"Alright, let's do that." Xue San Si considered it through, agreeing.

"Please." Fang Yuan smiled, standing still and extending his right palm.

Her eyes flashing with surging killing intent, Xue San Si shouted, "First move!"

Her slender arms glowed brightly, transforming into tiger claws.

The tiger claws tore through the sky, hitting Fang Yuan harshly.

Coughing up blood, Fang Yuan flew back like a cannonball and crashed into a massive boulder.

"Good hit!" Fang Yuan laughed, standing up. His chest was visibly caved in with at least four broken ribs.

Xue San Si's expression changed.

"Fang Zheng planned this well—the more injured he gets, the stronger he becomes. Should I strike harder or softer?"

"No, I should use all my strength to attack. Kill him in three hits!" Xue San Si considered as she made up her mind.

"Second move!"

Shouting, she activated her Gu, swelling to twice her size!

As she pounced at Fang Yuan, a beast phantom manifested behind her.

A fierce, muscular tiger with brownish-yellow fur roared toward the heavens with an imposing aura, sporting a pair of wings upon its back.

The tiger was king of beasts, and the Biao—a winged tiger—was king of tigers. *Three hounds make a molosser, five tigers make a Biao!*

With her strength already more than doubled, Xue San Si unleashed the power of a Biao!

Under this immense force, Fang Yuan flew several hundred steps, crashing into a mountain wall.

The mountain rocks collapsed, engulfing Fang Yuan.

Shoving aside rubble, Fang Yuan emerged with crippled arms, fractured bones, and torn skin.

Activating self-reliance Gu, his recovery speed far surpassed what flesh-bone Gu could achieve.

"Third move!" Anxious to deny Fang Yuan time to heal, Xue San Si shouted.

Flapping the large, brownish-yellow wings, Xue San Si soared into the sky.

Climbing nearly a kilometer high, she wheeled around and dove downward.

As everyone watched nervously, Xue San Si charged towards Fang Yuan.

The image of a Biao appeared again.

"With this move, you're finished!" Xue San Si growled with surging killing intent.

Fang Yuan looked up at her, activating golden shield Gu.

---

# Chapter 343: Shameless and Despicable

Seeing Fang Yuan activate golden shield Gu, she sneered inwardly.

"Rank three golden shield Gu? Even the advanced rank four golden bell shield couldn't withstand my attack!"

Fang Yuan looked up at Xue San Si with an indifferent gaze, mockery hidden deep within his pupils.

Fang Yuan's eyes flashed as he activated a Gu.

He charged left, then turned around.

He charged a hundred steps away.

"Little Beast King, you!" Enraged to see Fang Yuan suddenly flee, Xue San Si's eyes widened almost to tearing!

She tried desperately to control her dive, but her speed was too fast, allowing Fang Yuan to escape her attack range.

Before spectators could react, an enormous explosion detonated like thunder.

*BOOM!*

Xue San Si slammed into the ground, shaking the valley.

As the spectators reeled from the destructive impact, Fang Yuan suddenly pivoted and dashed into the smoke.

Horizontal charge Gu, vertical crash Gu!

In the center of the massive crater, Xue San Si was caked in dirt, cutting a sorry figure.

Bitter strength Gu, all-out effort Gu!

Fang Yuan hurried, an ominous glint shot out from his eyes.

Eight beasts roared one after another.

Eight beast phantoms—boar, bear, crocodile, bull, horse, turtle, elephant, and python—roared above Fang Yuan!

Strength Qi Gu!

With a thought from Fang Yuan, the eight beast phantoms plunged toward the crater.

Trembling as she struggled to pull her legs from the ground, Xue San Si suddenly heard beast roars overhead.

Looking up into darkness, she was engulfed by a torrential storm of attacks.

*Bam Bam Bam...* brutal sounds of battle echoed from the crater.

Within three minutes, battle ceased; Fang Yuan recalled the five surviving phantoms.

Xue San Si met a gruesome end, her mangled remains strewn across the crater.

Smoke and dust dispersed, the whole battlefield became clear.

At this outcome, the spectators erupted into an uproar.

"Sky Tiger is dead—another rank four Gu Master killed by the Little Beast King!"

Bai Ning Bing chuckled heartily, sweeping an indifferent gaze across the battlefield.

Surrounded by insults and ridicule, Fang Yuan stood calmly with hands behind his back, narrowing his eyes as if enjoying it.

To him, 'sincerity' was merely the weak's fear of deception, begging others to follow their rules.

Walking the demonic path, one should have no restraints and taboos.

So what if he went against the agreement?

So what if he did not keep his promise?

With sufficient strength, one could act as they pleased. What did others' hollow ridicule matter? Such futile words were merely the helplessness of the weak, unable to harm a single hair of his.

'Hehehe....this Xue San Si is really stupid. She was actually bound by reputation and came running over to accept my challenge.'

Fang Yuan sneered: "Reputation is merely a tool to be used. Yet so many foolishly value fame over life itself. Laughable! Of course, I did use some tricks in this match."

Unlike Tyrant Heng Mei and Fei Li, Xue San Si had flight-type Gu; had things turned bad, she could simply fly high out of Fang Yuan's reach.

Thus, Fang Yuan had proposed the agreement specifically to restrain her mobility.

When proposing the three strikes, he had never planned to abide by them.

Xue San Si fell step by step into his trap, lured forward by Fang Yuan's mounting injuries.

After the second strike, Fang Yuan deliberately healed in front of her. Anxious not to waste her efforts, Xue San Si impulsively soared into the sky to unleash her strongest attack.

Ultimately, Fang Yuan borrowed her own strength to exhaust her with minimal effort, then struck and killed her.

In a direct battle, Xue San Si had wings and would never have fallen so easily to Fang Yuan.

"That's right. I admit defeat, so what?" Fang Yuan scoffed loudly.

His voice reverberated in the valley.

The clamorous din suddenly quietened down...

Fang Yuan laughed heartily: "Want revenge for Xue San Si? Come on, everyone wanting revenge can attack me together!"

The spectators' fierce criticism ceased, plunging the surroundings into instant silence.

Demonic Gu Masters were suspicious loners who restrained one another, while righteous path cultivators dared not act rashly.

"Is there no one seeking revenge for her? Anyone?" Fang Yuan asked loudly again.

"If not, I'm leaving." Fang Yuan raised his brows, took a few steps, then paused. "I'm really leaving."

"Hahaha..." Fang Yuan laughed to the sky and strode away, quickly leaving the battlefield.

---

# Chapter 344: Talented without Virtue

"Big brother Li, you have to act..." Hu Mei Er leaned against Li Xian's chest, speaking coquettishly.

Li Xian was a rank four upper stage demonic merchant who rarely fought, making his living through connections.

Hu Mei Er pushed Li Xian and stood up aggrievedly: "Big brother Li, give me a straight answer! After all my begging, have you no pity for me?"

"My treasure, dealing with the twin demons is tricky. They're two rank four Gu Masters, while I'm only one person," Li Xian spread his arms helplessly.

Hu Mei Er snorted: "Fine, I'll lower my demand. Just deal with little beast king—Fang Zheng is the most hateful! You can leave Bai Ning Bing alone for now."

He shifted the topic: "The three kings inheritance opens soon; once it ends, I'll teach little beast king a lesson."

"Hmph, little temptress!" Back in his cave, the lust on Li Xian's face vanished, replaced by a cautious, gloomy expression.

"Trying to manipulate me into fighting little beast king? She truly thinks she's attained greater mastery in enchantment path. Ridiculous!" Li Xian smiled coldly. "I certainly have ways to deal with the twin demons. But when has Li Xian ever done unprofitable business? Hehe."

"Fang Zheng is far from simple. Challenging three demonic Gu Masters before the inheritance opened was definitely premeditated!"

"He established his reputation so that when the inheritance opens, no one dares block his path," Li Xian mused.

Ever since Fang Yuan killed Xue San Si, even he felt some fear towards this little beast king.

Killing Tyrant Heng Mei and Fei Li had not drawn Li Xian's attention, but Fang Yuan breaking his promise to kill Sky Tiger revealed just how thorny he was.

"Had I discovered him early on, I would have suppressed and killed him. But now, it's far too late..." Li Xian sighed deeply.

Fang Yuan had matured and had a companion. Though Li Xian possessed rank four upper stage cultivation, his specialty was business, not combat.

"Far from offending him, I'll build a cooperative relationship. Demonic figures always prioritize benefits."

"Still, by killing Xue San Si, little beast king offended her godfather, the rank four upper stage Gu Master Century Boy. He's been refining Gu in seclusion, but will certainly appear when the inheritance opens. He will surely target the twin demons then."

"I'll secretly befriend little beast king while watching from the sidelines, seizing my opportunity once the outcome is clear!"

Though Fang Yuan was only rank four initial stage, his nine eyes liquor worm produced bright golden primeval essence.

"My reputation has soared lately. Countless people hate and fear me, but what does that matter?"

"The three kings inheritance opens in two days. Unlikely as it is for anyone to trouble me now, I'll use this time to refine charging crash Gu."

---

# Chapter 345: Charging Crash Gu

When Fang Yuan chose horizontal charge and vertical crash Gu, he had planned ahead.

Fang Yuan was well prepared, and the recipe itself had a high success rate.

Two days later, Fang Yuan successfully refined a brand-new Gu: charging crash Gu.

Together, horizontal charge Gu and vertical crash Gu could only cover a hundred steps.

Charging crash Gu increased the distance to two hundred steps and halved the cooldown.

Primeval essence consumption increased slightly, but was negligible with his nine eyes liquor worm and treasure lotus.

"I now have six rank four Gu: bitter strength, charging crash, Yang Gu, nine eyes liquor worm, expend strength, and blood skull."

"All-out effort Gu, strength qi Gu, golden shield Gu, and the rest are still rank three, gradually failing to meet my needs."

Upgrading rank three all-out effort Gu required undefeated hundred battles Gu from the three kings inheritance, which had to wait.

The eight beast phantoms in his body were also not from rank four strength Gu and needed replacement.

"Only by replacing my ordinary beast phantoms with Biao and mastiffs can I display peak battle strength."

Bai Ning Bing was already waiting at the cave entrance, gazing toward the peak.

Fang Yuan followed her gaze and also looked up.

Enormous pillars of light rose from San Cha mountain's three highest peaks, piercing the clouds.

The three kings inheritance had opened.

"With this inheritance, my strength path Gu set will reach perfection!" Fang Yuan's burning gaze was quickly restrained.

Despite his outstanding battle prowess, it was not yet his turn to take the stage.

Four figures shot out from the cardinal directions, halting in midair to confront one another.

Rank four peak stage aura were let out without any concealment!

From the east came poison path Gu Master Long Qing Tian.

From the west came Wu clan elder Wu Shen Tong.

From the south came demonic expert Kong Ri Tian.

From the northern direction was Yi clan's elder Yi Chong.

These four were currently the strongest Gu Masters at San Cha mountain.

Righteous path's Wu Shen Tong and Yi Chong were only nominal allies, while Kong Ri Tian and Long Qing Tian would cooperate if pressed.

After a brief stalemate, Kong Ri Tian chose King Bao's inheritance, Long Qing Tian took King Xin's, and Wu Shen Tong chose King Quan's.

The remaining Gu Master, Yi Chong, hesitated before choosing King Bao's inheritance.

Amid the crowd's discussions, a group of Gu Masters ascended the peak.

Among them was a black-clothed boy who gazed deeply at Fang Yuan before entering.

"Hmph, Century Boy..." Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes, exuding a dangerous aura.

Century Boy was a strength path Gu Master and Xue San Si's patron godfather.

Century Boy had no time to settle scores now, but would certainly target Fang Yuan once it closed.

Fang Yuan harbored no fear, secretly looking forward to it.

"Once I upgrade my Gu to rank four, I can match peak stage experts! Three kings inheritance, here I come!"

Fang Yuan activated charging crash Gu and charged toward the peak.

Ascending rank four Gu Masters paled seeing the twin demons charge up, hastily parting to make way.

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing entered King Quan's inheritance together.

---

# Chapter 346: First entry into King Quan Inheritance

When his vision cleared, he found himself standing in the wilderness.

White sky, grey earth, and yellow grass—a world of merely three colors.

Dead silence reigned: no wind, leaves, or animal cries.

Though he had entered with Bai Ning Bing, Fang Yuan was currently alone.

But Fang Yuan was extremely calm.

"So this is King Quan's inheritance," he muttered softly, looking around.

The three kings inheritance was built inside a Gu Immortal blessed land.

"Bai Ning Bing was surely sent elsewhere in this small world, but as long as she advances through the inheritance, we'll meet along the way."

"Time flows thrice as fast here." In his aperture, the slumbering Spring Autumn Cicada had awakened, recovering at triple speed.

A rank four aperture could not withstand the pressure of a fully recovered Spring Autumn Cicada.

"One day here is three days outside; I must hurry." Fang Yuan felt urgent.

Thankfully, his memories made this rank five King Quan Inheritance familiar to him.

"If I'm not wrong, there should be a canine wild beast nearby..." Fang Yuan searched the surroundings.

A bark sounded from afar as an emaciated wild dog with faintly glowing green eyes charged toward him.

Fang Yuan frowned; encountering such an ordinary wild dog in the first stage was bad luck.

He stood quietly, letting the dog rush toward him.

Here, Fang Yuan could not use his other Gu worms—only rank one dog enslavement Gu.

Seeing the wild dog rush towards him, Fang Yuan activated the dog enslavement Gu in his aperture.

He used only a single drop of light gold primeval essence to activate it.

The dog enslavement Gu turned into jade light and flew into the wild dog.

The wild dog yelped as if heavily injured and collapsed to the ground.

This time, instead of baring its fangs, it sprawled on the ground, panting and wagging its tail.

Light flashed, and a second dog enslavement Gu appeared before him.

Recognizing it as both reward and key to the second round, Fang Yuan quickly refined it.

Once stored in his aperture, he vanished with a *swoosh*, teleported away by the blessed land's power.

Fang Yuan appeared on a low mound, surrounded by three wild dogs.

They charged like lightning toward Fang Yuan from three directions.

Fang Yuan commanded his wild dog forward, directing its ferocity against one of the attackers.

It could only intercept one, leaving the second to close in on him.

Fang Yuan repeated his earlier method, taming it with the second dog enslavement Gu.

As the third dog pounced, Fang Yuan split his focus, commanding both tamed dogs to attack.

Across five hundred years of experience, Fang Yuan had commanded countless beast groups, especially blood bats.

For this inheritance, he had specifically spent months in Shang clan city training dog-group manipulation.

Fang Yuan frowned: "The first dog is in terrible shape. Even if I win like this, only one will survive."

Thus, he directed the two dogs to fight while retreating.

Eventually, Fang Yuan successfully merged the two battles together.

His two dogs cooperated seamlessly while their enemies fought individually.

Two wild dog corpses lay on the ground, pooling with blood.

Fang Yuan's two dogs stood covered in wounds; the first was heavily injured, while the second was in better shape.

"Third round..." Fang Yuan observed his surroundings immediately.

This time, he stood atop a mound surrounded by six wild dogs.

With just two injured dogs, he faced three times their numbers.

Spotting a nearby pit, Fang Yuan's eyes brightened and he leaped inside.

With solid earthen walls guarding his back, he stationed both dogs in front.

Two healthy wild dogs lunged first, while four injured dogs lagged behind.

Six healthy dogs would have left him no chance, but with the terrain and his skilled control, he was confident of advancing!

---

# Chapter 347: What level of proficiency in enslavement path is this!

Dozens of dogs fought fiercely; Fang Yuan's smaller pack maintained tight coordination, shielding, retreating, and attacking with precision.

Fang Yuan stood atop a nearby hill, overlooking the battlefield and commanding his pack.

This was already his tenth battle in King Quan's inheritance.

Nineteen dogs remained standing across the battlefield, all accumulated through Fang Yuan's painstaking efforts.

"Difficulty spikes every ten rounds. I sacrificed nine dogs from twenty-eight to repel sixty enemies and clear this hurdle."

Among the three kings, King Quan specialized in enslavement path, King Xin in refinement, and King Bao in fire. Their chosen successors naturally followed these paths.

The first ten rounds ruthlessly eliminated most Gu Masters from other paths.

Especially in the final ten rounds, from the ninetieth to hundredth, the difficulty was an insurmountable chasm that even a monster like Fang Yuan could not cross.

He knew no matter which inheritance he chose, he could only charge to the ninetieth round with his strength.

Beyond the ninetieth round, only rank five Gu Masters stood a chance of clearing the trials.

In a split second, he was transported to another strange place.

Unlike the previous ten rounds, his vision was obscured by dense grey fog, making it impossible to discern directions.

Unflustered, Fang Yuan knew that starting from the tenth round, challengers were given choices.

To his left deep in the fog shone a lantern-like orange glow, accompanied by the clear barking of a dog.

Ahead stood an indistinct human shadow, seeming both near and far.

To his right, a ball of blue lightning crackled deep within the grey fog.

"The orange light represents loyal chrysanthemum akita dogs capable of exquisite tactics; the blue light signifies lightning symbol dogs, twice as fast as ordinary dogs. And the black figure should be my opponent, another Gu Master," Fang Yuan pondered.

After brief consideration, Fang Yuan walked straight ahead, abandoning the left and right paths.

As he advanced, the fog thinned and his control over his nineteen enslaved dogs strengthened.

Han Bu Liu stood still, calmly watching the person emerge from the fog.

Han Bu Liu, a forty-eight-year-old rank four middle stage righteous Gu Master, had entered King Quan's inheritance many times.

Seeing Fang Yuan walking slowly toward him through the thinning fog, Han Bu Liu recognized his opponent immediately.

As an enslavement path Gu Master, he was like a fish in water in King Quan's inheritance.

Challengers could only fight using Gu provided by the inheritance, sharply narrowing cultivation differences.

Fang Yuan stepped out of the fog, his eyes abyssal and his expression tranquil as water.

Han Bu Liu's pupils shrank in terror.

"It is little beast king!"

Han Bu Liu regained his senses: "This is the blessed land—Little Beast King cannot use his usual Gu set! A young strength path cultivator cannot possess enslavement path proficiency!"

Gleaming with greed, Han Bu Liu roared inwardly: "A heaven-sent opportunity! Killing Little Beast King will bring fame and a fortune in Gu! Dogs, charge!"

Crucially, because Gu Masters could not use their Gu worms in this blessed land, they could not detonate them upon death.

Outside, Gu Masters self-destructed their Gu upon death, but here, slain cultivators left their Gu intact, enticing murder.

With a shift of his mind, eleven dogs charged towards Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan was indifferent, he waited till these dogs neared him then waved his hand.

With a light wave, nineteen dogs rushed out from the grey fog behind him.

"Nineteen?" Han Bu Liu was extremely shocked, his eyes almost falling from their sockets.

An experienced enslavement master, he had managed to retain only eleven dogs after completing the tenth round despite his greatest efforts.

What level of proficiency in enslavement path was this!

"Eh? A flaw." Fang Yuan had been observing his opponent from the start.

Seeing Han Bu Liu hesitate, Fang Yuan grasped the chance, mobilizing his strongest dogs into an offensive arrow formation.

The next moment, this 'arrow' suddenly broke the opponent's formation.

Fang Yuan's remaining dogs formed two exquisite arcs, outflanking the opponent's formation and seizing control instantly!

---

# Chapter 348: Chrysanthemum Akita Dog

Han Bu Liu resisted desperately, but Fang Yuan shattered his tactics, effortlessly suppressing all resistance.

An hour later, only eight wild dogs remained—all belonging to Fang Yuan.

Pale and sweating profusely, Han Bu Liu stood dazed on the ground.

He dropped to his knees, begging: "Lord Little Beast King, spare my life! I only fought out of desperation. Your enslavement path mastery leaves me speechless in admiration. From today onward, you are my master!"

"How do you show sincerity—unless you accept a slavery Gu?" Fang Yuan chuckled, directing his dogs to surround him.

Han Bu Liu panicked: "L-Lord, inside this blessed land, Gu Masters cannot use their Gu worms. Besides, I don't have a slavery Gu."

"Then I have no choice." Fang Yuan waved coldly, and the eight dogs pounced.

"Lord Fang Zheng! No!" Han Bu Liu jolted up, screaming in fear, trying to escape.

It was too late; the eight dogs surrounded him, tearing into his body with sharp fangs.

Fang Yuan watched silently, until he was completely dead and motionless.

With a light wave of his hand, the eight dogs ceased their attack and parted to open a path.

But his stomach area was consciously left intact by Fang Yuan's control.

Placing his palm on Han Bu Liu's abdomen, his mind entered the aperture.

This was a standard rank four middle stage aperture.

Due to the blessed land's laws, all of Han Bu Liu's Gu worms were suppressed and motionless atop the primeval sea.

There were five enslavement path Gu, including two rank four Gu.

Fang Yuan retrieved them all, easily refining them into his own aperture.

Fang Yuan killed Han Bu Liu, having seven wild dogs left.

"Only seven injured dogs remain, but compared to my gains, the loss is insignificant."

"In this inheritance, Gu Masters are moving treasures! Slaying them strengthens oneself faster!" As heavenly power descended, Fang Yuan sneered with killing intent.

The misty fog appeared before him again.

Directly in front of him was a black shadow representing an enemy Gu Master.

To the left was another black shadow with spiky protrusions along its side.

To the right was an orange shadow representing the chrysanthemum akita dog.

"With only seven wild dogs and lacking battle strength, I cannot choose a Gu Master opponent. To the left is the hedgedog, covered in spikes with twice the attack power of an ordinary dog. The right leads to the chrysanthemum akita dog..."

Fang Yuan's eyeballs darted around, and he walked to the right.

Fang Yuan took large steps forward, and the fog thinned.

Near his leg, the seven wild dogs followed closely.

Dog enslavement Gu materialized in midair, landing in Fang Yuan's hands.

When the fog cleared, Fang Yuan received twenty rank one dog enslavement Gu.

Nearly sixty chrysanthemum akita dogs appeared before him.

These dogs had orange-yellow fur patterned with chrysanthemums, and long drooped ears covering their ear holes.

But Fang Yuan's expression changed.

Chrysanthemum akita dogs excelled at pack cooperation. Exposed on an open plain, Fang Yuan's seven injured dogs would easily be engulfed.

"I must reach that mound!" Surveying his surroundings, Fang Yuan sprinted toward the high ground.

He ran for five steps before the chrysanthemum akita dogs found him.

Immediately, the surrounding dogs converged into a river-like formation, charging toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan barely rushed to the top of the mound before the dogs encircled it.

Fang Yuan retreated until there was a cliff behind him.

The chrysanthemum akita dogs paused briefly before charging anew.

Because of the narrow terrain, the pack could only send fifteen dogs at once.

Fang Yuan quickly commanded his seven wild dogs to defend against them.

Simultaneously, he waved his hand, tossing out five dog enslavement Gu with perfect timing.

Five healthy chrysanthemum akita dogs instantly turned to join Fang Yuan, reversing the situation.

The original fifteen-against-seven shifted to ten against twelve in Fang Yuan's favor.

As companions turned into enemies, the dog pack panicked. Fang Yuan seized the opportunity to kill six, forcing the other four to retreat.

The chrysanthemum akita dog pack attacked Fang Yuan's defensive line again.

Calculating flawlessly, Fang Yuan deployed dog enslavement Gu at key moments, securing the healthiest targets while disrupting enemy formations.

After nearly ten attacks, the chrysanthemum akita dogs were repelled every time.

With three dog enslavement Gu left, the remaining dozen chrysanthemum akita dogs fled in fear.

"Without a dog king to command them, they fled easily." Fang Yuan exhaled turbid air, choosing not to pursue.

Though his wild dogs were reduced to three, he gained sixteen chrysanthemum akita dogs.

Under his control, injuries were distributed evenly, preserving the pack's combat strength.

---

# Chapter 349: Big Lightning Symbol Dog

Advancing steadily, Fang Yuan's pack grew to over eighty dogs by the end of the nineteenth round.

Among them were over forty chrysanthemum akita dogs, more than twenty lightning symbol dogs, and nineteen hedgedogs.

"Twentieth round." Fang Yuan had been counting in his heart.

Three glowing silhouettes appeared in the fog: left, center, and right.

Ahead were two hundred chrysanthemum akita dogs; to the left, one hundred fifty lightning symbol dogs; to the right, a hundred Yin dogs.

Fang Yuan eliminated the chrysanthemum akita dogs from his choice.

Led by a dog king, over two hundred chrysanthemum akita dogs would fight with deadly unity—a calamity for Fang Yuan's current force.

Next, he ruled out the Yin dog group.

Yin dogs were incorporeal energy, completely immune to ordinary physical attacks.

Fang Yuan could not harm them, as lightning symbol dogs lacked ranged lightning.

After thinking for a while, Fang Yuan chose the left path.

As the fog dispersed, the pack escorted him forward while a rank two dog enslavement Gu descended into his palm.

Emerging from the fog, he saw the lightning symbol dog pack roaming the area.

Atop the mound lay a larger dog, sleeping on the grass.

This was a hundred beast king — Big Lightning Symbol Dog!

"A godsend opportunity!" Assessing the battlefield, Fang Yuan's eyes shone as he made an instant decision.

He waved his hand, and sent out all his troops.

Over eighty dogs charged toward the big lightning symbol dog.

Reacting quickly, the big lightning symbol dog twitched its ears, opened its eyes, and stood up.

Raising its head, it howled to rally the nearby pack.

The defending lightning symbol dogs were quickly overwhelmed.

Fang Yuan maintained a tight formation, while the lightning symbol dogs were scattered and had yet to assemble around their king.

Seeing its subjects slaughtered, the big lightning symbol dog's ferocity erupted as it brandished its claws and lunged at Fang Yuan.

At Fang Yuan's command, lightning symbol dogs flanked, hedgedogs charged the king, and chrysanthemum akita dogs formed a protective screen.

The big lightning symbol dog was trapped in the middle of Fang Yuan's encirclement.

Trapping the king while blocking the outer pack exerted immense pressure on Fang Yuan, making his head throb as sweat poured from his forehead.

Ignoring the sweat, Fang Yuan gripped the rank two dog enslavement Gu, waiting for the right moment.

"Woof!"

Growing anxious, the big lightning symbol dog opened its mouth and spat a lump of blue plasma.

The sticky blue plasma splashed onto the grass, crackling and charring the earth.

"A rank two plasma Gu!" Fang Yuan immediately recognized the culprit.

Though the sudden blast engulfed several chrysanthemum akita dogs, Fang Yuan promptly ordered them to retreat.

The big lightning symbol dog continued firing plasma, covering a large swath of grassland.

Fang Yuan's brows were tightly knitted, his expression turning grim.

Without any choice left, Fang Yuan had to redeploy his troops.

He ordered the lightning symbol dogs inward against the king, while sending chrysanthemum akitas outward to defend.

Lightning symbol dogs moved freely across the plasma-affected ground; the electrical stimulation even boosted their speed.

Outmatched in speed, the chrysanthemum akita dogs formed a tight defensive circle on the perimeter.

"Endure! We have to endure this! I've uncovered its first Gu, now we must keep probing..." Despite the peril, Fang Yuan remained calm.

As time passed, Fang Yuan's predicament grew increasingly dire.

Only nine lightning symbol dogs and eleven hedgedogs survived the brutal melee.

The chrysanthemum akita dogs suffered the heaviest losses, falling from around fifty to under twenty.

He decided to strike!

"I can't wait any longer. Most likely, this big lightning symbol dog only has one Gu."

Rank two dog enslavement Gu!

As the big lightning symbol dog discharged another plasma bolt, Fang Yuan struck with his Gu.

The dog enslavement Gu flew straight into the big lightning symbol dog, embedding itself in its soul.

A formless psychic impact assaulted Fang Yuan through the dog enslavement Gu.

Fang Yuan gritted his teeth, enduring.

A mental connection formed. The dog that had fiercely wanted to tear Fang Yuan apart moments ago now barked and wagged its tail affectionately.

Hearing the dog king's call, the surrounding lightning symbol dogs halted their attack.

The risk paid off!

Over a hundred and twenty lightning symbol dogs surrendered.

Not to mention a hundred beast king possessing a plasma Gu.

Fang Yuan's battle strength surged!

---

# Chapter 350: Dog Guts Gu

Fang Yuan walked through the fog beside a large pack of dogs.

He commanded eleven hedgedogs, one hundred thirty-two lightning symbol dogs, seventeen chrysanthemum akitas, and the beast king.

Though he had survived twenty rounds and commanded his largest pack yet, Fang Yuan was not optimistic.

"Difficulty multiplies every ten rounds. Reaching the thirtieth round on my first attempt will not be easy."

"The deeper one goes in King Quan inheritance, the more advantageous it is for enslavement path Gu Masters. I walked the enslavement path in my previous life, but now I cultivate strength path."

"Yet the enslavement path is tough. First, feeding larger beast or worm groups is a massive burden. Second, subduing strong beasts or worms is difficult, risking mental backlash that can turn one into an idiot. Third, enslavement path Gu Masters rely too heavily on their beasts and worms; their bodies are weak, making them easy prey when isolated," Fang Yuan analyzed.

He opened his eyes, looking around.

Shadows of light stood to his left, right, and front.

Previously blurry, the light shadows had become crystal clear after twenty rounds.

The left shadow revealed about two hundred chrysanthemum akita dogs.

"Where can the hundred beast king be?" Fang Yuan looked around, and suddenly the light shadow changed, showing a Gu.

Fist-sized and pebble-shaped, the dark brown Gu had a smooth, oily sheen.

"Dog guts Gu," Fang Yuan recognized instantly. "Appearing after the beast group means that if I defeat these chrysanthemum akita dogs, I will receive a dog guts Gu..."

Without deciding yet, Fang Yuan looked at the middle shadow.

The shadow ahead showed a pack of blackish dogs.

This was the steel armor dog.

These dogs possessed dark, steel-like hides providing formidable defense.

Matching the big lightning symbol dog in size, the steel armor hundred beast king stood out clearly, and Fang Yuan spotted it almost immediately.

Immediately after, the light shadow flashed, and a Gu appeared.

Rank two dog enslavement Gu.

"Defeating the steel armor dogs will reward me with a dog enslavement Gu..."

The right shadow showed no dogs, displaying instead a mountainous scene of grayish cliffs, rocks, and swaying green trees.

"This is a chance to leave King Quan inheritance," Fang Yuan realized.

Never lacking courage, Fang Yuan resolutely gave up the exit, leaving himself two options.

"Defeating the chrysanthemum akita dogs yields a rank two dog guts Gu. When activated, it boosts pack courage, eliminating fear and unlocking their battle strength."

"Rank two dog guts Gu lets ordinary dogs suppress their fear of hundred beast kings, while rank three dog guts above mountain Gu resists thousand beast kings. It drives dogs into a frenzied tsunami of attacks, granting them the courage to overthrow even a beast emperor..."

"Dog guts Gu is necessary if I want to conquer King Quan inheritance."

Yet instead of moving left, he stepped straight forward—

He chose the steel armor dog group.

Though he coveted the dog guts Gu, greed did not blind him.

As Fang Yuan emerged from the fog, the vigilant steel armor dogs reacted immediately.

The big steel armor dog howled, rallying the pack to gather at its side.

Having spent his enslavement Gu, he could not subjugate the king.

He had to exterminate this pack to clear the round and earn another dog enslavement Gu.

# Chapter 351: Multitask Gu

The two dog groups were at a stalemate.

Fang Yuan's mixed pack formed their battle lines against a sea of black steel armor dogs.

Fang Yuan sent the big lightning symbol dog forward, waving his hand to launch his vanguard into battle.

As a hundred beast king, the big lightning symbol dog held its own against the swarm, easily overpowering ordinary steel armor dogs.

The big steel armor dog stepped out, finally halting the big lightning symbol dog's assault.

Lingering injuries from the previous fight soon weakened the big lightning symbol dog. Furthermore, as a speed specialist clashing head-on against a defensive powerhouse, it was at a clear disadvantage.

Unable to block its foe's attack, the big lightning symbol dog barked and spat a blast of blue plasma.

Rank two plasma Gu!

"This is it." Fang Yuan's eyes shone brightly, as his lips curled up.

Clustered tightly around their king, the steel armor dogs crowded together like a black bucket.

Plasma showered over the steel armor dogs, shocking them and breaking their formation into disarray.

While their metallic armor provided superior defense, it also made the steel armor dogs utterly vulnerable to electricity.

Seizing the chance, Fang Yuan ordered a charge, coordinating his outer pack with the big lightning symbol dog to unleash ferocious offensive power.

The tide turned swiftly; Fang Yuan regained control, wiping out most of the pack and encircling the big steel armor dog.

Fifteen minutes later, the big lightning symbol dog tore out its throat; the dog king fell indignantly in a pool of blood, dead.

Of the king's two Gu worms, one was destroyed in the fight and the other escaped.

Fang Yuan looked grim; slaying this hundred beast king had cost him half his pack, complicating the battles ahead.

A rank two dog enslavement Gu descended from above as his reward.

Fang Yuan instantly refined the Gu using heavenly power.

"This Gu is the key to clearing the next round." Resolved, Fang Yuan stepped into the fog.

Three shadows appeared: a large pack of corpse dogs on the left, Yin dogs in the center, and a massive swarm of hyenas on the right.

"Defeating the corpse dogs yields a white silver relic Gu, while the Yin dogs grant a two tasks at once Gu..."

Fang Yuan looked right; as the hyenas faded, a token appeared.

Rough and irregularly shaped, the token bore an ugly carving of San Cha mountain.

Yet the moment Fang Yuan saw it, his eyes brightened.

"King Quan travel token!" He immediately forgot about the white silver relic Gu, two tasks at once Gu.

"The hyena pack has two kings. Even if I win, severe losses will halt my advance."

Fang Yuan thought about it, and decided to take a risk.

Four hours later.

Dog corpses covered the mound, blood pooling among severed limbs.

Fang Yuan stood wounded on the mound, severely weakened without his Gu.

"I won regardless..." Fang Yuan sighed deeply, looking around at his remaining dozen dogs.

Heavenly power descended, sweeping him back into the fog.

All three paths led to dog packs; without the travel token, he would surely die.

He took out the travel token.

Gripping it tightly, he bit his tongue and spat blood onto it.

As blood stained the token, Fang Yuan whispered to it, "Woof."

The token flared with brilliant light, tearing through space and dragging Fang Yuan with it.

# Chapter 352: Casually killed

In a bamboo forest at San Cha mountain's waist, space shattered in a burst of light.

A calm youth with black hair and dark eyes appeared holding a token—Fang Yuan.

Several deep cracks now ran across the surface of the unsightly King Quan token.

The King Quan travel token could only be used three times before shattering into useless fragments.

Fang Yuan stored this travel token carefully and hurried towards his cave.

Along the way, both righteous and demonic Gu Masters cast strange, astonished looks at him.

"What happened?" Fang Yuan frowned, keenly sensing something significant had occurred.

Halfway to his cave, he casually grabbed someone and questioned.

Bai Ning Bing had killed a Tie clansman in King Quan inheritance, and Tie clan immediately discovered her upon exiting.

Tie clan Gu Masters surrounded the cave and trapped Bai Ning Bing.

Four rank four elders used iron cabinet Gu to seal the area.

Unable to force her way out, Bai Ning Bing remained under relentless siege.

"Such a major incident..." Fang Yuan frowned, holding the captive by the collar.

"Tie clan possesses rust flower Gu housed in their souls; slaying a clansman stains the killer with a formless scent. No wonder Bai Ning Bing was discovered," Fang Yuan murmured.

His hand struck like an eagle's claw, seizing the rank two Gu Master's neck.

*Crack!*

The Gu Master's eyes widened as his neck vertebrae were crushed, killing him instantly.

*Gasp...* The hidden Gu Masters watching from the shadows inhaled sharply.

"Have you seen enough? If so, get lost!" Fang Yuan shouted arrogantly, glaring around with a fierce gaze.

Spies promptly emerged from bushes, trees, and crevices.

These figures quickly retreated and disappeared within seconds.

Fang Yuan snorted, swept his gaze around once more, and strode away.

Following cautiously, the Gu Masters were surprised when Fang Yuan halted before a cave.

Though Bai Ning Bing was trapped, Little Beast King seemed unconcerned, visiting Li Xian.

Fang Yuan's arrival immediately attracted Li Xian's attention.

"Lord Little Beast King, what a rare guest! What brings you to my place?" Li Xian called out, approaching within twenty steps and cupping his fists.

Facing the volatile Fang Yuan, even the unscrupulous merchant Li Xian was on edge.

"Business, naturally. Won't you invite me in?" Fang Yuan smiled with an unreadable gaze.

"A customer is always welcome. Please enter." Li Xian stepped aside, letting Fang Yuan inside.

Fang Yuan strutted past him and glanced at Li Xian.

Li Xian smiled and walked alongside Fang Yuan into his cave.

# Chapter 353: Thunder Boar, Rock Crocodile

Inside the cave.

Li Xian invited Fang Yuan to take a seat, then sat beside him.

"Please have some tea." Li Xian opened his stubby fingers, conjuring a cup of fragrant tea on his palm.

Fang Yuan glanced at the tea without accepting it, sighing, "Eastern Sea's blue sky Gu is remarkably handy. Its capacity far surpasses peers of its rank, and it is exceedingly convenient."

"Oh? I didn't expect Sir Little Beast King to recognize this Gu." Li Xian's gaze flickered.

Fang Yuan smiled and said slowly, "This rank four blue sky Gu is fused from the rank three sky granary Gu. Its low success rate makes it rare even in Eastern Sea. Advancing it to rank five blue sky well Gu requires fusing it with sky well Gu, a natural Gu found only on Eastern Sea's Tian Jing island. In Southern Border, only Yi clan might possess the required sky well Gu."

"Blue sky Gu, sky well Gu, blue sky well Gu, and Tian Jing island... how does Little Beast King know all this?" Li Xian grew startled and suspicious.

"Sir Little Beast King, please taste the tea. What transaction do you wish to make?" Li Xian asked.

"It's just a simple transaction," Fang Yuan said, retrieving several Gu from his aperture to show Li Xian.

Li Xian narrowed his eyes at the sight of these Gu.

Shrewd as ever, Li Xian immediately realized: 'Who knew Little Beast King was also accomplished in enslavement path! Killing two Gu Masters in King Quan inheritance... what demonic talent!'

Pondering quickly, Li Xian spoke excitedly, "You came to the right place, Sir Little Beast King. I have a Gu that will surely interest you."

"Oh?" Fang Yuan raised his brows.

Li Xian opened his palm, revealing a floating sphere.

The ball shone with platinum light; upon closer inspection, it was an ant cluster of over a hundred flying ants crawling over one another.

"These are rank three gold eating flying ants. Tie clan elders used iron cabinet Gu to seal Lady White Demon. Yet iron cabinet Gu easily collapses against these flying ants. I've calculated that your Gu are worth just enough to buy them," Li Xian smiled warmly.

To his surprise, Fang Yuan glanced at the gold consuming flying ants once before looking away. "Who said I want this? Here is my list—I want everything on it."

Li Xian took the list, which contained numerous Gu worms and supplementary refinement materials.

Li Xian recognized that the requested items were all for strength path cultivation.

Li Xian probed, "Sir Little Beast King, their total price vastly exceeds the Gu you are selling."

"No matter," Fang Yuan waved his hand. "I want everything listed. Give me what you currently have, and keep an eye out for the rest. Money is no problem."

Unable to probe further, Li Xian chuckled and nodded in agreement.

Inside a cave, lights flickered.

Fang Yuan sat cross-legged on the ground, his full attention on the ball of paste before him.

The multicolored paste churned in midair, emitting a strange fragrance as fusion reached its critical stage.

Fang Yuan constantly threw in primeval stones.

Then, he quickly took out a thunderclap Gu.

The paste swallowed the rank three thunderclap Gu and flickered with lightning.

"It is time... all-out effort Gu."

Boar phantom immediately appeared above him.

Activating strength qi Gu, the boar phantom became menacing.

As the light dispersed, the boar phantom in mid-air transformed dramatically.

Lightning boar phantom!

"Success. With thunder boar strength, my battle strength increases again. Next is rock crocodile strength..."

At dawn over San Cha mountain, Fang Yuan walked out of his cave.

Upgrading to thunder boar and rock crocodile strength raised his battle strength by thirty percent!

"Bai Ning Bing..." Looking southeast, he saw a dark purple qi barrier covering the mountainside.

Fang Yuan let out a cold snort.

The four Tie clan elders were all rank four middle stage, while Bai Ning Bing was initial stage.

Had Tie clan truly intended to capture Bai Ning Bing, they would have done so already.

For one, they wanted to preserve strength for the three kings inheritance.

Second, they were held back by Bai Ning Bing's purple thorn token.

Finally, it was to bait Fang Yuan!

If Fang Yuan rashly charged in, the four elders could combine strength to activate rank five search and lock Gu.

# Chapter 354: Ancient Bronze Skin Gu

"Bai Ning Bing, surrender obediently. You cannot escape!" Tie clan's four old-timers occupied the four cardinal directions, locking Bai Ning Bing in the center.

Bai Ning Bing sat cross-legged on a boulder, holding primeval stones to replenish her primeval essence.

Bai Ning Bing slowly opened her calm eyes and said indifferently: "Tie clan old-timers, why waste words? If you want to capture or kill me, bring it on! Even if I die at your hands, I'll take a few of you with me."

She paused before continuing: "Maintaining this qi barrier must have drained much of your primeval essence, right? Hehe, I know you four excel at team tactics while I am only rank four initial stage. But if you kill me, even if you survive, you will be heavily injured. And if my partner appears, what will become of you?"

Meanwhile, merchant Li Xian arrived at Fang Yuan's cave. A Gu was handed over to Fang Yuan.

People called it 'ancient bronze skin Gu'.

"Li Xian, you truly live up to your fame as a demonic merchant, obtaining ancient bronze skin Gu so quickly. Trading with you was the right choice. I have no tea, so please have some wine."

"Call me Fang Zheng. Here is fifty thousand primeval stones as a deposit for future transactions," Fang Yuan said, handing over the sum.

Fang Yuan led Li Xian into the cave's depths and pointed to a stone vat: "I lack a fire type Gu. Please help me melt these bronze pieces into this stone vat."

Li Xian sighed in relief and smiled: "I happen to have a fire type Gu. It's no trouble at all."

Soon, Li Xian melted the bronze pieces into liquid metal, filling the stone vat nearly to the brim.

Fang Yuan piled coal stones beneath the stone vat to maintain the temperature.

Then, under Li Xian's astonished gaze, he leaped into the vat.

The sizzling molten bronze instantly burned away Fang Yuan's clothes. Submerged up to his neck, only his head remained exposed.

"Lord Little Beast King, what are you doing?!" Li Xian cried, already smelling burning flesh.

Fang Yuan activated ancient bronze skin Gu in his aperture, gritting his teeth in a smile: "Li Xian, didn't you know? Soaking in bronze liquid accelerates ancient bronze skin Gu's refinement by thirty percent."

"Fang Yuan, why haven't you appeared yet?" Bai Ning Bing sat cross-legged with half-narrowed eyes, sensing that the situation was turning grim.

"With poison vow Gu, he cannot abandon me. Yet he still hasn't shown up—is he still inside the inheritance, or in trouble?"

# Chapter 355: Yi Huo’s invitation

Fang Yuan continually used essence iron bone Gu and ancient bronze skin Gu to temper his body.

Meanwhile, he used nine eyes liquor worm to purify his light gold primeval essence into bright gold, strengthening his aperture foundation.

Cultivating bitterly without wasting a second, he felt his strength increase steadily each day.

"It has been over a month since leaving King Quan Inheritance. I am almost done with ancient bronze skin Gu, but fully mastering essence iron bone Gu will take longer unless I use time path Gu to accelerate time," Fang Yuan pondered.

"Unlike ordinary Gu Masters, I have Spring Autumn Cicada. Accelerating my time would speed up its recovery, signing my death warrant!"

Time inside San Cha mountain's blessed land flowed thrice as fast as outside. The time spent in King Quan Inheritance had already aided Spring Autumn Cicada's recovery, so Fang Yuan could only cultivate patiently.

Mid-cultivation, an unfamiliar voice called from outside the cave.

"Little beast king, could you come out to have a chat?"

Fang Yuan stepped outside and looked at the visitor with slight doubt: "So it is Elder Yi Huo."

A renowned fire path Gu Master in Southern Border known as 'blazing inferno', his cultivation was rank four peak stage.

"Little beast king, I came here for a simple reason: to invite you into Shang clan." Yi Huo entered without sitting, stating his purpose directly.

"Join Shang clan?" Fang Yuan raised a brow, sensing the swift, decisive demeanor of 'blazing inferno'.

"Right." Yi Huo nodded and smiled, looking straight at Fang Yuan. "You and Bai Ning Bing stayed at Shang clan and know our strength well, so I won't belabor it. More importantly, Bai Ning Bing has been trapped by Tie clan's four old-timers for two months. If you both join Shang clan, I will immediately rescue her."

In Fang Yuan's memories, Yi Huo was sent here by Shang clan, suppressing Wu Shen Tong and Yi Chong while defeating Kong Ri Tian and Long Qing Tian to temporarily dominate San Cha mountain.

Yet this time, Yi Huo had come to recruit him, defying Fang Yuan's expectations.

"Lord Yi Huo, is this invitation from Shang clan, or your own idea?" Fang Yuan asked, eyes flashing.

"Of course it is my idea. Fang Zheng, you nearly conquered the battle stage. A genius like you will gain immense benefits joining us."

"Lord Yi Huo, don't Shang clan's external elders lack authority to recommend talents?"

The title 'external elder' made Yi Huo frown slightly: "True. But if you become battle stage ruler, you will become an external elder. With your talent, that title is only a matter of time."

He explained patiently: "Fang Zheng, before I left, Lord Clan Leader made me a promise. As long as I achieve great deeds on San Cha mountain and take control, I will be bestowed the clan surname upon returning, becoming Shang Huo instead of Yi Huo. I will become a true Shang clan elder with authority to recommend talents."

Fang Yuan shook his head: "This matter is too significant; I must consider it properly."

Yi Huo's brows relaxed slightly: "In that case, I will give you three days. I'll return then, hoping for a satisfactory reply."

"Alright." Fang Yuan nodded and saw Yi Huo out of the cave.

Three days later.

"You still haven't decided?" Yi Huo looked at Fang Yuan suspiciously.

"This is serious; I truly need to consider it carefully," Fang Yuan answered heavily.

"Fine, I'll give you three more days. Fang Zheng, I think highly of you."

Another three days later.

"Lord Yi Huo, I've considered it thoroughly. I appreciate your goodwill, but I still prefer the demonic path's freedom," Fang Yuan replied sincerely.

"What? After three days, this is your answer?" Yi Huo said furiously.

"Lord Yi Huo, you can lead a horse to water, but you cannot make it drink." Fang Yuan's profound gaze carried severity within his calm words.

Yi Huo gritted his teeth and said coldly: "Fang Zheng, think carefully. Tie clan's four old-timers are formidable opponents. Without my help, Bai Ning Bing is doomed. Furthermore, the Sky Tiger Xue San Si you killed was Century Boy's goddaughter. Century Boy has been eyeing you—he is a rank four upper stage strength path Gu Master and a thorny demonic figure."

"Oh? Is that so?" Fang Yuan hesitated. "Then... let me reconsider. Give me ten days to think it through."

"Fine." Yi Huo thought and nodded. "That is enough time to think it through clearly. I trust you'll make the sensible choice. But do not drag this out—your woman is in crisis, and anything can happen."

Poison vow Gu was already destroyed, and he had reached rank four initial stage.

"There is no need to urgently rescue Bai Ning Bing; I should focus on cultivating and advancing. I no longer need bone flesh unity Gu, while she can draw attention outside. I'll take this chance to surpass her so I can easily suppress her later. With so many onlookers, those four Tie clan foxes won't dare go for the kill."

Ten days later.

"Lord Yi Huo, thank you for your goodwill. I have considered this from every angle, but I still believe freedom is best," Fang Yuan said earnestly.

Yi Huo's expression turned ugly: "Fang Zheng, stalling again and again, are you playing with me?"

"Lord Yi Huo, you wrong me; I have always been sincere. Right now, the three kings inheritance is paramount, and little time remains before it opens again."

At the mention of the inheritance, Yi Huo suppressed his fury, cast a deep look at Fang Yuan, and left the cave.

# Chapter 356: The hairy men

Five days later.

Three light pillars shot up from San Cha mountain, piercing the sky.

The fiery red pillar was King Bao's inheritance; the dazzling yellow pillar was King Quan's; the demonically charming blue pillar was King Xin's.

"Is this three kings inheritance? Sure enough, it gives off a magnificent feeling!" As long as I accomplish great achievements here, I can truly join Shang clan and change my surname to Shang, becoming a member of Shang clan.""

"Hmph, Little Beast King doesn't know what's good for him, refusing me repeatedly. But he knows secrets of the three kings inheritance; recruiting him would greatly help me control San Cha mountain."

Yi Huo felt his anger rise: "Once I leave the inheritance, I'll teach Little Beast King a lesson and show him his limits. But for now... I must clear away some obstacles."

'Pink butterfly gentleman' Kong Ri Tian!

'He who once dyed the sky blue' Long Qing Tian!

Wu clan elder Wu Shen Tong!

Yi clan elder Yi Chong!

All four were rank four peak stage experts who took the lead whenever the inheritance opened.

"Hahaha, King Bao's inheritance is mine! Who dares contend with me?" Yi Huo threw his head back and laughed wildly, crossing his arms to glare fiercely at the four.

As a fire path Gu Master like King Bao, King Bao's inheritance was naturally his best choice.

"Hmph, I'll take you on!" Kong Ri Tian snorted as his body burst into phoenix-winged golden butterflies.

Thousands of golden butterflies with blade-sharp wings enveloped Yi Huo.

"Blazing inferno, you've enjoyed quite the limelight lately," Long Qing Tian chuckled gloomily, thrusting a palm forward.

A greenish-blue palm of light swirling with poison gas shattered the air, striking toward Yi Huo's face.

"Let me spar too." Yi Chong sneered and suddenly moved.

Blue water waves surged up out of nowhere, engulfing Yi Huo within.

"Good." Smiling arrogantly, Yi Huo spread his arms and struck his chest with both palms.

Majestic flames suddenly burst forth.

The flames easily engulfed the water wave, incinerated the poison palm, and forced the phoenix-winged golden butterflies to retreat and reform into Kong Ri Tian.

"His rank five prairie fire Gu?" Yi Chong and Long Qing Tian's expressions changed at the massive fire.

The flames dispersed, and he strode toward King Bao's red light pillar.

With Yi Huo inside King Bao's inheritance, the other three exchanged glances, losing interest in fighting.

Kong Ri Tian and Long Qing Tian entered King Xin's inheritance, while Wu Shen Tong and Yi Chong entered King Quan's.

Fang Yuan climbed up to the peak and was immediately recognized.

"He is entering King Xin Inheritance."

From the peak to the mountainside, numerous Gu Masters watched Fang Yuan enter the light pillar.

"Fang Yuan... you bastard!" Bai Ning Bing watched from inside the qi barrier.

Pale with rage, she clenched her fists and gritted her teeth.

The four old-timers of Tie clan looked at each other.

Though they had trapped Bai Ning Bing, Fang Yuan's refusal to rescue her made their defensive posture an embarrassing irony.

The old man pondered for a while and made a decision.

He said heavily: "Request reinforcements! Shang clan sent help, and Tie clan must match it. Competition has intensified—only rank four peak stage powers can vie for a seat. Once the clan sends a rank four peak stage expert, combined with our rank four middle stage strength..."

Endless gray fog filled Fang Yuan's vision, confirming he had entered King Xin Inheritance.

Unable to discern direction in the fog, Fang Yuan felt Spring Autumn Cicada stir slightly in his aperture—time here was still three times faster than outside.

"I need to hurry." Fang Yuan felt a sense of urgency.

He grabbed a paper crane Gu and activated it.

A paper crane Gu served as the key to King Xin Inheritance. Except for it and Spring Autumn Cicada, all his other Gu were suppressed by heavenly power.

Reaching a hill, he saw a blurry human figure in the fog.

"Human... come out... compete... let's compete." The figure spoke in a coarse, halting voice.

Though humanoid with two arms and legs, he was extremely muscular and covered in reddish-brown hair across his chest, limbs, face, and buttocks.

"Compete... let's compete. Lose... die!" he roared, his bloodshot eyes glaring malevolently.

Just as beasts had mutations, humans had variations. The variant human before Fang Yuan was a hairy man.

Hairy men were covered in hair, even on their eyelids. Despite extremely low intelligence, they possessed innate talent in Gu refinement.

A group of hairy men served as obstacles in King Xin Inheritance. Gu Masters had to defeat them to advance; losers were torn to shreds and eaten.

"Come." Fang Yuan stood ten steps from the hairy man, his expression tranquil.

Several Gu and materials materialized in midair, floating gently into Fang Yuan's hands.

The hairy man simultaneously received the same items.

The Gu refinement had begun.

Winner lives, loser dies!

# Chapter 357: Legend of the Hairy Men

The earliest record of hairy men was in The Legends of Ren Zu.

Legend held that Ren Zu dug out his eyes, turning them into a son and daughter: Verdant Great Sun and Desolate Ancient Moon.

Verdant Great Sun was a heavy drinker who was once trapped in Ordinary Abyss, escaping by obtaining reputation Gu.

With reputation Gu, Verdant Great Sun's fame grew, and word of his heavy drinking soon spread worldwide.

After drinking deeply of world-famous wines, Verdant Great Sun awoke in danger. In his haze, a voice called from the darkness: "Verdant Great Sun, wake up! If you don't, you will be eaten..."

He awoke bound and being carried by a group of savages.

These savages had dark blue eyes and hair covering their bodies. A cauldron hung over a burning bonfire nearby.

"We are refining eternal Gu and only lacked a human catalyst. Heaven sending us Verdant Great Sun is truly worthy of celebration!"

"Born from Ren Zu's eye, Verdant Great Sun has ample spiritual energy. The refinement will surely succeed!"

"Throw him into the cauldron quickly! We will obtain eternal Gu and live forever..."

Verdant Great Sun paled with fright, shouting and struggling with all his might.

Just then, Verdant Great Sun heard that same voice in his mind.

"It is no use. These savages are hairy men, beloved by the world and innately gifted at refining Gu."

Forgetting his predicament, Verdant Great Sun asked in his mind, "Who are you?"

The voice laughed: "I am divine travel Gu. Whoever drinks four of the world's top wines gives birth to me in their heart. I can teleport you anywhere you wish."

Overjoyed, Verdant Great Sun pleaded, "Then quickly take me away from here!"

Divine travel Gu sighed: "It is no use. You can only use me while drunk, but you are sober now."

Divine travel Gu replied: "Human, I never intended to harm you; you simply drew on my power while drunk. Do not blame me. You escaped the tiger-striped honey bees thanks to me. One harm and one rescue—we can call it even."

He was thrown into the cauldron by the hairy men.

"Add vanity Gu!" A hairy man threw a Gu into the cauldron.

This strange Gu resembled a large blue crab, but with a hollow shell.

Seeing Verdant Great Sun, the crab-like vanity Gu grew thrilled: "You are Verdant Great Sun? I've heard of you, but never expected to meet you here! What good fortune—I am so excited!"

Verdant Great Sun snapped, "Can't you see the situation we are in?"

Vanity Gu stared wide-eyed at Verdant Great Sun, then beamed: "I understand now! To be popular, one must endure the heat. Thank you for the guidance! In return, Verdant Great Sun, I will do you a favor."

Saying so, the vanity Gu exploded.

The explosion was faint, producing only a soft bang as vanity Gu transformed into a formless poisonous wind that invaded the hairy men's hearts.

The hairy men's crystal-clear blue eyes turned red.

Verdant Great Sun came to his senses and shouted, "Your Gu refinement is nothing! Even if you live forever, so what? You are so ugly with hair all over your bodies—utter abominations!"

The hairy men loudly refuted him: "Nonsense! We hairy men are perfection itself—nothing is more beautiful than hair all over the body!"

Struck by inspiration, Verdant Great Sun retorted: "No matter how beautiful your hair is, could it match mine?"

Because he had drank vajra monkey's strong wine, his hair had become blazing fire.

Verdant Great Sun continued provoking them: "Even with eternity, you will never match my beauty! Look at my hair—radiant and charismatic as fire!"

Goaded by Verdant Great Sun, a hairy man finally snapped: "You have amazing hair? I can have it too—look at mine!"

He used a torch to ignite his entire body.

Divine travel Gu praised him lavishly: "Human, you are truly clever to think of this method!"

Escaping the cauldron, Verdant Great Sun smiled coldly: "Vanity makes them foolish. People often endure pain for false beauty, forsaking true goals."

Grand white moon gold, skull stones, dragon-side tooth, young autumn grass, fifty primeval stones, a flower boar Gu, and a boar cage Gu.

This was handed to Fang Yuan as the materials for Gu refinement.

Before him, the hairy man sat cross-legged, already refining Gu.

Though not a refinement path Gu Master, Fang Yuan had studied it in his past life and knew numerous recipes.

Without a specified recipe, he simply needed to refine a better Gu than the hairy man.

Observing the hairy man's process, Fang Yuan could already guess what he was refining.

Sneering inwardly, Fang Yuan feigned a flattering smile: "Lord hairy man, your refinement methods leave me in awe. As a true grandmaster, there is surely no Gu in this world you cannot refine!"

"Ah? Haha! You... tactful," the hairy man boomed happily.

This distraction immediately caused the Gu refinement to fail.

The hairy man's face twisted in terror: "No!"

Heavenly lightning descended, striking the hairy man into ashes.

"Hehe." Laughing indifferently, Fang Yuan gathered his materials and followed the paper crane Gu toward the next round.

# Chapter 358: New Trump Card

Light dispersed and a Gu slowly landed onto Fang Yuan's palm.

This was a water cage Gu, highly effective for capturing wild Gu.

Fang Yuan stored the water cage Gu and continued forward.

This was already the thirty-second round of King Xin Inheritance.

Focused on refinement path, King Xin Inheritance grew harder every ten rounds, with the hairy men becoming smarter and stronger.

In the first twenty-odd rounds, Fang Yuan easily won by flattering the hairy men to break their concentration and ruin their refinements.

From the thirtieth round onward, however, the hairy men were massive and remarkably intelligent, forcing him to compete fairly.

Fortunately, the materials Fang Yuan had stockpiled from the first twenty rounds gave him ample refinement options.

Thanks to his past-life experience and plentiful accumulation, Fang Yuan progressed rapidly, outperforming even his record in King Quan Inheritance.

From the fortieth round onward, King Xin Inheritance's difficulty surged severalfold.

Here, Gu Masters could use one of their own Gu, provided it belonged to the refinement path.

Lacking any refinement path Gu, Fang Yuan had also nearly exhausted his materials.

Upon reaching the forty-fourth round, Fang Yuan was given the chance to exit and took it.

Back on San Cha Mountain, the four old-timers remained outside the inheritance, keeping Bai Ning Bing trapped.

Fang Yuan sought out Li Xian for another transaction.

Li Xian received many things from Fang Yuan and was inwardly startled.

"I have bad news: the four old-timers requested reinforcements, and Tie clan has already dispatched a group that may target you, sir."

Unsurprised by the old-timers' predictable move, Fang Yuan asked, "Oh? Who did they send as reinforcements?"

"Their leader is Tie Ba Xiu, a rank four upper stage strength path Gu Master. Beside him is Tie Ruo Nan. Daughter of Divine Investigator Tie Xue Leng, she has already attained rank four initial stage at such a young age."

'She reached rank four after all. Just like in my past life, Tie clan sent Tie Ba Xiu...' Fang Yuan mused.

A round pearl quietly rested on Fang Yuan's palm.

This was a yellow gold relic Gu, rank four Gu!

"Receiving a yellow gold relic Gu from King Xin Inheritance was truly lucky," Fang Yuan sighed before activating it with a thought.

The yellow gold relic Gu dissolved into a golden ray, entering Fang Yuan's aperture.

Within his aperture, the ninety-percent primeval sea surged while Spring Autumn Cicada flickered in and out at the center.

Suppressed by Spring Autumn Cicada's aura, Fang Yuan had the cicada conceal itself before activating the Gu.

As dawn broke over San Cha Mountain the next morning, Fang Yuan slowly opened his eyes.

Rank four middle stage!

With the nine eyes liquor worm, he would possess rank four upper stage gold primeval essence!

He had officially surpassed Bai Ning Bing in cultivation, while his strengthened aperture walls could better bear Spring Autumn Cicada's pressure, delaying his death sentence.

"Next, it is refining Gu..."

Using his accumulated materials, he refined a new Gu. Bone wings Gu!

A rank four Gu, it was spotless white, light as a feather, yet possessed a bony texture.

At his command, the Gu flew to his back and spread into two pairs of folded wings covering nearly his entire back.

As he poured primeval essence into the bone wings Gu, intense pain attacked him. Bones erupted from his spine and ribs, piercing flesh to converge into two massive bone wings.

Ordinary bone wings were spotless white, but Fang Yuan's were pitch black, gleaming with the metallic luster of steel and iron.

His skeleton surpassed ordinary, having been reformed by iron bone Gu and continually tempered with rank four essence iron bone.

Consequently, his bone wings far surpassed ordinary ones in sturdiness; rather than simple bone wings, they were truly iron bone wings!

The bone wings responded effortlessly, feeling like a natural part of his body.

From five hundred years of past-life experience, he had already gained plentiful flight experience.

Unleashing them by surprise was sure to catch his enemies off guard.

His advanced cultivation and bone wings Gu became Fang Yuan's new trump cards!

"Now, to deal with Bai Ning Bing." Fang Yuan retracted the bone wings, a chilling light flashing in his eyes.

# Chapter 359: Obstruction

A group of seven people silently moved through a forest.

'After rushing nonstop for days, we are finally close to San Cha Mountain,' Tie Ba Xiu thought as he bounded over rocks and tree roots.

A renowned righteous expert wielding the rank five earth overlord Gu, he was called 'Present Age Overlord' and possessed strength surpassing ordinary rank four peak stage!

Despite his impressive battle prowess, he was not the group's leader; that role belonged to the young junior beside him—Tie Ruo Nan!

Since returning to the clan, Tie Ruo Nan had become one of its eight young masters, renowned as the 'Little Divine Investigator'.

When the four old-timers requested reinforcements, the clan named Tie Ruo Nan leader, placing even senior Tie Ba Xiu under her command.

Tie Ruo Nan had not intended to participate until unexpected intel revealed hidden details regarding Qing Mao Mountain.

Her father Tie Xue Leng had fallen there—a hero of a generation lost.

Compelled to investigate anything concerning her father, she personally accepted the order and hurried to San Cha Mountain.

"Bai Ning Bing... Fang Zheng... I will uncover the truth of Qing Mao Mountain!"

"Who is it?!"

At the front, Tie Ao Kai halted and shouted, alerting the group.

Standing atop a boulder with arms crossed, a figure looked down at them.

"Fang Zheng!" Tie Ruo Nan's pupils contracted as she blurted out.

"Tie Ruo Nan, we meet again." Fang Yuan's lips curled into a smile, grim intent showing in his expression.

"Fang Zheng, I trust you've been well?" Tie Ruo Nan sighed. "Did you come to compromise? There is no need. I know the Three Kings Inheritance is a fight to the death with no retreat. Bai Ning Bing merely killed our clansman in a last-ditch move. Come to Tie clan as a guest to clarify the matter; I will plead for leniency so the clan drops it."

Fang Yuan sneered: "Sin? She only killed someone. The difference is his background. Tie Ruo Nan, I never thought you so childish. You think to lure me to Tie clan and capture me? Once there, I would be *a dragon in shallow water, a tiger on flat plains*—completely at your mercy!"

Tie Ruo Nan frowned: "Do you feel no guilt over killing? I pledge on my honor to guarantee your safety and a fair trial."

Fang Yuan scoffed: "Divine Thief Lu Zuan Feng is currently infiltrating your Demon Suppression Tower. Tie clan cannot even protect yourselves, yet you guarantee my safety? Truly laughable."

"What? The clan kept the news of Divine Thief Lu Zuan Feng confidential. How could an outsider far away on San Cha mountain know about it so clearly?"

The seven people of Tie clan turned pale.

"Do you really believe such nonsense?" Tie Ba Xiu denied it immediately to protect Tie clan's reputation.

"Leader, the surroundings are clear; he is alone," Tie Ao Kai reported softly.

Tie Ruo Nan fixed her gaze on him: "Fang Zheng, you want to use this information to blackmail us for Bai Ning Bing's life? We can do that! But we must know how you learned this secret, or we will never let her go."

Fang Yuan laughed heartily: "Tie Ruo Nan, you are truly naive. Have you never heard of the tactic *besiege an enemy stronghold in order to strike at the reinforcements*?"

"What, you want to go against us?" Tie Ruo Nan's eyes widened in disbelief.

Disdaining to reply, he sneered and swooped down at them from above like a northern goshawk.

"This aura... how could he be at rank four middle stage?!"

# Chapter 360: One vs Seven (1/4)

Fang Yuan had concealed his aura with breath concealment Gu, but as he attacked, his true cultivation burst forth.

With a dull thud, Tie Ba Xiu reacted first, meeting Fang Yuan's fists head-on in mid-air.

Tie Ba Xiu landed steadily, while Fang Yuan was repelled backward.

"Fang Zheng, you want to fight us alone? You cannot act rashly to rescue someone—it is not too late to pull back. If you continue to fight, you will truly be setting yourself against our Tie clan! If that happens, your partner's life will also be on the line."

"Hehehe." Fang Yuan chuckled, his gaze brimming with killing intent. "So what if you're Tie clan? If I fail to save Bai Ning Bing, it's just her bad luck. In any case, I did my best."

Fang Yuan sneered at Tie Ao Kai: "I am alone. This secluded path is perfect for me to slaughter you all."

"Death?" Fang Yuan started laughing loudly without any restraint.

Fang Yuan abruptly stopped laughing, stepped heavily, and charged like a ferocious tiger descending the mountain.

"Fight!" Tie Ruo Nan shouted grimly, knowing battle was inevitable.

Tie Ba Xiu shielded Tie Ruo Nan, Tie Dao Ku and Tie Xian Hua stepped forward, and Tie Ao Kai flanked.

Their formation opened up like a sack, displaying seamless tactical coordination.

Activating charging crash Gu, Fang Yuan plunged fearlessly into the Tie clan's formation.

An umbrella lotus bloomed over Tie Xian Hua, forming a solid protective shield.

Fang Yuan sneered, a white elephant phantom appeared above him.

Unsurprisingly, Fang Yuan sent the blocking Tie Xian Hua flying.

Rather than following up, Fang Yuan ignored her and charged straight toward Tie Ruo Nan.

Tie Ruo Nan knelt with hands pressed to the ground, creating puppets immediately.

The grass at her feet surged to a man's height, intertwining within emerald-green light into a grass puppet.

Rattan shell grass soldiers depleted enemy essence, and a dozen could combine into a rank four grass sword elite soldier.

In moments, eighteen rattan shell grass soldiers stood beside Tie Ruo Nan.

"Tie Ruo Nan..." Killing intent blazing, Fang Yuan locked his icy gaze onto her.

Rivaling Bai Ning Bing in talent and investigating Qing Mao Mountain, she could not be left alive.

Tie Ruo Nan stood firm, trusting her comrades while continuing to produce rattan shell grass soldiers.

"Block him!"

Tie Ao Kai flicked his sleeves, unleashing a large swarm of golden needle mosquitoes.

Tie Dao Ku charged Fang Yuan, activating hand blade Gu and swift shadow Gu.

Fang Yuan laughed and directly clashed head-on without evading it.

Mosquitoes punctured his skin, while Tie Dao Ku slashed deep wounds across his chest.

Fang Yuan laughed wildly, striding forward with head held high as blood flowed from his wounds.

With bitter strength Gu, the heavier Fang Yuan's injuries grew, the stronger his combat power became.

Sure enough, three more beast phantoms appeared above Fang Yuan.

Tie Xian Hua charged only to be swatted away, Tie Dao Ku retreated helplessly, and Tie Ao Kai could only hover at the sidelines.

"Good, tyrant strength Gu!" Just as Fang Yuan neared Tie Ruo Nan, Tie Ba Xiu shouted and stepped forward.

A thunderous blast echoed from their collision, sending both back seven or eight steps.

Though Fang Yuan commanded four beast phantoms, Tie Ba Xiu's tyrant strength Gu granted him overlord strength.

"Interesting, again!" With glowing eyes, Fang Yuan charged once more.

Tie Ba Xiu greeted him head-on, firmly blocking his way.

Swarming golden needle mosquitoes, flower rain, and hand blades rained down upon Fang Yuan from all directions.

With worsening wounds, Fang Yuan deployed golden shield Gu for defense while self-reliance Gu healed him.

Fang Yuan grew stronger as he fought, but Tie Ba Xiu unexpectedly did the same!

Tie Ba Xiu's rank five earth overlord Gu drew endless power from the ground, steadily strengthening his tyrant strength Gu.

Fang Yuan and Tie Ba Xiu clashed like humanoid beasts, trading crushing blows that shattered stones and dust.

With a sudden howl, a beast phantom manifested above Fang Yuan.

Wreathed in lightning, baring savage tusks, and radiating a ferocious aura—the thunder boar phantom!

Umbrella lotus Gu broke, scattering petals into the air.

"Xian Hua, persevere!" Tie Mu caught the blood-vomiting Tie Xian Hua and promptly began healing her.

Meanwhile, a squad of rattan shell grass soldiers closed ranks to shield them.

Nearly a thousand grass soldiers surrounded Tie Ruo Nan as she continuously produced more.

Fang Yuan was firmly entangled with Tie Ba Xiu now.

# Chapter 361: One vs Seven (2/4)

The battle carried on like wildfire.

"No wonder he blocked us with such prowess... but *two fists cannot rival four hands*. We still have seven people! Fang Zheng, you have lost." Tie Ruo Nan declared.

Nearby, several rattan shell grass soldiers merged to form rank four grass sword elite soldiers.

"Fang Zheng, you couldn't escape even if you grew wings! This is the price for overestimating yourself!" Tie Mu added from beside Tie Xian Hua.

"Impossible to escape?" Fang Yuan shoved Tie Ba Xiu back, smiling coldly. "Let's test that."

With a sharp jolt of pain, a pair of metallic, pitch-black bone wings unfurled fully from Fang Yuan's back!

Under the Tie clan's astonished gazes, Fang Yuan flapped his wings and lifted smoothly into the air.

"Fang Zheng can fly?! No wonder he arrogantly intercepted us," Tie Dao Ku realized.

Tie Ruo Nan's expression darkened; grass soldiers were useless against airborne targets, shattering her encirclement.

"What are you all panicking for!" Tie Ba Xiu's loud shout echoed in their ears.

"How can flying type Gu be easy to control? By nature, humans walk on the ground, if they want to fly and soar freely, they need a lot of painstaking practice! And to battle while flying? It needs even more bitter training! Even Verdant Great Sun, son of the great Ren Zu, died while flying. How much time could it have been since this little beast king obtained his flying type Gu?"

"Take him down! Fang Zheng, you trapped yourself in the air!" Tie Ba Xiu yelled.

"You think so...?" Fang Yuan smiled indifferently, pity in his eyes.

"Golden needle mosquito... huh?" Tie Ao Kai went stiff.

A similar situation played out on, more or less, everybody.

Tie Ba Xiu's heart sank—Little Beast King had planned this.

"No matter, I have blade qi Gu for long-distance fighting!" Tie Dao Ku stood up.

"I have snowball Gu to make Little Beast King pay," another Tie clan Gu Master added.

"Little Beast King, get down here!" Tie Dao Ku unleashed a ray of blade qi.

Three snowballs moved in to block Fang Yuan's retreating path.

Folding his left wing, Fang Yuan spun agilely, slipping safely through the gaps between the snowballs.

The Tie clan felt stunned and helpless as Fang Yuan nimbly and confidently evaded countless attacks.

"Little Beast King, we will let you retreat today, but beware next time," Tie Ruo Nan warned.

As if hearing the most ridiculous joke in the world, Fang Yuan burst into loud laughter.

"What are you laughing at?" The dread in Tie Ba Xiu's heart intensified.

"Why would I run? The real battle has only begun!" Fang Yuan activated all-out effort Gu and strength qi Gu simultaneously.

Green bull, horse, stone turtle, white elephant, and black python—five beast phantoms solidified and slammed down.

Rocks crumbled and grass soldiers were pulverized while the Tie clan retreated frantically.

"Don't let him control the field!" Tie Ba Xiu roared, striking with fist qi.
