However, Fang Yuan flapped his wings and rose higher, easily dodging the attack.

Tie Ba Xiu sighed helplessly; his fist qi was ranged, but its reach was short.

Strength path lacked range, making Fang Yuan's ancient strength qi Gu uniquely deadly.

Reminded by Tie Ba Xiu, the other Tie clansmen deflected the beast phantoms and launched aerial counterattacks.

He dodged most strikes, repelling the rest with golden shield Gu.

"Die!" Having hovered above, Fang Yuan spotted an opportunity and pounced down.

"Not good, run!" The thought barely surfaced before Fang Yuan's hands seized his shoulders.

Fang Yuan pulled apart his arms, ruthlessly tearing them off.

Blood spurted like a waterfall as both limbs were torn clean off Tie Mu's shoulders.

Fang Yuan clapped his hands together with crushing force, bursting Tie Mu's head like a watermelon!

Blood and brain matter sprayed over Fang Yuan, gore clinging to his clothes.

Fang Yuan savored the thick stench of blood, thrilled with intense excitement.

"Tie Mu!" Witnessing his death, the Tie clansmen glared with fury hot enough to burn the heavens and seas!

# Chapter 362: One vs Seven (3/4)

"You killed Tie Mu, I want you dead!" Tie Xian Hua raised umbrella lotus Gu, shouting as she pounced.

Fang Yuan waited for Tie Xian Hua to draw near before brushing past on outspread wings.

Her face went blank as blood erupted from her neck, sending her severed head flying.

After several indiscriminate divebombs, Fang Yuan flew toward Tie Ruo Nan.

"Oh no, his target is young master!" Tie Dao Ku quickly reinforced her.

Fang Yuan suddenly changed directions, landing quickly in front of Tie Dao Ku.

Expression icy, Fang Yuan traded blows with Tie Dao Ku without evading.

Fang Yuan activated golden shield Gu, but it quickly broke under the barrage.

Even with bronze skin's defense, Fang Yuan's flesh was sliced open, spraying blood.

"My primeval essence is spent!" Tie Dao Ku's attacks suddenly halted.

Fang Yuan smiled, grabbing his neck and twisted.

With a snap, Fang Yuan broke his neck, killing Tie Ruo Nan's trusted blade expert.

"No!" Tie Ruo Nan's eyes turned red with grief and fury, flames of rage nearly consuming her.

Under her command, the rattan shell grass soldiers surged into a green tidal wave to engulf Fang Yuan.

Blood seeped from Tie Ruo Nan's face under the intense strain of manipulating her puppets.

"Too young," Fang Yuan sneered, flapping his wings to ascend.

The grass soldiers collapsed into a chaotic heap, restricting Tie Ba Xiu's movement.

"This is the weakness of teamwork! Once cooperation fails, you become each other's greatest burden." Fang Yuan hovered high above, surveying the battlefield emotionlessly before shifting his gaze toward Tie Ao Kai.

As an investigative Gu Master, Tie Ao Kai had been circling the outskirts since the battle began.

Capable of escaping to seek reinforcements, he had to die first.

"I must escape to San Cha mountain and report to the four Tie clan elders!" Terrified, Tie Ao Kai fled the battlefield at full speed.

Moments later, Fang Yuan flew back carrying his severed head.

"AHHHH! Gu Yue Fang Zheng! Gu Yue Fang Zheng! You are dead, you are dead meat! You killed my Tie clansmen, you heinous being, there is no salvation for you. Don't run if you have guts, what kind of man are you to run away from me. You have no balls you coward, come fight with me, I will tear you to shreds, and grind your bones into ashes!" Tie Ba Xiu was extremely furious, giving off a thunderous scream.

Fang Yuan chuckled, not falling for the taunt: "Heinous? Heh, I've killed so many people in the past, why didn't you call me heinous back then? Because I killed a Tie clansmen, that makes me a heinous villain? Hehe, if that is so, then I love such a sin, let me kill a few more and add a few more sins to my name."

Fang Yuan spread his wings and resumed divebombing, slaughtering the remaining Tie clansmen.

Soon, only Tie Ruo Nan and Tie Ba Xiu remained on the battlefield.

Tie Ruo Nan fought frantically while Tie Ba Xiu remained formidable if Fang Yuan grew careless.

"Tie Ruo Nan has lost her reasoning, but there is a better way. Tie Ba Xiu must protect her; I can use her to eliminate him indirectly."

Deciding his course, Fang Yuan retrieved primeval stones to recover his primeval essence.

Simultaneously, he inspected his body, using self-reliance Gu to heal his critical injuries.

# Chapter 363: One vs Seven (4/4)

"Tie Ruo Nan cannot fight and I lack ranged attacks. We must retreat to San Cha mountain and join the four old-timers!"

"Moreover, Brother Xue Leng, I cannot let your child die before me!" Tie Ba Xiu decisively dragged Tie Ruo Nan into a retreat.

Fang Yuan activated strength qi Gu; solid beast phantoms crashed down to block Tie Ba Xiu.

Tie Ba Xiu struck like lightning with punches and kicks, scattering the phantoms.

Fang Yuan shifted targets, directing all the beast phantoms toward Tie Ruo Nan.

"Despicable!" Tie Ba Xiu cursed, moving to shield Tie Ruo Nan.

Had Tie Ba Xiu fled alone, Fang Yuan could not have stopped him; protecting her was his doom.

Ignoring Tie Ba Xiu's curses and provocations, Fang Yuan hovered airborne and bombarded him with beast phantoms.

"Why does he have so much primeval essence?" Despair gripped Tie Ba Xiu, unaware Fang Yuan possessed the Heavenly Essence Treasure Lotus.

Tie Ba Xiu knocked the struggling Tie Ruo Nan unconscious and fled with her under his arm.

"We are near the foot of the mountain; I just need to hold on a bit longer." Tie Ba Xiu shook, his vision blurring.

Refusing to let him escape, Fang Yuan intensified his aerial assault with relentless fury.

Near San Cha mountain, Gu Masters began appearing in the surroundings.

"There is a battle going on, it is little beast king!"

From a dark corner, Hu Mei Er yawned before the purple barrier: "Brother Li Xian, is your information reliable? Why hasn't Little Beast King appeared?"

Inside the iron cabinet Gu's light barrier, Bai Ning Bing remained trapped by the four Tie clan old-timers.

As the two whispered, a clamor erupted.

"It's Tie Ba Xiu! Tie clan's 'present age overlord' is being hunted—Little Beast King intercepted the reinforcements!" Li Xian stared slack-jawed.

The four old-timers recalled iron cabinet Gu and rushed to rescue him, abandoning the trapped Bai Ning Bing.

Tie Ba Xiu held an unrevealed trump card, but Fang Yuan stayed cautious, bombarding him strictly from above.

"Hahaha, you were just one step away, Tie Ba Xiu—you failed!" Fang Yuan laughed as a rock crocodile phantom crashed down explosively, sending Tie Ba Xiu rolling.

"It's over." With a cold expression, Fang Yuan pointed and delivered the fatal blow.

"No, not yet. Iron cabinet Gu!" With his remaining energy, Tie Ba Xiu pressed his palm to Tie Ruo Nan's back.

A square iron cabinet rapidly formed, sealing Tie Ruo Nan tightly within.

Beast phantoms tore Tie Ba Xiu to pieces, but the iron cabinet held firm, leaving Tie Ruo Nan unscathed inside.

"Ba Xiu!" the four Tie clan old-timers howled in grief and fury, arriving a step too late.

"Little beast king, Tie clan will not rest until you are dead!" The four old-timers glared venomously at Fang Yuan.

"Is that so? I killed six of you, including Tie Ba Xiu. You four want to fight? Then fly up." Fang Yuan sneered.

# Chapter 364: Soaring Reputation!

The four Tie clan old-timers unleashed their auras and attacked, but Fang Yuan lightly flapped his wings and evaded, his agility drawing gasps from onlookers.

"Old-timers, I haven't settled the score for trapping me yet." Bai Ning Bing strode over with a grim expression, casting a complex glance at Fang Yuan.

Wary of Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing, the four old-timers tightly guarded Tie Ruo Nan's iron cabinet at their center.

Knowing he could not have killed Tie Ba Xiu without the burden of Tie Ruo Nan, Fang Yuan felt his battle intent fade.

After intense combat, his aperture's primeval essence was depleted, whereas the four Tie clan old-timers were in peak condition.

Fang Yuan called out: "My partner killed one clansman and you trapped her; I killed six, so how long will you trap me?"

"Little beast king, don't push it! You slaughtered our clansmen, killed Elder Ba Xiu, and hunted our young master—you are Tie clan's sworn enemy! I swear on my life, I will capture you, throw you into Demon Suppression Tower, and make you suffer endless torment!" the leader roared.

"Then Tie clan is my sworn enemy too! I will slaughter every Tie clansman I see!" Fang Yuan laughed, taking off.

Bai Ning Bing also left.

The battle's outcome swept across San Cha mountain with lightning speed.

Soon after, news of Fang Yuan fighting seven Tie clansmen alone and killing Tie Ba Xiu spread throughout Southern Border.

"Fang Zheng fought seven and killed six Tie clansmen, including Tie Ba Xiu?!" Emerging from the inheritance, Elder Yi Huo, one of Shang clan's five great experts, received this incredible news.

"Fang Yuan's strength surged with flight, while Tie Ba Xiu lacked long-range capability and was burdened by Tie Ruo Nan, leading to his death. In pure prowess, Tie Ba Xiu surpassed Little Beast King. What a pity I can no longer recruit him!" Yi Huo lamented.

"Little Beast King must know secrets of the three kings inheritance! How else could he master such profound flying techniques so suddenly?" Li Xian sat with narrowed eyes, sharp light gleaming within.

To Li Xian, the conclusion was clear: the blessed land's accelerated time flow was where Fang Yuan had secretly mastered flight.

"The question is, how do we profit? Little Beast King is too strong to oppose directly," Li Xian muttered, mind racing.

"We can't, but others can. People can be manipulated, hehehe," Hu Mei Er laughed coquettishly.

"You mean leak the information?" Li Xian pondered briefly, then slapped his thigh. "Brilliant plan, absolutely brilliant!"

# Chapter 365: Hu Immortal Blessed Land opens

Fang and Bai sat cross-legged, Fang Yuan's palms pressed against Bai Ning Bing's back to transfer primeval essence.

Standing up, she spoke coldly, gloating over his misfortune: "Have you heard the rumors? People claim you hold the great secret of the three kings inheritance. They say you mastered flight through the inheritance. Hehe, watch yourself. Tie clan is furious, publicly naming you their enemy and spreading arrest warrants across Southern Border."

Remaining seated, Fang Yuan replied indifferently: "Hehe, who doesn't know our relationship? If Tie clan comes for me, you're on their hit list too."

"Hmph. Cultivate until midnight while I stand guard. We'll switch after." With that, Bai Ning Bing walked out.

In the depths of the cave, Fang Yuan remained alone.

"By now, Divine Thief Lu Zuan Feng must have raided Demon Suppression Tower repeatedly and caused massive havoc, putting Tie clan under immense pressure. Rallying their strength to deal with me will take months. I can ignore them for now."

"The rumors must be Li Xian and Hu Mei Er's doing. Li Xian still has utility, and a rank six Gu Immortal backs Hu Mei Er, so I must plan carefully."

"The three kings inheritance is too vast to claim entirely; I can only take the finest portions. If I secure its core treasures, like the undefeated hundred battles Gu, this trip will be a success. My strength will surge enormously, providing vital support for the upcoming battle of Yi Tian mountain."

Thoughts whirled in Fang Yuan's mind before he dispersed them and thought of Central Continent.

"By my calculation, the Hu Immortal blessed land on Tian Ti mountain must have opened. Feng Jin Huang became a world-shaking Gu Immortal precisely because of it..."

Meanwhile in Central Continent, at the foot of Tian Ti mountain.

Elite disciples from Central Continent's ten great sects gathered together.

Among the crowd, the real Gu Yue Fang Zheng exhaled slowly; the months-long competition was finally over.

Fang Zheng's ironbeak flying crane group and Lord Sky Crane's guidance from the spirit incubating flea enabled him to push through and earn a high ranking.

Golden light blossomed as a vermillion gatehouse slowly rose above the mountain.

Stairs extended from the gatehouse into a rainbow bridge, landing right before the elite disciples.

"Enter according to your competition ranking," an ethereal voice resonated clearly in the disciples' ears.

Crowned with a phoenix coronet, graceful and dignified, her beauty was unrivaled.

She was the first-place winner of the competition—Feng Jin Huang!

After two dozen disciples entered, it was Gu Yue Fang Zheng's turn.

Fang Zheng crossed the rainbow bridge into the gatehouse, dizziness striking as swirling colors enveloped him.

"Heeheehee..." A cute and young girl suddenly appeared in his sight.

"Are you another fated person seeking Hu Immortal Inheritance? Soon, you will see a mountain. Only the first to scale its peak can obtain me. You'd better hurry—those ahead of you have a huge lead. Heeheehee..." Clad in rainbow clothes, the young girl swished a snow-white fox tail playfully behind her, innocent curiosity shining in her large, round eyes.

"Who are you?" Startled and confused, Fang Zheng could not fathom why a little girl would appear in this Gu Immortal inheritance.

The girl vanished, and Fang Zheng plunged downward.

He tried activating his Gu worms, but a formless force sealed his aperture completely.

# Chapter 366: Land Spirit

Fang Zheng fell flat on his face on the ground.

Far away, there was a tall and precipitous crystal mountain.

Far ahead, tiny human silhouettes were already scaling the towering crystal mountain.

Lord Sky Crane's anxious voice echoed from within: "Foolish disciple, what are you waiting for? Climb Dang Hun mountain at once!"

"Master!" Fang Zheng was pleasantly surprised. "Who was that girl? And why can't I use any Gu?"

"Get moving and seize every second! Listen carefully while I explain!" Lord Sky Crane urged—

"The Hu Immortal Inheritance is an intact Gu Immortal legacy. Only one inheritor can claim it, while everyone else leaves empty-handed. That girl is the inheritance itself. Reach the peak first, and you will become her master and claim the Gu Immortal inheritance."

"Master, I'm so far from the mountain while they're already climbing. Without movement Gu, how can I possibly win?"

Lord Sky Crane snorted: "This is the Hu Immortal blessed land! Her enduring will seals all Gu. The others cannot use Gu either. You must rely purely on physical strength to reach the peak and complete the trial."

Fang Zheng asked dejectedly: "We entered moments apart—why is their lead so massive?"

Lord Sky Crane replied: "Time flows differently here—one day outside equals five days within. That delay was magnified, making the competition ranking a crucial head start."

"Master, if Gu worms are sealed here, how can you still speak to me? You're truly incredible!"

Lord Sky Crane laughed: "My remnant soul can speak only through our Supreme Elder, Lord He Feng Yang. I can speak to you only through our Supreme Elder, Lord He Feng Yang! Remember, Fang Zheng: only Gu Immortals can contend with Gu Immortals."

"This blessed land possesses a land spirit—its living will. If an Immortal attacks by force, the land spirit self-destructs, destroying everything. Thus, they sent you elite disciples."

"In this competition, Spirit Affinity House's Feng Jin Huang was truly peerless—a genius among geniuses, a phoenix among mortals looking down on all. With your limited strength, losing to her was only natural. Yet Immortal Crane Sect has not lost. You are our hope to turn the tables!"

"Master, isn't the sect expecting too much of me? Without Gu, how can I possibly win?"

Lord Sky Crane continued: "Fang Zheng, this crystal peak is Dang Hun mountain from the legends of Ren Zu!"

"Dang Hun mountain!" Fang Zheng gave a startled cry.

"Entering Dang Hun mountain's range inflicts soul shocks that intensify toward the peak, scattering weak souls."

"Naturally, this is a righteous path inheritance, unlike dangerous demonic ones. The land spirit has suppressed Dang Hun mountain's might, giving you elite disciples a chance to reach the peak. Anyone unable to continue is expelled by the land spirit, meaning your lives aren't at risk."

"Surviving your clan's destruction tempered your soul beyond your peers, giving you a firm will. But a gap remains compared to Feng Jin Huang and Xiao Qi Xing."

"That is where Lord He Feng Yang's plan shines. I will use my soul to support you all the way to the peak!"

Fang Zheng felt torn: "With Master's help, I have a chance to win. But isn't cheating unfair? What if other Gu Immortals find out?"

Lord Sky Crane snorted: "Foolish boy, this isn't cheating—it's ability. Claiming a Gu Immortal inheritance through our own capability is entirely fair! Entering triggers the land spirit's backlash, so even if they notice anything, they can only watch helplessly! Hahaha!"

"Hahaha!" Lord Sky Crane laughed. "You're not so foolish after all. Right, that girl is the Hu Immortal land spirit. Now charge forward boldly. Dang Hun mountain is tough to climb; it will take at least a year. To be safe, I'll stay quiet from now on and only assist in secret. With my help, you have an eighty percent chance to seize the Hu Immortal inheritance! Once you obtain the Hu Immortal inheritance, you'll rise from beggar to tycoon in a single leap, ascending to the heavens in one step!"

"Understood, Master! I will do my best and not disappoint you!" Fang Zheng's eyes shone with fierce resolve.

# Chapter 367: It is not easy to be a godfather these days

Inside a brightly lit cave on San Cha mountain, demonic Gu Masters feasted.

"Come, everyone, eat and drink your fill, don't hold back!" Century Boy boomed from the head seat, greeting the room.

Clad in black robes, Century Boy looked like a young child despite being over one hundred and eighty years old.

Demonic Gu Masters he guided took him as their godfather. Over time, this flock of godchildren grew into a formidable force.

His godchildren packed the banquet alongside distinguished demonic figures like Rock Lizard Li Qiang and Fiery Star Bao Tong.

"Refinement grandmaster Feng Tian Yu arrived and agreed to refine Gu for Yi Huo. Whatever the Gu, Yi Huo's combat strength will surely soar."

"A few days ago, Little Beast King killed Yu Cang—his fourth strength path kill this month," someone whispered.

Century Boy's ears twitched, his expression souring at the words "little beast king".

Fang Yuan had killed his goddaughter Xue San Si, but after Fang Yuan slew Tie Ba Xiu, Century Boy quietly dropped the matter.

"Hmph! Little Beast King's strength stems from the three kings inheritance. Knowing its secrets, he gains far more than us on every run. Li Xian, you've dealt with him and know his situation. Isn't that right?" Rock Lizard Li Qiang called out.

Li Xian nodded: "Indeed. Little Beast King has repeatedly sold accurate inheritance secrets. For example, disrupting the hairy men's Gu refinement in King Xin inheritance's early stages, along with insights on King Quan inheritance."

"Little Beast King definitely conceals deeper secrets. His luck has struck, and his strength continues to soar thanks to the three kings inheritance. Now he's a top contender to claim the three kings inheritance!" Fiery Star Bao Tong said over his wine, brimming with envy.

"Ever since arriving, he has challenged strength path Gu Masters, claiming he will restore the ancient strength path's glory."

"Century Boy, aren't you a strength path Gu Master too? That brute Fang Zheng is utterly tyrannical," Li Qiang warned.

Century Boy put on a tough front: "Hmph! Little Beast King's rampant arrogance will soon bring his doom. He killed my goddaughter Xue San Si, and I'll avenge her eventually. But the inheritance comes first; I'll settle scores once it ends."

"I heard Tie clansmen have secretly arrived at San Cha mountain..."

Just then, a voice echoed from outside the cave.

"Century Boy, I am Fang Zheng, get out!"

The raucous banquet instantly fell dead silent.

"That's Little Beast King's voice—he really came," Li Xian remarked, stifling a smile.

*Crash!* Century Boy slammed his wine cup down, gritting his teeth as he shouted outside: "Little Beast King, I'm here! What do you want?"

Fang Yuan shouted back: "Century Boy, you host a grand banquet yet snub me? Since you cultivate the strength path too, come out and exchange moves with me!"

Unwilling to fight to the death, Century Boy looked around and shouted furiously: "Little Beast King, don't be so insolent! Picking a fight here shows complete disregard for all the heroes gathered inside! Lord Rock Lizard Li Qiang, Lord Fiery Star Bao Tong, and demonic prodigy Li Xian are all here. Are you courting death?"

The guests shifted uncomfortably, inwardly cursing Century Boy for trying to drag them down with him.

Fang Yuan retorted: "Century Boy, you coward! I'm here to find trouble specifically with you! I will restore the ancient strength path's glory. A puny coward like you only shames it—come out and accept your death!"

# Chapter 368: Tearing the boy apart

Century Boy fumed, thinking: "Little Beast King only killed Tie Ba Xiu thanks to flight. As long as I focus on defense, I'll be in no mortal danger."

"Little Beast King, you arrogant brat! Today I'll teach you that age brings wisdom!" Century Boy marched out, shouting furiously.

"Cut the crap, take this!" Fang Yuan sneered as Century Boy emerged, lunging forward like a descending tiger with a blast of wind.

All-out effort Gu!

Beast phantoms appeared instantly, causing Fang Yuan's strength to surge.

With a resonant bellow, a large green bull phantom materialized above his head.

This was the mutated kunlun bull phantom, possessing immense strength!

Unable to guard in time, Century Boy was launched like a ball, smashing through over ten trees before stopping.

Strength Qi Gu!

Fang Yuan's shoulder twitched, shooting out a trace of strength qi.

The thunder boar phantom entered the strength qi and solidified, charging Century Boy.

He could now seamlessly coordinate strength qi Gu and beast phantoms even at close range.

Solid beast phantoms posed the greatest threat to Century Boy, though destroying one merely reverted it to strength qi.

Thoroughly suppressed and unable to catch his breath, Century Boy resolved to retreat.

He turned his body, towards the cave.

"Afraid, Century Boy?" Fang Yuan activated charging crash Gu, pursuing relentlessly.

"With me here, where can you go?" Bai Ning Bing leaped in, blocking Century Boy's path.

Century Boy never expected Bai Ning Bing's ambush and was knocked off balance.

Seizing the opening, Fang Yuan unleashed a flurry of attacks.

His luck peaked as the thunder boar, rock crocodile, and kunlun bull phantoms all manifested at once.

Before Century Boy could even scream, Fang Yuan seized both his legs and tore him in half.

Blood pouring down his face, Fang Yuan glared around: "Century Boy fled like a rat. He deserved death!"

He laughed: "Come, let's drink. Li Xian, still want three kings inheritance intel? We have business."

Fang Yuan entered the banquet and took the host's seat that once belonged to Century Boy.

"Sit down, everyone. Whoever dares leave is disrespecting me, Little Beast King!" Fang Yuan threatened, glaring around.

Rank four upper stage cultivators like Li Qiang and Bao Tong swallowed their rage and sat down in silence.

Fang Yuan smiled thinly: "Since everyone shows respect, I'll grant you some benefits."

He then casually shared details regarding the life-saving token in the three kings inheritance.

# Chapter 369: Understanding of the Demonic Path

"To think there was such an escape in the three kings inheritance. With that token, I could move freely without retreating early," Bao Tong lamented.

Li Qiang toasted Fang Yuan: "Lord Little Beast King's words are worth a thousand gold. I toast your victory!"

Leaving his cup untouched, Fang Yuan waved at Century Boy's godchildren: "I eliminated the root evil today, so I'll spare you. If you don't want to stay, get lost. Scram, don't block my view!"

An uproar ensued as many awkwardly fled, instantly emptying the cave by half.

However, among the godchildren of Century Boy, some still remained.

"Lord Fang Zheng, my savior!" A godson knelt weeping. "Century Boy forced me to accept him as father—you are my savior!"

"Hahaha..." Fang Yuan laughed. "Pleasant to the ears. Good, good."

His smile vanished: "Bootlickers! Murder is murder, crime is crime—I scorn such hypocritical praise. I kill because I want to. Screw off! If you want revenge, build your strength; I'll be waiting!"

"Hmm?" Fang Yuan snorted, sending a beast phantom charging forward to slay one on the spot.

Still holding his raised cup, Li Qiang masked his embarrassment with a strained smile: "Lord Little Beast King, when cutting grass, one must pull the roots. I will kill them in your stead to thank you for the information."

After a pause, Fang Yuan said: "Since treading the demonic path, I have never feared offending others. As long as I keep growing stronger, what is revenge? If ten people seek revenge, I'll kill ten; if a hundred seek revenge, I'll kill a hundred. If the whole world seeks revenge, I will exterminate the whole world! If someone succeeds in taking revenge on me, it only means I wasn't strong enough, didn't try hard enough, and neglected my cultivation—I would deserve to die!"

Having intimidated everyone, Fang Yuan did not push further, smiling: "Let's drink."

He then resumed discussing the three kings inheritance, revealing numerous secrets.

Only Li Xian was left bewildered: "What is Little Beast King plotting? Why reveal such precious information? What is he after?"

"Young Master Ruo Nan, the dead cannot return. My condolences," Tie Xuan Zhi consoled her.

Tie Ruo Nan stared at the tombstones before her in a daze.

Tie Mu, Tie Dao Ku, Tie Xian Hua, Tie Ao Kai, Tie Ba Xiu...

"I failed you all—I didn't fulfill my duty as a leader!"

Just then, an aged voice spoke from behind: "Months have passed—is child Ruo Nan still like this?"

Standing before him was Tie clan's previous generation clan leader, Tie Mu Bai!

Standing before the tombstones with his back to Tie Ruo Nan, he caressed the stele and sighed: "Tie clansmen are buried where they die—a rule since our founding. Do you know why?"

"Because to Tie clansmen, dying on the battlefield is the greatest honor—as it was for your father Tie Xue Leng. When I die, it will be the same. And when you die, it will still be the same."

# Chapter 370: The Glory of Tie clan

"Since our founding, Tie clan has upheld justice, with countless clansmen sacrificing their lives. The youths who died here won't be the first or the last. Do you understand?" the old man continued.

"Tie Ruo Nan, the criminal Little Beast King Fang Zheng is still out there harming people."

Tie Mu Bai paused, turning to look at Tie Ruo Nan before asking calmly: "Do you want to uphold justice?"

A spark flickered in Tie Ruo Nan's lifeless eyes as she forced out a hoarse word: "Yes."

"Good." The old man nodded. "Starting now, I will impart Tie clan's metal path to you."

Eight days later...

The three kings inheritance opened once more; red, yellow, and blue light pillars pierced the clouds, visible from thousands of miles away.

Righteous and demonic cultivators looked toward the peak—there stood the grizzled old man, Tie Mu Bai.

Not far away, Yi Huo, Kong Ri Tian, Long Qing Tian, Yi Chong, and Wu Shen Tong wore grim expressions.

Moments ago, this old man had effortlessly suppressed and defeated the combined strength of all five.

With Tie Mu Bai's appearance, the power structure on San Cha mountain completely changed.

"From this day onwards, no demonic Gu Master may enter three kings inheritance," Tie Mu Bai announced.

"Heaven's laws are vast, and justice will prevail. Though a demonic inheritance, the three kings inheritance shall serve our righteous path. As long as we unite, our sparks will gather into a bright light covering all of San Cha mountain, leaving no darkness behind."

Tie Mu Bai stepped into the inheritance.

This opening of the three kings inheritance lasted over half a month.

After it closed, Tie Mu Bai hosted a banquet for all righteous Gu Masters.

"Among the great clans, Shang clan has always produced abundant talent. Has Shang Yan Fei become clan leader now? He was outstanding from youth, and you are not bad either. Take a seat," Tie Mu Bai recalled.

Wu Shen Tong said darkly: "Old Clan Leader Tie, your presence drove away the demonic factions, but unfortunately Fang Zheng fled days before your arrival."

"You mean Little Beast King?" Tie Mu Bai smiled placidly, without a hint of anger.

"That youth is remarkable, with impressive accomplishments. Rising alone from nothing, he swiftly became a prominent star of the demonic path. Though seemingly rash and unreasonable, he plans meticulously and schemes well. It was no fluke that several Tie clan youths died by his hands," Tie Mu Bai continued.

"Do you find it strange?" Tie Mu Bai glanced around, smiling faintly. "Why would the glory of Tie clan openly praise our mortal enemy?"

"Hehehe, praising an enemy may seem to boost their prestige and dampen our morale, but how could resolute wills be shaken by mere words? By acknowledging your enemies, you recognize their strengths, guard against your weaknesses, and take them seriously. Never let hatred blind your wisdom."

Tie Mu Bai suddenly asked, "Ruo Nan, what do you think of Fang Zheng?"

"Yes." Tie Ruo Nan stepped forward and reported, "Though I despise him, I must admit Fang Zheng is exceptional. He dares to take risks, yet acts only after careful scheming. Though a strength path Gu Master, he clearly possesses an extraordinary investigative method, as proven by his ability to intercept us."

"Elder Tie Ba Xiu and I took a secluded route that fooled everyone, yet Fang Zheng detected it and intercepted us precisely. Little Beast King may stay in the limelight, but he conceals himself deeply and must never be underestimated."

"Good analysis." Tie Mu Bai nodded with approving eyes.

"Ruo Nan, Fang Zheng is your mission. Capture or kill him, wash away your humiliation, and claim your own glory. "Our glory belongs to all generations who uphold righteousness.

"Likewise, every one of us must uphold the righteous path's glory. Come, drink this cup to shine righteousness onto the world, letting this radiance dispel the darkness and eradicate the demonic path!"

# Chapter 371: Just let him fly a bit higher

The Legends of Ren Zu Chapter Two, Section Three —

A vat of delicious wine sat before Verdant Great Sun, but he frowned, unwilling to drink.

"Divine travel Gu, you have harmed me deeply. Now I dare not drink, fearing you will transport me into peril while drunk," Verdant Great Sun sighed.

Ashamed, divine travel Gu replied, "Fly into azure heaven, find its bamboo forest, and pluck a jade bamboo the color of the sky. Next, go to blue heaven at night and gather eight-sided diamonds from the star fragments. At dawn, fly upward and use the rising sun's light of glory to turn me into fixed immortal travel Gu. Once I become that Gu, I will no longer send you to random places when you are drunk."

Yet reflection dampened his hopes: "Oh Gu, bound to the earth since birth, I am neither light as clouds nor winged like birds. How can I fly into azure heaven to pluck jade bamboo? How can I collect eight-sided diamonds from star fragments, let alone fly toward the rising sun?"

Divine travel Gu said, "Humans cannot fly, but we can ask cognition Gu, the mother of wisdom Gu."

Verdant Great Sun quickly found cognition Gu and asked how to fly.

Cognition Gu answered, "Cognition is the wings of freedom. But everyone's thoughts differ, so your wings depend on yourself."

With that, cognition Gu became a warm light that shone upon Verdant Great Sun.

Within the light, pure white feathered wings appeared on Verdant Great Sun's back.

Cognition Gu warned, "This pair of wings is called 'self." They grant great freedom, but beware: do not bask excessively in sunlight, or your 'self' will expand or shrink.""

"Remember: the higher you fly, the harder you fall," Cognition Gu said profoundly.

Overjoyed with his cognition wings of 'self,' Verdant Great Sun flew into the sky.

Verdant Great Sun flew higher and higher, finally reaching the limits of azure heaven.

In blue heaven, he searched the stardust until he found an eight-sided diamond.

At dawn the next day, Verdant Great Sun flew toward the rising sun.

No ordinary sunlight, this light of glory shone on all living beings and penetrated the river of time.

Holding the jade bamboo left and the eight-sided diamond right, Verdant Great Sun flew toward the sun, summoning divine travel Gu.

Basking in glory's light, divine travel Gu swallowed the star fragment and entered the jade bamboo.

"Verdant Great Sun, keep flying toward the sun so I can metamorphose into fixed immortal travel Gu. Watch your wings of 'self." Once my transformation finishes, fly into the clouds immediately!" divine travel Gu urged."

Soon, the cocoon burst open, and a green-glowing butterfly emerged: "I succeeded! I am now fixed immortal travel Gu!"

Dancing happily, fixed immortal travel Gu suddenly shouted: "Verdant Great Sun, look at your wings!"

Under the sunlight, Verdant Great Sun's wings had grown to three times their original size.

"The larger my wings, the stronger they are, and the higher I can fly," Verdant Great Sun laughed.

"How could I fall?" Verdant Great Sun boasted, just as his wings expanded to their limits and exploded.

Losing his wings, Verdant Great Sun plummeted toward the ground.

Southern border, Huo Tan mountain.

Fang Yuan emptied his wine cup under the sunlight, sighing comfortably: "Such soothing wine."

Beside him, Bai Ning Bing sat without touching the wine, gazing toward San Cha mountain instead.

"Still drinking? Months have passed, the three kings inheritance opened twice, and Tie Mu Bai expelled all demonic Gu Masters from San Cha mountain. Are we just going to keep lurking on Huo Tan mountain and watching?" Bai Ning Bing grumbled.

Fang Yuan replied calmly, "No rush. Tie Mu Bai is a rank five peak stage expert and the former Tie clan leader. Let him fly higher and higher."

Raising his wine cup toward San Cha mountain, he smiled faintly and muttered, "Come, Senior Tie Mu Bai, this junior offers you a toast."

# Chapter 372: Land spirit — Afterlife of the Gu Immortal

Central continent, Hu Immortal blessed land.

Named Dang Hun mountain, it was entirely pink, radiating dreamy and illusory colors.

Elite disciples from the ten sects scrambled like ants to climb the mountain.

Near the mountain waist, Fang Zheng gripped the cliff face, pale and gasping with sweat.

Dang Hun mountain inflicted agonizing soul shocks. The higher Fang Zheng climbed, the dizzier he grew, his soul teetering on collapse.

"Can't hold on? Then give up. Look how far you've fallen behind—you have no hope," the Hu Immortal land spirit taunted, appearing before Fang Zheng.

The land spirit resembled a little girl with snow-white skin, innocent black eyes, and a fluffy white fox tail behind her.

After she left, the spirit incubating flea in Fang Zheng's aperture vibrated as Lord Sky Crane spoke.

"Fang Zheng, hang in there! You must pass the mountain waist to give us a chance at victory. Endure! You still have soul potential to tap."

Fang Zheng asked, "Master, what is a land spirit? If I caught her, could I have skipped the climb?"

Lord Sky Crane scolded: "You are ignorant! A land spirit is formed when a dying Gu Immortal's will and soul fuse with the blessed land's power."

"She was formed from Gu Immortal Bai Hu, retaining only her lingering obsession without memories. Do not think her powerless—within this blessed land, she commands heavenly power and can combat a Gu Immortal! She can seal any rank one to five Gu worm at will; only rank six Immortal Gu move freely. You are too reckless. Climb properly and abandon such unrealistic thoughts," Lord Sky Crane chided.

Lord Sky Crane added, "If you become owner of the blessed land, you gain her loyalty. Within this land, the land spirit equals a Gu Immortal!"

Lord Sky Crane continued, "Yet the land spirit cannot leave. The true essence of the Hu Immortal inheritance is this vast, rich blessed land. Fang Zheng, you are still too low-level and have much to learn. A blessed land without a spirit would be far inferior."

Fang Zheng asked curiously, "Master, what happens to a spiritless blessed land?"

Lord Sky Crane answered, "A spiritless blessed land is doomed to destruction. Like a slumbering dragon whose flesh and blood are devoured by all, it will never awaken before dying. Fang Zheng, once you become the lord of the blessed land, you must offer its resources to Immortal Crane Sect. The sect nurtured you this far, and you must repay it. When the sect grows stronger, it protects you better. You understand this, right?"

"I understand. Without the sect, I would have no chance at this inheritance or revenge against my brother. I will repay the sect and revive you, Master!" Fang Zheng nodded, brimming with gratitude.

As the demonic cultivators shouted in discussion, the wailing of a hundred ghosts echoed as dark clouds abruptly eclipsed the blue sky.

From the dark clouds, a shrill voice sneered: "Gagaga, Tie Mu Bai, you emerged from seclusion without greeting your old friend, hmm?"

"Wu Gui, hiding under a mountain all these years? Have you come to taste defeat again?" Golden light burst from the mountain peak.

"Winning by one move a decade ago doesn't make you superior! Today you die!" The dark clouds formed a giant hand grabbing toward Tie Mu Bai.

"Old tricks." Snorting, Tie Mu Bai became a streak of golden light, tearing through the dark clouds into the sky.

"Wu Gui, when I began my Gu journey, you were an established expert I had to avoid. Fifty years later, I could escape you. Eighty years later, you were helpless against me. A century later, you lost. Today, a decade further, you will lose your life."

"Tie Mu Bai, you only boast because you rely on Tie clan! With your resources, I would already be a Gu Immortal!" Wu Gui shrieked.

"But, did you think I have no backup?" Hahaha!" Wu Gui breathed roughly, but started laughing wildly."

Amid his laughter, a third rank five peak stage aura erupted.

"Tie Mu Bai, did you miss me over the years?" a gloomy, shrill voice echoed widely.

Tie Mu Bai could not hide his shock: "Ku Mo, you actually came!"

# Chapter 373: Intense Battle of Rank Fives

Despite being a rank five peak stage expert, Wu Gui could not defeat Tie Mu Bai alone.

When fellow rank five peak stage Ku Mo appeared, the battle shifted to two demonic experts pincering Tie Mu Bai.

Fighting one against two exceeded Tie Mu Bai's power, yet the two demonic Gu Masters remained wary of each other.

Golden light flared as a four-clawed golden dragon tore through dark clouds, stirring wind and rain with a far-reaching roar.

"Hmph, just a rank four golden dragon Gu—are you here to make a fool of yourself?" Ku Mo snarled disdainfully. "Watch me tear it to pieces!"

Enveloped by a whirlwind, the four-clawed golden dragon's severe wounds instantly healed, and it roared, soaring upward once more.

"The rank four golden breeze Gu's healing rivals rank five Gu!" Watching on, Li Xian narrowed his eyes as he recognized the golden whirlwind.

Following an unearthly cry, a shining jet-black halo slammed into the four-clawed golden dragon.

The four-clawed golden dragon could not resist it and exploded.

Tie Mu Bai wore the exemplary defensive golden chainmail of the rank four golden coat Gu, his body coated in gold-red light from the rank four golden aurora Gu that granted flight.

Wu Gui rode a dark cloud, his mouth sharply protruding like a black beak.

Ku Mo slowly flapped a pair of bone wings, his body covered in bone armor with colorful sharp spikes jutting from his elbows, knees, and shoulders, resembling a malevolent multicolored humanoid hedgehog.

Terrified, everyone hurried off San Cha mountain one after another.

Only a few rank four peak stage masters dared remain on the mountain.

The three rank five experts unleashed their rank five Gu.

Tie Mu Bai's turn gold Gu turned everything it struck into lifeless golden sculptures.

Ku Mo's soft bones Gu melted bones into paste across miles.

Wu Gui's pitch black Gu spread darkness, restricting any Gu below rank six.

"This is the power of a rank five Gu? Terrifying—rank four defensive Gu can barely resist it, let alone rank three..." From a distant corner, Bai Ning Bing watched the San Cha mountain battle intently, her blue eyes grave.

"This is the show you were waiting for?" Bai Ning Bing looked back at Fang Yuan with realization. "Tie Mu Bai is fighting one against two. This will alter San Cha mountain's balance, the demonic path suppressing the righteous. Is this why you waited for months?"

Gazing into the distance, Fang Yuan shook his head indifferently: "Rank five Gu are rare; many rank five Gu Masters have none and must use rank four Gu. A rank five Gu Master's strength is determined by the rank five Gu they possess and their quantity."

"Ku Mo and Wu Gui have one or two rank five Gu, but Tie Mu Bai is backed by Tie clan and has at least three."

Bai Ning Bing's expression shifted: "You mean Tie Mu Bai will win this battle?"

Fang Yuan replied: "Gu worms are trump cards; once exposed, they can be countered. Tie Mu Bai won't reveal his full hand. This battle has no winner or loser."

"They chose San Cha mountain for their battle, making their objective clear—the three kings inheritance!"

"The inheritance contains rank five Gu to increase their strength. With such treasure ahead, how could they battle recklessly? Unless there was a massive disparity in strength, which clearly isn't the case here, this battle can only end in a draw."

Facing such an opponent, Bai Ning Bing felt deeply weighed down: "Such unfathomable shrewdness... how can I take the yang Gu from him to become male again?"

# Chapter 374: Calamity and Tribulation of the Blessed Land

Nearly a thousand screeching, ash-grey mountain apes tightly surrounded Tie Ruo Nan.

Taking a deep breath, Tie Ruo Nan waved her hand and unleashed a cloud of golden needle Gu.

The golden needle Gu pierced the apes; some froze in place, some died of poison, and others went berserk, attacking their companions.

Combined with poison liquid Gu it became poisonous, and paired with stiff Gu it immobilized enemies. Paired with chaotic mind Gu it induced madness, and combined with vitality Gu it healed.

Tie Ruo Nan had mastered these four tactical combinations in just seven to eight days, allowing her to defeat nearly a thousand mountain apes single-handedly.

Watching impassively, Tie Mu Bai was full of praise: "Ruo Nan possesses outstanding comprehension and resolve—she will become a pillar of Tie clan."

"Old clan leader." Tie Ruo Nan climbed the peak toward Tie Mu Bai and cupped her hands in greeting.

"Good. Mastering this versatile tactic in such a short time is remarkable," Tie Mu Bai praised calmly, waving his hand.

Tie Mu Bai casually flicked his hand, sending out three golden needle Gu.

The three golden needle Gu flew and pierced into a mountain ape's head.

One plunged into its skull while the other two pierced its temples.

Screaming in pain, the mountain ape leaped twice before kneeling before Tie Mu Bai.

Strangely, it had lost all bodily control, kneeling reverently and motionless, unable even to cry out.

Tie Mu Bai said: "Golden needle Gu combined with create fog Gu forms golden fog, specializing in piercing defenses. When roaming Southern Border at age twenty-eight, I used this move to dominate the Luchuan river region."

"Combined with puppet control Gu, it can seize control of living bodies. I used this move to turn thirty-eight of them, capturing the entire group and eliminating the evil."

"Lord old clan leader, I will not let you down, golden needle Gu will not shame your name in my hands!" Tie Ruo Nan spoke resolutely.

The old man gratefully nodded and patted Tie Ruo Nan's shoulder.

"You have Tie clan blood in your veins and must shoulder the clan's future. I leave little beast king Fang Zheng to you as your test—do you have confidence?"

"Rest assured, old clan leader—Fang Zheng has fallen into the demonic path, and I will claim his head!" Tie Ruo Nan vowed.

"Good. Remaining humble in victory and undaunted in defeat, drawing strength from tribulation—few youngsters can manage this. Maintain this, and you will surely become the glory of Tie clan! Now, I shall teach you the principles behind these two tactics, my insights, and the other combinations derived from them."

After pondering, she asked: "Lately, the three kings inheritance's opening duration has grown shorter, and fewer Gu Masters can enter. The light pillars are dimmer and narrower. Is the blessed land truly nearing its end?"

Tie Mu Bai nodded: "It is indeed so."

"You are not yet ready for certain truths. All things exist in balance—with darkness, light; with water, fire; with blessings, calamities." He sighed: "Every blessed land faces an earthly calamity every ten years and a heavenly tribulation every hundred years. This blessed land originated from an ancient Gu Immortal before the three kings remodeled it."

"This blessed land is ancient and near the end of its lifespan. A land spirit could prolong it, but unfortunately none exists here."

"A blessed land without a land spirit is like a sinking ship. Anyone can enter to plunder treasures; the more they take, the larger the holes become and the faster it sinks. It will collapse in less than ten years once its immortal essence is exhausted."

# Chapter 375: Gathering of Experts

"To think even blessed lands face destruction..." Tie Ruo Nan sighed after hearing this secret.

"Nothing in this world is indestructible. In the vast universe's cycle of fate and survival, mortals are mere specks of sand at the bottom of the sea, utterly insignificant. Only by ascending to Gu Immortal can we transcend mortality and build a foundation—turning from sand into an island capable of resisting the waves," Tie Mu Bai lamented.

"When the blessed land is destroyed, wouldn't those inside be in grave danger?" Tie Ruo Nan asked.

"Indeed," Tie Mu Bai nodded. "When a blessed land perishes, the winds of assimilation reduce everything within to primeval essence. Even Gu Immortals fear this wind, for it destroys all."

Tie Mu Bai waved his hand: "No need to worry. Having entered the blessed land many times, I have observed and come to understand it deeply. Destruction is still far off, but restrictions on Gu Masters will continue to diminish. Gradually, we will be able to use more and more Gu. Eventually, the blessed land will be full of loopholes, allowing us to enter and exit at will."

"The final period will bring the decisive battle when everyone plunders at full power. By then, San Cha mountain will host many more rank five experts."

"Finally, the mountainside," Fang Zheng panted, sweat dripping from his forehead.

Climbing this far had squeezed out all his soul's potential.

The incubating flea in his aperture spoke: "Very good, disciple! Now leave it to your master!"

A formless energy flowed into the deepest part of Fang Zheng's soul.

Reinvigorated, Fang Zheng surged upward, rapidly catching up to Feng Jin Huang and the other disciples.

The ten Gu Immortals outside the blessed land immediately noticed Fang Zheng's unusual condition.

"He Feng Yang, how generous. A spirit incubating flea isn't precious, but to keep it functioning, you used one's own way Gu, didn't you?"

One's own way Gu allowed other Gu worms to function in the blessed land.

"Spirit Affinity House has Feng Jin Huang and Spirit Butterfly Valley has Xiao Qi Xing. Immortal Crane Sect couldn't compete otherwise," He Feng Yang replied.

On San Cha mountain, three light pillars soared into the sky.

Just as Tie Mu Bai predicted, experts gathered on San Cha mountain as a storm brewed.

Yun clan's young leader Yun Luo Tian, a wind path master who reached rank three peak stage at twenty-three, was now thirty-five and a step away from rank four peak stage.

A month later, San Cha mountain saw its fourth rank five Gu Master.

The lord of Wu mountain, he was a rank five demonic poison cultivator and regional overlord.

Two months later, Wu clan's Wu Lan Shan arrived.

Wu clan's Wu Lan Shan arrived next, clashing fiercely with Wang Xiao to a draw.

Soon after, renowned demonic cultivator Chou Jiu arrived.

Known as the killer ghost doctor, Chou Jiu was a rank five healing master and one of Southern Border's four great doctors.

Eccentric by nature, he held a bizarre rule: anyone he healed had to kill someone for him in return.

Time passed quickly as the three kings inheritance opened and closed repeatedly.

At the mountain foot, two people appeared.

People soon recognized them, crying out in shock: "The black and white twin demons are back!"

# Chapter 376: Fang Yuan courts death

On the mountain peak, three light pillars of red, blue, and yellow appeared before the crowd.

"Old clan leader Tie, you first," Wu Lan Shan politely offered to Tie Mu Bai.

Tie Mu Bai looked at the three pillars, nodding: "Then I will go first."

Tie Mu Bai took the lead, entering King Xin Inheritance.

Wu Gui and Ku Mo avoided King Xin Inheritance to stay clear of Tie Mu Bai.

After Tie Mu Bai, Wu Lan Shan, Wang Xiao, and the others selected their respective entrances and entered.

Tie Ruo Nan stood among the rank four Gu Masters, her brows furrowed.

"Fang Zheng, it's you!" Tie Ruo Nan stepped out, staring fiercely at Fang Yuan.

Exhaling deeply, Tie Ruo Nan declared: "Little beast king, your life is mine. One day I will slay you to appease my fallen clansmen!"

With that, she turned and entered the light pillar, vanishing.

Fang Yuan frowned deeply.

"Tie Ruo Nan... I cannot let you grow further."

Controlling the killing intent in his heart, Fang Yuan walked towards the inheritance entrance.

"Hold it." A righteous Gu Master suddenly stepped forward, blocking their path.

Clad in fluttering white robes, the challenger was Yun clan's young leader, Yun Luo Tian.

"Little beast king, I heard you killed Tie Ba Xiu," Yun Luo Tian said, glancing at Bai Ning Bing.

He pointed arrogantly: "Using a flying Gu to kill him was nothing but a cheap trick."

Fang Yuan looked at him with an eerie smile: "You're right, I used a loophole. My apologies."

Yun Luo Tian sneered: "Since you admit it, step aside. With so many heroes here, it is not your turn yet."

Fang Yuan stepped back: "Sir makes sense. It was my fault—please go ahead."

Yun Luo Tian laughed loudly and entered the light pillar.

After Yun Luo Tian entered, Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing stepped into King Bao Inheritance together.

Once the spinning world stabilized, Fang Yuan assessed his surroundings.

A grey-white sky hung over reddish ground littered with volcanic rocks, rising steam, and grey dust.

"Devil again, they descended again!"

Volcanic rocks flipped open as tiny humans emerged.

These baby-sized, tender pink figures were exceedingly cute, each bearing a round red egg in the center of their chest.

At this moment, Fang Yuan could only use one exploding egg Gu.

The round required him to exterminate these red eggmen using the exploding egg Gu.

Facing the assaulting red eggmen, Fang Yuan did not move.

These attacks accumulated, quickly injuring him heavily.

Bathed in blood and gasping for breath as death closed in, not even using exploding egg Gu could change the situation now.

Having brought himself into such mortal peril, Fang Yuan's lips curled into a smile.

"This moment has finally arrived!"

# Chapter 377: Refining an Immortal Gu!

A bunch of short red eggmen encircled Fang Yuan, attacking constantly.

Just then, a formless energy descended and swept Fang Yuan away!

When Fang Yuan awoke from his slumber, the scenery before him had completely changed.

"I've already healed your injuries." A large tortoise opened its wrinkled eyes slowly, staring at Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan slowly rose and touched his body; his injuries were indeed healed.

He had courted death relying entirely on past-life knowledge at huge risk without using Spring Autumn Cicada. Having reached the main hall, his all-in gamble had paid off.

Ceasing his laughter, Fang Yuan faced the huge tortoise: "Land spirit Ba Gui, you finally awakened from your slumber."

"You know me?" The tortoise's voice vibrated with suspicion in its eyes.

"Indeed, and I know this blessed land's secret," Fang Yuan nodded.

"This blessed land belonged to an ancient strength path Gu Immortal who died resisting tribulations. His dying wish fused with the blessed land's heavenly power to form the land spirit—you, Ba Gui. As long as I fulfill his dying wish, I become the blessed land's new master, am I right?"

The huge tortoise nodded: "Correct. Fulfill it, and you become my master."

"And that wish is to refine the second aperture Gu, right?" Fang Yuan asked.

The original master had created the second aperture Gu recipe, but died in a tribulation just before the crucial step.

The fated person had to be a strength path Gu Master possessing beast phantoms and sufficient strength.

Next, this strength path Gu Master had to be on the brink of death to receive this opportunity.

Upon awakening, the land spirit immediately searched for the fated person.

Hearing Fang Yuan mention second aperture Gu, Ba Gui was stunned: "You know much."

Fang Yuan directly asked: "Refining an Immortal Gu fundamentally requires immortal essence. How much immortal essence remains in the blessed land?"

The floor cracked open and a bronze cauldron slowly rose into the hall.

The three-legged cauldron held the remaining immortal essence.

Very little remained—only a thin layer at the bottom, slowly depleting drop by drop over time.

"Still, this is far better than in my past life. As long as I ration it..." Fang Yuan pondered.

"Second aperture Gu grants an additional aperture! With my A grade aptitude, one aperture holds ninety percent primeval essence. With a second aperture, I will have double primeval essence, double recovery speed, and a second Gu set. My battle strength will surge. Once I succeed, I will be the strongest of my rank!"

As a reborn Gu Immortal who had refined Spring Autumn Cicada, he possessed precious experience crafting Immortal Gu.

He had already eliminated past-life competitors like Xue San Si and Century Boy.

"That Gu Immortal made ample preparations. With such a foundation, I have at least a fifty percent chance of success."

When refining Spring Autumn Cicada, his success rate was under twenty percent; fifty percent for second aperture Gu was exceptionally high!

# Chapter 378: He is dead (Double Chapter)

If the Gu Masters in the blessed land discovered the main hall, they would attack collectively and disrupt the refinement.

Fang Yuan demanded: "Land spirit, tell me the recipe for refining second aperture Gu."

Ba Gui nodded: "Listen well, this is the full recipe."

*"Rotten soil blood powder, treasure flower in the earth. Jade bone into petals, ice muscles into stalks, golden relic into the flower's heart. Bright stellar fire, gather ice and snow into plains. Yang clouds burn with elixir fire below, yin clouds scatter golden sand above; add beast phantoms between until lightning crackles, creating beast strength placenta, and collect apertures... Wild grass grows rampant, blood qi like the sea. Three hundred years spring, five hundred years autumn. With boundless divine opportunity, roam the wilds, adding third watch and another third watch to obtain nine. Nine as extreme, refinement is complete!"*

The early stage involved hundreds of materials, the middle stage consumed numerous rank four and rank five Gu, and later steps grew even more arduous, ultimately requiring another rank six Immortal Gu!

"Not good. Refining second aperture Gu is far harder than I estimated; past-life intel only covered the early recipe, mixed with heavy misinterpretations."

Fang Yuan's heart sank; hearing the recipe dropped his fifty percent confidence to thirty.

"Do not worry, vast amounts of materials and Gu worms for the refinement are already stored in this hall."

At the land spirit's words, dark blue light suffused the main hall.

Dazzling materials filled the hall alongside at least five thousand Gu worms from rank one to rank five—including over six hundred rank four and no less than eighty rank five Gu!

"So many top-tier materials! There are even over ten yellow gold and eight purple crystal relic Gu! Using them, I could reach rank five peak stage in moments!"

Fang Yuan briefly considered abandoning the refinement to seize these Gu.

Guarded by the land spirit, they could only be used for the refinement.

"One crucial Gu worm remains," Ba Gui said.

Within the blue light rested a confined Immortal Gu.

Resembling a round yellow gem, it was hollow inside, containing a ball of purple smoke.

"This is the rank six Immortal Gu — Divine Travel." The land spirit introduced.

Divine travel Gu could transport a Gu Master anywhere, but required intoxication and could not control its destination.

"Divine travel Gu is one of the four great movement Gu, yet with such a fatal flaw, who dares use it? No wonder the blessed land's original master chose it to refine second aperture Gu."

Ba Gui resealed the Gu: "When do you plan to begin refinement?"

"Let me study the recipe first." Fang Yuan sat cross-legged, closing his eyes.

"Land spirit, let's start the refinement!"

"Alright," the land spirit responded, handing Fang Yuan two materials and a Gu worm.

With Ba Gui's assistance, Fang Yuan mixed green pond rotten soil and eight desolate blood powder.

Once evenly mixed, he planted the earth treasury flower into the soil.

The earth treasury flower instantly decayed, unable to endure the toxic soil and violent blood powder.

After several failures, Fang Yuan finally planted the earth treasury flower successfully.

The soil and blood powder reached a subtle balance, transforming the earth treasury flower Gu.

This completed the recipe's first step—*rotten soil blood powder, treasure flower in the earth*.

Next came the second step, requiring jade bone Gu, ice muscle Gu, yellow gold relic Gu, and expert refinement techniques.

The third step required stellar fire prairie Gu and snowy plain Gu to achieve balance.

The fourth step—*'Yang cloud burns with elixir fire below and yin clouds scatter sand like gold above'*—tested multitasking ability.

Fang Yuan used yang cloud Gu with elixir fire Gu, then activated yin cloud Gu and golden sand Gu.

Orange elixir fire rose from the yang cloud while golden sand drizzled from the yin cloud.

"This is the juncture! Go, white elephant." Fang Yuan activated all-out effort Gu, summoning the phantom.

The white elephant phantom plunged into the clouds, ground down by elixir fire and golden sand.

He pointed, sacrificing the black python next.

Stone turtle and horse phantoms flew from his body next.

The four beast phantoms intertwined into a colorful whirlpool, yet something was still lacking.

"Strange! Why haven't they fused?" Fang Yuan was baffled by this first obstacle.

Looking at the failing refinement, Fang Yuan realized: "Their nature!"

The white elephant was steady, black python cold and coiling, stone turtle immovable as a mountain, and horse swift as the wind.

Under his control, the four phantoms displayed their true dispositions.

*Boom!*

Thunder crackled and a strange transformation began.

The yin cloud sank while the yang cloud rose, merging into a unified entity.

"A strength path Gu Master must understand their true nature to trigger fusion." Fang Yuan exhaled in relief.

Amid continuous rumbles, the mist unified in color before suddenly exploding.

A disc-like rank five Gu remained, its surface resembling an embryo of grass and soil with a ferocious multi-beast chimera in the center.

Recognizing the beast strength placenta Gu, Fang Yuan collapsed asleep from exhaustion.

"Next is gathering apertures!" Standing up, he told the land spirit: "Ba Gui, it is time."

Ba Gui replied straightforwardly: "Alright, I can send you anywhere in the blessed land."

A large map displaying every scene in the blessed land appeared before Fang Yuan.

"Here." Fang Yuan scanned the map, fixing his gaze and pointing.

The next moment, Fang Yuan vanished and reappeared before Tie Mu Bai.

Tie Mu Bai was startled by Fang Yuan's sudden arrival.

Before he could speak, Fang Yuan waved his hand, summoning a rock crocodile phantom.

*Bam!*

The crocodile's tail struck Tie Mu Bai's head, shattering it like a watermelon—

He, was dead.

# Chapter 379: All according to plan

Fang Yuan grabbed the corpse's arm, sending his consciousness into the aperture.

Crystal clear aperture walls contained an ebbing and flowing primeval sea.

Above the sea darted Tie Mu Bai's vital Gu, the rank five turn gold Gu.

Turn gold Gu held immense power: any living being struck by it turned to pure gold, instantly perishing.

Beside turn gold Gu, a yellow-gold current flowed at the bottom of the sea.

This was the rank five liquid metal Gu, providing flawless shifting defense.

An inconspicuous eyeball rested in the sea, flashing with golden light.

This was the vajra stare Gu.

Upon activation, making eye contact allowed it to directly strike the opponent's soul.

Using ordinary eyes risked blindness; two golden eye Gu were required to use it safely.

Besides these three rank five Gu, Tie Mu Bai possessed four top-quality rank four metal path Gu: golden dragon Gu, golden breeze Gu, golden coat Gu, and golden aurora Gu.

Fang Yuan retrieved beast strength placenta Gu and activated it near the stomach.

Tie Mu Bai's aperture was drawn into the placenta Gu and devoured by the wild beast inside.

After devouring the aperture, the placenta Gu became lustrous and dense.

"Absorbing Tie Mu Bai's aperture raised the second aperture Gu's aptitude to forty percent!"

"The more apertures absorbed, the higher the aptitude. Next, I must slaughter Gu Masters to feed this placenta Gu!"

He had slain Tie clan's group precisely to provoke them into dispatching a rank five Gu Master.

"These rank four and five Gu Masters are all fuel to refine second aperture Gu!"

Spring Autumn Cicada's aura could only suppress rank four Gu. Fang Yuan could not control these three rank five Gu, but heavenly power restricted them, leaving them completely immobilized.

Refined by Fang Yuan's primeval essence, they yielded and changed owners.

"Teleporting you and supporting your attack expended considerable immortal essence," the land spirit warned.

Fang Yuan stopped laughing, his expression calming: "I have already considered that."

"Ba Gui, the three kings remodeled the blessed land, costing you control. Overwriting these changes consumes too much immortal essence. Dividing the remaining essence into sixteen portions, our earlier expenditure was half a portion."

Ba Gui was stunned: "You are absolutely correct. Calculating the expenditure so accurately amazes me!"

"I estimate that among the sixteen portions, at least eight are needed to refine second aperture Gu. Of the remaining eight, four must sustain the blessed land, slightly over three to kill Gu Masters, and the remainder reserved for emergencies."

Ba Gui replied: "Refining the Immortal Gu requires ten portions, while maintaining the inheritance takes two. Once you kill enough people, I will close the blessed land."

"No," Fang Yuan shook his head. "Primeval stones can substitute in certain steps, saving two portions. Furthermore, the entrance must stay open the entire time."

"Why?" Land spirit asked.

Fang Yuan replied: "Closing the inheritance would expose my killings, attracting even stronger foes."

"Even if closed, expelling the remaining Gu Masters would waste precious immortal essence."

"In later stages, dwindling immortal essence will bring the blessed land to the brink of collapse, opening loopholes to the outside world. Once your strength falls, this hall will be discovered and attacked by the Gu Masters."

The land spirit said: "Substituting primeval stones requires at least thirty million. Do you have that many?"

"I only have a million, but rank four and five Gu Masters are immensely wealthy. After killing them, will we lack primeval stones?"

Ba Gui nodded: "My assistance will dwindle near the final step, so we shall follow your plan."

# Chapter 380: Slaughtering Rank Five

A battle between dogs ended.

Surrounded by hundreds of steel armor dogs, rank five demonic Gu Master Ku Mo felt pleased.

Space tore open and a young man appeared.

"Eh? Who is it!" Ku Mo was shocked!

He laughed: "The person who wants your life!"

Saying so, he activated golden dragon Gu.

Ku Mo screamed: "How can you use Gu!"

Golden dragon came attacking, scratching and almost smashing his entire upper body.

Fang Yuan had intended only to crush Ku Mo's skull, but the fierce golden dragon had destroyed his entire upper body.

Fang Yuan stepped forward, extending his mind into the aperture to loot it.

Ku Mo had eight Gu worms, but only one rank five: the sinister soft bones Gu.

This Gu could soften the bones of any living being within a twenty-five kilometer radius.

After claiming Ku Mo's Gu worms, Fang Yuan used beast strength placenta Gu to devour his aperture.

Nourished further, the placenta Gu gained a faint luster, smoothing out like crude porcelain.

Elsewhere, Wu Gui commanded chrysanthemum akita dogs against a pack of lightning symbol dogs.

Fang Yuan suddenly appeared behind Wu Gui.

Without a word, Fang Yuan launched a bone spike.

*Puchi!*

The bone spike pierced Wu Gui's heart, pinning him into the ground.

"Who..." he gasped as blood gushed from his mouth.

The lightning symbol dogs swiftly engulfed the akitas and pounced toward Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan unleashed the golden dragon to slaughter the attacking dogs.

Fang Yuan quickly approached Wu Gui's corpse, collecting his Gu worms and feeding the aperture to beast strength placenta Gu.

Wu Gui's rank five pitch black Gu resembled an oval black stone radiating peculiar darkness.

When activated, it emitted black smoke that partially sealed Gu worms below rank six.

He placed pitch black Gu into his aperture and stowed the rank four Gu away.

Housing five rank five Gu and numerous rank four Gu, his aperture was already under heavy pressure.

Most importantly, the recovering rank six Spring Autumn Cicada was exerting immense pressure on his aperture.

"Triple time flow accelerates Spring Autumn Cicada's recovery. I must refine the second aperture Gu quickly to relieve this pressure."

Near the refinement's end, the decaying blessed land would connect to the outside world, exposing him to attacks from Gu Masters.

"The final step with divine travel Gu requires total concentration while leaving my body in grave danger!"

"I still have Bai Ning Bing. Bound by the poison vow, she will protect me."

Thinking so, Fang Yuan vanished, and appeared before Bai Ning Bing.

"Fang Yuan, how did you get here?" Bai Ning Bing asked, shocked and suspicious.

# Chapter 381: All must die!

"Bai Ning Bing, do you still remember our vow? Do you still want Yang Gu?" Fang Yuan asked.

"As if you don't know." Bai Ning Bing snorted.

"You need my help? Speak plainly, or I refuse."

"Soon I will be besieged by Gu Masters, and I need you to protect my Gu refinement."

"A siege by Gu Masters? Plenty want you dead. But refining Gu? What Gu are you refining?"

Fang Yuan was straightforward and explained the entire situation directly.

"The land spirit is alive? You are refining an Immortal Gu? And you killed Tie Mu Bai, Wu Gui, and Ku Mo?"

Without another word, Fang Yuan displayed his loot.

"These Gu!" Bai Ning Bing lost her composure in disbelief.

"You really killed them?" Bai Ning Bing stared at Fang Yuan, shock clear in her eyes.

"Time is pressing. Next, I will send you to King Quan Inheritance; advance according to my instructions. Gather as many dog beasts as possible," Fang Yuan urged.

"Hmph, I did not say I will help you." Bai Ning Bing's gaze flickered.

Fang Yuan smiled: "If I succeed, Yang Gu will be yours. After leaving San Cha mountain, you are free to go. Either way, I will refine this Gu."

Bai Ning Bing was exasperated: "You shameless bastard, using the poison vow to coerce me?"

Fang Yuan's tone softened: "Ning Bing, this is cooperation. Since Qing Mao mountain, our cooperation has overcome every peril. Have you not always wanted Yang Gu? Obtaining it restores your male body. Or have you grown fond of your female identity?"

"It is settled then." Fang Yuan laughed, handing Bai Ning Bing a dog enslavement Gu before disappearing.

Her surroundings shifted as the land spirit sent her to King Quan Inheritance.

Fang Yuan suddenly appeared in front of Wu Lan Shan.

"Little Beast King! You can enter and exit at will?" Wu Lan Shan retreated a step.

Fang Yuan pointed his little finger, shooting out a bone spear.

*Puchi!*

With a soft sound, the bone spear pierced Wu Lan Shan's heart like lightning.

Wu Lan Shan stared incredulously: "You... can use Gu?"

Her aura dissipated in death.

The moment she died, mysterious light enveloped her three rank five Gu worms and whisked them away.

Fang Yuan knew Wu clan possessed Immortal Gu No Loss, capable of retrieving precious Gu worms.

Fang Yuan collected the valuable rank four Gu, then used beast placenta Gu to swallow Wu Lan Shan's aperture.

Nourished further, beast placenta Gu resembled delicate pottery and felt sleek to the touch.

Its aptitude had increased to seventy-five percent.

While Tie Mu Bai's aperture had raised it to forty percent, consuming Wu Gui, Ku Mo, and Wu Lan Shan combined added only thirty-five percent.

"Of course, this depends on each Gu Master's foundation. There are five rank five Gu Masters on San Cha mountain in total. Only Tie Mu Bai, Ku Mo, and Wu Gui are rank five peak stage, with Tie Mu Bai possessing the deepest foundation from Tie clan's vast resources. Wu Lan Shan and Wang Xiao are both rank five upper stage. Chou Jiu is only initial stage. But no matter—Yi Huo, Kong Ri Tian, and the rest must all die!"

The land spirit was thoroughly familiar with the three kings inheritance.

Guiding Bai Ning Bing cost almost no immortal essence yet provided immense help.

Making the best choice in every round, Bai Ning Bing's strength snowballed continuously.

"Next, you face an opposing Gu Master whose dog beasts were depleted in battle. I will confine him so you can slay him and seize his Gu, greatly bolstering your forces," the land spirit instructed.

Bai Ning Bing nodded and slowly walked out of the fog.

"It is you..." Her opponent was clearly shocked at her sight.

Her opponent was Yun clan's young leader Yun Luo Tian, who had antagonized them before.

Yun Luo Tian sneered: "Tough luck running into me—you don't even have half my dog beasts..."

He stopped mid-sentence, eyes bulging like a choked rooster.

From the mist behind Bai Ning Bing, large numbers of dog beasts emerged in succession.

Endlessly...

# Chapter 382: Continue Killing

"Impossible! How can you have so many dog beasts?!" Yun Luo Tian shrieked, pointing at Bai Ning Bing as he trembled like he had seen a ghost.

He gritted his teeth: "Don't be smug, Bai Ning Bing! Once out, I will expose your secret to everyone, especially the rank five Gu Masters! You are finished!"

Saying so, he took out a token and used it.

But nothing happened.

Bai Ning Bing's dog army surrounded him and slaughtered his hounds.

"You disabled my token! Impressive methods, but realize this: I am Yun clan's young clan leader. Kill me, and the entire Yun clan will never let you off!"

"I don't even fear Tie clan; would I fear your tiny Yun clan?" Bai Ning Bing snickered.

Bai Ning Bing willed, and the closest dog barked before pouncing on Yun Luo Tian.

Bai Ning Bing shook her head in disdain, walking forward and keeping Yun Luo Tian's Gu worms.

The land spirit teleported his corpse, sending it to Fang Yuan.

Fang Yuan stepped on Wang Xiao, demanding the location of Wu mountain.

"You want the path into Wu mountain? Stop dreaming! Kill me if you want; you will never learn the true route inside."

"Wu mountain is a natural fortress, virtually impregnable against any force of righteous Gu Masters. Wang Xiao relied on it to reign freely as a regional despot. If I claim it as my base, I will have no worries regarding rank five resources," Fang Yuan thought.

Just then, Yun Luo Tian's corpse was transported over with a soft chime.

Fang Yuan kicked Wang Xiao aside, approaching the corpse to let beast strength placenta Gu devour the aperture.

"Yun clan's young leader!" Recognizing Yun Luo Tian and witnessing Fang Yuan use beast strength placenta Gu, Wang Xiao felt his heart chill.

Seeing Fang Yuan approach, Wang Xiao yelled: "Wait! We can negotiate—I will give you the mountain's route, but you must swear on my poison vow Gu to spare me..."

"Speak! Which is the path into Wu mountain?" Fang Yuan shouted sharply, crushing Wang Xiao's right wrist underfoot.

"Let me go and I'll tell you!" Wang Xiao screamed in pain.

"Hmph, stubborn!" Fang Yuan stomped again, shattering Wang Xiao's left kneecap to bits with a sickening snap.

Sweating profusely and shuddering in agony, Wang Xiao gritted his teeth and stared deeply at Fang Yuan, refusing to speak.

"I must refine Gu soon and cannot risk being attacked by you," Fang Yuan sighed, delivering the fatal blow to Wang Xiao.

Afterward, he smoothly took the Gu worms and devoured the aperture.

Devouring the apertures raised beast strength placenta Gu's aptitude to eighty-three percent, but immortal essence consumption had exceeded two portions.

"Not good, immortal essence expenditure exceeds my estimate. Originating from the antiquity era, this blessed land is too old—it's a miracle it still stands today."

# Chapter 383: Profound Concept of the Universe, Red Lotus Demon Venerable

"Young man, primeval essence expenditure has deviated from the plan; you should stop now," the land spirit noticed and suggested.

Fang Yuan's expression turned cold, immediately retorting: "That won't do—eighty-three percent isn't enough, my plan is to reach ninety percent!"

"Most things in life do not go as wished. How can we get everything? Young man, you are blinded by greed and losing rationality," the land spirit said calmly.

Fang Yuan cursed inwardly. Without the land spirit, he could not use immortal essence. Since it wanted to stop, he had to persuade it.

"Ba Gui." Fang Yuan exhaled turbid air, softening his tone. "Slightly exceeding essence expenditure is no big deal; my plan included spare essence."

"Young man, your calculation is flawed. What if refinement fails? Complications are inevitable, and failure will waste immortal essence. If you spend it now and fail even slightly, refining the second aperture Gu will fail," the land spirit retorted.

"Young man, stop taking risks. If you persist, I will refuse to cooperate, revoke your trial qualification, and leave the opportunity for others," the land spirit warned firmly.

"We can spare the others, but that rank five Gu Master must die. As one of Southern Border's four great doctors, his extensive connections make eliminating him necessary for our safety," Fang Yuan urged.

"You already planned for that girl and the beast groups to stall them during refinement, while I risk my life to protect you. The most important thing is the Gu refinement's success," the land spirit rejected.

"You still want to kill? Impossible, I forbid it! Immortal essence cannot be squandered like this; we must preserve it for Gu refinement." The land spirit warned: "Demonic Gu Masters always take risks. Young man, why do you think the righteous path flourishes and suppresses the demonic path? Because the demonic path is too extreme, greedy, and reckless, while the righteous path seeks steady stability, immovable as a mountain."

"Ba Gui, you are wrong. Demonic cultivators live harsh lives and must resort to extremes. If they didn't seize every benefit and take risks, how could they cultivate or compete with the righteous path? Greed, selfishness, extremism, and risk-taking are the survival laws of the demonic path. For sufficient benefits, what is walking a tightrope? One misstep means doom, yet making the impossible possible is the thrill of the demonic path! A demonic cultivator's life is like intense, alluring wine," Fang Yuan declared.

The land spirit sighed deeply: "Young man, your demonic nature is too entrenched to turn back; it will destroy you. *If heaven commits a sin, it can be forgiven; but if one commits a sin himself, he deserves death*."

Fang Yuan laughed: "What is sin? That sinners deserve death is merely the weak's helpless hope, spread so others will abide by it and protect them. As for me, I want to sin yet roam freely across the world—a demonic overlord who slaughters living beings and revels in everything I can take. I will slaughter these Gu Masters, successfully refine the second aperture Gu, and emerge the greatest winner! Ba Gui, feel this—what do you think it is?"

With that, Fang Yuan activated Spring Autumn Cicada, leaking its aura into the world for the first time.

The land spirit was flabbergasted!

"This is the aura of a rank six Immortal Gu—Spring Autumn Cicada! As a mortal, how can you possess such an Immortal Gu?"

"Why can't a mortal own an Immortal Gu? To tell you the truth, I refined Spring Autumn Cicada myself," Fang Yuan said.

"I understand! You were a Gu Immortal who used Spring Autumn Cicada to be reborn in the past and alter history!" the land spirit gasped.

"Oh? Ba Gui, you know about Spring Autumn Cicada? Please enlighten me," Fang Yuan quickly inquired.

"Since the immemorial era, space path and time path formed the foundation of heaven and earth. Space path represents the four directions, while time path represents past, present, and future. In my blessed land's prime, it spanned 6,000 km² with time flowing at six times normal speed," the land spirit said.

The land spirit continued: "Spring Autumn Cicada is a time path Gu. Its renowned master who left his name in history was Red Lotus Demon Venerable! He resisted the Immortal Court and shattered the chains of fate, giving future generations hope to grasp their own destiny."

"Red Lotus Demon Venerable?! History's most mysterious Demon Venerable?" Fang Yuan gasped.

"If history is a series of still images, the River of Time is the thread connecting them. Using Spring Autumn Cicada, you traveled upstream to inject your memories into an earlier image."

"So that's it." Fang Yuan benefited greatly from land spirit's words.

"To think you were a Gu Immortal who refined Spring Autumn Cicada. You are truly the most suitable candidate to refine this Gu. Fine, since you are so confident, I'll trust you this once. Who do you want to kill?" The land spirit gave in.

# Chapter 384: Scheming Ghost Doctor, Secret Door of Life and Death

"To think this inheritance was a trap, and the true victor is you, Little Beast King!" Chou Jiu spat from the ground.

"Little Beast King, I admire your talent—rising from a vagabond to such heights. Though I'm older, I had no such accomplishments at your age.
