You are a dragon among men destined to soar and roam freely. I have no wish to oppose you; I'd like to trade information on the Door of Life and Death for my life."

Just then, the land spirit transmitted urgently: "Kill him quickly! He's frantically activating his token and Gu worms. Suppressing them is causing heavy immortal essence expenditure."

Fang Yuan was startled, almost doubting his ears. "Door of Life and Death?"

The Door of Life and Death was momentous—an ancient forbidden land recorded in The Legends of Ren Zu, akin to the River of Time and Ordinary Abyss.

*Chou Jiu knows the location?!* Fang Yuan's heart leaped. His expression turned cold as he warned sharply, "Chou Jiu, activate your Gu worms once more and I will kill you immediately."

Chou Jiu's expression changed, his pupils shrinking as he slowly sat up. "You can sense me using Gu worms!"

A thought flashed in his eyes. "You can use Gu while I cannot. There's only one explanation—the land spirit is alive and you subdued it. So this blessed land held such a secret. The land spirit must be old and frail, or it would have expelled the Gu Masters already instead of having you eliminate us personally."

"Since you control the blessed land, I'll stop this futile struggle. Spare my life, and what you gain will exceed your imagination," Chou Jiu said, ceasing his covert actions.

Fang Yuan laughed noncommittally. "Tell me about the Door of Life and Death. You know its location? It is a forbidden ground on par with the River of Time, its vast interior reflecting the five regions of the main world. Legend says unique natural Gu reside within, like life Gu and death Gu."

Chou Jiu nodded: "I know its location. I've even explored it repeatedly, capturing life Gu and death Gu to refine one life one death Gu. This is why I became one of Southern Border's four great doctors."

"In that case, tell me, and I may spare your life," Fang Yuan promised.

Chou Jiu shook his head: "The secret is too momentous. I swore before the sea oath Gu never to reveal details to outsiders. If I speak, I will turn to dust, erased from this world. However, if you join Shadow Sect as one of us, I can tell you."

"Sea oath Gu? Shadow Sect?" Fang Yuan frowned.

"Yes, my sect is Shadow Sect. Our founding ancestor was a rank eight Gu Immortal of the Immemorial Era who found the Door of Life and Death and placed it in his blessed land. Shadow Sect went on to nurture many Gu Immortals; joining will bring you endless benefits."

Fang Yuan hesitated briefly before sneering, "Nice plan, Chou Jiu. If I join Shadow Sect, you escape danger and I must call you senior brother. In such traditional sects, respecting seniority is paramount. I'd be bound by oaths and restrictions, forced to surrender my gains on San Cha mountain and perform missions in the name of loyalty, wouldn't I?"

"Shadow Sect?" Fang Yuan sneered, shaking his head. "Even if you aren't lying, so what? It's a miracle a sect founded in the Immemorial Era still exists. Those past Gu Immortals are mostly dead by now. Otherwise, Shadow Sect would be a dominant force in Southern Border, rather than leaving you to struggle alone single-handedly."

Fang Yuan laughed heartily. "I take all the benefits for myself. Even a sea oath has an obvious weakness. The oath must face a specific sea or mountain; once that body of water or mountain is destroyed, the oath loses its effect."

Chou Jiu composed himself: "It's useless. I swore before a sea inside Shadow Sect's blessed land. To destroy it, you need the location, but I can only reveal the location once the sea is destroyed. It's a dead loop."

Fang Yuan sneered, killing intent surging: "Since nothing can be done, forget it. You can die now."

Chou Jiu sighed deeply: "Go ahead and kill me then."

Fang Yuan's bone spike pierced Chou Jiu's shoulder inches from his heart.

"You... you!" Chou Jiu pointed at Fang Yuan, his trembling voice full of fear and shock: "Little Beast King, you madman! You really meant to kill me! That is the Door of Life and Death! Back then, even Ren Zu spent all his effort to find it. It is an unparalleled holy land controlling life and death—obtain and comprehend it, and you will attain immortality!"

Fang Yuan looked at him coldly, speaking plainly: "If you can't tell me the secret, what use do I have for you?"

"You can join Shadow Sect!" Chou Jiu sweated profusely from pain and fear.

Fang Yuan snorted coldly, raising his hand and aiming again.

Chou Jiu waved frantically, shouting: "Wait, don't be rash! There are other methods—we can use poison vow Gu to establish mutual trust!"

"I do not need your trust." Fang Yuan replied coldly.

"Then use slavery Gu! I'll acknowledge you as master—just don't kill me!"

"Controlling you requires a rare rank five slavery Gu. Even with one, could you reveal Shadow Sect's blessed land?" Fang Yuan retorted.

Chou Jiu's expression froze.

Recognizing this, he laughed bitterly: "To think I, Chou Jiu, would die here today."

"Dying by my hand is your honor," Fang Yuan replied plainly.

Chou Jiu looked at Fang Yuan and nodded seriously: "True. With your talent and disposition, barring bad luck, you will surely become a scourge of the demonic path. Begging you is pointless. Dying by your hand is better than being killed by those scumbags outside. Actually, I have a plan for you to learn about the Door of Life and Death."

Saying so, a cunning light flashed across Chou Jiu's eyes.

# Chapter 385: Kindness, Grudge, Affection, Hatred

"Oh? What method?" Fang Yuan's eyebrows raised.

"My original name was Zeng Ah Niu, a farmer gathering herbs. I once fell from a cliff but luckily stumbled into Shadow Sect's blessed land. Passing the land spirit's test, I became a Shadow Sect disciple. The sect had only two disciples: myself and a senior sister."

"Senior sister Chen Jiu was beautiful as an immortal fairy. Raised by the land spirit without ever leaving the blessed land, she was completely innocent. I was born hideous, constantly bullied and mocked, but senior sister was always warm and gentle. Over time, I fell in love with her. We swore before the blessed land's sea to protect each other, cultivating together and venturing deep into the Door of Life and Death to capture life Gu and death Gu..."

"That was the happiest time of my life, which I thought would last forever. But after an earthly calamity, the land spirit fell dormant and a breach opened to the outside, allowing a scoundrel to sneak in."

"He was heavily injured. Had I known what would happen, I would have killed him on the spot! Instead, I saved him. He claimed the surname Shang and had blood-red hair. Using exceptional eloquence and pretty-boy looks far better than mine, he deceived senior sister with flowery words during his recovery, winning her favor. Innocent and naive, senior sister grew delighted talking with him, eventually doting on him in every possible way."

"We argued constantly over this. Once that thief recovered, I wanted to expel him, but senior sister's heart had changed. Breaking our oath, she injured me and defected with that scoundrel!"

"I was filled with regret! I regretted my disastrous compassion, hated Chen Jiu for falling in love with another, and loathed that despicable scoundrel for robbing what I cherished. Once recovered, I left the blessed land to hunt this adulterous pair across Southern Border, only to discover that scoundrel was the leader of Shang clan!"

Chou Jiu forced a smile at Fang Yuan's indifference: "You've already guessed their identities. Yes, that thief is current Shang clan leader Shang Yan Fei, and senior sister Chen Jiu is Doctor Su Shou. Shang clan is a super clan while I was alone. I roamed Southern Border for years striving against that adulterous pair! Unfortunately, I will die here today, unable to fulfill my wish."

"Hehehe." Fang Yuan silently listened to end and suddenly laughed.

Looking at Chou Jiu on the ground with gleaming eyes, Fang Yuan said: "Killer ghost doctor, you made quite a plan. By your words, Shadow Sect has only you and Chen Jiu. If you die, I must obtain information on the Door of Life and Death from Shang Yan Fei and Doctor Su Shou. Why would they easily reveal Shadow Sect's blessed land? We would inevitably fight, and no matter who wins, the outcome favors you."

"Hahaha... Little Beast King, your meticulous thinking and domineering methods make you truly formidable. You are right, that is my plan—an open scheme you've seen through. Will you still inquire about Shadow Sect's blessed land from them?" Killer Ghost Doctor laughed heartily.

Fang Yuan looked at the rank five Gu Master before sighing, "Of course."

Fang Yuan could not ignore such enormous benefits. Knowing Chou Jiu's scheme full well, he would still charge right in.

Chou Jiu grew calm: "It matters not if I die now. Longevity is possible, but immortality is hopeless. Even Immortal and Demon Venerables turn to ash in the end. Little Beast King, I will end my own life—you need not do it."

Saying so, he committed suicide by biting off his tongue!

Blood sprayed as life faded; the ghost doctor, future head of the four great doctors, died just like that.

"To think killing this ghost doctor would yield such vital information. Shadow Sect's blessed land, the Door of Life and Death... controlling them would build my hegemony. I must revise my plans."

"Doctor Su Shou's real name is Chen Jiu, while Killer Ghost Doctor named himself Chou Jiu—hatred born from love. And after being captured, he exposed the past and was executed by Shang Yan Fei."

Fang Yuan briefly inspected and stored it, then used beast strength placenta Gu to devour Chou Jiu's aperture.

Looking at Chou Jiu's corpse, he chuckled, "Chou Jiu, you are truly cunning. Without my past-life memories, I really would have been fooled."

Although Chou Jiu was dead, he was hiding a chance of resurrection.

"Today, I'll make an exception for you." Fang Yuan ruthlessly hacked Chou Jiu's corpse into minced meat.

"Chou Jiu, resurrect from this and I'll praise your skill! Hahahaha..."

# Chapter 386: He also died

"This is the forty-eighth round." Wu Shen Tong counted in his heart.

As Wu clan's heavily nurtured enslavement path Gu Master, Wu Shen Tong's attainments were top-tier.

"Who is my next opponent?" Wu Shen Tong walked through the fog with a solemn gaze.

"In enslavement mastery, I am top-tier in Southern Border and virtually unmatched on San Cha Mountain. Only two pose any threat: Wu Gui, a rank five Gu Master who formerly walked this path, though he switched paths due to resource constraints, and Zhang San San, a true enslavement master whose cultivation falls below mine."

Beside him, his group dog guarded him closely.

From the mist ahead emerged a pack of greenish-purple dogs with rotting skin.

"Oh, corpse dogs." Wu Shen Tong laughed lightly.

He glanced at his own pack resting on the ground—around a hundred and twenty strong.

Beyond countless heavy tai dogs, green hua, smoke song, light heng, and star heng dogs poured from the mist!

How is this possible!" Wu Shen Tong screamed: "Green hua, smoke song, light heng and star heng all have differing specialties, they are on the same level, known as the five great mountain dogs collectively, able to form a grand formation! How did they get so many dogs?""

Most unbelievable of all, the enemy not only commanded vast numbers of all five breeds, but also their respective dog kings!

"Who is it? Has a rank five enslavement Gu Master arrived on San Cha Mountain? Impossible! Even a rank five couldn't field such an army! I must see who it is!"

Clutching his token, Wu Shen Tong stared into the fog.

From the fog, a persona appeared.

With a soft sound, Fang Yuan appeared behind him.

Fang Yuan placed his palm on his head, squeezing.

From the fog, Bai Ning Bing walked out casually.

"How many rounds are left?" She looked at Fang Yuan, yawning as if half-asleep.

# Chapter 387: Rank Five Slavery Gu

Fang Yuan needed Wu Shen Tong's aperture, while Bai Ning Bing needed his enslavement Gu worms and accumulated dog packs.

Upon Wu Shen Tong's death, his hounds were absorbed into Bai Ning Bing's troops, bolstering her massive canine army by ten percent.

Surveying the vast pack, Fang Yuan nodded approvingly: "Your dog army has taken shape. With this group, you can smoothly snowball to the final round of King Quan's inheritance. Remember, the final rounds feature packs of lion mastiffs. These mutated beasts far outmatch the five mountain dogs and were King Quan's trump cards. Beyond them are two dog emperors—ancient mutated beasts far stronger than ordinary ones. Once King Quan's right and left hands, each rivaling a rank five Gu Master, you must subdue them!"

Bai Ning Bing nodded and departed to continue clearing rounds.

"Reborn through Spring Autumn Cicada, you truly know the three kings inheritance inside and out." The land spirit praised: "Immortal essence only suffices to kill two more people. Who is next?"

Fang Yuan nodded and said without hesitation: "Zhang San San."

Beast placenta Gu had reached eighty-nine percent aptitude, shining like a gem compared to its formerly rough, muddy state.

Zhang San San was merely rank four upper stage, a stage below experts like Yi Huo and Kong Ri Tian.

Yet in Fang Yuan's eyes, he posed the greatest threat.

He was an enslavement master second only to Wu Shen Tong and Wu Gui.

Fang Yuan reasoned, "Bai Ning Bing isn't an enslavement master; she is a novice with hounds. Commanding so many beasts will overwhelm her—it would be remarkable if she avoids mistakes. Fortunately, she need only maintain a massive formation around the main hall and wait quietly, without active maneuvering."

During refinement, Fang Yuan would be occupied and defense would rely entirely on the dogs, making rival enslavement masters the greatest threat.

Soon enough, Fang Yuan smoothly dispatched Zhang San San.

But his death gave Fang Yuan an unexpected surprise.

From Zhang San San's aperture, Fang Yuan obtained a rank five slavery Gu.

"With this rank five slavery Gu, I can enslave an expert to assist me."

Thoughts flashing like lightning, Fang Yuan quickly settled on a candidate.

"Him. Land spirit, transport me," Fang Yuan instructed decisively.

Moments later, Feng Tian Yu knelt before Fang Yuan: "This subordinate Feng Tian Yu greets master!"

"Feng Tian Yu, you refined a rank five Gu while at rank three. Though it perished half a day later, it made your name. Now your clan faces extinction, and you are no longer the promising young clan leader," Fang Yuan said indifferently.

Feng Tian Yu looked up: "Serving Lord as my master is my greatest honor."

"Good. Now proceed through the rounds according to my directions. King Xin's inheritance holds the undefeated hundred battles Gu—bring it to me."

"Subordinate will give his all to fulfill Master's command!" Feng Tian Yu replied promptly.

Bound by the enslavement Gu, he was now Fang Yuan's utterly devoted slave.

A renowned refinement grandmaster in Southern Border, he was also the primary beneficiary of King Xin's inheritance in Fang Yuan's past life.

Having a refinement grandmaster by his side would be an immense asset during the final refinement.

Having subdued Feng Tian Yu, Fang Yuan considered his final target.

The land spirit suddenly interrupted: "Not good, something has happened!"

"What?" Fang Yuan asked, catching panic in the land spirit's voice.

"A rank four Gu Master found a weakened zone where heavenly power allows using a single Gu. He is using a rank five Gu to spread poison everywhere. Within eight minutes, he will breach the blessed land and open a passage to the outside world!"

Fang Yuan immediately sensed the seriousness of the matter.

A breach connecting to the outside world would double immortal essence consumption. Unchecked, the widening hole would eventually attract winds of assimilation.

"I've excised most compromised areas, but three lie in crucial locations and cannot be severed. He was lucky enough to stumble into one and realize it. Look for yourself." The land spirit sighed.

It was none other than Long Qing Tian!

He was currently activating his sole rank five poison Gu—jade sky.

All around him, earth and sky were suffused with pale green light.

Jade sky Gu was his trump card—an ancient, virtually extinct poison that had terrorized Southern Border.

# Chapter 388: Jade Sky Qing Tian, Hu Immortal Jin Huang

"The heavenly suppression here is weak enough to use one Gu. By poisoning the blessed land with jade sky Gu, I can connect to the outside world."

"Once a path opens, I can use Gu freely without following the Three Kings' rules, turn this place into my base, and claim all the rewards! Hehehe... who's there?!"

Long Qing Tian sensed something and turned around, seeing one person far away.

"Little Beast King Fang Zheng?" Long Qing Tian squinted, surprised by his presence.

Without a word, Fang Yuan flicked his hand, shooting out a bone spike.

The jade sky poison had already contaminated the area. Lacking detoxification methods, Fang Yuan kept his distance, engaging from afar.

Within a few exchanges, Fang Yuan defeated Long Qing Tian.

Long Qing Tian's corpse was pale jade-green, thoroughly poisoned by jade sky Gu.

Before dying, the demonic expert had smiled darkly and activated jade sky Gu to poison himself.

"As expected of a demonic head." Fang Yuan breathed out deeply.

Jade sky Gu carried an immemorial poison only Southern Border's four great doctors could cure.

Devouring the aperture required touching the corpse; infection would dissolve Fang Yuan into green light within two hours.

"My last portion of immortal essence was spent on Long Qing Tian. Beast strength placenta Gu can only reach eighty-nine percent aptitude."

"Worse than the placenta Gu, this land is infected with jade sky poison. When it rots into blue-green light, a massive breach will form."

"A healthy blessed land could excise the rot, but this land is too ancient and depleted. This breach is the final straw. Time is running out—I must hurry."

With his slaughter concluded, Fang Yuan returned to the hall to race against time refining Gu.

As days passed, Spring Autumn Cicada's pressure intensified.

Fang Yuan pushed his limits, sleeping only an hour a day.

Laboring desperately, he progressed rapidly despite a few unavoidable failures, earning high praise from the land spirit.

In the grand hall, a water-tank-sized sphere of multicolored light spun in midair.

Controlling it with bloodshot eyes, Fang Yuan muttered, "Retrieve ninety grams of willow orpiment."

Focusing intently, Fang Yuan called out as the light turned yellow, "Retrieve thirty snowball Gu."

The snowball Gu merged into the light, shrinking the sphere while retaining its color.

Eventually, it turned into a tiny inconspicuous soil-yellow rock.

Fang Yuan carefully cradled the rock, exhaling deeply; two days and a night of refinement had finally concluded.

"Land spirit, how much immortal essence left?" He asked.

"Five portions," the land spirit replied weakly.

Eighteen days had past since Long Qing Tian was killed.

"Only five portions left, and refinement is merely two-thirds complete. Land spirit, what date is it?" Fang Yuan asked grimly.

"According to your calendar, today is the nineteenth of October."

"October nineteenth... in five days, refinement reaches the final step. October twenty-fourth... isn't that when Feng Jin Huang claimed Hu Immortal blessed land in history?" Fang Yuan recalled.

"My reach cannot touch Central Continent yet. Feng Jin Huang claiming Hu Immortal blessed land is an astronomical gain."

# Chapter 389: Only one step left

October 21st.

The land spirit brought bad news: a rank five expert had entered the blessed land.

Fang Yuan recognized him: "Xiao Mang of Xiao clan, with rank five extreme light Gu. He came, just like in my previous life."

The land spirit gasped, "Extreme light Gu? That means Xiao Mang can summon immemorial light of glory! He is a massive threat!"

Fang Yuan scoffed: "Relax. His extreme light Gu is damaged; he can only use it thrice a month without destroying it."

The land spirit exhaled in relief: "That's good. I've grown weaker; at the final juncture, you must rely on yourself."

"Hehe, I've always preferred relying on myself." Fang Yuan chuckled, silently resuming his Gu refinement.

October 22nd.

Fang Yuan spat blood, his vision darkening as he nearly lost consciousness.

"Refinement failure causes backlash. I've failed three times despite perfect execution; this step relies on luck, and time is running out!"

Pale-faced, Fang Yuan struggled through the painful backlash and began his fourth attempt.

Under four portions of immortal essence remained in the bronze cauldron.

October 23rd.

Fang Yuan stopped, eyes shining brightly as he gazed at the Gu in his hand.

Resembling a legless, antennaless beetle with a plump belly, its form was vague—like crude, lifeless earthenware.

The land spirit rejoiced: "You've refined the false Gu! Now only one step remains to complete the true second aperture Gu!"

"Right, only one step remains." Fang Yuan sounded both relieved and solemn.

*"Three hundred years as spring, five hundred years as autumn... Nine as extreme, the refinement is complete."* This final step required lifespan Gu, divine travel Gu, and two third watch Gu.

"Land spirit, what has changed in the blessed land?" Fang Yuan asked.

"Two forces have arrived with dozens of rank three Gu Masters, each led by a rank four; their momentum is formidable." The land spirit projected the scenes.

"Che clan and Zuo clan... *tsk*, both clan leaders are leading, and most elders have come." Fang Yuan recognized them immediately.

"Besides them, experts like Li Xian, Hu Mei Er, Yi Huo, and Kong Ri Tian will arrive. At the climax, I must focus entirely on refinement, leaving defense to the land spirit, Bai Ning Bing, and Feng Tian Yu. Yet this is only the external danger."

"Accelerating my time flow ninefold with two third watch Gu will nourish Spring Autumn Cicada, drastically increasing pressure on my aperture. That is the internal danger."

"Surrounded by danger, I can only persevere. Only one step remains to reach the summit, and I will bet on this."

October 24th.

Guided by the land spirit, two people stood outside the main hall.

"Master!" Feng Tian Yu knelt before Fang Yuan, offering a Gu worm.

Looking like an ordinary piece of limestone, it was the undefeated hundred battles Gu.

"Subordinate cleared the hundredth round, claiming King Xin's inheritance and recruiting these hairy men."

Beside him stood several hundred hairy men covered in dense fur, waiting quietly.

"Good." Fang Yuan nodded and praised calmly, unsurprised.

Fang Yuan then walked towards Bai Ning Bing.

Bai Ning Bing studied the bronze hall with a flicker of understanding: "So this is the blessed land's center."

She glared at Fang Yuan: "Hmph, you'd better remember your promise."

Fang Yuan smiled: "You can be at ease."

He looked past her at the endless horde of nearly a hundred thousand dog beasts.

"Hold a defensive formation as arranged. No matter how enemies provoke you, never initiate an attack. Remember this at all costs," Fang Yuan warned.

"Fine. It's your arrangement, so success or failure doesn't concern me," Bai Ning Bing replied coldly.

"Hehe, succeed or fail, I'll give you the Yang Gu," Fang Yuan smiled.

"Hmph, you better do what you said."

"Two light pillars vanished—King Xin and King Quan's Inheritances have been taken!" At dawn, commotion erupted among the Gu Masters on San Cha mountain.

"The two inheritances were claimed, so why hasn't anyone else come out?"

"They're trapped inside. The blessed land nears destruction; soon the passage will open wide for everyone to enter and exit at will," a resonant voice declared.

"Lord Xiao Mang!" The righteous path Gu Masters instantly recognized the speaker.

Smiling proudly under everyone's gaze, Xiao Mang announced: "Now I'll use extreme light Gu to open the passage for us!"

With wide eyes, he furiously mobilized his primeval essence and raised his fists high.

Killer move — *Immemorial Light Fist!*

Activating three Gu at once dimmed the sky.

Rays of light coalesced into a mountain-sized fist that struck into the void.

*Bam!*

Piercing the formless membrane, the strike shook the blessed land and opened a massive passage to the outside world.

# Chapter 390: Mo Wu Tian

On San Cha mountain peak, only one light pillar remained.

Xiao Mang had penetrated the blessed land, creating a loophole.

"Hmph, I had to beg to come to San Cha mountain, but arrived late! How could I compete with those ahead of me? Only by breaching the land and stirring chaos can I profit!"

"As for this blessed land, the more people that goes in, the more burden it will have to bear. Hehehe, the heavenly power will get weaker and weaker. So what if you guys obtained the inheritance? I can completely use my Gu worms to seize them! King Bao, King Quan and King Qin Inheritance, they are all mine! If I can't obtain them, then you guys can also stop thinking of them!"

"Alright, that should do." Fang Yuan surveyed his surroundings, nodding in satisfaction.

The bronze main hall was situated atop a hill with gentle surrounding slopes.

For over two hours, Fang Yuan had arranged formations with Bai Ning Bing, briefing her on every contingency.

*BOOM...*

Just then, the world trembled, dislodging dust from the bronze hall's ceiling.

The land spirit called: "Xiao Mang breached the blessed land! Swarms of Gu Masters are rushing in—everything has fallen into chaos!"

Fang Yuan chuckled without any panic.

"Chaos is good. Xiao Mang wants to profit, while I need chaos to buy time." Fang Yuan's dark eyes gleamed eerily.

"Eh? A rank five Gu Master slipped in with the crowd... To fool my senses until he attacked, this young man is remarkable!" the land spirit said suddenly.

Fang Yuan frowned at this unexpected development: "Who is it?"

The image showed a young man with waist-length black hair, deep purple eyes, and a wild, crazed disposition.

"Mo Wu Tian!" Fang Yuan's gaze narrowed in recognition.

This demonic genius possessed an ancient soul path inheritance, far outmatching Fang Yuan in power.

"Mo Wu Tian never came to San Cha mountain in my past life! Has my rebirth already affected someone this strong?"

The land spirit warned: "He is charging straight here!"

"Tie Mu Bai's absence will soon breed suspicion. We must begin immediately! Land spirit, raise the fog. Feng Tian Yu, come assist me!"

The hairy men were stationed outside to guard the main hall, forming the final line of defense.

Watching Fang Yuan and Feng Tian Yu depart, Bai Ning Bing's eyes glinted coldly.

The rising fog spread rapidly, cloaking the main hall and the dog beasts across the hill.

Fang Yuan and Feng Tian Yu sat cross-legged before the bronze cauldron.

"This is the final step, the true crucial moment!" He inhaled deeply, eyes clear as water.

"Let's begin." Fang Yuan tossed the false second aperture Gu into the bronze cauldron.

Burning without fire, the bronze cauldron rapidly consumed the thin layer of immortal essence at its base!

Combusting into blue smoke, it rose gracefully to envelop the false second aperture Gu.

Floating above the cauldron, the false Gu transformed into dazzling yellow light within the blue smoke.

Fang Yuan and Feng Tian Yu focused fully on merging the blue smoke and yellow light.

Eventually, the blue smoke formed floating beads of growing grass, while the yellow light became fluttering flowers drifting down into them.

"It is time!" Fang Yuan drew a dagger and slit his artery, releasing essence blood.

Only through essence blood would the second aperture Gu belong to Fang Yuan rather than becoming ownerless.

Essence blood surged into the smoke; sizzling loudly, the blue smoke and yellow light merged into a surging sea-like red cloud.

The cloud evolved: the blood sea calmed into a field, sprouting a vast expanse of blood-scarlet paddy.

Fang Yuan exhaled turbid air and promptly used a Gu to heal his wound.

"'Three hundred years as spring, five hundred years as autumn'... next is lifespan Gu! Ba Gui!" Fang Yuan shouted.

On standby, the land spirit immediately produced two lifespan Gu at Fang Yuan's call.

One large and one small, the lifespan Gu felt rough like ginseng or tree roots.

Fang Yuan tossed the three-hundred-year lifespan Gu into the cloud.

Swallowing the lifespan Gu, the cloud boiled violently.

It transformed into a long, blue-scaled snake struggling to slip from Fang Yuan's control!

Gritting his teeth, Fang Yuan glared with completely bloodshot eyes!

He poured all his focus and strength into restraining the struggling cloud.

# Chapter 391: Demonic Path Alliance Army

Seconds felt like days until the cloud finally settled.

Only through quick reactions and Feng Tian Yu's assistance had he stabilized the situation.

Digesting the lifespan Gu, the cloud turned lush and blue-green, matching the recipe's description: *"three hundred years as spring."*

"That Gu Immortal must have only theorized the recipe. Next is the five-hundred-year lifespan Gu, but I shouldn't rush."

"He wiped the sweat off his forehead, calling the land spirit: "Ba Gui, show me the image of the poisoned land.

Long Qing Tian's corpse lay on the ground, the surrounding sky and earth dyed deep green.

"Jade sky poison runs too deep; this rotting region will form a loophole even greater than Xiao Mang's."

Though every second counted, Fang Yuan suppressed his anxiety and remained still.

Five minutes later, a boom shook the blessed land and hall.

The land spirit sighed; the blessed land's destruction speed doubled!

"Continue refining Gu!" Fang Yuan tossed the five-hundred-year lifespan Gu into the cloud.

The cloud rumbled again, fighting Fang Yuan's control.

The cloud resisted with fierce force—like a green snake transforming into a flood dragon.

Feng Tian Yu quickly helped, and together they suppressed the cloud's transformation.

Five minutes later, the cloud cleared to show golden wheat ripe for harvest—the "five hundred years as autumn" scene.

As the matured cloud dispersed, it shrank from water-tank size into a new Gu, falling down.

Fang Yuan caught it—resembling a golden peanut shell patterned with scarlet blood traces.

At this stage, the true second aperture Gu gestated within the peanut shell.

Only a powerful external force could eliminate the false and manifest the real, completing the Immortal Gu.

That external force was none other than rank six divine travel Gu!

"Next, we use an Immortal Gu to refine an Immortal Gu!" Fang Yuan prepared for the critical step.

"Strange! Why have we not seen Lord Tie Mu Bai after so long!"

As searchers looked toward the fog, Mo Wu Tian arrived alone.

"There is the aura of a Immortal Gu..."

Through deep purple eyes, Mo Wu Tian peered through the fog, spotting the vast horde of dogs.

Such formidable defenses were impossible to breach alone; he needed external help.

Moving like lightning across a hundred miles, he landed before Hu Mei Er.

Hu Mei Er paled in fright before recognizing him: "Young Master Wu Tian, you've reached rank five!"

She gulped, trying to alleviate this shock as she forced out a smile: "That's great, you have rank five strength, you have the ability to gain great rewards from this situation. These green eggmen have huge numbers, they have built structures and castles, I cannot break through. There is a green egg empress inside; capturing her will bring endless fortune."

"Hehehe." Mo Wu Tian looked at the castle, laughing lightly: "Back then, King Bao, Wang Ba Dan, had eight egg empresses, and produced numerous eggmen. King Xin, Wang Xiao Ya's inheritance has divulge information Gu and undefeated hundred battles Gu. King Quan, Wang Er Gou, had two dog emperors, Ying Ming and Ba Huang. This is the essence of the three kings inheritance, but none of them interests me."

Mo Wu Tian laughed loudly: "The land spirit isn't dead—a deceased Gu Immortal's treasure awaits in the deepest area!"

"What?!" Hu Mei Er was stunned.

"Thick fog and over a hundred thousand dogs guard it. The two of us alone cannot reach the central hall," Mo Wu Tian chuckled deeply.

Hu Mei Er immediately joined Mo Wu Tian to recruit demonic Gu Masters.

Lured by the Gu Immortal's treasure, demonic merchant Li Xian, Fiery Star Bao Tong, Rock Lizard Li Qiang, and others gathered.

Mo Wu Tian returned to the fog leading a demonic army thousands strong.

"They're finally here..." Sitting on the hill, Bai Ning Bing slowly stood.

With the land spirit's help, Bai Ning Bing clearly observed the demonic alliance army.

Mo Wu Tian observed for a while before saying: "Li Qiang, bring three hundred people and attack head-on. After a few dozen steps, you will face the steel armor dogs. Fight as you retreat, move towards the left and engage with the chrysanthemum akita dogs. Defeat them and you will charge towards the south-east direction."

"Hu Mei Er, lead five hundred men left and fight your way north through the hounds."

"Bao Tong, take eight hundred men down the right path southwest. If you cannot break through within forty-five minutes, retreat!"

"Li Xian, hold four hundred men in reserve to receive returning troops."

"Then what about you, Mo Wu Tian?" Li Xian asked, his eyes gleaming meaningfully.

"I will guard the base. Why, do you have a problem?" Mo Wu Tian glanced coolly at Li Xian with purple eyes.

With orders set, the four demonic experts led their troops into the fog.

# Chapter 392: Demonic and Righteous attacking together

"It's coming." Her eyes gleaming nervously, Bai Ning Bing quickly mobilized the dog group.

The motionless dog army moved like a giant millstone, grinding the Gu Masters like beans into paste. Within moments, over half were dead, while others recognized the danger and fled immediately.

"Young master Wu Tian, did you see clearly?" Li Qiang questioned. "I charged in according to your instructions. After a fruitless slaughter, we could only come back!"

Mo Wu Tian looked solemn: "Someone is manipulating these dogs in an ever-changing formation, which is why you failed."

Mo Wu Tian shook his head. "The fog is too thick to identify them, but there is definitely a mastermind. Only human intelligence could react so flawlessly. But no matter, I have some understanding of the enslavement path, from now you, you have to attack like this, to break through their encirclement and obtain the Gu Immortal treasure!"

"Mo Wu Tian, explain this! We risked our lives and lost many men without even glimpsing the treasure. As a rank five Gu Master, put in some effort instead of standing around!" Bao Tong screamed furiously, clutching his wounds.

"An explanation? Hehe, I'll give you one." Mo Wu Tian chuckled sinisterly, his purple eyes glowing.

"You!" Caught off guard, Bao Tong froze, staring at Mo Wu Tian.

Fiery Star Bao Tong, a famous rank four fire path Gu Master, was slain by Mo Wu Tian's mere stare!

"You wanted an explanation, so I gave one. Satisfied? Anyone else unhappy can speak up." Mo Wu Tian looked at the corpse, waiting for an answer.

Having asserted his dominance, Mo Wu Tian sneered: "Bao Tong's Gu remain. Divide them among yourselves as compensation from me."

Li Xian reacted first, darting like a rabbit onto Bao Tong's corpse to snatch his Gu.

This killer move pitted souls directly; the loser's soul dispersed instantly, leaving no chance to detonate their Gu.

"*Cough, cough.*" Li Xian coughed. "Guarded by beasts, that must be the central control hall holding the Gu Immortal's fortune. But how do we breach these dogs?"

Mo Wu Tian sighed: "Spread the news to everyone, righteous and demonic alike.., say that Tie Mu Bai and the others entered the hall, obtained the inheritance, and are blocking everyone outside to hoard the treasure."

"Finally stopped." On the slope, Bai Ning Bing breathed a sigh of relief, yet frowned.

Fang Yuan's counter-tactics were already half-spent; a few more waves would push her to her limit.

Elsewhere, Xiao Mang abandoned King Bao's inheritance upon hearing news of the fog. Hearing a subordinate's report, Xiao Mang grabbed him by the collar: "A Gu Immortal treasure is hidden in the fog, and people are attacking it right now?!"

"Let's go!" Xiao Mang quickly led his subordinates away.

"Their numbers are growing." Bai Ning Bing frowned as nearly ten thousand people gathered outside the fog.

"The righteous path is organizing to charge," Hu Mei Er urged. "Young master Wu Tian, won't you lead the demonic Gu Masters?"

"No matter. With such thick fog and steel armor dogs forming walls, what can their vanguard do?" Mo Wu Tian sneered, refusing to act.

Inside the hall, the Gu refinement had reached a crucial stage.

After half a day, the golden peanut shell collided with divine travel Gu.

*"Divine opportunity is unlimited, travel through the lands! Activateeeeeeeeeee!"* Fang Yuan screamed, using all his energy to activate divine travel Gu.

Channeled by the land spirit's immortal essence, divine travel Gu dissolved into light inside the golden peanut shell.

The qualitative change had begun!

# Chapter 393: Group Attack

Commanding the righteous path, Xiao Mang launched repeated assaults that met disastrous failure and heavy losses.

"Prepare the next wave," Xiao Mang ordered, but this time, few stepped forward.

"Where is Wu clan's Wu Shen Tong? With him, we could breach the fog and reach the central hall!" Xiao Mang asked.

Xiao Mang frowned: "Wu Shen Tong, Wu Gui, and Zhang San San are missing. Is one of them controlling these dogs?"

"It seems I must act." He snorted, brilliant light gleaming in his eyes. "Fall back, everyone."

Xiao Mang mobilized purple crystal primeval essence from his surging primeval sea.

Killer move — *My Glorious Spear!!*

He raised a finger towards the fog.

Blinding white light coalesced into a spear 1.8 meters long and 0.6 meters thick.

The devastating light spear pierced the fog, striking the mound at breakneck speed.

A fierce explosion occurred.

Inside the hall, Fang Yuan and Feng Tian Yu were disrupted mid-refinement, both coughing up blood.

As the blinding glare faded, Bai Ning Bing saw thousands of dogs vaporized where the spear struck, turning the mound into a crater.

Under Bai Ning Bing's command, countless dogs rushed to fill the crater, reestablishing the defensive line.

With the fog blown away, everyone could now see clearly.

Over ninety thousand dogs blanketed the mound, forming an iron defense.

"Lord Xiao Mang, let us cooperate." Shifting like a black smoke phantom, Mo Wu Tian appeared before Xiao Mang.

"Mo Wu Tian immediately understood Xiao Mang's intentions. He was extremely disdainful towards such hypocrisy, but he said: "Then in fifteen minutes, we will attack from the front and back together.

Returning to his faction, Mo Wu Tian sneered: "We'll attack simultaneously from both sides to see who reaches the hall first!"

Meanwhile, Xiao Mang announced: "The demonic scum strike in fifteen minutes. We will attack once they draw the dogs' attention!"

"Kill!" Fifteen minutes later, the attack began.

Righteous and demonic were far away, attacking from two directions.

Hidden away, Bai Ning Bing used the land spirit's divine sense to monitor the battlefield.

Amidst the chaotic battle, Yan Jun advanced steadily, like a leisurely traveler.

When a ferocious dog king pounced, Yan Jun turned into a hollow shadow, letting the beast pass harmlessly through him.

As Gu Masters unleashed their full might against the dogs, the sheer chaos pushed Bai Ning Bing to her limits, leaving her flustered.

# Chapter 394: Success at long last!

Hidden away, Bai Ning Bing exerted all her strength to direct the dogs, gradually stabilizing the situation with the land spirit's help.

The five mountain dog breeds held a firm circular formation, acting like a dam against the surging Gu Masters.

Amidst the slaughter, Tie Ruo Nan and the four old-timers advanced toward the bronze hall. Tie Ruo Nan nodded firmly, eyes steely: "Right, our priority is still Little Beast King Fang Zheng!"

Mo Wu Tian sought Bai Ning Bing's position—killing her would instantly shatter the dog formation and scatter the pack.

"Found you!" Mo Wu Tian's eyes locked onto Bai Ning Bing's hiding spot.

The demonic fog sped across the battlefield, closing within a thousand paces of Bai Ning Bing in mere breaths.

"I have trump cards too!" Startled but composed, Bai Ning Bing willed: "Bite him, Ba Huang."

The hill-sized dog emperor leaped over a hundred feet, crashing directly into Mo Wu Tian.

Just as Bai Ning Bing exhaled in relief, a dazzling silver light shot across the battlefield, appearing right before her.

"Hehehe. Demonic junior, look closely." The silver light materialized into rank five righteous expert Xiao Mang!

"Ying Ming!" Enduring the immense pressure, Bai Ning Bing mentally dispatched the second dog emperor.

"A dog emperor!" Xiao Mang scoffed, but after several fierce exchanges, he was forced to fight cautiously.

Inside the bronze hall, Fang Yuan gasped heavily for air, while Feng Tian Yu lay unconscious on the ground.

"Success!" Fang Yuan was overjoyed—he had successfully refined divine travel Gu.

Only one final step remained to obtain second aperture Gu!

"Refining divine travel Gu is incredible! Entrusting this to you was the right choice—the hardest step is behind us! Next, you need to use third watch Gu and it will be a success without the least bit of difficulty."

With those final words, the land spirit Ba Gui faded away until it vanished completely.

Ba Gui's death meant immortal essence was no longer accessible.

For the final step, vast quantities of primeval stones could replace immortal essence.

*Using third watch Gu next will be simpler. But how is the battle outside?* Calming his mind, Fang Yuan heard shouting and commotion outside the hall.

"Fang Yuan, careful! A rank four Gu Master breached the formation and is heading your way!" Bai Ning Bing warned.

"Hmm." With a grim expression, Fang Yuan slowly stood up.

# Chapter 395: Bai Ning Bing?

With the land spirit dead, Fang Yuan was blind to the battle outside, yet his past life's memories allowed him to deduce what was happening.

"The only rank five experts, Xiao Mang and Mo Wu Tian, will be delayed by the two dog emperors. Charging through the dog formation would be simple for him."

"Get up!" Fang Yuan said in a cold voice.

"Mas... master..." Feng Tian Yu woke up pale and swaying, bowing weakly to Fang Yuan.

"Go outside and block the rank four phantom path Gu Master, even at the cost of your life," Fang Yuan ordered ruthlessly.

"Subordinate will do his utmost!" Feng Tian Yu bit his lip and stepped out.

"Second aperture Gu is the priority; sacrificing a refinement path grandmaster is nothing." Fang Yuan sat cross-legged, watching the shifting radiance overhead.

Nearly complete, second aperture Gu oscillated between real and illusory, form and formless; it was immovable and could only persist for six hours.

"Third watch Gu, go." Composing himself, he focused entirely on beginning the final step.

As the first third watch Gu wound down, the orb of light solidified into the Gu's final shape.

"Now, the second third watch Gu..." Seeing victory in sight, even Fang Yuan grew excited.

Bai Ning Bing warned: "Fang Yuan, careful! Tie Ruo Nan broke the line and is heading for you!"

Locked into the refinement, Fang Yuan could not disengage, leaving him entirely defenseless.

"Bai Ning Bing, what are you doing?! Guard me immediately! I cannot move—if I die, poison vow Gu will kill you too!" Fang Yuan transmitted furiously.

The hall's great door was pushed open violently.

Tie Ruo Nan stepped inside, dazed for an instant before crying out in fierce delight: "Little Beast King, today I take your head!"

Attacking before her words even settled, she waved her hand, unleashing countless golden needles.

A blast of icy wind formed a wall, blocking every golden needle.

The next moment, Bai Ning Bing appeared at the hall entrance.

Battered and soaked in blood, a deep gash from shoulder to sacrum exposed her bone.

"Quit talking nonsense and focus on refining the Gu!" Bai Ning Bing gritted her teeth and cursed before engaging Tie Ruo Nan.

Several moves later, Bai Ning Bing gasped: "I can't hold on! Fang Yuan, I'm going to self-destruct!"

"Your aptitude has already returned to a hundred percent?" Fang Yuan was startled.

"Hold on unless you can't control it!" Fang Yuan shouted, unable to turn away from the refinement.

"It's too late."

The next moment, a freezing aura permeated the hall and the temperature dropped rapidly.

Fang Yuan looked back to see the hall freezing into ice and snow as Bai Ning Bing floated midair, her body crystallizing just as it had on Qing Mao Mountain.

"The legendary Northern Dark Ice Soul physique?" Tie Ruo Nan cried in shock, but was too late—ice sealed her like an insect in amber.

The frost showed no sign of stopping and spread toward Fang Yuan.

"Damn it!" Anxious as the ice reached his body, Fang Yuan had no choice but to activate Yang Gu.

Yang Gu landed on Bai Ning Bing, instantly merging yang aura and icy chill into a whirlpool of primeval essence—recreating the scene from Qing Mao Mountain years ago!

His heart thumping in shock, Fang Yuan focused all his attention on refinement, ignoring everything behind him.

Under his control, the glowing clouds finally condensed into Second Aperture Gu!

"Immortal Gu! I finally succeeded!" Fang Yuan's heart surged with joy.

Just then, a sharp ice blade thrust through his back and pierced out from his chest.

Fang Yuan's pupils shrunk to needle-size and he looked behind with difficulty —

"Bai Ning Bing? You!"

"Fang Yuan, to think you'd fall into a trap one day!" Tie Ruo Nan walked over slowly, eyes full of hatred.

# Chapter 396: Bai Ning Bing!

Fang Yuan sat cross-legged on the ground, the rank six Second Aperture Gu floating before him.

Bai Ning Bing, having regained his male body, stood expressionlessly behind him holding an ice blade.

Tie Ruo Nan walked over, staring at Fang Yuan with mixed grief and joy: "Fang Yuan, you never imagined this day would come, did you?"

"You never expected to be defeated by me," Bai Ning Bing smiled faintly. "Ever since Qing Mao Mountain, I've been plotting how to regain my male body."

"You willingly handed over the Gu once on Qing Mao Mountain. Reenacting that was the key—the moment of my self-destruction would be when you used Yang Gu."

"With that premise, I plotted in secret." Bai Ning Bing smiled faintly. "I couldn't truly self-destruct in case you withheld Yang Gu. So, I chose..."

"Ice crystal Gu." Fang Yuan's expression was dim as water.

"I chose Ice Crystal Gu because Snow Fairy Gu transformed my appearance too distinctly to hide, and combining it with Ice Explosion Gu mimicked the Northern Dark Ice Soul physique's self-destruction to fool you completely."

"Hmph, if I was not concentrating on refining Gu, I would definitely discover the peculiarity, how could you have succeeded so easily then?" Fang Yuan scoffed.

Bai Ning Bing nodded solemnly: "Indeed. You are meticulous to a fault. I knew there was a high risk of failure, and unforeseen circumstances almost made me abandon the plan entirely."

"The clever loophole in your poison vow with Shang Ya Zi seemed overly confident. And though I investigated the Bai clan contract, leaks still occurred, making me doubtful. I know you always prepare for worst-case scenarios. So I couldn't help but wonder—what if you weren't bound by Poison Vow Gu at all? What would I do then?"

Fang Yuan coughed up a mouthful of blood. Though the ice blade pierced his heart, healing Gu kept him barely alive.

Ignoring the injury, he rasped: "The person who helped you—was it Doctor Su Shou?"

"That wrench!" Fang Yuan cursed, realizing something. "Wait, you didn't just meet once—there was a second time!"

Bai Ning Bing smiled, blue eyes gleaming eerily. "You guessed it. I deliberately lost to Yan Tu. Losing a vital Gu was a small price to clear your suspicion. I took the gamble."

Doctor Su Shou's method was to intentionally trigger the lethal poison vow, purge the poison upon death, and revive him.

Freed from the vow, Bai Ning Bing contacted Tie clan and revealed the truth to Tie Ruo Nan.

"Fang Yuan, surrender obediently. Outside, our enslavement path grandmaster Tie Bai Qi is holding off the experts with the dog beasts. Outside this hall, the Tie clan four old-timers are using Iron Cabinet Gu and Qi Flow Gu to seal the space. Surrender is your only way out."

Tie Ruo Nan held Second Aperture Gu in her hand, pronouncing Fang Yuan's fate.

Fang Yuan was silent.

# Chapter 397: The battle has just begun

"There are truly countless geniuses in this world..." After a solemn pause, Fang Yuan looked up and sighed.

"Bai Ning Bing, what a Bai Ning Bing... I underestimated you. Your plan was flawless, and my fixation on the Immortal Gu made me careless!"

"Hehehe," Fang Yuan sneered. "You haven't killed me because you want to know the Immortal Gu's effects and its recipe in my mind."

With the land spirit dead and the blessed land collapsing, loopholes abounded; without heavenly power suppressing them, Gu Masters could freely use their Gu.

With a single thought, Fang Yuan could self-detonate all his Gu, and neither Bai Ning Bing nor Tie Ruo Nan could stop him.

"Tie Ruo Nan, since Tie Mu Bai and the others died by my hands, you need this Immortal Gu to redeem yourself, or lose your young master status," Fang Yuan laughed sinisterly.

Tie Ruo Nan replied: "Bringing back an Immortal Gu will earn me immense rewards from Tie clan's Gu Immortals. And if I obtain its refinement recipe from you and present it to the clan, I will immediately become Tie clan's young clan leader."

Taking a deep breath, she stared at him: "As for you, Gu Yue Fang Yuan, I won't kill you. Refining an Immortal Gu with a mortal body shows spectacular talent. Surrender, and I will place you in Tie clan's Demon Suppression Tower for reformation. Once reformed, you can leave the tower to serve Tie clan and the righteous path, atoning for your sins. That is your best outcome."

"Demon Suppression Tower..." Fang Yuan squinted. "How noble! You want the Immortal Gu? Fine, let's negotiate."

Fang Yuan discussed terms while secretly monitoring the commotion outside the hall.

Spring Autumn Cicada was his best chance, but the risk of death was immense. Fang Yuan refused to use it unless left with no alternative.

"My true opportunity lies in the experts waiting outside the hall. With just the dog group and Tie clan's four old-timers, they cannot block them all forever. As long as they fight their way in here, the situation would be completely overturned."

A purple light barrier enveloped the bronze hall, securely guarded from four directions by Tie clan's four old-timers.

At the entrance, Tie Bai Qi looked down upon the Gu Masters as he directed the dog pack.

White-haired with a gleaming third eye on his forehead, he was a rank five enslavement path elder and former general under Tie Mu Bai.

"Damn it, Tie clan is taking the immortal treasure!" Xiao Mang fumed anxiously, but Dog Emperor Ying Ming barred his way.

"Bastard, you forced my hand!" Xiao Mang growled, flying skyward.

The sky bore a massive hole tens of kilometers wide, revealing the bright, cloud-dotted daylight of the outside world.

Xiao Mang flew out of the hole, raising his hands.

Sunlight gathered in his palms into an immense orb of dazzling light.

Immemorial radiance infused the orb, causing a qualitative transformation imbued with ancient glory!

The orb exploded into a torrent of light, each droplet dazzling like a fragment of the sun.

Killer move — Torrential Light Stream!

The luminous river surged through the rift, crashing down upon the blessed land.

"Oh no!" Tie Bai Qi tried to intercept it, but the fully formed light river could only be slightly mitigated.

The cascade of light slammed into the purple barrier like a waterfall.

The barrier shattered almost instantly, and the four old-timers maintaining it coughed blood, severely injured.

Though weakened after breaking the barrier, the light river plunged straight into the bronze hall.

The hall ruptured, and the waterfall swept toward Fang, Bai, and Tie.

Bai Ning Bing and Tie Ruo Nan's pupils shrank as they instinctively dodged.

Seizing his chance, Fang Yuan reacted immediately and mobilized his primeval essence.

Warp Gu!

With a swoosh, Fang Yuan vanished on the spot.

"We're letting him escape?" Bai Ning Bing looked at Tie Ruo Nan.

"With Fixed Star Gu, our four old-timers can track him anywhere. Letting him waste his energy running saves us trouble."

"Hold onto Second Aperture Gu for now; I will repay this a millionfold!" Fang Yuan surveyed his surroundings, realizing he was only a short distance outside the bronze hall.

Swarmed by attacking dog beasts, he quickly retreated.

Though Tie Ruo Nan held Second Aperture Gu, Fang Yuan retained control—having refined it himself, a single thought could detonate it.

Carrying the Immortal Gu outside his aperture would leak its aura, making him the immediate target of every expert outside.

"With the Immortal Gu in hand, Tie Ruo Nan will draw all attacks while I recover and wait for my chance!"

Heaven was not on his side, however; several Gu Masters quickly spotted him and shouted.

Surrounding Gu Masters immediately attacked him.

The first to attack, however, was a pack of heavy tai dogs.

Besieged by righteous and demonic cultivators alike, Fang Yuan was also specially targeted by Tie Bai Qi.

# Chapter 398: So close to success, yet it was all for naught

"God damn it!" Fang Yuan cursed, waving his hand and summoning a golden dragon.

The commotion drew Mo Wu Tian and Xiao Mang's attention.

Xiao Mang hurled an elephant-sized hand of light to seize Fang Yuan.

"Mo Wu Tian's Gentle Feelings Gu!" Fang Yuan broke free, but the light hand caught up.

The light hand exploded, sending Fang Yuan hurtling through the air.

Falling rapidly, he yelled: "Tie Ruo Nan took a peerless Immortal Gu and is refining it now!"

Mo Wu Tian and Xiao Mang's attention were instantly diverted.

Catching his breath, Fang Yuan activated Wu Gui's movement Gu, cloaking himself in dark clouds to escape.

Four serpentine chains materialized from midair, binding his limbs.

The next moment, Fang Yuan reappeared in the bronze hall.

Tie clan's four old-timers stood in four directions, surrounding him.

At this moment, in central continent, Tian Ti mountain.

In Hu Immortal blessed land, the competition for ownership reached its final moments.

"Fang Zheng, hold on, victory is near!" Lord Sky Crane urged from within him, voice weak and weary.

"Just one opponent left..." Fang Zheng gritted his teeth, swaying and drenched in sweat, sustained only by sheer will.

He climbed step by step, surpassing Feng Jin Huang and taking the lead!

He was now less than a meter from the mountain peak.

Feng Jin Huang shouted, and a beautiful pair of wings grew from her shoulders.

"The legendary Immortal Gu — Dream Wings!"

Dream Wings existed only in dreams, consuming the Gu Master's spirit and soul rather than immortal essence.

As a mortal, forcefully using Dream Wings would severely damage her soul.

"Whatever the cost, victory is mine!" Feng Jin Huang soared past a shocked Fang Zheng.

Folding her wings, Feng Jin Huang landed at the cliff edge, gasping for breath.

"I actually lost!" Fang Zheng stared wide-eyed in dejection.

The sole victor of this contest, the new owner of Hu Immortal blessed land!

Southern border, San Cha mountain, bronze hall.

"Hehehe, Fang Yuan, we meet again." Tie Ruo Nan laughed heartily before him.

Bai Ning Bing sighed: "It's futile, Fang Yuan. I secretly planted Fixed Star Gu in your left arm. With it tracking you, Infinite Search and Lock can drag you back from anywhere. You've lost."

"Bai Ning Bing!" Fang Yuan roared, struggling frantically as the chains clanked loudly.

"I planted Fixed Star Gu while you nurtured my aperture with yellow gold primeval essence. You had no clue, did you?" Bai Ning Bing mocked coldly.

"Fixed Star Gu... well done, Bai Ning Bing, well done!" Fang Yuan gritted his teeth, glaring at him.

Tie Ruo Nan urged: "Fang Yuan, Infinite Search and Lock has sealed your Gu worms and primeval essence. You have no hope. You could detonate your Gu worms, but as a smart person, you know what serves your interests best."

"It's over, Fang Yuan. You will spend the rest of your life in the Demon Suppression Tower." Bai Ning Bing sighed, watching his captured rival with mixed feelings of joy and sorrow.

"Thank you for the excitement you brought to my life all these years. You relieved my loneliness and boredom. My life ahead will be even more thrilling, and you should feel honored to have been part of it." Bai Ning Bing bowed sincerely to Fang Yuan.

# Chapter 399: Spring Autumn Cicada

Fang Yuan looked up at the sky beyond the bronze hall.

"5:45pm, at this time, Feng Jin Huang should've just inherited Hu Immortal blessed land?" Fang Yuan thought.

In his previous life, he had joined demonic Gu Immortals to conquer Hu Immortal blessed land, killing Feng Jin Huang.

"With Dream Wings, the inheritance was destined to be hers. I needed Second Aperture Gu to counter that!"

He sighed inwardly, but his dazed expression quickly turned resolute.

He had a chance to reverse the situation, he had not lost yet!

That is because, he had Spring Autumn Cicada.

Infinite Search and Lock could seal rank five Gu, but not a rank six Immortal Gu!

Fang Yuan looked up at Bai Ning Bing one last time. Failure meant the end.

Fang Yuan self-detonated!

Spring Autumn Cicada burst with green and orange light as a mystical, profound aura spread.

All of Fang Yuan's Gu worms, primeval essence, flesh, and soul detonated.

Spring Autumn Cicada became a brilliant speck carrying Fang Yuan's remaining will, tearing through space into the world's most famous forbidden ground—the river of time.

This was Fang Yuan's third time using Spring Autumn Cicada.

As dangerous waves crashed, Spring Autumn Cicada exhausted its energy, struggling into a droplet.

Fang Yuan blinked, and the image before him had changed!

"Hold on, we can negotiate! I will reveal the true path if you guarantee my safety. I have a poison vow Gu..."

Hearing a familiar voice begging for life, Fang Yuan looked down to see Wang Xiao.

Stunned, his heart pounded as his expression shifted into unconcealed joy.

"Hahaha, I succeeded again! I won the bet—I've rebirthed!" Fang Yuan spread his arms, laughing loudly.

He first inspected his aperture.

Inside his aperture, vital Gu Spring Autumn Cicada was extremely weak again, its lustrous body withered like an autumn leaf.

Spring Autumn Cicada concealed itself in deep sleep, regaining strength from the river of time.

"This alleviates Spring Autumn Cicada's threat!" Fang Yuan laughed even louder, his eyes shining brightly.

This was still the blessed land; beneath his feet lay Wang Xiao, beside the corpse of Yun clan's young leader Yun Luo Tian.

Thinking of Bai Ning Bing, Fang Yuan's laughter abruptly stopped.

That traitor had schemed for years to betray him right after refining second aperture Gu.

"I was too careless—focused on Feng Jin Huang and refining an Immortal Gu, I ignored the true demon lurking beside me! Yet Bai Ning Bing schemed for years to numb my vigilance before striking the fatal blow. Heh, I am only mortal, not an invincible god."

"Everyone has advantages—Northern Dark Ice Soul physique, clan backing, Immortal Gu from dreams. Meanwhile, I struggled for centuries cultivating Spring Autumn Cicada."

"Emotions surging at the thought, Fang Yuan smiled brilliantly and recited: "

*Viewing thousands of mountains in deep crimson*

*Layers of trees dyed red*

*On the limpid blue water*

*Hundreds of barges race against currents*

*Hawks spread their wings in the vast sky*

*Fish swim freely under deep water*

*All living things vie for freedom under the autumn sky*

*Pondering over the immensity of the universe*

*I ask this boundless and misty earth*

"*Who rules the fate of this world?"*

The Great Dao is impartial. Every being has the chance to rise—it depends on seizing opportunities and fighting!

With that thought, Fang Yuan's bitterness, hatred, shock, grief, and joy vanished like smoke.

His heart cleared, demonic resolution gleaming in his eyes.

He started to think calmly.

"Next, I spent great effort exposing Spring Autumn Cicada to convince the land spirit, then killed killer ghost doctor Chou Jiu, Wu Shen Tong, and Zhang San San. I unexpectedly obtained a slavery Gu from Zhang San San, but before I could kill more, an accident forced me to kill Long Qing Tian. Due to the jade sky Gu's poison, I gained nothing."

# Chapter 400: Life is but a few hundred years, it is all a grand gamble!

"Fixed star Gu is a noose around my neck. Removing it normally requires extensive time. To remove fixed star Gu immediately, unless Bai Ning Bing takes it back, I must chop off my left forearm..." Fang Yuan burned with this urge.

Losing his left arm would alert Tie clan and Bai Ning Bing that Fang Yuan knew the truth.

"I cannot alert them prematurely. My sole advantage is foresight—Tie clan and Bai Ning Bing are still useful to me. If events derail from my past life, I will squander my rebirth advantage completely."

"Wait!" Suddenly, Fang Yuan's body shook.

"Maybe... *take a step back, and the sea and sky widen*? Should I consider a broader perspective?" An absurd thought appeared in his head.

"If I succeed, my gains will be overwhelming!" Fang Yuan's eyes shone brilliantly.

He laughed softly: "To think I have grown so timid. As long as there is hope, I must act bold. Only immense benefit drives me relentlessly forward. Fortune accompanies risk—why build a foundation slowly when I can ascend to heaven in one step!"

"Death if I fail, soaring like a dragon if I succeed. Life is but a few hundred years—a grand gamble!"

"Little Beast King, don't be rash! You want the path into Wu mountain, we can negotiate," Wang Xiao begged.

"Oh? You plan to tell me Wu mountain's entrance?" Fang Yuan frowned, looking at Wang Xiao.

"Hmph!" Fang Yuan's gaze sharpened; pressing down with a crack, he smashed Wang Xiao's head to bits.

Taking Wang Xiao's Gu, Fang Yuan used beast strength placenta Gu to devour his and Yun Luo Tian's apertures.

"Beast strength placenta Gu reaches eighty-three percent aptitude. Ba Gui, do you want me to stop?" Fang Yuan asked.

Land spirit: "Eh? How did you know my thought?"

"Of course I know." Fang Yuan snorted, releasing Spring Autumn Cicada's aura: "Ba Gui, feel this—what do you think it is?"

The land spirit was speechless!

"This is the aura of rank six Spring Autumn Cicada! How can a mortal possess such an Immortal Gu?"

Fang Yuan went straight to the point: "Ba Gui, I am a future Gu Immortal who rebirthed into the past."

"What?!" The land spirit was shocked beyond words, overwhelmed by the revelation.

Fang Yuan spoke arrogantly: "I returned from the future and know everything. Ba Gui, I am your next master—I succeeded in refining second aperture Gu in my previous life..."

"If you really refined second aperture Gu, its success rate has risen by thirty percent!" the land spirit said happily.

"Cut the chatter, Ba Gui. Coming from the future, I know the lurking dangers. First, I need to kill someone!"

A moment later, Long Qing Tian died by Fang Yuan's hands.

In his past life, Long Qing Tian entered a weakened area and used jade sky Gu to poison the blessed land, opening a loophole that caused immense damage and endless trouble for Fang Yuan.

This time, Fang Yuan used heavenly power to suppress Long Qing Tian early, killing him easily.

"Young man, you eliminated a grave threat early. Possessing Spring Autumn Cicada, you truly came from the future..." the land spirit exclaimed.

Wasting no time, Fang Yuan swiftly seized Long Qing Tian's Gu worms and devoured his aperture.

"Bai Gui, quickly transfer me to this place."

The next moment, Fang Yuan appeared before demonic enslavement path master Zhang San San.

Before Zhang San San could react, Fang Yuan attacked and ended his life.

Zhang San San was a rank four Gu Master possessing a rank five slavery Gu—Fang Yuan's primary motive for killing him.

With another flash, Fang Yuan appeared before killer ghost doctor Chou Jiu.

"Eh? Little Beast King! How did you..." Chou Jiu gasped in shock upon seeing Fang Yuan.

With an impassive expression, Fang Yuan activated the rank five slavery Gu.

The slavery Gu burst into yellow light, landing upon Chou Jiu.

Chou Jiu shouted: "F*ck! Rank five slavery Gu! *You can kill me but not humiliate me*—you dream of enslaving me..."

Chou Jiu sneered, sweating profusely: "Little Beast King, enslavement path depends on soul foundation. Controlling men is far harder than beasts. Man is the spirit of all beings—controlling someone a rank higher is pure delusion, hehe... ahhh!"

Chou Jiu screamed in pain, his smug laughter cut short by Fang Yuan's slap.

This disruption tilted the soul contest as yellow light sank into Chou Jiu's body.

Once fully merged, Chou Jiu would become Fang Yuan's most loyal slave!

# Chapter 401: Battle begins once more

Shocked, Chou Jiu mobilized his soul to resist the yellow light.

Fang Yuan beat Chou Jiu across the floor, commanding his strength path Gu worms.

Chou Jiu's distraction allowed the yellow light to fuse deeper into his body.

Only a sliver of light remained near his forehead, unable to fuse.

Fang Yuan laughed coldly: "Zeng Ah Niu, don't you want revenge? Chen Jiu betrayed you and is living well with Shang Yan Fei."

"How do you know?!" Caught by surprise, Chou Jiu lost focus as the light merged into his body.

Chou Jiu's expression instantly changed, kneeling before Fang Yuan.

"This subordinate greets master!"

Enslaving Chou Jiu yielded the clue to the door of life and death, alongside immense influence across both factions.

The only flaw was the heavy burden placed on Fang Yuan's soul.

"Chou Jiu, did you use poison vow Gu with Doctor Su Shou to swear eternal love?" Fang Yuan asked.

"That is true, but Master, how did you know?" Chou Jiu answered, bewildered.

"Hmph, since you used poison vow Gu, do you know how Doctor Su Shou lifted its restrictions?" Fang Yuan narrowed his eyes.

"Chen Jiu couldn't negate it directly. For Shang Yan Fei, she let the poison vow kill her, then resurrected herself with healing techniques to break free!"

Hearing this, Fang Yuan grew furious and kicked Chou Jiu away.

Fang Yuan had always wondered how Bai Ning Bing broke the poison vow—Doctor Su Shou had freed her.

"Let the past be past. Chou Jiu, go to King Xin Inheritance and retrieve undefeated hundred battles Gu for me," Fang Yuan instructed.

"This subordinate will do all that he can!" Chou Jiu answered.

Days passed and immortal essence expenditure remained far lower. Drawing on past experience, Fang Yuan completed steps on his first try.

Finally, October 24th came again.

"Master, this subordinate has succeeded!" Chou Jiu knelt, presenting the Gu to Fang Yuan.

Appearing plainly like a grey stone disc, it was none other than undefeated hundred battles Gu.

Stowing undefeated hundred battles Gu, Fang Yuan gave instructions and sent Chou Jiu away in secret, completely unnoticed by Bai Ning Bing.

Fang Yuan returned to the bronze hall, and Bai Ning Bing had already arrived.

Behind her, a hundred thousand dog beasts rested or milled about.

Fang Yuan smiled lightly: "Bai Ning Bing, hand your Gu worms to the land spirit."

"What?" Bai Ning Bing was shocked: "Aren't I commanding these dogs?"

"The land spirit's manipulation is better than yours," Fang Yuan explained.

The land spirit worried: "Managing immortal essence while refining Gu takes focus—I may struggle to control the dogs too."

Fang Yuan chuckled with confidence: "No worries, I have pawns among the enemy. There are ample primeval stones here for you to manipulate the dogs."

"Is that so..." Ba Gui hesitated, but recalling Fang Yuan's secret identity, he ultimately agreed.

"As for you, Bai Ning Bing, come with me. I need someone trustworthy to protect me during refinement." Fang Yuan patted her shoulder with an affectionate smile.

Bai Ning Bing snorted: "As you wish. So long as you live, whether refinement succeeds is not my concern."

Fang Yuan and Bai Ning Bing entered the hall together.

Fang Yuan sat down while Bai Ning Bing stood guard.

"Ning Bing, stay calm—some things cannot be rushed," Fang Yuan said placidly, completely devoid of anxiety.

# Chapter 402: All is within my control

Killer move — Immemorial Light Fist!!

Standing atop San Cha mountain, Xiao Mang unleashed his killer move, dimming the sky.

Light rays formed a hill-sized fist, punching through the blessed land's barrier.

The blessed land shook as an enormous breach opened a passage for Gu Masters to enter.

"Charge, everything in the blessed land is ours!"

Amidst pure chaos, cheering crowds surged into the blessed land, drastically increasing the pressure upon it.

"Hehehe, a bunch of idiots!" Xiao Mang sneered inwardly and stepped into the blessed land.

The bronze hall also recovered from the tremors.

The land spirit transmitted: "So you were waiting for the tremor to pass! Had it hit during refinement, the consequences would be unimaginable."

Fang Yuan smiled: "I am your future master. Ba Gui, understand that I am the best candidate to refine the Immortal Gu. We must avoid the next two tremors as much as possible. Alright, let's begin the Gu refinement!"

Fang Yuan threw the false second aperture Gu into the cauldron.

With the land spirit's cooperation, the bronze cauldron burned without fire as the remaining immortal essence combusted!

Combusting immortal essence formed blue smoke that rose gracefully, enveloping the false second aperture Gu.

Floating above the bronze cauldron, the false Gu transformed into dazzling yellow light under the blue smoke.

Fang Yuan concentrated on harmonizing the blue smoke and yellow light.

Mo Wu Tian arrived alone at the edge of the dense fog.

Killer Ghost Doctor Chou Jiu had leaked the news and was leading a massive demonic crowd toward the hall.

"With little brother Mo Wu Tian's help, these mere dogs are nothing to worry about!" Chou Jiu welcomed him enthusiastically.

The massive demonic crowd looked formidable, yet they lingered rather than charging toward the main hall.

"Sir Chou Jiu, time waits for no one! Why aren't we charging the hall?" Mo Wu Tian urged, brows knotted.

Chou Jiu chuckled: "There is strength in numbers. Gathering more people will reduce the danger when we finally charge."

Chou Jiu pushed Mo Wu Tian to organize the assault team, insisting that only volunteers could go.

The demonic crowd readily agreed, advancing along the three paths per Mo Wu Tian's plan.

"Not good, someone is attacking from outside!" the land spirit suddenly warned during the refinement.

"No need to worry. Command the battle outside while I stabilize the refinement," Fang Yuan said calmly.

Following Fang Yuan's instructions, the land spirit commanded the dog beasts, repelling the demonic crowd.

Unwilling to concede, Mo Wu Tian formed a second assault team, but they suffered heavy losses and retreated in defeat.

Chou Jiu stepped forward: "Everyone knows my rule: one life saved for one taken. I will treat you all first."

Moved, even more demonic Gu Masters abandoned Mo Wu Tian to side with Chou Jiu.

"Let's wait for the righteous path to gather. If we breach the formation only for them to reap the benefits, that would be disastrous."

Mo Wu Tian gritted his teeth audibly, wishing he could slaughter the old bastard.

Time dragged on until the righteous Gu Masters gathered under Xiao Mang's leadership.

After several failed assaults, Xiao Mang unleashed his killer move once more.

A 1.8m long and 0.6m thick spear of light landed on the mound.

A fierce explosion blasted shockwaves across the area. As the dazzling light faded, the fog shrouding the mound dispersed.

The bronze hall and the majestic dog beast army were revealed in full view.

The immortal treasure lay before both factions, yet breaching such a massive dog formation seemed impossible.

"The second tremor has passed, delaying their attack. Let's resume the refinement!" Fang Yuan called the land spirit.

While the two factions remained in a stalemate, Fang Yuan resumed the refinement.

By now, the blue smoke and yellow light had completely merged.

The blue smoke transformed into grass beads, while yellow light became fluttering flowers.

Fang Yuan drew a dagger and slit an artery, releasing his essence blood.

Infused with essence blood, the smoke and light sizzled and transformed into a surging red cloud.

After evolving, the sphere of smoke settled into a field lush with red wheat.

*"Wild grass grows wildly, blood qi like the sea. Three hundred years as spring, five hundred years as autumn..."* He drew a lifespan Gu.

Fang Yuan tossed it into the blood field's smoke, which instantly roiled like boiling water.

Prepared this time, Fang Yuan easily took control and gradually calmed the cloud of smoke.

The land spirit warned: "Not good, the Gu Masters are attacking together! We might run out of time!"

"No need to worry, everything is under control," Fang Yuan sneered, retrieving a second lifespan Gu.

# Chapter 403: Bai Ning Bing, have you acted enough?

Only ten minutes into the assault, the mound was already strewn with blood and corpses.

"Sir Chou Jiu, at this slow pace, when will we ever breach the bronze hall?" Mo Wu Tian urged.

Chou Jiu snickered: "Moving slowly lets the righteous path take the brunt so we can profit from the chaos."

As Fang Yuan's slave, Chou Jiu's sole goal was to stall for time.

"Chou Jiu, what are you thinking? We must charge aggressively, not crawl like this! You old fool, can't you see? We're already in deep trouble! If we stop, we'll be surrounded and in far worse danger than the righteous path!" Mo Wu Tian shouted, stomping in fury.

Chou Jiu glared and shouted back: "Junior Mo Wu Tian, what do you know? Look how many died following your lead earlier! When we fight the righteous faction for the immortal treasure, they will be our enemies—we must weaken them!"

Furious, Mo Wu Tian yelled: "Old fool, stay and die if you want—I am seizing the immortal treasure!"

Saying so, he moved ahead and charged towards the bronze hall.

Displaying their full might, experts neared the bronze hall, led by two prominent figures on the frontlines.

It was none other than Xiao Mang and Mo Wu Tian.

Their momentum was short-lived as the two dog emperors, Ba Huang and Ying Ming, appeared to block their advance.

"It's time." Blood-soaked on the frontline, Tie Ruo Nan halted, catching her breath.

Guarding her were the Tie clan's four old-timers and Tie Bai Qi.

Tie Bai Qi unleashed his beast army to clear a path through the dogs. "The Immortal Gu is paramount!" Tie Ruo Nan gritted her teeth and led the four old-timers inside.

Engrossed in refining Gu, Fang Yuan could do nothing else.

Fang Yuan stopped the land spirit: "Ba Gui, don't overreach; you cannot afford distraction while controlling the dog packs. Bai Ning Bing, intercept her!"

Bai Ning Bing snorted coldly, eyes narrowing as she intercepted Tie Ruo Nan in fierce combat.

Before Fang Yuan, the cloud of smoke roiled violently.

Without Feng Tian Yu's aid, his progress was sluggish, having only just added the five-hundred-year lifespan Gu.

Behind him, as Tie and Bai fought, Tie clan's four old-timers joined in.

Outmatched and overwhelmed, Bai Ning Bing shouted: "Fang Zheng, I can't hold them! How much longer do you need?"

The smoke absorbed the lifespan Gu, transforming into blood fields laden with golden wheat.

A few exchanges later, Bai Ning Bing gasped: "I can't hold on! Fang Yuan, I'm self-destructing!"

Frost rapidly gathered as chilling air swept the hall, plunging the temperature.

Fang Yuan turned with difficulty to see the hall turning into a frozen world. Bai Ning Bing floated aloft, her body crystallizing into ice just as it had during her self-detonation on Qing Mao Mountain.

"The Northern Dark Ice Soul physique?" Tie Ruo Nan cried out in shock, but she was too late—ice sealed her like an insect in amber.

The ice continued spreading towards Fang Yuan.

"Bai Ning Bing? Bai Ning Bing!" Fang Yuan called with apparent worry, but received no response.

"Damn it!" Fang Yuan cursed, rising and sending Yang Gu toward her.

Midway, however, Yang Gu wheeled around and returned to his right hand.

Simultaneously, the smoke condensed into a Gu worm and landed in his left hand.

Resembling a golden peanut shell, the Gu dazzled with bright red blood markings across its surface.

Fang Yuan chuckled, deploying liquid metal Gu to block the encroaching frost.

He looked at Bai Ning Bing: "Have you acted enough?"

The frost came to a stop.

Fang Yuan laughed again: "Did you enjoy cooperating with the Tie clan?"

Dropping the act, Bai Ning Bing reformed her flesh body and stared in shock and suspicion: "How did you—"

"In Shang Clan City, you broke your vow, then secretly contacted Tie Ruo Nan and schemed with the four old-timers. Did you think I was unaware?"

Bai Ning Bing landed, dumbfounded and stunned beyond words.

Tie Ruo Nan shattered the ice, rallying the old-timers: "Demon, you cannot escape today!"

Fang Yuan chuckled, raising his left arm: "You rely on fixed star Gu? What if I sever my arm?"

"Hahaha!" Tie Ruo Nan suddenly laughed.

Tie Ruo Nan shouted: "Stop bluffing! Once the experts outside break in, how will you defend that half-finished Immortal Gu?"

"Do you think you can still succeed? Impossible! You have no time left before the hall is overrun. Where can you run? Can you fly out? Hehe, you only way out now is to surrender to our Tie clan, and contribute that half-done Immortal Gu to us, as well as surrender the old clan leader's Gu worms, and enter the Demon Suppression Tower for reform, that's the only way you can keep your life."

# Chapter 404: Refining Immortal Gu again!

Tie Ruo Nan stepped forward, eyes razor-sharp: "Fang Yuan, you are smart enough to understand your predicament!"

"I understand my situation perfectly," Fang Yuan replied calmly, "and I understand yours even better."

A cold smile touched his lips: "The refinement may be incomplete, but that leaves me with enough immortal essence to slaughter you all!"

Just then, Ba Gui appeared tearfully: "It's useless! Slaying them will expend too much immortal essence, leaving none to sustain the refinement. Worse, the blessed land is riddled with holes and will soon collapse into nothingness!"

"Young man, your miscalculations have ruined us; the refinement has failed!" the land spirit wailed.

"No, there is still hope—the refinement has not failed yet," Fang Yuan smiled.

"Ba Gui, do you still want to refine the second aperture Gu or not?" Fang Yuan transmitted.

"Could you have a way?" Hope flickered in Ba Gui. "You must have anticipated this!"

"No, this exceeds my expectations. As a mere mortal, I cannot turn the tide," Fang Yuan confessed frankly. "Ba Gui, I failed you; this refinement is indeed a failure."

Then he shifted tone: "Yet this setback is only temporary; hope remains."

"I am the only one who knows the recipe, and the half-finished Gu is with me. You must protect me."
