"Of course I have to protect you! You are the only person who satisfied the requirements of the blessed land and are also a reborn Gu Immortal, there is a huge chance of the refinement of the second aperture Gu succeeding in the future even if it is a failure this time."

"Good. But your protection is temporary; soon you will perish, and our remaining immortal essence cannot eliminate every enemy in the blessed land. Even if you teleport me outside, packs of Gu Masters await on San Cha Mountain," Fang Yuan smirked sinisterly.

"Then how should I protect you?" the land spirit asked.

Fang Yuan chuckled meaningfully: "Ba Gui, do you recall chapter two, section three of The Legends of Ren Zu?"

The land spirit hesitated: "You mean... But first, you need the light of glory from the immemorial era."

Fang Yuan chuckled with satisfaction, pointing toward the ceiling of the hall: "Look, the light has arrived!"

Impatient with Ying Ming's resistance, Xiao Mang flew into the sky.

Killer move — Torrential Light Stream!

The roof shattered instantly as a cascading waterfall of light surged toward Fang Yuan.

"The light of glory of the immemorial era!" the land spirit cried in shock.

Fang Yuan activated his bone wings and liquid metal Gu with a hearty laugh.

Under Tie Ruo Nan and Bai Ning Bing's stunned gazes, Fang Yuan flapped his wings, soaring straight into the river of light!

"*Time waits for no man*—land spirit, assist me: divine travel Gu!"

Immortal Gu divine travel landed on his palm.

"Jade sky Gu!"

A beam of green light shot from his aperture into his hand, forming a hollow jade-green bamboo.

"Something is wrong—I know him! Everything Fang Yuan does carries deep purpose. We must stop him!" Bai Ning Bing urged, an ominous premonition swelling in her heart.

"Four old-timers!" She shouted.

"Understood!" The four old-timers took positions in the four cardinal directions, clasping their wrists in formation.

Killer move — Infinite search and lock!

Almost simultaneously, Fang Yuan's left arm glowed eerie blue, faintly revealing fixed star Gu.

Space tore open around him, spawning four python-like chains.

"Reacting only now? Too late!" Fang Yuan sneered.

Divine travel Gu plunged into his left arm, devouring fixed star Gu.

Losing their connection to fixed star Gu, the four old-timers coughed up blood.

Having devoured fixed star Gu, divine travel Gu entered the hollow jade sky Gu and swiftly formed a cocoon.

# Chapter 405:  Spreading my wings and flying away today, becoming an immortal and whipping the phoenix on another day! (Double Chapter)

The immemorial light of glory shone upon the cocoon.

Yet divine travel Gu was not enough; The Legends of Ren Zu recorded—

Fearing divine travel Gu would teleport him into danger while drunk, Verdant Great Sun worried. Ashamed, divine travel Gu instructed: "Fly into azure heaven among the nine heavens to find a bamboo forest. Pluck a jade bamboo colored like the jade sky. Then go to blue heaven at night and collect eight-sided diamonds from within star fragments. At dawn, use the rising sun's light of glory to turn me into fixed immortal travel Gu, ending my random teleportations."

Divine travel Gu, jade sky Gu, fixed star Gu, and immemorial light—all conditions were fulfilled!

Realizing this upon rebirth, Fang Yuan abandoned second aperture Gu, shifting his objective to refining fixed immortal travel Gu.

Absorbing the sunlight, the cocoon trembled slightly as an Immortal Gu's aura leaked out uncontrollably.

As the Immortal Gu's aura intensified, an interruption struck at the crucial moment.

"Trying to use my power to refine an Immortal Gu? Hmph!" Realizing this, Xiao Mang immediately halted his killer move.

The torrential light stream ended!

He activated two third watch Gu targeting the cocoon.

Time accelerated ninefold at once, causing the Immortal Gu's aura to surge!

Fang Yuan used undefeated hundred battles Gu, guaranteeing the refinement's success!

The cocoon broke open, and a green butterfly flew out — fixed immortal travel Gu!

Fang Yuan flew out from the light waterfall as fixed immortal travel Gu circled him, its fluttering wings scattering beautiful pollen-like green light.

"You actually refined fixed immortal travel Gu, incredible! As expected of a future Gu Immortal." The land spirit praised with a sigh.

Fang Yuan laughed: "Fixed immortal travel Gu lets one roam anywhere worldwide, but it requires immortal essence to activate."

Ba Gui replied: "Immortal essence remains in the cauldron. Picture your destination clearly; the image must closely match reality."

"I understand that."

Ba Gui sighed earnestly: "Using fixed immortal travel will let you escape, but without immortal essence, the blessed land will perish immediately. Death is a relief for me. I only hope you refine second aperture Gu in the future and don't waste this opportunity. Before we part, do you have anything to say to me?"

The land spirit did not know Fang Yuan was about to take an even greater risk. But life was a gamble—without taking chances, how could one succeed?

*If a man lacks aspirations grand as heaven, he lets down his eight-foot frame!*

Brimming with lofty ambition and boiling anticipation, Fang Yuan felt an urge before the crowd and recited a poem.

*Since antiquity land immortals pass like wind; today three kings return to eternal slumber.*

*Who escapes failure? Verdant Sun died in regret. Start anew and proclaim oneself king.*

*A carp swims up the heavenly falls, traveling desolate lands alone to defy heaven.*

*Spreading wings to fly today, becoming immortal and whipping the phoenix another day!*

The land spirit shouted: "Grand aspirations, I will send you on your journey!"

Immortal essence entered fixed immortal travel Gu; jade light flashed, and Fang Yuan vanished into thin air.

Only his clothes remained, floating in the air like a severed kite.

Central Continent, Tian Ti mountain.

On Dang Hun Mountain in Hu Immortal blessed land, the battle for ownership was nearing its end.

"I am the great Feng Jin Huang, how could I lose? I've never lost since birth, and today is no exception! Come forth, dream wings!"

With Feng Jin Huang's shout, beautiful wings sprouted from her shoulders.

Under Fang Zheng, stunned gaze, she overtook him, taking back her head.

Her dream wings folded at the cliff edge, panting heavily from severe soul dizziness.

Her hands were already at the peak's edge; she was just one step away!

She mustered her remaining strength to pull herself up, but at that exact moment—

Jade light flashed, revealing a man upon the mountain peak.

The man was completely naked, blood dripping from a puncture in his left forearm. His youthful, muscular physique exuded the aura of a battle-hardened veteran.

"Brother?!" Shocked, Fang Zheng slipped and fell down the cliff.

Raising her snow-white neck, Feng Jin Huang stared up at Fang Yuan, frozen like a wide-eyed statue.

"I really came here? Tsk, fixed immortal travel Gu has such a weakness, it cannot transport clothes along with me. But thankfully, I placed my Gu worms inside my aperture, and brought them along."

"Eh? Isn't this person below me Feng Jin Huang?"

The Biography of Feng Jin Huang proved accurate. Arriving one step ahead under rules permitting Immortal Gu, he was now Hu Immortal blessed land's owner!

"The gamble paid off! I gained something even better—Hu Immortal blessed land and fixed immortal travel Gu!" Fang Yuan laughed triumphantly.

"Unfortunately, she holds dream wings; I cannot kill her yet." Glancing down with regret, Fang Yuan raised his right foot.

Under ten Gu Immortals' watch, his right foot stepped onto Feng Jin Huang's face, smooth as jade against his sole.

"Scram." Fang Yuan kicked the dazed, weakened Feng Jin Huang down the cliff.

Fang Yuan turned around, facing the land spirit.

"Little Hu Immortal, aren't you calling me you master?"

"Ma... master..." The little girl stared at Fang Yuan, shocked.

Recovering her senses, she quickly shielded her large crystal-clear eyes with small hands.

Blushing furiously, she lowered her head, stomped her foot, and squeaked: "Master, have you no shame? You're so old already, put on some clothes quickly!"
